### [1] [Symmetry-aware super-resolution of crystal orientation maps via invariant latent-space learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10898)
*Umang Garg,Warren Zamudio,McLean P. Echlin,Samantha H. Daly,Tresa M. Pollock,B. S. Manjunath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出对称群感知超分辨率注意力网络（SG-SRAN），在商空间 SO(3)/G 上实现 EBSD 晶体取向图的超分辨率重建，以极少可训练参数达到与千万级参数骨干网络相当的精度，并具备跨合金零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EBSD 可实验测定晶体取向图，但采集时间约束限制了空间分辨率。由于取向数据位于商空间 SO(3)/G（G 为晶体对称群），标准欧氏插值会混合对称等价表示并模糊晶界，因此需要将晶体对称性与边界保持嵌入网络设计的超分辨率方法。

Method: SG-SRAN 采用冻结的局部等距编码器，将等价取向映射至共同隐表示，使欧氏距离近似于错取向角；在该空间内执行超分辨率，并将每个高分辨率 token 限制在特征一致的局部支撑域内以防止跨晶界混合；最后通过基于字典的解码器恢复有效取向。

Result: 在 FCC 与 HCP 基准上，SG-SRAN 仅用 27–49k 可训练参数即匹配 1500–1600 万参数的骨干网络，同时取得最低的 p68 误差、最高的反极图保真度，并能零样本迁移至未见合金。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将晶体对称群与边界保持作为设计先验内嵌于网络结构，可在商空间 SO(3)/G 上以极高参数效率实现高保真取向图超分辨率，并具备对未见材料的泛化能力。

Abstract: Crystal-orientation maps are physical fields defined only up to crystal symmetry; electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) resolves them experimentally, but acquisition-time constraints limit spatial resolution. Unlike conventional images, EBSD data lie on the quotient space $\mathrm{SO}(3)/G$, where $G$ is the crystal-symmetry group. Standard Euclidean interpolation can therefore mix symmetry-equivalent representations and blur grain boundaries. We introduce the Symmetry-Group-Aware Super-Resolution Attention Network (SG-SRAN), which incorporates crystal symmetry and boundary preservation by design. A frozen, locally isometric encoder maps equivalent orientations to a common latent representation in which Euclidean distance approximates misorientation. Super-resolution is performed in this space, with each high-resolution token restricted to a feature-consistent local support to prevent cross-boundary mixing. A dictionary-based decoder then recovers valid orientations. Across FCC and HCP benchmarks, SG-SRAN matches 15-16 million parameter backbones using only 27-49k trainable parameters, while achieving the lowest p68 errors, highest inverse-pole-figure fidelity, and zero-shot transfer to unseen alloys.

</details>

### [2] [Geometric Ginzburg-Landau theory of charge ordering and commensurability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10678)
*Aneesh Agarwal,Rutvij Gholap,Mohammad Saeed Bahramy,Robert-Jan Slager*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究揭示量子几何（quantum geometry）在电荷密度波（charge density wave, CDW）形成中的核心作用，提出包含新贡献的有效Ginzburg-Landau理论，并给出CDW形成与公度性转变（commensurability transitions）的一般判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何概念近期在物理响应、超导与光学跃迁等领域带来新见解，且在近平色散（nearly flat dispersion）体系中效应最显著；但其在CDW形成这一影响材料输运（如金属-绝缘体转变）的重要现象中的作用尚不明晰。

Method: 推导了一个包含此前未探索贡献的有效Ginzburg-Landau理论（Ginzburg-Landau theory），在其中电子-声子相互作用（electron-phonon interactions）纯粹表现为电子量子几何的增强或抑制，并据此建立CDW形成与公度性转变的一般判据。

Result: 以一类过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenides）为基准进行验证，解决了长期存在的难题：既有纯动能CDW判据无法描述正确的有序波矢（ordering wavevector）。

Conclusion: 该理论提供了与多个实验电荷有序体系直接相关的稳健判据与基础见解，并可直接推广至超导等声子介导相（phonon-mediated phases），以及用于研究此类状态间的相互作用；更一般地，该框架为探究量子几何在相变中的作用提供了范式。

Abstract: The concept of quantum geometry has recently led to reinvigorated insights in a wide range of fields including physical responses, superconductivity, and optical transitions, with effects most pronounced in systems with nearly flat dispersion. Here, we show that it plays an essential role in charge density wave formation (CDW) -- an important physical phenomenon that is responsible for driving various sharp changes in material transport properties including metal-insulator transitions. We derive an effective Ginzburg-Landau theory including uncharted contributions and, as a highlight, discover a general criterion for both CDW formation and commensurability transitions where underlying electron-phonon interactions manifest purely as electronic quantum geometric enhancements/suppressions. We benchmark our framework in a class of transition-metal dichalcogenides and resolve a longstanding puzzle where well-established purely kinetic CDW criteria fail in describing the correct ordering wavevector. Besides rendering robust criteria and fundamental insights that are immediately relevant to several experimental charge ordering systems, our theory can also be applied directly to other phonon-mediated phases such as superconductivity, and can be used as an important tool to explore the interplay between various such states. More generally, our framework provides a recipe for investigating the role of quantum geometry in phase transitions.

</details>


### [3] [Lattice 2-group symmetries: operators, defects, and gauging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10682)
*Lucas Z. Brito,Salvatore D. Pace*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作为有限 2-群对称性（2-group symmetry）在 ${2+1}$ 维量子格点系统中的实现提供了显式构造，涵盖分裂 2-群与中心 2-群两类，并给出相应局域哈密顿量、可解极限及规范理论格点实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论中对 2-群对称性已有丰富预期，但其在具有有限维张量积希尔伯特空间的格点系统（lattice system）中缺乏具体实现。该文旨在搭建格点实现与场论预言之间的对应，检验 $rho$ 与 $[\beta]$ 的各类表现。

Method: 在 ${2+1}$ 维张量积希尔伯特空间上构造格点对称算符：针对 Postnikov 类 $[\beta]\in\mathcal{H}^3(G,A)$ 平凡的分裂 2-群（split 2-group）和 $G$ 作用 $rho$ 平凡的中心 2-群（central 2-group）；分析算符变换、对称性缺陷（defect）结构，并通过规范化（gauging）得到对偶融合 2-范畴（fusion 2-category）对称性；进一步提出局域对称哈密顿量族并取可解极限。

Result: 格点分裂 2-群对称算符为在位（onsite）形式，而中心 2-群算符非在位、需引入辅助比特（ancilla）方可化为在位；两类实现中 $rho$ 与 $[\beta]$ 的表现与量子场论预期一致。所提哈密顿量的可解极限给出自发 2-群对称性破缺相与含对称性富集拓扑序（symmetry-enriched topological order）的相；其中一个极限下规范化哈密顿量即对应 2-群规范理论（2-group gauge theory）的精确可解格点实现。

Conclusion: 该文建立了有限 2-群对称性的格点实现框架，验证了格点构造与场论预言的吻合，并借助可解极限给出可计算基态简并度的 2-群规范理论格点模型。

Abstract: We construct and study lattice realizations of finite 2-group symmetries in ${2+1}$d quantum lattice systems with finite-dimensional tensor-product Hilbert spaces. We focus on two broad classes of 2-groups with 0-form symmetry group $G$ and 1-form symmetry group $A$: split 2-groups with trivial Postnikov class ${[β]\in\mathcal{H}^3(G,A)}$, and central 2-groups with trivial action ${ρ\colon G\to\text{Aut}(A)}$. In both cases, we construct symmetry operators on the full tensor-product Hilbert space that become 2-group symmetry operators when restricted to the topological subspace of the lattice $A$ 1-form symmetry. While the lattice split 2-group symmetry operators are onsite, the lattice central 2-group symmetry operators are not, and can only be made onsite after introducing ancillae. We extensively explore various manifestations of $ρ$ and $[β]$ for these lattice 2-group symmetry operators and demonstrate their agreement with expectations from quantum field theory. These manifestations arise in the transformation of operators carrying symmetry charge, the structure of lattice 2-group symmetry defects, and the dual fusion 2-category symmetries obtained by gauging the lattice 2-group symmetries. We further propose families of local symmetric Hamiltonians for both classes of lattice 2-group symmetries and identify exactly solvable limits lying in phases with spontaneous 2-group symmetry breaking and nontrivial symmetry-enriched topological order. In one such limit, the gauged Hamiltonians are exactly solvable lattice realizations of the corresponding 2-group gauge theories, whose ground-state degeneracies we calculate.

</details>


### [4] [A Unified Theory of Collective Magnon and Orbiton Excitations in Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10708)
*Bishal Das,Chanchal K. Barman,Aftab Alam*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在装饰方格子（decorated square lattice）上构建扩展的 Kugel'-Khomskiĭ 自旋-轨道模型，统一描述由非磁环境不等价与关联驱动轨道有序两种机制稳定的交变磁性（altermagnetism），并导出互不杂化但相互依赖的磁振子（magnon）与轨道振子（orbiton）激发及其手性劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁性作为一种净磁化为零却具有动量依赖自旋劈裂的共线磁相近期受到关注；除非磁环境不等价机制外，强电子关联可通过轨道有序在等价非磁环境中产生自发交变磁性。已有研究多集中于电子结构，尚缺乏统一理解这两类微观机制所对应的集体激发。

Method: 在装饰方格子（decorated square lattice）上建立同时包含非磁环境不等价与关联驱动轨道有序的扩展 Kugel'-Khomskiĭ 自旋-轨道模型，并采用自洽平均场自旋波-轨道波（spin-wave orbital-wave）形式求解；同时利用互补的经典蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟考察有限温度行为。

Result: 模型展示出互不杂化但相互依赖的磁振子（magnon）与轨道振子（orbiton）激发，并呈现特征性手性劈裂（chiral-splitting）。该劈裂源于两种微观贡献：来自非磁环境不等价的晶格依赖项，以及在等价非磁环境中依然存在的轨道有序诱导交换各向异性项。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了两类交变磁性对应的集体激发。有限温度分析显示自旋波与轨道波近似会因虚假的一级相变（1st-order transition）而失效，而经典蒙特卡洛模拟恢复出预期的连续二级相变（2nd-order）行为，从而为理解交变磁体中的磁振子与轨道振子集体激发提供了统一微观框架。

Abstract: Altermagnetism has recently emerged as a distinct collinear magnetic phase exhibiting momentum-dependent spin-splitting despite vanishing net magnetization, as a consequence of inequivalent non-magnetic environments. Lately, it has been proposed that strong electronic correlations may yield spontaneous altermagnetism due to orbital ordering even for equivalent non-magnetic environments. While previous studies have largely focused on the electronic structure, a unified understanding of the collective excitations associated with these two different microscopic mechanisms stabilizing altermagnetism remains absent. Here, we develop an extended Kugel'-Khomskiĭ spin-orbital model on a decorated square lattice that simultaneously incorporates inequivalent non-magnetic environments and correlation-driven orbital ordering within a common theoretical framework. Employing a self-consistent mean-field spin-wave orbital-wave formalism, we demonstrate the emergence of mutually unhybridized but interdependent magnon and orbiton excitations exhibiting characteristic chiral-splitting. We show that the splitting originates from two distinct microscopic contributions: a lattice-dependent term arising from inequivalent non-magnetic environments and an orbital-order-induced exchange-anisotropy term that survives even for equivalent non-magnetic environments. The proposed framework therefore unifies the collective excitations associated with both types of altermagnetism. We further investigate the finite-temperature evolution of the coupled spin-orbital system, revealing the breakdown of spin-wave and orbital-wave approximations through spurious 1st-order transitions, while complementary classical Monte Carlo simulations recover the expected continuous 2nd-order behaviour. Our work establishes a unified microscopic framework for understanding collective magnon and orbiton excitations in altermagnets.

</details>


### [5] [Competing Interlayer Loop Currents and Superconductivity in the Bilayer $t$-$J_\perp$-$V$ Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10926)
*Luciano Zinni,Fabricio Gómez,Jun Zhan,Matías Bejas,Xianxin Wu,Andreas P. Schnyder,Andrés Greco*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用受控大-$N$展开研究双层镍酸盐（nickelate）的单轨道 $d_{x^2-y^2}$ 双层 $t$-$J_\perp$-$V$ 模型，发现层间自旋交换 $J_\perp$ 同时驱动面外 $s$ 波超导与面外键序相（$z$-BOP），二者竞争形成穹顶状超导区与丰富相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加压与薄膜双层镍酸盐中高-$T_c$ 超导的发现，及其与铜氧化物（cuprate）超导体的潜在相似性，使该体系成为凝聚态物理活跃课题；层间交换耦合（interlayer exchange coupling）在其中作用关键，需从强关联模型层面予以厘清。

Method: 采用受控大-$N$ 展开（controlled large-$N$ expansion），在平均场（mean-field）层次及其之上，研究单轨道 $d_{x^2-y^2}$ 双层 $t$-$J_\perp$-$V$ 模型，其中 $V$ 表示库仑相互作用（Coulomb interactions），重点考察面外自旋交换相互作用 $J_\perp$。

Result: $J_\perp$ 同时触发面外 $s$ 波超导与面外键序相（out-of-plane bond-order phase，$z$-BOP）不稳定性；$z$-BOP 产生以虚部为主导的复 $z$ 轴跃迁，驱动面外电流并诱导面内电流，在竖直格方块（vertical plaquette）上自发形成破坏时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）的环流态（loop-current state）。环流相与超导的竞争给出穹顶状超导区，最优超导出现在 $z$-BOP 量子临界点（quantum critical point）附近。

Conclusion: 所得相图包含纯环流区、低掺杂共存相、较高掺杂的纯超导态以及关联金属态（correlated metallic state）四个区域。

Abstract: The recent discovery of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in pressurized and thin-film bilayer nickelates, featuring a strong interlayer exchange coupling, and their potential similarities with cuprate superconductors, has made this a very active topic in condensed matter physics. In the present paper we study the strongly correlated one-orbital ($d_{x^2-y^2}$) bilayer $t$-$J_\perp$-$V$ model for nickelates, where $V$ denotes the Coulomb interactions, using a controlled large-$N$ expansion at and beyond the mean-field level. Focusing on the out-of-plane spin exchange interaction ($J_\perp$), we find that it triggers both out-of-plane $s$-wave superconductivity and an out-of-plane bond-order phase ($z$-BOP) instability. The $z$-BOP gives rise to a complex $z$-axis hopping dominated by its imaginary component, which drives out-of-plane currents and induces in-plane ones, spontaneously forming on the vertical plaquettes a loop-current state that breaks time-reversal symmetry. Competition between this loop-current phase and superconductivity yields a dome-shaped superconducting region, with optimal superconductivity occurring near the $z$-BOP quantum critical point. The resulting phase diagram features a pure loop-current region, a low-doping coexistence phase, a pure superconducting state at higher doping, and a correlated metallic state.

</details>


### [6] [Optically induced metallic state with persistent monoclinic symmetry in NdNiO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11238)
*Christian Tzschaschel,Felix Utsch,Qi Song,Spencer Doyle,Grace A. Pan,Charles M. Brooks,Julia A. Mundy,Clemens von Korff Schmising,Stefan Eisebitt*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在 NdNiO$_3$ 光致绝缘体-金属转变（insulator-to-metal transition）中，金属性与结构对称性在非平衡条件下解耦：金属反射率在较低激发通量下即出现，而正交对称性需更高通量才恢复，从而稳定一种兼具金属反射率与绝缘相单斜对称性的亚稳"单斜金属"态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联材料（correlated materials）中电子有序与结构有序在非平衡条件下是否仍保持耦合，是核心未解问题；该研究旨在直接追踪二者在光致相变过程中的演化关系。

Method: 采用时间分辨光学反射率（time-resolved optical reflectivity）与对对称性敏感的二阶谐波产生（second-harmonic generation, SHG）技术，同时追踪 NdNiO$_3$ 光致绝缘体-金属转变中的金属性与对称性。

Result: 金属反射率（metallic reflectivity）在显著低于正交高温对称性（orthorhombic high-temperature symmetry）恢复所需的激发通量下即出现，稳定了一种结合金属相反射率与绝缘相单斜对称性（monoclinic symmetry）的亚稳态；仅当通量大幅提高时，对称性才完全恢复至高温相。

Conclusion: 结果证明了金属性与结构对称性在非平衡条件下的解耦，并确立了同步多探针光谱（simultaneous multiprobe spectroscopy）作为识别关联材料中涌现相（emergent phases）的有力手段。

Abstract: Understanding whether electronic and structural order remain coupled under nonequilibrium conditions is a central challenge in correlated materials. Here, we simultaneously track metallicity and symmetry across the photoinduced insulator-to-metal transition in NdNiO$_3$ using time-resolved optical reflectivity and symmetry-sensitive second-harmonic generation. We find that metallic reflectivity emerges at significantly lower excitation fluence than restoration of the orthorhombic high-temperature symmetry. As a result, optical excitation stabilizes a metastable state that combines the reflectivity of the metallic phase with the monoclinic symmetry of the insulating phase, revealing an optically induced monoclinic metal. Only at substantially higher fluences does the symmetry fully recover to that of the high-temperature phase. These results demonstrate a nonequilibrium decoupling of metallicity and structural symmetry and establish simultaneous multiprobe spectroscopy as a powerful approach for identifying emergent phases in correlated materials.

</details>


### [7] [Instabilities in self-consistent diagrammatic approaches and how to cure them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11405)
*Marcel Gievers,Herbert Eßl,Stefan Rohshap,Anna Kauch,Alessandro Toschi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作指出，在关联量子材料（correlated quantum materials）计算中，自洽图解方法（self-consistent diagrammatic approaches）于中强电子相互作用区域出现的“误导性收敛（misleading convergence）”源于迭代格式的稳定性条件，而非图解描述本身失效；通过反转自洽不动点雅可比矩阵（Jacobian）的不稳定特征方向重构迭代流程，可在全参数范围内稳定物理不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自洽图解方法虽被广泛用于计算关联量子材料的物理性质，但在最受关注的参数区域（中强电子相互作用）其适用性反而受到严重限制。核心障碍是“误导性收敛（misleading convergence）”，即迭代格式无论数值精度如何都会收敛到非物理不动点。

Method: 在玻色子交换形式（boson-exchange formalism）的通用图解框架下，对若干已有的自洽多电子方法进行显式验证，定位问题根源；并采用近期提出的方案（Phys. Rev. Lett. 137, 016502 (2026)），通过反转与自洽算法不动点相关的雅可比矩阵（Jacobian）的不稳定特征方向来重新定义迭代流程。

Result: 验证表明，误导性收敛的根源完全在于相应迭代格式的稳定性条件，而非自洽图解描述的内在失效。在可精确求解的测试模型（exactly solvable models）上进行的系统计算显示，所考虑图解格式的物理不动点在整个参数范围内（包括最具挑战性的非微扰/强耦合区域）均被成功稳定。

Conclusion: 该见解提供了简单且通用的补救方法，使自洽图解方案的物理不动点在整个参数范围内、包括强耦合（strong-coupling）等困难区域都能稳定获得，从而扩展了此类方法在关联量子材料计算中的适用范围。

Abstract: While self-consistent diagrammatic approaches are widely used to compute the physical properties of correlated quantum materials, their applicability may get severely hindered precisely in the parameter regions, where the most exciting physics is observed. One of the major issues, referred to as "misleading convergence", is the tendency of iterative schemes to converge to unphysical fixed points for intermediate-to-strong electronic interactions, regardless of numerical accuracy of the computation. Here, we explicitly verify that the origin of this problem in several established self-consistent many-electron approaches, defined in the general diagrammatic framework of the boson-exchange formalism, resides exclusively in the stability condition of the respective iteration schemes, and not in an intrinsic breakdown of their self-consistent diagrammatic description. This insight enables a simple and general remedy, as recently proposed in Phys. Rev. Lett. 137, 016502 (2026): The redefinition of the iterative procedure, by inverting the unstable eigendirections of the Jacobian associated to the fixed point of the self-consistent algorithm. We illustrate the successful outcome of this procedure by means of systematic calculations performed on testbed, exactly solvable, models. Our results demonstrate that the physical fixed point of the diagrammatic schemes we considered can be stabilized, de facto, across the entire parameter range, including the most challenging nonperturbative/strong-coupling regimes.

</details>


### [8] [Topological band structure of excitons in octochlore KTb$_{3}$F$_{10}$: an analog of the Luttinger Semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11466)
*Olivier Demortier,Françoise Damay,Paul McClarty,Stéphane Raymond,Jacques Ollivier,Sylvain Petit*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体固体中能带结构的对称性与拓扑是凝聚态物理的核心主题，已推广至电子能带、光子晶体、声子与磁激发等平台；而磁系统通常具有磁长程有序与时间反演对称性破缺，其可能的能带结构仍有待拓展。本文旨在研究以单态（singlet）简单乘积为基态、在其上存在色散晶体场激发（crystal field excitation）的磁系统中的能带结构。

Method: 采用高分辨率中子谱学（high-resolution neutron spectroscopy）测量 KTb$_{3}$F$_{10}$（八氯格（octochlore lattice）的实验实现）中玻色型激子（bosonic exciton）的色散关系；并结合理论建模分析磁相互作用与能带拓扑在晶体对称性和时间反演对称性下的相互作用，其中主导耦合为长程磁偶极（magnetostatic dipolar）相互作用。

Result: 实验揭示了 KTb$_{3}$F$_{10}$ 中玻色型激子的色散关系，并在散射强度中观察到捏点（pinch point）特征。长程磁偶极相互作用在布里渊区中心（zone center）的奇异性传递至散射强度；当偶极作用范围截断至有限壳层数时，捏点仍以受立方对称性（cubic symmetry）保护的三带二次能带接触（quadratic band touching）形式存在。

Conclusion: 该三带二次能带接触模型与 Luttinger 半金属（Luttinger semi-metal）相关，提供了一个描述捏点散射强度出现的通用长波长模型，并可涵盖真实长程偶极模型。研究拓展了磁系统可能具有的能带结构范围。

Abstract: Understanding the symmetry and topology of band structures in crystalline solids has been among the defining themes of a generation of condensed matter physicists, with implications across many different platforms including electronic bands, photonic crystals, phonons and magnetic excitations. In the latter systems, there is usually magnetic long-range order and time reversal symmetry breaking. Here we further enrich the possible band structures available to magnetic systems by examining dispersive crystal field excitations above a ground state that is a simple product of singlets. In these systems, there is an interplay of magnetic interactions and band topology with both crystal and time reversal symmetries. Using high-resolution neutron spectroscopy we reveal the dispersion relations of bosonic excitons in KTb$_{3}$F$_{10}$, an experimental realization of the octochlore lattice. We show that there are pinch point features in the scattering intensity. The dominant coupling in this system is the long-range magnetostatic dipolar interaction, whose singularity at the zone center is passed onto the scattering intensity. However, the pinch points survive when the range of the dipoles is truncated to finite shell number in the form of a three-band quadratic band touching protected by cubic symmetry. This model, which is related to the Luttinger semi-metal, provides a general long wavelength model for the emergence of pinch point scattering intensity encompassing also the true long-range dipolar model.

### [9] [Certified reduced-basis emulation of conformal field theories on the fuzzy sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11675)
*Virgil V. Baran*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作结合确定总角动量态上的精确对角化（exact diagonalization）与认证约化基自举模拟器（certified reduced-basis emulator），将模糊球（fuzzy-sphere）正则化下三维共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）的临界点搜索成本降低约两个数量级，并成功复现已知模型结果、定位 O(2) 模型临界线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊球正则化虽可实现三维 CFT 的数值研究，但需要在微观耦合参数空间中逐点检验临界性，搜索成本高昂。因此需要一种能在耦合空间中连续重构能量与可观测量的高效方法，以降低定位 CFT 的计算代价。

Method: 采用基于具有确定总角动量（total angular momentum）态的精确对角化，配合认证约化基模拟器（certified reduced-basis emulator）；后者由每个系统尺寸下少量精确解出发，连续重构整个耦合区间内的能量、可观测量及其参数导数。

Result: 方法复现了已发表的模糊球 Ising 模型基态能量分析，以及三味（three-flavor）模糊球实现的 O(2) Wilson–Fisher CFT 共形数据（含参数导数定义的量）。在无 CFT 先验输入下应用于 O(2) 模型，基态能量判据定位临界耦合，与规模大一倍以上的密度矩阵重整化群（density matrix renormalization group, DMRG）结果相差在半百分点以内，并绘制出耦合平面上的临界线。

Conclusion: 基态判据可稳健地定位临界线，但其所选定的线上具体点确定度较弱；所计算的维度在临界线其他位置上与自举（bootstrap）结果最为接近。搜索成本降低约两个数量级。

Abstract: Fuzzy-sphere regularization gives numerical access to three-dimensional conformal field theories (CFTs), but locating one requires testing for criticality across the parameter space of microscopic couplings. We reduce the search cost by about two orders of magnitude, combining an exact diagonalization based on states having definite total angular momentum with a certified reduced-basis emulator that reconstructs energies, observables and their derivatives continuously across the couplings from a handful of exact solutions per system size. The combination reproduces the published ground-state-energy analysis of the fuzzy-sphere Ising model and the published conformal data of a three-flavor fuzzy-sphere realization of the O(2) Wilson--Fisher CFT, including quantities defined by parameter derivatives. Applied to the O(2) model with no CFT input, the ground-state-energy criterion locates the critical coupling to within half a percent of a density matrix renormalization group value obtained on systems more than twice the size, and maps the critical line of the model across the coupling plane. Ground-state criteria locate the critical line robustly, but the point they select along it is weakly determined, and the computed dimensions come closest to the bootstrap elsewhere along the line.

</details>


### [10] [Robust Spin-1 Haldane Topology under Itinerant Doping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11591)
*Satoshi Nishimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作研究铁磁 Hund 交换耦合的两条 t–J 链中自旋-1 Haldane 对称保护拓扑（SPT）相的空穴掺杂行为，DMRG 结果显示掺杂后弦序、自旋能隙与 Haldane 纠缠结构在较宽掺杂范围内共存，构成具有 C1S0 低能描述且仅含单一无能隙电荷模式的金属性掺杂 Haldane SPT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究可迁移载流子（itinerant hole doping）如何影响自旋-1 Haldane 对称保护拓扑（symmetry-protected topological, SPT）相，即掺杂是否破坏其拓扑特征，以及其低能物理如何被重新组织。

Method: 方法上采用耦合铁磁（ferromagnetic, FM）Hund 交换的两条 t–J 链模型，并使用密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）进行计算，辅以纠缠熵标度分析与稀薄极限（dilute-limit）解析。

Result: 结果显示弦序（string order）、有限自旋能隙与 Haldane 纠缠结构在较宽掺杂范围内共存，且纠缠熵标度与单一无能隙电荷模式一致。低掺杂时短程电荷关联减少自旋-1/2 缺陷梯级（defect rungs）密度，高掺杂时其位置近乎无关联而自旋被集体纳入关联自旋扇区。掺杂 Haldane SPT 终止于完全自旋极化的 FM 相，该相在高掺杂处形成再入（reentrant）区域；稀薄极限分析将两个 FM 边界分别归因于弱 Hund 耦合下的自旋–电荷因子化（spin–charge factorization）与强 Hund 耦合下梯级三重态束缚（rung-triplet binding）导致的配对迁移率降低。

Conclusion: 结论表明可迁移载流子在保持 Haldane 自旋结构特征信号的同时对其进行了重组，从而支持一种金属性掺杂 Haldane SPT 相及其 C1S0 低能描述。

Abstract: We study itinerant hole doping of the spin-$1$ Haldane symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phase in two $t$--$J$ chains coupled by ferromagnetic (FM) Hund exchange. Density-matrix renormalization group calculations show that string order, a finite spin gap, and the Haldane entanglement structure coexist over a broad doping range. Combined with entanglement-entropy scaling consistent with a single gapless charge mode, these results support a metallic doped Haldane SPT with a $C1S0$ low-energy description. At low doping, short-range charge correlations reduce the density of spin-$1/2$ defect rungs, whereas at larger doping their positions become nearly uncorrelated while their spins are collectively incorporated into the correlated spin sector. The doped Haldane SPT terminates in a fully spin-polarized FM phase, which forms a reentrant pocket at high doping in the global phase diagram. Dilute-limit analyses trace the two FM boundaries to spin--charge factorization at weak Hund coupling and to rung-triplet binding with reduced pair mobility at strong Hund coupling. Our results show how mobile carriers reorganize the Haldane spin structure while preserving its characteristic signatures.

</details>


### [11] [Acoustic Wave-Function Imaging of Sublattice Physics in a Kagome Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11678)
*L. Müller,N. Endres,B. Geldiyev,S. Widmann,K. Burkard,M. Ünzelmann,F. Reinert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究在厘米尺度构建声学 kagome 晶格，测量完整激发谱并成像各格点波函数的振幅与相位，从而首次直接观测到 kagome 晶格中具有亚晶格分辨的能带结构，尤其是平带与两类 van Hove 奇异点处的亚晶格织构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 kagome 能带结构包含狄拉克锥（Dirac cone）、鞍点 van Hove 奇异点（van Hove singularity, vHS）与平带（flat band），这些特征在三格点基元内表现出不同的亚晶格局域性；然而，该亚晶格特征的实验证明十分困难，需同时获取实空间波函数及其动量空间织构。

Method: 在厘米尺度上实现声学 kagome 晶格，测量完整激发谱，并对每个晶格位置的波函数进行成像，获取其振幅与相位信息，以研究整个能带结构中随动量变化的亚晶格织构。

Result: 实验获得了平带以及混合亚晶格与纯亚晶格 van Hove 奇异点处的动量依赖亚晶格织构，结果与最小紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）计算高度一致。

Conclusion: 该工作构成了对 kagome 晶格中亚晶格分辨能带结构的首次直接实验观测。

Abstract: The canonical kagome band structure hosts a Dirac cone, saddle-point van Hove singularities (vHS), and a flat band. Characteristically, these features exhibit distinct sublattice localization within the three-site basis of the kagome lattice. However, experimental proof of this sublattice character is challenging and requires direct access to both the real-space wave function and its associated momentum-space texture. Here, we implement an acoustic kagome lattice on the cm scale and measure the full excitation spectrum, thereby imaging the wave function at each lattice site, including both amplitude and phase. This enables investigation of the momentum-dependent sublattice texture across the complete band structure, particularly at the flat band and at the mixed- and pure-sublattice van Hove singularities. Our results are in excellent agreement with minimal tight-binding model calculations and constitutes the first direct experimental observation of the sublattice-resolved band structure in a kagome lattice.

</details>


### [12] [Unraveling the Kagome Antiferromagnetic $3J$ Model and Its Materials: An Integrated Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11745)
*Xin Lu,Andreas Raikos,Menghan Song,Zezong Li,Lankun Han,Shiliang Li,Sylvain Capponi,Zi Yang Meng,Chengkang Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用大规模密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）与神经网络量子态（NQS）模拟，研究三不等价耦合的笼目（kagome）反铁磁海森堡模型（3J模型），识别出介于两个磁有序相之间的中间量子自旋液体（QSL）相，并借助XTRG与tanTRG热张量网络方法计算比热，与实验观测相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究针对候选狄拉克量子自旋液体（Dirac QSL）材料YCu$_3$(OH)$_6$Br$_2$[Br$_{1-x}$(OH)$_x$]设计的3J模型，旨在连接微观模型与实验可测量信号。其动机是理解阻挫量子磁体中的QSL现象，并以3J材料作为代表性案例。

Method: 采用大规模密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）并辅以神经网络量子态（NQS）模拟确定基态与有限温度性质；同时使用指数（XTRG）与切空间（tanTRG）热张量网络方法计算模型比热，以直接对比实验。

Result: 在两个磁有序相之间识别出一个中间QSL相，且该QSL与各向同性极限下的笼目自旋液体基态相分离。磁有序相的比热随温度在约为耦合强度$J_{hex}$某一分数处呈现肩峰（shoulder），该肩峰在QSL相中消失；这些普适行为与3J材料中有序样品及QSL候选样品的实验比热一致。

Conclusion: 该工作将微观模型与实验可测量特征相联系，展示了理解阻挫量子磁体中QSL现象的综合研究途径；3J材料作为代表性案例，为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: We investigate the ground-state and finite-temperature properties of the kagome antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model with three inequivalent couplings, dubbed the 3J model, which is designed for the candidate Dirac quantum spin liquid (QSL) material YCu$_3$(OH)$_6$Br$_2$[Br$_{1-x}$(OH)$_x$] (see, e.g., Zeng et al., 2024). Employing large-scale density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) supplemented by neural quantum states (NQS) simulations, we identify an intermediate QSL phase between two magnetically ordered phases. We also find that this QSL is separated from the kagome spin liquid ground state at the isotropic limit. To establish a direct comparison with experiments, we compute the specific heat of the model by means of advanced exponential (XTRG) and tangent-space (tanTRG) thermal tensor-network methods. In the magnetically ordered phase, the specific heat over temperature exhibits a shoulder at a temperature that is a fraction of the coupling strength $J_{hex}$, which disappears in the QSL phase. These universal behaviors are consistent with the experimentally observed specific heat in 3J materials for both ordered and QSL candidate samples. Our work thus connects microscopic models with experimentally measurable signatures, exemplifying an integrated approach (see Meng et al., 2026) to understanding QSL phenomena in frustrated quantum magnets, with 3J materials serving as a representative case and providing a foundation for future studies.

</details>


### [13] [Learning Quantum Matter through Attention in Complex Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11792)
*Mingrui Jing,Erdong Huang,Jizhe Lai,Enji Xiong,Jin-Guo Liu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出面向磁性莫尔连续体中相互作用电子的复数 Psiformer（Complex Psiformer），将复数隐特征、厄米幅度注意力与磁边界条件及费米子反对称性结合，并在有限超胞基准上验证其相对实数版本的能量优势。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性多电子波函数需要振幅与相位联合优化，复数内部表示能否改善变分搜索是神经波函数设计中的实际问题。该研究旨在检验复数架构在磁性莫尔体系中的变分表现。

Method: 构建复数 Psiformer，融合复数隐特征（complex hidden features）与厄米幅度注意力（Hermitian-magnitude attention），并施加磁边界条件与费米子反对称性。在相同优化步数下，与实数 Psiformer 及各自的神经 Hartree-Fock 参考进行对比。

Result: 在两个有限超胞中，复数 Psiformer 达到的能量低于实数 Psiformer，且两者均优于各自的神经 Hartree-Fock 参考。25 胞体系中五个训练种子的平均复数优势为每电子 1.458 meV，观测离散度更小；单独训练的双电子复数态相对有限组态相互作用（configuration interaction）参考的能隙小于实数对应态。通量扫描显示复数态中密度关联非单调、高磁通下蜂窝平均密度调制更弱，而连通涨落持续存在；规范不变电流图提供了局域环流的定性比较。

Conclusion: 基准结果支持该组合架构作为变分拟设（variational ansatz），用于研究有限磁性体系中的能量与电荷排布。

Abstract: Magnetic many-electron wavefunctions require amplitude and phase to be optimized together. Whether a complex internal representation improves this variational search is a practical question for neural wavefunction design. We introduce Complex Psiformer for interacting electrons in a magnetic moiré continuum, combining complex hidden features and Hermitian-magnitude attention with magnetic boundary conditions and fermionic antisymmetry. After the same number of optimization steps, Complex Psiformer reaches lower energies than Real Psiformer in two finite supercells. Both Psiformers also improve on their respective neural Hartree-Fock references. Across five training seeds in the 25-cell system, the mean Complex advantage is 1.458 meV per electron, with a smaller observed spread. A separately trained two-electron Complex state has a smaller energy gap to a finite configuration interaction reference than its Real counterpart. In the Complex states, flux scans show nonmonotonic density correlations and weaker honeycomb mean-density modulation at higher flux, while connected fluctuations persist. Gauge invariant current maps provide a qualitative comparison of local circulation in the optimized states. These benchmarks support the combined architecture as a variational ansatz for studying energies and charge arrangements in finite magnetic systems.

</details>


### [14] [Block entropy area based non-local fermionic mode optimization with gradient disentanglers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11811)
*Miklós Antal Werner,Gero Friesecke,Andor Menczer,Kornél Kapás,Örs Legeza*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于块熵面积（block entropy area）的模优化算法，用于优化以矩阵乘积态（matrix product states, MPS）表示的相互作用费米子多体量子态，并在二维格点模型与 Fe₄S₄ 团簇上验证其优于早期近邻模旋转与重排协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用费米子多体态的 MPS 表示受纠缠结构限制，早期基于近邻模旋转与重排的优化方案效果有限，需要更系统、可扩展的纠缠最小化手段。

Method: 以全局代价函数即块熵面积为优化目标，由其余量梯度构造长程、非相互作用的有效解纠缠哈密顿量（disentangler Hamiltonian）；再借助基于投影分裂（projector splitting）的含时变分原理（time-dependent variational principle, TDVP）模拟由其驱动的含时薛定谔方程，从而最小化代价函数。

Result: 将密度矩阵重整化群（density matrix renormalization group, DMRG）与上述基于梯度的纠缠最小化相结合，形成高效低秩迭代基态算法，并同时给出优化的 MPS 单粒子基；在二维相互作用费米子格点模型与 Fe₄S₄ 团簇上验证了方法的鲁棒性，且优于使用近邻模旋转与重排的早期协议。

Conclusion: 基于块熵面积的梯度式纠缠最小化可与 DMRG 耦合，形成兼具基态求解与单粒子基优化功能的低秩迭代算法，在二维费米子格点与 Fe₄S₄ 团簇中表现稳健且优于既有近邻模优化方案。

Abstract: We introduce a systematic block entropy area based mode optimization algorithm for many-body quantum states of interacting fermions represented by matrix product states. From the gradient of a global cost function, the block entropy area, a long-ranged, non-interacting effective disentangler Hamiltonian is formed. We then simulate the time-dependent Schrödinger equation driven by the disentangler Hamiltonian by employing the time-dependent variational principle based on projector splitting, and minimize the cost function. The combination of the density matrix renormalization group with this gradient-based entanglement minimization forms an efficient low-rank iterative ground-state algorithm that also provides an optimized single-particle basis for matrix product state representation. We demonstrate the method on two-dimensional lattice models of interacting fermions and the Fe${_4}$S${_4}$ cluster, and show its robustness and superiority over earlier protocols using nearest-neighbor mode rotations and reorderings.

  <summary>Details</summary>

### [18] [Selected Coupled Cluster Guided by Selected Configuration Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10829)
*Minsik Cho,Troy Van Voorhis*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 Selected Coupled Cluster（SCC），一种任意阶（arbitrary-order）稀疏耦合簇方法，在由选中组态相互作用（Selected Configuration Interaction, SCI）波函数引导的紧致振幅空间内显式求解 CC 残差方程，并在多种化学体系上优于 SCI、SCI+PT2、CCSD/CCSDT 与 DMRG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合簇（Coupled Cluster, CC）理论是高阶相关波函数方法中应用广泛的一种，但其在高阶截断时计算成本随阶数急剧增长，限制了实际应用。同时，现代 SCI 方法已成功利用行列式空间中基态波函数的稀疏性，这启发作者将类似的稀疏思路与指数形式的 CC 拟设（ansatz）结合。

Method: 作者提出 Selected Coupled Cluster（SCC），一种任意阶稀疏 CC 方法，在 SCI 波函数引导的紧致振幅空间内显式求解 CC 残差方程，并在该稀疏振幅空间中优化 CC 振幅。方法通过一个试验性的即时编译（Just-in-Time, JIT）实现进行验证。

Result: 在多种化学体系上，SCC 与 SCI、SCI+PT2、标准秩截断稠密 CC（CCSD、CCSDT）及 DMRG 进行了对比测试。结果显示：SCC 随 SCI 变分阈值（variational threshold）呈现快速单调收敛，优于 SCI，并在紧阈值区间超过 SCI+PT2；在高维拓扑体系上也能给出准确能量，而 DMRG 在该类体系中随键维数（bond dimension）收敛缓慢。

Conclusion: SCC 为现代 SCI 工作流提供了一种高效的即插即用式（drop-in）增强方案。

Abstract: Coupled Cluster (CC) theory is one of the most popular correlated wavefunction methods, yet the steep computational cost scaling of CC truncated at higher rank limits its application. Modern Selected Configuration Interaction (SCI) methods successfully exploit the sparsity of ground-state wavefunctions in the determinant space, motivating an analogous approach with the exponential CC ansatz. In this work, we introduce Selected Coupled Cluster (SCC), an arbitrary-order sparse CC method that explicitly solves the CC residual equations within a compact amplitude space guided by an SCI wavefunction. SCC optimizes the CC amplitudes in the sparse amplitude space. Using a pilot Just-in-Time (JIT) compiled implementation, we benchmark SCC against SCI, SCI+PT2, standard rank-truncated dense CC (CCSD, CCSDT), and DMRG across various chemical systems. We demonstrate that SCC exhibits fast, monotonic convergence with the SCI variational threshold, outperforming SCI and surpassing SCI+PT2 in the tight-threshold regime. SCC also provides accurate energies for high-dimensional topologies, where DMRG typically converges slowly with bond dimension. SCC thus presents an efficient drop-in enhancement for modern SCI workflows.

</details>


### [19] [Breaking Water at Graphene Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11602)
*Samuel G. H. Brookes,IniOluwa C. Popoola,Fabian Berger,Kara D. Fong,Angelos Michaelides,Christoph Schran*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials），揭示了溶剂化（solvation）如何定性地改变缺陷石墨烯-水界面（defective graphene-water interfaces）上水分解（water dissociation）的反应机理。溶剂化环境下，单个空位（single vacancy, SV）通过碱性与酸性两条竞争路径分解水，产生活性中间体并增强界面吸附。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水在固体表面的分解是腐蚀、催化、电化学与光伏等过程的基础，缺陷常作为分解的活性位点，但溶剂化对该类位点上水分解的影响尚缺乏理解。该研究旨在阐明溶剂化如何调控缺陷位点上的水分解行为。

Method: 采用先进的机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials），对缺陷石墨烯-水界面上的水分解过程进行模拟与探索。

Result: 溶剂化在石墨烯单空位（SV）处定性地改变了反应机理，并开启了孤立水分子所不具备的路径。气相过程仅经由单一协同通道（concerted channel）；而溶剂化后的SV通过两条竞争路径分解水：生成SV-H与OH-(aq)的碱性路径，以及生成SV-OH与H3O+(aq)的酸性路径。这些低势垒路径产生不同的化学吸附中间体，并增强石墨烯-水的吸附。

Conclusion: 即便是一个简单的碳空位，也会产生异常丰富的界面化学，将表面化学与界面电荷及润湿性（wettability）耦合，对碳的功能化（functionalization）与纳米流体输运（nanofluidic transport）具有启示意义。

Abstract: Water dissociation at solid surfaces underpins processes ranging from corrosion and catalysis to electrochemistry and photovoltaics. Defects often serve as reactive sites for dissociation, yet how solvation influences water dissociation at such sites remains poorly understood. Here, we use state-of-the-art machine-learned interatomic potentials to explore water dissociation at defective graphene-water interfaces. We show that solvation qualitatively changes the reaction mechanism at a graphene single vacancy (SV), opening pathways that are absent for an isolated water molecule. Whereas the gas-phase process proceeds via a single concerted channel, the solvated SV splits water through two competing pathways: a basic route forming SV-H and OH-(aq), and an acidic route forming SV-OH and H3O+(aq). These lower-barrier pathways produce distinct chemisorbed intermediates that enhance graphene-water adsorption. Accordingly, even a simple carbon vacancy gives rise to unexpectedly rich interfacial chemistry, coupling surface chemistry to interfacial charge and wettability, with implications for carbon functionalization and nanofluidic transport.

</details>

### [20] [Influence of magnetic fields on the performance of spin-orbit torque magnetic random-access memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11397)
*Jiaxin Liu,Yuqing Zhou,Guoyi Shi,Kaiming Cai*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种在不改动磁隧道结（MTJ）膜层结构的前提下，利用后段制程（BEOL）中垂直互连通道（VIA）内的磁性填充材料工程化局部杂散磁场，以补偿本征偏置场并抑制自旋轨道矩磁随机存储器（SOT-MRAM）的写电流不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOT-MRAM具备高速、超高耐久性与先进半导体工艺兼容性，但MTJ中的本征偏置场（源于参考层杂散场与层间耦合）导致临界翻转电流不对称与能耗升高。既有补偿方案通常需在MTJ叠层中引入额外磁性层，增加制程复杂度并限制晶圆级集成。

Method: 通过在BEOL工艺中于VIA通道内采用磁性填充材料来工程化局部杂散磁场，从而在不修改MTJ叠层的情况下实现偏置补偿；采用微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations）验证所需辅助场的提供情况，并优化MTJ相对于磁性填充结构的位置。

Result: 优化MTJ位置后，写电流偏置比（write-current bias ratio）由传统设计的21.6%降至1.3%；该方法同样适用于面内磁各向异性（in-plane magnetic anisotropy）SOT-MTJ，偏置比由19.8%降至-0.2%。尺寸缩放到原尺寸20%（MTJ直径约10 nm）时补偿效果依然有效。

Conclusion: 该VIA磁性填充的偏置补偿策略在不改动MTJ叠层的前提下可显著抑制写电流不对称，并具备良好的尺寸缩放特性，显示出面向高密度SOT-MRAM集成的应用潜力。

Abstract: Spin-orbit torque magnetic random-access memory (SOT-MRAM) offers high speed, ultrahigh endurance, and compatibility with advanced semiconductor processes, making it a promising candidate for next-generation nonvolatile memory. However, intrinsic bias fields in magnetic tunnel junctions (MTJs), originating from reference-layer stray fields and interlayer coupling, cause asymmetric critical switching currents and increased energy consumption. Existing compensation approaches usually introduce additional magnetic layers into the MTJ stack, which increases fabrication complexity and limits wafer-scale integration. Here, we propose a bias-compensation strategy without modifying the MTJ stack by engineering local stray magnetic fields through magnetic filling materials in vertical interconnect access (VIA) channels during the back-end-of-line process. Micromagnetic simulations show that the proposed magnetic filling layer can provide the required auxiliary field for deterministic switching and significantly suppress write-current asymmetry. By optimizing the MTJ position relative to the magnetic filling structure, the write-current bias ratio is reduced from 21.6% in the conventional design to 1.3%. The approach is also applicable to in-plane magnetic anisotropy SOT-MTJs, reducing the bias ratio from 19.8% to -0.2%. Scaling analysis further demonstrates that the compensation effect remains effective when the device size is reduced to 20% of the original dimension (MTJ diameter approximately 10 nm), indicating its potential for high-density SOT-MRAM integration.

</details>

### [21] [Bayesian Superiority in On/Off analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11683)
*Gleb Babenov,Petr Satunin*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该论文在 On/Off 问题中系统比较了三种无信息先验（non-informative priors）下的贝叶斯判据与经典频率派 Li-Ma 方法，通过蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）以第一类与第二类错误率为指标进行评估。结果显示，采用 Jeffreys 先验的贝叶斯判据错误率更低，且在后底分布相对泊松分布（Poisson distribution）过离散（overdispersion）时更具稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 On/Off 问题中，需在未知背景下检验信号是否存在，而传统频率派 Li-Ma 方法的性能在先验选择与背景建模偏差下的表现尚需系统评估。该研究旨在通过错误率视角，比较贝叶斯判据与 Li-Ma 方法的优劣，并考察背景分布偏离理想假设时的稳健性。

Method: 针对多种背景水平开展蒙特卡洛模拟，使用平坦先验（flat prior）、Jeffreys 先验与尺度不变先验（scale-invariant prior）三种无信息先验构建贝叶斯判据，并与经典频率派 Li-Ma 方法对比。首先以第一类错误率（Type I error rate）评估各判据，随后通过模拟非零信号，以第二类错误率（Type II error rate）进行进一步比较；此外还考察了背景分布相对泊松分布过离散的情形。

Result: 采用 Jeffreys 先验的贝叶斯判据在第一类与第二类错误率上均低于 Li-Ma 判据。同时，当背景分布相对泊松分布出现过离散时，贝叶斯判据相比 Li-Ma 判据表现出更强的稳健性（robustness）。

Conclusion: 在 On/Off 问题的信号检验中，基于 Jeffreys 先验的贝叶斯判据在错误率控制与对背景过离散的稳健性方面优于经典 Li-Ma 方法，提示贝叶斯方法可作为该问题的有效替代方案。

Abstract: We present a detailed comparison of Bayesian criteria with three non-informative priors - flat, Jeffreys, and scale-invariant - for testing a signal against an unknown background and compare them with the classical frequentist Li-Ma approach in the On/Off problem. We perform Monte Carlo simulations for various background levels and evaluate the Li-Ma and Bayesian criteria by their Type I error rates. We then simulate a nonzero signal and compare the criteria in terms of Type II error rates. We find that the Bayesian criterion with the Jeffreys prior yields lower Type I and Type II error rates than the Li-Ma criterion. In addition, we show that the Bayesian criteria are more robust than the Li-Ma criterion when the background distribution is overdispersed relative to the Poisson distribution.

</details>

### [22] [Henstock--Kurzweil Gauge Integral in the Non--Gaussian Regime: A Machine--Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10793)
*Yuri N. Berdinsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在 Lean 4 与 Mathlib 中机器验证了非高斯泛函积分（non-Gaussian functional integrals）的构造，基于 Henstock–Kurzweil 规范积分与 Chernoff 乘积逼近，并给出单模及多模积分的有限性、正则性与因子化等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非高斯泛函积分常依赖发散微扰级数、Wick 旋转（Wick rotation）或 zeta 正则化等间接手段。本文旨在提供一个完全直接、可直接机器检验的替代构造路径。

Method: 采用 Henstock–Kurzweil 规范积分（gauge integral）与 Chernoff 乘积逼近（Chernoff product approximations），研究对象为正定矩阵 A 与四次相互作用项 lambda·Σφ_i^4 构成的玻色作用量；用 Chernoff/Lie–Trotter 分裂处理自由与非高斯生成元的非对易性，并在 Lean 4 中形式化。

Result: 证明单模积分 I(ω, j, λ) 在 [0, ∞) 上有限、严格为正、单调且对 λ 无穷可微，其导数由同一权重的 φ^{4k} 收敛积分给出而非发散微扰级数；M 模影响泛函（influence functional）因子化为单模积分，并以其高斯值（Gaussian value）为上界。

Conclusion: 该构造直接有效，无需 Wick 旋转、Wiener 测度、zeta 正则化或从虚时间（imaginary time）解析延拓；全部结论在 Lean 4 中形式化，HkNonGaussian.lean 无 sorry，仅使用 propext、Classical.choice、Quot.sound 三个标准公理，并给出 Duffing 振子、CEV 局部波动率、Wilson–Cowan 神经场及非高斯量子库四个示例。

Abstract: We develop a machine-checked construction of non-Gaussian functional integrals using the Henstock--Kurzweil gauge integral and Chernoff product approximations. The central object is a finite family of bosonic modes with action S(phi) = (1/2) phi^T A phi + lambda * sum_i phi_i^4, where A is positive definite. We prove that the one-mode integral I(omega, j, lambda) is finite, strictly positive, monotone and infinitely differentiable in the coupling lambda on [0, infinity). Its derivatives are given by convergent integrals of phi^{4k} with the same weight, not by the divergent perturbative series. The M-mode influence functional factorises into one-mode integrals and is bounded by its Gaussian value. A Chernoff / Lie--Trotter splitting handles the non-commutativity of the free and non-Gaussian generators. All statements are formalised in Lean 4 with Mathlib; the accompanying file HkNonGaussian.lean is free of sorry and uses only the standard axioms propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Four illustrative applications are worked out at the level of explicit formulas: the Duffing oscillator, local volatility (CEV) in finance, Wilson--Cowan neural fields, and non-Gaussian quantum reservoirs. The construction is completely direct and does not use Wick rotation, Wiener measure, zeta-regularisation or analytic continuation back from imaginary time.

</details>

Abstract: We study the particle number distribution of locally interacting bosonic quasi- free states, which arise in various areas of mathematical physics. We show that the particle number decomposes into an infinite sum of independent geometrically distributed random variables, confirming predictions from the physics literature. This representation yields exponential tail bounds for the particle number, a law of small numbers together with a large deviation principle at logarithmic speed when the lattice spacing diverges, and a central limit theorem when it vanishes. ### [25] [Why we should condition denoising diffusion generative models on windows of past observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10924)
*Matthias Morzfeld,Daniel Hodyss*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出将扩散模型（diffusion model）条件化于短时窗的过去观测（past observations），以替代传统循环式数据同化（data assimilation, DA）依赖的时间相关先验（time-dependent priors），在不重新训练的前提下逼近完全循环系统的最小后验误差（posterior error）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化是依赖时间相关先验、将信息从过去观测传播至未来循环的循环过程；而基于去噪扩散生成建模（denoising diffusion generative modeling）的标准方法使用固定训练集，导致先验静态化并忽略过去观测，从而产生比循环式DA更大的后验误差。将时间相关先验纳入生成模型则需频繁且昂贵的重新训练，因此需要一种更高效的折中方案。

Method: 受线性系统理论（linear systems theory）启发——即卡尔曼滤波（Kalman filter）的预测对过去观测的依赖呈指数衰减——作者将扩散模型条件化于短时窗的过去观测。具体提出两套框架的训练流程：一是给定一组过去观测预测当前状态的扩散DA系统；二是扩散“直接观测预测”（direct observation prediction, DOP）系统，即给定过去观测预测未来观测。

Result: 在一个典型线性系统（canonical linear system）上验证表明，只要时间窗足够长，两套系统均可在无需重新训练的情况下达到完全循环DA/DOP系统所具有的最小后验误差。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) is, traditionally, a cycling process that relies on time-dependent priors to propagate information from past observations to future cycles. Using denoising diffusion generative modeling for DA is challenging because standard approaches use a fixed training data set, which in turn leads to a static prior that ignores information from past observations. Because past observations are ignored, DA systems with static priors lead to larger posterior errors than cycling DA systems. Incorporating time-dependent priors into generative models, however, requires expensive and frequent retraining. Motivated by linear systems theory - where the dependence of a prediction of a Kalman filter on past observations decays exponentially - we condition diffusion models on short windows of past observations. Specifically, we describe training procedures for two frameworks: a diffusion DA system predicting the current state given a set of past observations, and a diffusion ``direct observation prediction'' (DOP) system, predicting future observations given a set of past observations. Using a canonical linear system, we show that both systems can achieve the minimal posterior error characteristic of a fully-cycled DA/DOP system, without re-training, provided the time windows are long enough. The linear setup ensures analytical tractability, avoids confounding neural network training errors, and confirms that conditioning on windows of past observations is required for efficient and accurate diffusion-based DA or DOP.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 以辛结构（symplectic structure）的约束性要求为出发点，仅规定其须如哈密顿方程（Hamilton's equations）那样将速度与动量相关联，并对所有能量同时成立；进而引入闭二形式（closed two-form）对典范结构作扭曲，并考察动量映射的度与场进入牛顿第二定律（Newton's Second Law）的阶数之关系，以及 Randers 度量（Randers metric）、非仿射联络的输运律、不定号（indefinite signature）与球面上的磁单极通量（monopole flux）等情形。

Result: 满足要求的辛结构恰为典范结构及其被底空间闭二形式的扭曲；场提供二形式，粒子提供其前乘子并构成性地等同于其电荷。动量映射的度决定场进入牛顿第二定律的阶数：反对称双线性形式不产生洛伦兹力（Lorentz force），而 Randers 度量则产生之，其与黎曼度量的差为速度的线性项。该度量已内含于扭曲结构中（作为自由能某一能级上的原函数），其输运律为非线性，尚无已知仿射联络可适用。不定号下长度与动力学分离，极值曲线由最短转为最长；在圆球上磁单极通量不留下此类度量，但输运仍存，且给定作用量单位后预量子化（prequantisation）将电荷限制在格点上。

Conclusion: 每个电荷-质量比获得其自身的一套几何，因此依功能主义判据（functionalist criterion），其中任何一个都不是带电粒子的时空。这意味着带电粒子的"时空"并非唯一，而是随电荷-质量比分化的一族几何。

Abstract: We ask how much of the geometry a charged particle moves in is fixed by its motion, and how much a particle must bring. We ask of a symplectic structure only that it relate velocity to momentum as Hamilton's equations do, and we ask it of every energy at once. In particular, we show that the structures meeting that demand are the canonical one and its twists by a closed two-form of the base. A field provides the two-form, a particle the multiplier before it, which we identify constitutively with its charge. Thus, a single energy governs a family of structures, and each particle takes the one its charge fixes. We then ask what a particle must bring to be given a momentum, and we show that the degree of that map settles the degree at which a field enters Newton's Second Law. An antisymmetric bilinear form returns no Lorentz force, whilst a Randers metric returns one --- a length whose difference from a Riemannian one is linear in the velocity. Moreover, we find that metric already within the twisted structure, as its primitive over a level of the free energy, and its law of transport to be nonlinear, no affine connection being known to serve. Under an indefinite signature the length parts from the dynamics, and the extremals turn from shortest to longest. On the round sphere a monopole flux leaves no such metric, whilst the transport remains and prequantisation, given a unit of action, restricts the charge to a lattice. In this manner, we conclude that each charge-to-mass ratio receives a geometry of its own, so that by a functionalist criterion none of them is the spacetime of a charged particle.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 所得公式精确判定诱导形式的定号性，从而回答了伴随论文中的定号性问题。定号性蕴含输出表示可酉化（unitarizability）；当该表示不可约时，逆命题亦成立。

Abstract: The Katz-Long-Moody construction associates to a representation of the semidirect product of a free group and a braid group, defined by the Artin action, and a nonzero parameter a new representation of the same group. On the pure braid group it corresponds to Haraoka's multiplicative middle convolution for KZ-type equations. For unitary input and a convolution parameter on the unit circle other than one, the construction equips the quotient representation with a canonical non-degenerate invariant Hermitian form. We give a closed formula for its signature in terms of the eigenangles of the input local monodromies, the eigenangles of their ordered product, and the convolution parameter. The formula accounts for the kernel of the form before passage to the quotient and for signature changes at resonant parameters. It determines precisely when the induced form is definite, answering the definiteness problem posed in the companion paper. Definiteness implies unitarizability of the output representation; the converse holds when that representation is irreducible.

As applications, we compare the rank-one construction explicitly with Haraoka's invariant form for Pochhammer systems, recover the Gauss case of the Beukers-Heckman interlacing criterion, and determine the definite parameter intervals for the Hecke and Temperley-Lieb specializations.

</details>

### [31] [Modified wave operators for nonlinear Schrödinger equations in the full subcritical long range regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11095)
*Jia Shen,Yifei Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文在完全次临界长程情形 $0<p<2/d$ 下，为非线性薛定谔方程（nonlinear Schrödinger equation）构造修正波算子（modified wave operators），给出 $p>1/d$ 时的新渐近展开式，并借助非线性终态正规形（nonlinear final-state normal form）处理 $p\le 1/d$ 中不保持快速衰减的长程三角耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果仅对部分柯西数据（Cauchy data）类建立大时间渐近，且 $p<1/d$ 的精确渐近展开尚属未知；本文旨在完整覆盖全次临界长程区间 $0<p<2/d$，尤其填补 $p\le 1/d$ 的空白。

Method: 对 $1/d<p<2/d$，在半径递减的解析空间（analytic spaces）中通过迭代构造波算子。对 $p\le 1/d$，由 Fuchsian 方程（Fuchsian equation）与输运方程（transport equation）耦合的有限截断构造轮廓，并引入非线性终态正规形消除长程耦合，配合特定解析空间中的混合迭代。

Result: 在 $1/d<p<2/d$ 时给出含相位修正 $|W(x/t)|^p$ 项的渐近近似式；在 $p\le 1/d$ 时构造出轮廓，并得到 $2\le q\le\infty$ 的定量 $L^q$ 渐近性，以及在所设定的解析渐近类中的唯一性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，借助非线性终态正规形与混合迭代，可将修正波算子构造推广至全次临界长程范围，并在解析渐近类中确立唯一性。

Abstract: We construct modified wave operators for the nonlinear Schrödinger equation $i\partial_tu+\frac12Δu=|u|^pu$ in the full subcritical long-range case $0<p<2/d$, with small, nonvanishing, analytic final data $W(x)$ with bounded logarithmic gradients. Previous results established large-time asymptotics for selected classes of Cauchy data. Moreover, the exact asymptotic expansion for $p<1/d$ remained unknown.
  When $1/d<p<2/d$, our result gives the approximation $$\frac{1}{(it)^{\frac{d}{2}}} e^{\frac{i|x|^2}{2t}} W\left(\frac{x}{t}\right) \exp\left[ -i\frac{t^{1-\frac{dp}{2}}-1}{1-\frac{dp}{2}} \left|W\left(\frac{x}{t}\right)\right|^p \right]$$ The wave operator is constructed by an iteration in the analytic spaces with decreasing radius.
  When $p\le 1/d$, we construct the profile from a finite truncation of a Fuchsian equation coupled with a transport equation. This profile still leaves a long-range triangular coupling whose terminal integral does not preserve the required fast decay class. The construction yields quantitative $L^q$ asymptotics for $2\le q\le\infty$ and uniqueness in the prescribed analytic asymptotic classes. The central new ingredients are a nonlinear final-state normal form that removes this long-range coupling and a mixed iteration in particular analytic spaces.

</details>


### [32] [Diffeomorphism Invariance of the Favre-Filtered Compressible Navier--Stokes System under Spacetime Dilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11566)
*Yuanya Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在坐标 $(Lt, Lx_1, Lx_2, Lx_3)$ 下写出的带亚格子闭合项的 Favre 滤波可压缩 Navier--Stokes 方程组，等价于标准坐标下方程组经底流形光滑微分同胚作用所得，且所有 $L>0$ 的解空间典范同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚格子闭合项使滤波方程在坐标伸缩下的结构不变性此前未获严格论证，而该性质关系到不同尺度参数化系统之间解的可比性。论文旨在从微分几何角度严格确立这一变换表达式的成立。

Method: 方法包含三部分：自足地处理无穷维流形（infinite-dimensional manifolds）与光滑场构型的 Fréchet 流形（Fréchet manifold）；定义单参数伸缩群（one-parameter dilation group）$\{\varphi_L\}_{L>0}$，验证其为微分同胚并计算切丛与余切丛作用；证明构型丛截面上的诱导拉回作用与全导数算子（total derivative operator）对易，从而第一射流延拓（first-jet prolongation）将方程子流形映到自身，并逐方程验证残差。

Result: 连续性方程、动量方程与能量方程的逐项验证表明，每个残差均被同一非零因子 $L^{-1}$ 相乘（等价地，在变换坐标下恒为零），因此解集保持不变。由此确认参数 $L>0$ 的系统族满足微分同胚变换表达式，且所有 $L$ 对应的解空间典范同构（canonically isomorphic）。

Conclusion: 带亚格子闭合项的 Favre 滤波可压缩 Navier--Stokes 方程在尺度伸缩坐标下的系统族，构成由底时空流形微分同胚所联系的一组等价方程；解空间不随参数 $L$ 改变而实质变化。

Abstract: We prove that the system of Favre-filtered compressible Navier--Stokes equations with subgrid-scale closure terms, written in the coordinates $(Lt,Lx_1,Lx_2,Lx_3)$ for an arbitrary parameter $L>0$, is obtained from the system written in standard coordinates $(t,x_1,x_2,x_3)$ by the action of a smooth diffeomorphism of the underlying spacetime manifold. The proof begins with a self-contained treatment of infinite-dimensional manifolds and the Fréchet manifold of smooth field configurations. We then define the one-parameter dilation group $\{φ_L\}_{L>0}$, verify that each $φ_L$ is a diffeomorphism, and compute its tangent and cotangent actions. The induced pullback action on sections of the configuration bundle is shown to commute with the total derivative operator, which implies that the first-jet prolongation of $φ_L$ maps the equation submanifold of the system onto itself. Equation-by-equation verification---continuity, momentum, and energy---confirms that every residual is multiplied by the common nonzero factor $L^{-1}$ (or, equivalently, is identically zero in the transformed coordinates), so that the solution set is preserved. We conclude that the family of systems parameterised by $L>0$ satisfies the diffeomorphism transformation expression and that solution spaces are canonically isomorphic for all $L$.

</details>

*Zi-Tong Fan,Hong-Jian He,Yu-Chen Wang,Yue Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究由两个暗物质粒子成分（χ1, χ2）组成的非弹性暗物质（inelastic dark matter, iDM），指出其在核反冲（nuclear recoil, NR）高能区可优先产生信号，从而以更小的散射截面自然解释 LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验新报告的高反冲能事件超出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用单成分暗物质（one-component DM）模型存在局限，iDM 被视为其最小扩展。iDM 与靶核散射的独特之处在于可在放热（exothermic）与吸热（endothermic）过程中于高核反冲能区产生信号。

Method: 采用非弹性暗物质-原子核散射（inelastic DM-nucleus scattering）框架，分析放热与吸热两类过程在高核反冲能区的信号特征，并据此评估所需散射截面与相关实验约束。

Result: 在放热情形下，信号不依赖玻尔兹曼速度分布（Boltzmann velocity distribution）的尾部，所需暗物质-原子核散射截面显著降低，从而大幅放宽 IceCube 中微子搜寻给出的严格限制；该机制可自然解释 LZ 实验新报道的高反冲能事件超出。

Conclusion: 双成分非弹性暗物质为解释高反冲能区事件超出提供了可行方案，并相较单成分模型缓解了中微子实验限制带来的张力。

Abstract: We study the inelastic dark matter (iDM) that consists of two-component dark matter particles $(χ_1,χ_2)$ and serves as a minimal extension beyond the commonly used one-component DM models. A distinctive feature of such iDM scattering with the target nuclei is to favorably produce signals at high nuclear recoil (NR) energy region in both exothermic and endothermic processes. In particular, for the exothermic dark matter scenario, the signal does not rely on the tail of the Boltzmann velocity distribution. As a result, the required DM-nucleus scattering cross section is significantly reduced, which in turn helps to substantially relax the stringent constraints imposed by IceCube neutrino searches. Using the inelastic DM-nucleus scattering, we naturally explain the newly reported event excess at high recoil energy with the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment.

</details>


### [34] [The Dark Dimension and Majorana Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10668)
*Arturo de Giorgi,Dhruv Pasari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于沼泽地（Swampland）纲领所预言的介观暗维度（Dark Dimension）场景，本文推导了体马约拉纳中微子（bulk Majorana neutrino）随紧致化半径变化的地面实验约束，并给出无中微子双贝塔衰变的有效马约拉纳质量闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沼泽地纲领（Swampland program）的最新进展暗示存在尺度为 0.1–10 μm、含 1–10 keV 体马约拉纳费米子（bulk Majorana fermion）的介观暗维度，因而有必要检验该场景与现有地面中微子实验的相容性。

Method: 先确定质量谱与混合结构（mixing structure），再将模型与 Daya Bay 中微子振荡数据、KATRIN 贝塔衰变上限及 KamLAND-Zen 无中微子双贝塔衰变（neutrinoless double beta decay）极限进行对比，并推导有效马约拉纳质量的闭式表达式。

Result: Kaluza-Klein 塔（Kaluza-Klein tower）在核动量超过紧致化标度与马约拉纳标度时有效屏蔽无中微子双贝塔衰变；在暗维度窗口内贝塔衰变给出最强约束，将允许的汤川耦合（Yukawa coupling）压低至 O(10^{-3})；振荡与无中微子双贝塔衰变探测分别对应更小与更大的马约拉纳质量，具有互补性。

Conclusion: 在暗维度假设之外，层级式马约拉纳质量（hierarchical Majorana masses）可削弱上述约束，表明约束强度依赖于质量谱的具体形式。

Abstract: Recent developments in the Swampland program motivate the existence of a mesoscopic Dark Dimension of size $0.1-10~μ$m with bulk Majorana fermions in the $1-10$ keV range. Motivated by this, we derive terrestrial constraints on Majorana bulk neutrinos as a function of the compactification radius. After determining the mass spectrum and mixing structure, we confront the model with Daya Bay neutrino-oscillation data, the KATRIN beta-decay bound, and the KamLAND-Zen neutrinoless double beta decay limit. For the latter, we obtain a closed-form expression for the effective Majorana mass and show that the Kaluza-Klein tower efficiently screens neutrinoless double beta decay when the nuclear momentum exceeds the compactification and Majorana scales. In the Dark-Dimension window, beta decay provides the strongest constraint, pushing the allowed Yukawa couplings down to $\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$. Oscillation and neutrinoless double beta decay searches remain complementary, probing smaller and larger Majorana masses, respectively. For completeness, outside the Dark-Dimension assumptions, we investigate how hierarchical Majorana masses can weaken the constraints.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弹性暗物质散射图像难以涵盖高能核反冲事件所暗示的物理过程，因此需要考察非弹性散射（inelastic scattering）等替代机制以解释LZ合作组新近报告的约248 keV事件。

Method: 在广义手征U(1)_{B-L}框架下构建非弹性标量暗物质模型：对称性破缺后暗区标量产生两个近简并态并存在小幅质量劈裂，较轻态作为暗物质候选者并与Z'玻色子发生非对角耦合，导致吸热非弹性散射（endothermic inelastic scattering）。研究分析其运动学、遗迹丰度与实验限制。

Result: 该非弹性运动学压制低速暗物质的贡献，而使高速尾部对更高能量核反冲更为重要；据此识别出与观测遗迹丰度（relic abundance）及现有实验限制相符的参数空间，并讨论了与LZ观测相关的非弹性散射运动学。

Conclusion: 非弹性标量暗物质情景可在广义手征U(1)_{B-L}框架内一致地容纳该暗物质遗迹丰度与实验约束，并为近期LZ观测提供可能的解释，同时给出相关Z'玻色子的互补对撞机（collider）探测前景。

Abstract: The high-energy nuclear recoil event with $E_R\simeq248$ keV recently reported by the LZ collaboration, with a background-only significance of $2.6σ$, offers an intriguing window into dark matter scattering beyond the conventional elastic picture. We study an inelastic scalar dark matter scenario within a generalized chiral $U(1)_{B-L}$ framework. After symmetry breaking, the dark sector scalar gives rise to two nearly degenerate states with a small mass splitting. The lighter state serves as the dark matter candidate and couples off-diagonally to the $Z'$ boson, leading to endothermic inelastic scattering. The resulting kinematics suppress the contribution from low-velocity dark matter while making the high-velocity tail increasingly relevant for nuclear recoils at higher energies. We identify the parameter space consistent with the observed relic abundance and current experimental constraints, and discuss the inelastic scattering kinematics relevant to the recent LZ observation, together with the complementary collider prospects for the associated $Z'$ boson.

</details>

*T. V. Iser,V. Li,E. G. S. Luna*

Main category: hep-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清弹性散射所提取的胶子质量标度是否受幺正化效应污染。基本输入为雷吉化 Landshoff–Nachtmann 双胶子交换（Reggeized Landshoff–Nachtmann two-gluon exchange），其中软坡密子（soft Pomeron）由色单态（color-singlet）的动力学大质量胶子对表示。

Method: 在玻恩能级计算对数型与幂律型质量解；随后将同一输入嵌入程函（eikonal）与 $U$-矩阵（$U$-matrix）方案进行幺正化处理。并检验结果对 ATLAS–TOTEM 数据选择、胶子质量跑动（running）及幺正化方案的稳定性，追踪质量移动的来源。

Result: 玻恩能级解给出 $m_g=299$–$422~\MeV$，处于通常唯象范围内；嵌入程函与 $U$-矩阵方案后，优选值升至 $m_g=749$–$1101~\MeV$。该约 2.5 倍的增强对数据选择、胶子质量跑动及幺正化方案均保持稳定，其来源被追溯到基本双胶子核与物理碰撞参数剖面（impact-parameter profile）之间的非线性映射。

Conclusion: 由弹性散射推断的质量标度实为经幺正性修饰的红外标度（unitarity-dressed infrared scale），由非微扰胶子传播子（nonperturbative gluon propagator）与多次交换动力学共同决定。

Abstract: We study the effect of $s$-channel unitarity on the dynamical gluon mass scale, $m_g$, extracted from high-energy elastic $pp$ scattering. The elementary input is a Reggeized Landshoff--Nachtmann two-gluon exchange, in which the soft Pomeron is represented by a color-singlet pair of dynamically massive gluons. At Born level, the logarithmic and power-law mass solutions give $m_g=299$--$422~\MeV$, in the usual phenomenological range. When the same input is embedded in the eikonal and $U$-matrix schemes, the preferred values move to $m_g=749$--$1101~\MeV$. The enhancement, by a factor close to $2.5$, is stable against the ATLAS--TOTEM data choice, the running of the gluon mass, and the unitarization prescription. We trace this shift to the nonlinear mapping between the elementary two-gluon kernel and the physical impact-parameter profile. The scale inferred from elastic scattering is therefore a unitarity-dressed infrared scale, fixed jointly by the nonperturbative gluon propagator and by multiple-exchange dynamics.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息观测量（quantum-information observables）近年成为核物理与粒子物理的新工具，但既有研究多集中于等自旋体系（如 $Λ\barΛ$）。推广至不等自旋强子末态面临根本困难：qubit-qutrit 密度矩阵含35个独立自旋参数，而衰变仅能获取23个，因而需要新的层析与纠缠判定框架。

Method: 针对自旋 $\tfrac{1}{2}\otimes1$ 体系（qubit-qutrit），构建 QQQT 量子层析技术；并基于该非完备密度矩阵（incomplete density matrix）推导纠缠认证（entanglement certification）的精确判据，应用于 $Λφ$ 与 $ΛK^{*0}$ 的衰变末态分析。

Result: 在所给摘要范围内，方法结果表现为：确立了非完备密度矩阵下纠缠认证的精确判据，并指明 $Λφ$ 与 $ΛK^{*0}$ 的 QQQT 相较 $Λ\barΛ$ 体系提供了探测非微扰量子色动力学（nonperturbative QCD）强子化（hadronization）的新途径。

Conclusion: QQQT 使自旋 $\tfrac{1}{2}\otimes1$ 体系在信息受限条件下仍可进行纠缠判定，并可用于直接比较由真空产生的纠缠夸克对（entangled quark pairs）在强子化为重子或矢量介子过程中的自旋演化。

Abstract: Quantum-information observables have emerged in recent years as new tools in nuclear and particle physics, from entanglement in top-quark pairs to spin correlations in $Λ\barΛ$ production. Extending these studies to unequal-spin hadronic final states poses a fundamental challenge: the $6\times6$ density matrix of a qubit-qutrit system contains 35 independent spin parameters, but the decays of $ΛV$ pairs, with $V=φ$ or $K^{*0}$, provide access to only 23 due to the hidden vector polarization from the strong decay. In this Letter, we formulate a qubit-qutrit quantum tomography (QQQT) technique for these spin-$\tfrac{1}{2}\otimes1$ systems and establish exact criteria for entanglement certification from the \textit{incomplete} density matrix. Compared with the $Λ\barΛ$ system, QQQT of $Λφ$ and $ΛK^{*0}$ provides a new probe of nonperturbative QCD hadronization, enabling a direct comparison of the spin evolution of entangled quark pairs produced from the vacuum as they hadronize into a baryon or a vector meson.

</details>

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在双部分子散射（double parton scattering）的因子化假设下，有效截面（effective cross section）仅能给出质子的部分子横向间距（transverse partonic separation）的严格下界，而不能给出普适上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双部分子散射被视为探测质子结构的独特手段，通常用有效截面 σ_eff 表征。论文旨在厘清 σ_eff 究竟能揭示部分子在质子内横向间距 r 的何种信息。

Abstract: Double parton scattering provides a unique probe of proton structure and is commonly characterized by the effective cross section $σ_\mathrm{eff}$. We examine, under the factorized ansatz for the double-parton-scattering cross section and without assuming any specific parametric form for the transverse profile, what the effective cross section can reveal about the transverse separation, $r$, between partons in the proton. We derive a sharp lower bound on the transverse partonic separation, $\langle r^2\rangle \ge 4σ_\mathrm{eff}/(9π)$, and demonstrate through explicit counterexamples that previously posited upper bounds do not hold. Our results establish, within the effective-cross-section parametrization of double parton scattering framework, that $σ_\mathrm{eff}$ can provide a sharp lower bound on partonic separation but cannot provide a general upper bound.

</details>

Second, we formulate the spectral decomposition as a map between the full field-theoretic spectrum and the observable particle sector. This cosmological layer is a downstream phenomenological test and is not used as evidence that pole loss has occurred.

  <summary>Details</summary>

</details>

### [77] [Fermion quantum field theory on curved and non-inertial backgrounds in standard-Minkowski form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11738)
*Fabio Di Pumpo,Tobias Asano,Alexander Friedrich,Gil Paz,Enno Giese*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在任意世界坐标下通过广义时间伽马矩阵（generalized temporal gamma matrix）识别出费米场正则结构的公共几何因子，并构造局域场重定义（local fermion-field redefinition）将其化归为标准 Minkowski 形式，从而把背景与叶层依赖转移到变换后的拉格朗日量与场算符中。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该程序恢复了标准 Minkowski 时空惯用的正则归一化，可用于费米子模式量子化与粒子数算符；变换后的拉格朗日量呈广义一阶薛定谔（Schrödinger）形式，给出熟知的静能项、时空-磁耦合以及时间导数与空间导数分离的领头非相对论极限。

Abstract: Quantum field theory on curved and non-inertial backgrounds contains background- and foliation-dependent quantities in the canonical Lagrangian, the hypersurface inner product and bilinear form, as well as in the equal-time anti-commutation relations. In this work, we determine a local fermion-field redefinition that brings these canonical structures into their standard-Minkowski forms, i. e., the forms they assume in Cartesian inertial coordinates on Minkowski spacetime, where the zeroth world coordinate is identified as the coordinate of time. We identify the generalized temporal gamma matrix as the common geometric factor governing the canonical temporal structure of all three quantities. Using a field redefinition, we transform this generalized temporal gamma matrix to its standard-Minkowski form, thereby mapping the fermionic inner product and the equal-time anti-commutation relation to their standard-Minkowski expressions, while transferring the explicit background and foliation dependence to the transformed Lagrangian and fermion-field operators. We show that such a field redefinition necessarily consists of a local rescaling and a fixing of the local Lorentz frame. The transformed Lagrangian consequently assumes a generalized first-order Schrödinger form, leading to the familiar rest-energy term and spacetime-magnetic couplings, as well as to the leading non-relativistic limit, in which temporal derivatives are separated from spatial ones.

### [78] [Logarithmic supertranslations as asymptotic symmetries of gravity at null infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11785)

This fully establishes that the asymptotic symmetry group of gravity in the asymptotically flat context contains the logarithmic supertranslations.

</details>


### [79] [Optimizing the dilaton potential in holographic QCD via phase-transition constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11800)
*Irina Ya. Aref'eva,Alexander V. Polevov,Pavel S. Slepov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文针对自底向上全息QCD（bottom-up holographic QCD）中势重构法所得膨胀子势依赖全息边界条件的问题，提出直接构造一个能重现重构法相变特征的膨胀子势，并以轻夸克模型为例验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自底向上全息QCD中，势重构法（potential reconstruction method）虽能有效刻画极端条件下QCD的唯象性质，但重构得到的膨胀子势（dilaton potential）依赖于所选全息边界条件，存在不唯一性。为规避该问题，需要一种不单纯由真空解导出、而能再现重构法相变特征的势构造方案。

Method: 以轻夸克模型（light-quark model）为例，构造一个旨在重现该模型相结构主要特征的膨胀子势；参数在零化学势（zero chemical potential）下确定，方式为最小化直接法与重构法模型中视界位置（horizon position）随温度依赖关系的偏差，并拟合至相同的临界终点（critical endpoint, CEP）位置。

Result: 所得最小势模型（minimal-potential model）在较小化学势下仍能令人满意地恢复轻夸克模型的物理。其给出的一阶相变（first-order phase transition, FOPT）线与重构法结果接近；并再现了上升的类康奈尔夸克-反夸克势（Cornell-like quark-antiquark potential），直至强子尺度（hadronic distance）ℓ=1.5 fm，该尺度被用作禁闭的唯象有限距离判据。同时，该模型中禁闭/去禁闭交叉（confinement/deconfinement crossover）的位置也与原轻夸克模型对应结果接近。

Conclusion: 通过直接在零化学势下标定参数并拟合临界终点，构造的最小势模型可在小化学势范围内有效重现重构法所获轻夸克模型的相结构与禁闭相关物理，为回避重构势对边界条件的依赖性提供了一种可行途径。

Abstract: In bottom-up holographic QCD (HQCD), two primary approaches are commonly employed: the potential reconstruction method, where the background geometry is fixed a priori and the dilaton potential is derived, and the direct method, where the potential is specified explicitly. While the reconstruction method is highly effective for capturing the phenomenological properties of QCD under extreme conditions, a subtlety arises in that the reconstructed potential depends on the chosen holographic boundary conditions.
  To address this issue, we propose constructing a dilaton potential designed to reproduce the typical features of HQCD phase transitions obtained by the reconstruction method, rather than one derived solely from HQCD vacuum solution. Using a light-quark model as an example, we construct a potential that captures the principal features of the phase structure of this model. Its parameters are determined at zero chemical potential by minimizing the deviation between the temperature dependence on the horizon position in the direct and reconstructed models and by fitting to the same position of a critical endpoint (CEP).
  The resulting minimal-potential model satisfactorily recovers the physics of the light-quark model even at relatively small chemical potential. Specifically, it yields a first-order phase transition (FOPT) line close to those obtained from the reconstruction method. Additionally, the minimal-potential model reproduces a rising Cornell-like quark-antiquark potential up to the hadronic distance $\ell=1.5$ fm, which we use as a phenomenological finite-distance criterion for confinement. The location of the confinement/deconfinement crossover in this minimal-potential model is also close to its counterpart in the original light-quark model.

</details>


### [80] [Chiral Spin(7) Sigma Models and Topological Modular Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11856)
*Xingyang Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 Spin(7) 流形上构造了典范的手征 $\mathcal N=(0,1)$ sigma 模型，通过左行费米子丛的秩七结构与诱导 String 结构，计算其拓扑模形式第一挠群中的类，并给出该类非零的充要条件：目标流形欧拉数为奇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于构造无内部反常的精确手征量子场论（chiral QFT），并理解 Spin(7) 几何中内部反常（internal anomaly）、引力反常（gravitational anomaly）与有限群反常（finite-group anomaly）的抵消与遗留条件。摘要指出需寻找能实现非零拓扑模形式类的紧致无挠 Spin(7) 目标。

Method: 方法为在 Spin(7) 流形上构造典范手征 $\mathcal N=(0,1)$ sigma 模型；利用 Spin(7) 结构固定左行费米子的七秩丛，使其二次指数与切表示匹配。取左行丛的横截面，其 1D 零点轨迹具有诱导 String 结构，并计算该轨迹在拓扑模形式（topological modular forms）第一挠群中的类。同时考察环面 Joyce 作用与对角 Joyce 族的可规范性与反常。

Result: 左行费米子丛秩差导致恰遗留一个右行 Majorana–Weyl 费米子的引力反常，而内部反常抵消。该拓扑类非零当且仅当目标欧拉数为奇。环面上的标准 Joyce 作用无内部有限群反常，可规范化定义精确手征自旋 QFT；对角 Joyce 族（由坐标反射与保持 Cayley 形式的半平移生成）的轨道折叠欧拉数恒为偶。Fermat 六次曲面的自由反全纯商是紧致无挠 Spin(7) 目标，欧拉数为 1305，可实现非零类。

Conclusion: Spin(7) 手征 sigma 模型提供了可控的反常结构：内部反常抵消而保留单一引力反常，且拓扑模形式类的非零性由目标欧拉数奇偶决定。多数已知可规范构造（如对角 Joyce 族）给出偶欧拉数从而平凡类，而 Fermat 六次曲面的反全纯商给出奇欧拉数 1305 的实现，验证了非零类的存在。

Abstract: We construct canonical chiral $\mathcal N=(0,1)$ sigma models on Spin(7) manifolds. The Spin(7) structure fixes the rank-seven bundle of the left-moving fermions, whose quadratic index matches the tangent representation, so the internal anomalies cancel while the rank difference leaves the gravitational anomaly of one right-moving Majorana--Weyl fermion. A transverse section of the left-moving bundle has a 1D zero locus with induced String structure, and we compute its class in the first torsion group of topological modular forms. The class is nonzero precisely when the Euler characteristic of the target is odd. The standard Joyce action on the torus has no internal finite-group anomaly and can be gauged to define an exact chiral spin QFT. We show that the orbifold Euler characteristic is even throughout the diagonal Joyce family generated by coordinate reflections and half-shifts preserving the Cayley form. By contrast, a free antiholomorphic quotient of the Fermat sextic is a compact torsion-free Spin(7) target with Euler characteristic 1305 and realizes the nonzero class.

</details>


### [81] [Non-invertible Selection Rules from Generalized Discrete Gauging of Finite Non-Abelian Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11895)
*Hiroshi Ohki,Shohei Uemura*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究离散 $H$-规范（$H$-gauging）理论中的非可逆选择定则（non-invertible selection rules），提出一个包含广义场变换的 $H$-规范模型统一框架，可处理非阿贝尔群（non-Abelian groups）情形。通过分析半直积（semidirect product）$G \rtimes H$ 并引入投影特征标（projected characters），给出 $n$-点裸耦合非零的充要条件，并揭示剩余场分量服从由 Clebsch-Gordan 系数控制的融合式代数（fusion-like algebra）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有离散整体对称群（discrete global symmetry group）$G$ 的理论在经离散 $H$-规范后会产生非可逆选择定则，原有基于标准张量积分解（tensor product decomposition）或共轭类（conjugacy classes）的选择定则不再充分，尤其当群为非阿贝尔时 $H$ 引发的变换会非平凡混合 $G$-多重态（$G$-multiplet）的内部自由度。因此需要一个系统框架来刻画此类理论及其耦合限制。

Method: 建立 $H$-规范模型的一般框架，纳入广义场变换（generalized field transformations），自然容纳非阿贝尔群及多维不可约表示（multidimensional irreducible representations）；通过分析完整半直积 $G \rtimes H$，引入投影特征标（projected characters）推导 $n$-点裸耦合非零的充要条件，并检验剩余场分量的代数结构。

Result: 投影特征标给出非零 $n$-点裸耦合的充要条件；剩余场分量服从一个结合性的融合式代数（associative fusion-like algebra），其结构由 Clebsch-Gordan 系数决定。在现象学层面，这些选择定则限制允许的相互作用并对耦合常数施加特定关系。结论通过 $Δ(54) \cong Δ(27)\rtimes \mathbb{Z}_2$ 与 $S_4 \cong A_4 \rtimes \mathbb{Z}_2$ 等具体例子加以说明。

Conclusion: 源自离散 $H$-规范的非可逆选择定则可借助 $G \rtimes H$ 的投影特征标系统描述，其框架适用于非阿贝尔群情形，并对允许相互作用与耦合常数关系提供约束，可通过具体群例子（如 $Δ(54)$、$S_4$）验证。

Abstract: We investigate non-invertible selection rules originating from the discrete $H$-gauging of theories with an underlying discrete global symmetry group $G$. To systematically describe these theories, we formulate a general framework for $H$-gauged models that incorporates generalized field transformations. Our approach naturally accommodates non-Abelian groups, for which multidimensional irreducible representations play an essential role. In such models with non-Abelian groups, the transformations induced by $H$ non-trivially mix the internal components of $G$-multiplets, potentially projecting out specific degrees of freedom. Consequently, conventional selection rules based on standard tensor product decompositions or conjugacy classes become insufficient. By analyzing the full semidirect product $G \rtimes H$, we introduce projected characters to derive necessary and sufficient conditions for non-vanishing $n$-point bare couplings. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the remaining field components obey an associative fusion-like algebra governed by their Clebsch-Gordan coefficients. Phenomenologically, these selection rules restrict allowed interactions and impose specific relations among coupling constants. We illustrate our results through concrete examples, including $Δ(54) \cong Δ(27)\rtimes \mathbb{Z}_2$ and $S_4 \cong A_4 \rtimes \mathbb{Z}_2$.

</details>
