### [82] [Mitigating Sensor Cracking using the Interposer Strategy in the ATLAS ITk Strip Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10891)
*ATLAS ITk Strips Collaboration*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该论文提出在 ATLAS ITk Strip 模块中引入中间层（interposer，即软胶粘剂层与 kapton 隔离层）以解耦热应力，从而缓解硅传感器（silicon sensor）因热应力导致的开裂。原型验证表明该设计显著降低传感器热应力，在相关工作温区内未出现传感器断裂，该策略已被采纳为 ATLAS ITk Strip 桶部（barrel）的主要防开裂方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATLAS ITk Strip 模块中的硅传感器存在因热应力（thermal stress）导致开裂的风险，需要有效的缓解策略。论文旨在通过模块结构设计的改变来解耦热应力，解决传感器开裂问题。

Method: 在模块叠层（module stack-up）中加入软胶粘剂（soft adhesive）层与 kapton 隔离层构成的中间层（interposer），以解耦热应力；开展材料测试验证新增组件，开发将新层集成到模块组装流程中的新工艺，并进行设计验证与质量控制测试。

Result: 设计验证与质量控制测试结果显示，新设计显著降低了传感器所承受的热应力；在装载中间层模块的原型支撑结构测试中，于 ATLAS 运行的相关温度范围内未观察到传感器断裂。

Conclusion: 中间层策略被采纳为 ATLAS ITk Strip 桶部（barrel）中主要的传感器开裂缓解措施（sensor cracking mitigation）。

Abstract: This paper introduces the strategy of using an interposing layer to mitigate thermal-stress-induced fracturing of silicon sensors in ATLAS ITk Strip modules. This strategy involves a change in the module design in which layers of soft adhesive and a kapton separator, known as an interposer, are added to the module stack-up to decouple thermal stresses. This paper discusses the interposer design and its implementation in the ATLAS ITk Strip barrel, including material tests to validate the additional components, the development of new processes to integrate the new layers into the module assembly workflow, results from design validation and quality control testing of prototype interposed modules, and studies indicating that the new design significantly reduces the thermal stress experienced by the sensor. Tests of prototype support structures loaded with interposed modules have shown no sensor fractures in the relevant temperature range for operation in ATLAS, resulting in the adoption of the interposer strategy as the primary sensor cracking mitigation in the ATLAS ITk Strip barrel.

</details>


### [83] [Post-fabrication trimming of a 1024-pixel, single-photon counting, Microwave Kinetic Inductance Detector array with a pixel pitch of 150 micron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10146)
*Wilbert Ras-Vinke,Hessel Schulte,David J. Thoen,Kevin Kouwenhoven,Steven A. H. de Rooij,Tonny A. H. M. Coppens,Jochem J. A. Baselmans,Pieter J. de Visser*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文针对微波动态电感探测器（MKID）阵列中由制备与紧密排布引起的频率散射（frequency scatter）问题，提出一种后制备校正方法，在150 μm像元间距的1024像素阵列上将频率散射从1.1×10⁻²降至3.5×10⁻⁴，良率由76%提升至94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波动态电感探测器（microwave kinetic inductance detectors, MKID）具备单光子计数与能量分辨能力、零读出噪声、极低暗计数，并兼容频分复用（frequency-division multiplexing），适合大规模成像阵列。然而阵列良率常受制备引起的频率散射导致的谐振器碰撞（resonator collisions）限制；作者进一步发现紧密像素排布也会显著增加频率散射。

Method: 开发了一种后制备校正方法（post-fabrication correction method），适用于像元间距（pixel pitch）为150 μm的紧密排布阵列。该方法在单个倍频程读出带宽（single octave of readout bandwidth）内复用（multiplexed）的1024像素阵列上进行了验证。

Result: 频率散射由1.1×10⁻²降至3.5×10⁻⁴，良率（yield）由76%提升至94%。

Conclusion: 该后制备校正方法可有效缓解紧密排布阵列中的频率散射问题，显著提高阵列良率，适用于大规模MKID成像阵列的制备。

Abstract: Microwave Kinetic Inductance Detectors are superconducting resonators capable of single-photon counting with energy resolving capability at visible and near-infrared wavelengths. Their zero read noise, extremely low dark counts, and compatibility with frequency-division multiplexing make them well suited for large imaging arrays. The detector yield of these arrays is often limited by the fabrication-induced frequency scatter leading to resonator collisions. We find that the tight packing of the pixels can add significant frequency scatter as well. We developed a post-fabrication correction method that is suitable for tightly packed arrays with a pixel pitch of $150\ \mathrm{μm}$. We demonstrate this method on a 1024-pixel array multiplexed on a single octave of readout bandwidth and show a reduction in frequency scatter from $1.1\times 10^{-2}$ to $3.5\times 10^{-4}$ and an increase in yield from $76\%$ to $94\%$.

</details>

### [84] [GW Explorer: A Beginner's Guide -- Developing a Computational Gravitational-Wave Outreach Curriculum for High School Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10717)
*Rachel Langgin,Bradlee Tejeda,Carl-Johan Haster*

Main category: physics.ed-ph

TL;DR: 该论文介绍 GW Explorer: A Beginner's Guide，一套面向高中生的引力波（gravitational-wave, GW）天体物理科普课程，通过交互式 Python Jupyter notebook 将基础物理与动手计算结合，并提供自主与工作坊两种教学形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引力波资源多面向初学者与高年级学生，在高中（pre-college）层面存在明显空白，本文直接针对该缺口设计课程。

Method: 课程整合基础物理与实操计算，采用自主学习和工作坊两种形式：前者学生在 Google Colab 等云平台独立完成，后者在内华达大学拉斯维加斯分校研究生导师指导下进行；内容涵盖引力、时空、引力波源、干涉测量探测与数据分析。

Result: 课程在本地高中课堂的实施为内容与节奏提供了依据，问卷调查结果显示学生在概念理解与编程信心方面均有提升。

Conclusion: GW Explorer 提供了一个可扩展、开放获取的框架，使真实天体物理研究能够进入高中课堂。

Abstract: We present GW Explorer: A Beginner's Guide, an outreach curriculum designed to introduce high school students to gravitational-wave (GW) astrophysics through interactive Python Jupyter notebooks. Most existing GW resources target beginning audiences and advanced students, leaving a gap at the pre-college level that we directly address. The curriculum integrates foundational physics with hands-on computation implemented through both self-directed and workshop-based instructional formats. In the self-directed format, students completed the curriculum independently on cloud-based platforms such as Google Colab. During the workshop format, students worked through the same activities under the guidance of University of Nevada, Las Vegas graduate student mentors. Topics span gravity, spacetime, GW sources, interferometric detection, and data analysis. An implementation in local high school classrooms informed the content and pacing, and survey results demonstrate gains in conceptual understanding and coding confidence. GW Explorer offers a scalable, open-access framework for authentic astrophysics research in the high school classroom.

</details>

### [85] [Irrationality Measure Controls Long-Wavelength Charge Fluctuations in Quasiperiodic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10948)
*Junmo Jeon,Shiro Sakai*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文证明准周期序（quasiperiodic order）的无理性度量（irrationality measure）所决定的算术分类对长波长电荷涨落（long-wavelength charge fluctuations）具有直接物理后果：红外标度（infrared scaling）由无理频率的无理性指数与壳层电荷分布（hull charge profile）的大谐波衰减共同支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期序由无理频率刻画，其有理逼近能力（即无理性度量）定义了不同的算术类，但这一纯数论分类是否具有物理意义尚待明确。论文旨在建立该算术分类与长波长电荷涨落之间的直接联系。

Method: 在平移协变（translation-covariant）准周期系统中，将无理频率的无理性指数与壳层电荷剖面的大谐波衰减作为两个关键量，分析其对电荷涨落红外标度的支配作用；并通过区分壳层中稳定能隙内占据态与近费米（near-Fermi）态来分离贡献；结果推广至一般平移协变多频准周期系统。

Result: 所有代数无理频率（algebraic irrational frequencies）共享相同的算术标度；而逼近性极好的超越无理频率（transcendental frequencies）可表现出显著增强的红外涨落标度。由壳层中稳定能隙与费米能级分隔的占据态仅贡献解析的红外背景，非平凡标度完全来自近费米态。

Conclusion: 准周期序的算术分类具有直接的物理后果，无理性指数的差异导致可观测的电荷涨落红外标度差异；该框架可推广到一般平移协变多频准周期系统。

Abstract: Quasiperiodic order is characterized by irrational frequencies whose rational approximability known as irrationality measure defines distinct arithmetic classes. We establish that this arithmetic classification has direct physical consequences for long-wavelength charge fluctuations. In translation-covariant quasiperiodic systems, the infrared scaling of charge fluctuation is governed by the interplay between the irrationality exponent of irrational frequency and the large-harmonic decay of the hull charge profile: the latter determines the available charge weight, while the former controls how efficiently that weight is transferred to the infrared. Consequently, all algebraic irrational frequencies share the same arithmetic scaling, whereas exceptionally well-approximable transcendental frequencies can exhibit strongly enhanced infrared fluctuation scaling. We further prove that occupied states separated from the Fermi level by a gap stable throughout the hull contribute only an analytic infrared background, leaving the nontrivial scaling to near-Fermi states. Our results extend to general translation-covariant multi-frequency quasiperiodic systems.

</details>

### [86] [Spectral properties of Random Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11011)
*Anastasis Kafetzopoulos*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文以 Stieltjes 变换方法为技术工具，建立随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）的基础框架，证明样本协方差矩阵经验谱分布（empirical spectral distribution）到 Marchenko-Pastur 分布的最优收敛，并结合 Dyson 方程方法给出局部律（local laws）的普适性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为随机矩阵理论提供理论基础，并推进对随机样本协方差矩阵谱行为（rigidity 与收敛速率）以及多分布族之间插值模型局部律（local laws）的理解。

Method: 采用 Stieltjes 变换方法（Stieltjes transform method）证明经验谱分布的收敛；同时引入 Dyson 方程方法（Dyson equation method）研究介于 Marchenko-Pastur 分布、椭圆律（elliptical law）与圆律（circular law）之间插值的随机矩阵模型。

Result: 证明了随机样本协方差矩阵经验谱分布到确定性 Marchenko-Pastur 分布的最优收敛，给出了关于该矩阵特征值刚性（rigidity）及其收敛速率的新结果，并针对插值模型得到了新的局部律。

Conclusion: 经由上述工作，所得到的局部律（local laws）可被视为具有普适性（universal）。

Abstract: We give the theoretical foundations of random matrix theory through the definitions of a random matrix, a random probability measure and the corresponding empirical spectral distribution. The technical tool we use is the Stieltjes transform method through which we prove optimal convergence of the empirical spectral distribution of random sample covariance matrices to the deterministic Marchenko-Pastur distribution. We also give new results about the rigidity of the eigenvalues of this random sample covariance matrix and the rate of their convergence. We then define the Dyson equation method to prove new local laws about a random matrix model that interpolates between the Marchenko-Pastur distribution, the elliptical law and the circular law. Through our work these local laws can be considered universal.

</details>


### [87] [Recurrence and transience of random walks with drift $ρx^α/t^β$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11046)
*Ngo P. N. Ngoc,Tuan-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文解决了Menshikov与Volkov（2008）关于$\mathbb R_+$上马尔可夫随机游走分类中遗留的临界线情形，证明了当$2\beta-\alpha=1$且$\alpha\in(-1,1)\setminus\{0\}$时游走的常返性，并给出了完整的常返与暂态判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Menshikov与Volkov曾研究一族条件漂移依赖于时间与位置、量级为$\rho x^\alpha t^{-\beta}$的马尔可夫随机游走，但其分类在临界线$2\beta-\alpha=1$、$\alpha\in(-1,1)\setminus\{0\}$的情形仍为公开问题，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者在临界线上证明了常返性，并建立了常返与暂态（recurrence and transience）判据，从而完成$-1<\alpha<1$、$\beta\ge 0$范围内的完整分类；证明不依赖马尔可夫性质（Markov property），且对增量（increments）的假设弱于Menshikov与Volkov所设条件。

Result: 证明了临界线$2\beta-\alpha=1$（$\alpha\in(-1,1)\setminus\{0\}$）情形下游走的常返性，并给出覆盖$-1<\alpha<1$、$\beta\ge 0$的常返与暂态判据，从而完成了该参数范围的分类。

Conclusion: 该工作封闭了Menshikov与Volkov留下的临界情形，将分类结果推广至更一般的非马尔可夫设定及更弱的增量假设下，完善了此类随机游走的常返性理论。

Abstract: Menshikov and Volkov [Electron. J. Probab. 13 (2008)] studied recurrence and transience of a class of Markovian random walks on $\mathbb R_+$ whose conditional drift depends on both time and position and is of order $ρx^αt^{-β}$ with $ρ>0$. The case on the critical line $2β-α=1$, with $α\in(-1,1)\setminus\{0\}$, remained open. We prove recurrence in this remaining case. Furthermore, we establish recurrence and transience criteria that complete the classification for $-1<α<1$ and $β\ge 0$, without assuming the Markov property and under weaker assumptions on the increments than those imposed by Menshikov and Volkov.

</details>


### [88] [Negative correlation for the random-cluster model below one on high-degree regular graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11578)
*Pengfei Tang,Zibo Zhang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究随机簇模型（random-cluster model）在簇参数 \(0<Q<1\) 下的负相关性猜想，证明了一类满足双尺度边等周条件的高度数正则图序列中任意两不同边的指示变量具有严格负协方差，并表明该结论与任意有限图上的成对猜想等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机簇模型在 \(0<Q<1\) 时被猜测具有负相关性（negative dependence），但在一般有限图上连成对负相关（pairwise negative correlation）都尚未解决。论文旨在通过限制到高度数正则图序列来简化问题，同时说明这种限制不本质削弱原猜想。

Method: 方法基于聚合物表示（polymer representation）与簇展开（cluster expansion），并结合对两边相关性主导贡献的几何刻画。所处理图序列需满足双尺度边等周条件（two-scale edge-isoperimetric condition）。

Result: 对每个固定 \(p\in(0,1)\)，序列中所有充分大的图在距离为 \(o(d/\log d)\) 的任意两不同边指示变量间具有严格负协方差，且关于 \(Q\in[0,1)\) 与边的选择一致成立，包含 \(Q=0\) 端点（即条件连通的伯努利键渗流）。等周假设被高度数扩展图族与均匀随机正则图（依概率趋于一）满足，也涵盖超立方体与固定边长高维离散环面等乘积几何。

Conclusion: 限制到发散度数的正则图序列不本质削弱成对负相关猜想：对所有此类图序列（即使仅限距离 \(o(d/\log d)\) 的边对）成立，等价于在任意有限图上成立。论文由此在广泛的高度数正则图类中严格证明了成对负相关。

Abstract: The random-cluster model with cluster parameter \(0<Q<1\) is conjectured
  to exhibit negative dependence, but even pairwise negative correlation
  between distinct edges remains open on general finite graphs. We first
  show that restricting the problem to regular graphs of diverging degree
  does not essentially weaken the pairwise conjecture: validity for all
  such graph sequences, even when restricted to edge pairs at distance
  \(o(d/\log d)\), is equivalent to validity on arbitrary finite graphs.
  We then prove strict pairwise negative correlation for a broad class of
  high-degree regular graph sequences satisfying a two-scale
  edge-isoperimetric condition. More precisely, for every fixed
  \(p\in(0,1)\), all sufficiently large graphs in the sequence have
  strictly negative covariance between any two distinct edge indicators
  at distance \(o(d/\log d)\), uniformly in \(Q\in[0,1)\) and in the
  choice of the two edges. In particular, the result includes the
  \(Q=0\) endpoint, corresponding to Bernoulli bond percolation
  conditioned to be connected.
  The isoperimetric hypothesis is satisfied by high-degree
  expander families and, with probability tending to one, by uniformly
  random regular graphs of diverging degree; its two-scale form also
  allows product geometries such as hypercubes and fixed-side
  high-dimensional discrete tori. The proof is based on a polymer
  representation and cluster expansion, together with a geometric
  identification of the leading contribution to the two-edge
  correlation.

</details>


### [89] [Precise Delocalisation and Gumbel Laws for Eigenvectors of Wigner Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11630)
*Mohammed Osman*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明了 Wigner 矩阵（Wigner matrices）特征向量的退局域化界（delocalisation bound），其中最大元素尺寸与概率衰减指数之间存在精确关系，并给出单条及全体特征向量最大元素的 Gumbel 分布结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画 Wigner 矩阵特征向量的退局域化现象，并建立最大矩阵元素尺寸与概率衰减指数之间的精确关系，同时确定特征向量最大元素的极限分布。

Method: 证明基于三种工具：容斥原理（inclusion-exclusion principle）、Erdős--Xu 的小概率比较（small probability comparison），以及高斯可除矩阵的部分对角化（partial diagonalisation of Gaussian divisible matrices）。

Result: 得到特征向量的退局域化界，且界中最大元素尺寸与概率衰减指数的关系是精确的；此外，单个特征向量与全体特征向量的最大元素均服从 Gumbel 分布（Gumbel distributed）。

Conclusion: 论文确立了 Wigner 矩阵特征向量最大元素的退局域化定量刻画与 Gumbel 极限分布，验证了基于容斥与小概率比较方法的有效性。

Abstract: We prove a delocalisation bound for eigenvectors of Wigner matrices with the precise relationship between the size of the largest entry and the decay exponent of the probability. We also prove that the largest entry of an individual eigenvector and the largest entry of all eigenvectors are both Gumbel distributed. The proof is based on the inclusion-exclusion principle, the small probability comparison of Erdős--Xu, and partial diagonalisation of Gaussian divisible matrices.

</details>


### [90] [Fourier decay of Gaussian multiplicative chaos and boundary geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11761)
*Marcu-Antone Orsoni,William Verreault*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文提出研究高斯乘法混沌（Gaussian multiplicative chaos）Fourier 变换的新方法，确定任意光滑对数相关协方差、任意维数下环面上该类随机测度的 Fourier 维数（Fourier dimension），并给出欧氏空间中有界集与超曲面上混沌的精确公式与尖锐界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯乘法混沌的 Fourier 变换性质此前缺乏系统刻画，尤其是边界几何与多重分形集中如何共同影响 Fourier 衰减尚不清晰；同时 Garban 与 Vargas 关于圆周情形的猜想需要更简洁的证明路径。

Method: 发展一种研究高斯乘法混沌 Fourier 变换的新方法，针对任意光滑对数相关协方差（smooth log-correlated covariance）与任意维数分析环面上的随机测度，并推导欧氏空间中有界集与超曲面上的尖锐界与精确公式。

Result: 确定了环面上该类随机测度在所有维数、所有光滑对数相关协方差下的 Fourier 维数；获得自然类有界集与超曲面上混沌的尖锐界与精确公式，揭示边界几何与多重分形集中（multifractal concentration）共同支配 Fourier 衰减，并存在边界严格降低 Fourier 维数的情形；在圆周情形给出 Garban–Vargas 猜想的更简洁新证明。

Conclusion: 边界几何与多重分形集中共同决定高斯乘法混沌的 Fourier 衰减，边界在某些区域可严格降低 Fourier 维数；所提方法亦为相关猜想提供更简短的证明。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a new approach to studying the Fourier transform of Gaussian multiplicative chaos. We determine the Fourier dimension of these random measures on tori for every smooth log-correlated covariance and in every dimension, and prove sharp bounds and exact formulas for chaos on natural classes of bounded sets and hypersurfaces in Euclidean space. These results highlight how boundary geometry and multifractal concentration jointly govern Fourier decay, including regimes in which the boundary strictly reduces the Fourier dimension. In the circle setting, this gives a substantially simpler new proof of a conjecture of Garban and Vargas.

</details>

### [91] [Admissible Fourier Lengths, KAM Reducibility, and Spectral Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11025)
*Xueyin Wang,Jiangong You*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文建立了一类基于"容许傅里叶长度"的单频SL(2,R)余圈扰动KAM可约化理论，以适配傅里叶宽度代替欧氏光滑性作为迭代正则性度量，并应用于准周期薛定谔算子的谱分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典KAM可约化理论依赖欧氏长度|n|下的解析或Gevrey光滑性，难以处理非单调傅里叶长度所对应的粗糙扰动（如无处可微的Weierstrass型扰动）。作者希望统一并扩展这些设定。

Method: 引入容许傅里叶长度（admissible Fourier length）ℓ，并以正适配傅里叶宽度（positive adapted Fourier width）度量迭代所需正则性；该长度同时控制傅里叶衰减、截断与共振尺度以及小除子（small divisors）的算术条件，从而构造扰动KAM可约化（KAM reducibility）迭代格式。

Result: 该框架涵盖经典解析与Gevrey情形；非单调ℓ的选取可容纳经典无处可微的Weierstrass型扰动，以及不属于任何正Hölder类的连续扰动。谱应用上，得到对应准周期薛定谔算子对所有相位为纯绝对连续谱、积分态密度（integrated density of states）1/2-Hölder连续；Aubry对偶（Aubry dual）在Lebesgue几乎处处对偶相位下具纯点谱，本征函数在ℓ诱导度量下指数局域化。

Conclusion: 该理论统一并推广了经典解析/Gevrey可约化框架，且能构造具纯绝对连续Cantor谱的无处可微准周期势，显示伸张傅里叶长度在谱理论中的有效性。

Abstract: We develop a perturbative KAM reducibility theory for one-frequency $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$ cocycles based on an admissible Fourier length $\ell$. The regularity relevant to the iteration is measured by positive adapted Fourier width rather than ordinary smoothness in the Euclidean length $|n|$. The same length governs Fourier decay, truncation and resonance scales, and the arithmetic condition controlling the small divisors. This framework contains the classical analytic and Gevrey settings, while non-monotone choices of $\ell$ allow classical nowhere differentiable Weierstrass-type perturbations and continuous perturbations outside every positive Hölder class. As spectral applications, we obtain purely absolutely continuous spectrum for every phase and $1/2$-Hölder continuity of the integrated density of states for the associated quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators. The Aubry dual has pure point spectrum for Lebesgue almost every dual phase, with eigenfunctions exponentially localized in the metric induced by $\ell$. We also construct nowhere differentiable quasiperiodic potentials with purely absolutely continuous Cantor spectrum.

</details>

### [92] [Emergence of Gravity's Dynamical and Topological Sectors from Pre-geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10619)
*Andrea Addazi,Giuseppe Meluccio*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作确定了4D前几何引力理论的完整基本组成单元：在未破缺相中，将SO(1,4)或SO(2,3)规范理论与类希格斯场耦合，并在广义协变性刚性约束下，五个不可约不变量构成所有前几何作用量的最小生成集，对称性自发破缺后统一生成爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量、宇宙学常数项及全部4D拓扑不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在为引力提供一个统一的前几何起点，使引力的动力学部分与拓扑部分能够从共同来源中涌现，并为量子引力、暗能量现象学及引力理论中的拓扑相研究提供基础框架。

Method: 方法基于未破缺相中SO(1,4)或SO(2,3)规范理论与类希格斯场耦合，施加广义协变性刚性约束，并通过枚举独立场单项式确定最小生成集L_MM、L_W、L_J、L_θ与L_ϑ；随后考虑对称性自发破缺。

Result: 这五个不可约不变量可生成任意前几何作用量（含其任意泛函），并在对称性破缺后共同产生由爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量、宇宙学常数项以及高斯-博内、庞特里亚金、霍尔斯特和尼-杨等4D拓扑不变量组成的涌现引力理论；同时发现联系普朗克质量与宇宙学常数的跷跷板机制及若干拓扑扇区耦合常数关系。

Conclusion: 引力的动力学扇区与拓扑扇区可统一于同一前几何来源，此为本工作的核心结果；该框架奠定了引力的前几何基础，并可作为量子引力、暗能量现象学与引力拓扑相研究的统一出发点。

Abstract: We identify the complete set of fundamental building blocks for a 4D pre-geometric theory of gravity. Based on a gauge theory of $SO(1,4)$ or $SO(2,3)$ coupled to a Higgs-like field under the rigid constraint of general covariance in the unbroken phase, these building blocks - $\mathcal{L}_\text{MM}$, $\mathcal{L}_\text{W}$, $\mathcal{L}_{J}$, $\mathcal{L}_θ$ and $\mathcal{L}_\vartheta$ - constitute the minimal generating set of independent field monomials from which any pre-geometric action, including arbitrary functionals thereof, can be constructed. While the most general pre-geometric theory can extend beyond linear combinations, these five irreducible invariants serve as the 'atomic' constituents of all possible pre-geometric dynamics. Upon spontaneous symmetry breaking, they collectively generate an emergent gravitational theory consisting of the Einstein-Hilbert action, the cosmological constant term and all 4D topological invariants: the Gauss-Bonnet, Pontryagin, Holst and Nieh-Yan terms. The unification of gravity's dynamical and topological sectors from a common pre-geometric source represents the central result of this work. We also uncover a see-saw mechanism linking the Planck mass and the cosmological constant, as well as several novel relations for the coupling constants of the topological sector, inclusive of the Barbero-Immirzi parameter. This framework establishes the pre-geometric foundations from which all aspects of gravitation can dynamically emerge, providing a unified starting point for quantum gravity, dark energy phenomenology and the study of topological phases in gravitational theories.

</details>


### [93] [Dilaton gravity can enhance quantum coherence and reduce entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10624)
*Zejun Wang,Zhihong Liu,Yu-Xuan Wang,Xiao-Li Huang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究 GHS 膨胀子黑洞对 Dirac 场多种量子资源的影响，发现纠缠被抑制而相干性被增强，且最大纠缠初态在弯曲时空中未必保持最大纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在超越单模近似（single-mode approximation），考察膨胀子黑洞引力场对不同量子资源的作用是否存在差异，以服务于弯曲时空中的相对论量子信息处理。

Method: 以 Garfinkle-Horowitz-Strominger（GHS）膨胀子黑洞背景下的 Dirac 场为对象，用 negativity 刻画量子纠缠（quantum entanglement），用 l1-范数（l1-norm）与相对熵（relative entropy of coherence）刻画量子相干性（quantum coherence）。

Result: 增大膨胀子参数持续抑制纠缠，强引力区仅余有限残余量；而量子相干性被增强。初始最大纠缠态在 GHS 膨胀子时空传播后并非总具有最大 negativity，特定条件下某些非最大纠缠态可保持更强纠缠。

Conclusion: 膨胀子黑洞引力效应具有资源依赖性（resource-dependent），对非局域量子关联与局域量子叠加影响方式本质不同，为弯曲时空中量子资源的操控与保护提供新见解。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of the Garfinkle-Horowitz-Strominger (GHS) dilaton black hole on different quantum resources of Dirac fields beyond the single-mode approximation. By employing the negativity to characterize quantum entanglement and the $l_1$-norm and the relative entropy of coherence to characterize quantum coherence, we demonstrate that these resources exhibit remarkably different responses to the gravitational field. Specifically, increasing the dilaton parameter continuously suppresses quantum entanglement, leaving only a finite residual amount in the strong-gravity regime, whereas quantum coherence is enhanced, indicating that the dilaton-induced spacetime affects nonlocal quantum correlations and local quantum superposition in fundamentally different ways. Furthermore, we show that an initially maximally entangled state does not always possess the largest negativity after propagating in the GHS dilaton spacetime; instead, under appropriate conditions, certain non-maximally entangled states can retain stronger entanglement than the maximally entangled one. These findings reveal the resource-dependent nature of gravitational effects in dilaton black hole backgrounds and provide new insights into the manipulation and protection of quantum resources for relativistic quantum information processing in curved spacetime.

</details>


### [94] [Wave optical imaging of an oscillating electric dipole orbiting a black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10689)
*Filipe Nazaré,João S. Santos,Vitor Cardoso,José Natário*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文从第一性原理出发研究绕克尔黑洞（Kerr black hole）运动的振荡电偶极子的电磁辐射与波动光学成像，推导弯曲时空中的有效4-电流，并给出包含相对论束流、引力透镜与爱因斯坦环的成像结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有电磁辐射描述多依赖几何光学近似，缺乏对弯曲时空中运动电磁源超越几何光学（beyond-geometric-optics）效应的第一性原理基准。

Method: 推导弯曲时空中点状振荡电偶极子的有效4-电流（4-current），利用黑洞微扰理论（black hole perturbation theory）计算未来零无穷远处的辐射场，并建立弯曲时空中运动电磁源的波动光学成像框架。

Result: 获得绕行电偶极子的图像，呈现相对论束流（relativistic beaming）、引力透镜（gravitational lensing）与爱因斯坦环（Einstein rings）；通过比较相反螺旋度（helicity）旋转偶极子的图像研究偏振相关散射，发现位移大致随辐射频率的倒数减小。

Conclusion: 结果为克尔时空（Kerr spacetime）中电磁辐射的超越几何光学描述提供了第一性原理基准。

Abstract: We study the electromagnetic radiation and wave-optical imaging of an oscillating electric dipole orbiting a Kerr black hole. We derive the effective 4-current associated with a pointlike oscillating electric dipole in curved spacetime, and use black hole perturbation theory to compute the resulting radiation field at future null infinity, from first principles. We then develop a wave-optical imaging framework for a moving electromagnetic source in curved spacetime. We obtain images of an orbiting electric dipole, displaying relativistic beaming, gravitational lensing, and Einstein rings. We study polarization-dependent scattering by comparing the images produced by spinning dipoles with opposite helicities, finding a displacement that roughly decreases with the inverse of the radiation frequency, as expected for a beyond-geometric-optics effect. Our results provide a first-principles benchmark for beyond-geometric-optics descriptions of electromagnetic radiation in Kerr spacetime.

</details>


### [95] [Binary-black hole spin population results may be driven by prior degeneracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10753)
*Noah E. Wolfe,Asad Hussain,Jack Heinzel,Salvatore Vitale*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于摘要，该工作质疑此前关于双黑洞自旋倾向位于轨道平面内的暂定证据，指出此类结论可能由易测的"有效自旋"参数外推而来，而有效自旋无法唯一约束天体物理形成通道；仅利用边缘有效自旋分布即可复现自旋倾角分布中的峰值，故作者提出几何图景并认为当前对偏好自旋取向的推断是伪证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波（gravitational waves）携带并合双黑洞自旋信息，其自旋相对轨道的取向（"倾角"，tilt）虽难以测量，却可用于区分双黑洞双星的天体物理形成机制（astrophysical formation mechanisms）。多项分析曾报告暂定证据（tentative evidence）显示黑洞自旋倾向位于双星轨道平面内，因此有必要检验该结论是否可靠。

Method: 作者指出上述结果很可能是从更易测量的"有效自旋"（effective spin）参数外推得到，该参数刻画双黑洞的引力波旋近（inspiral）过程，但可能无法唯一约束其天体物理形成。为验证，作者仅借助边缘有效自旋分布（marginal effective spin distributions）的信息，复现出自旋倾角分布中的峰值；并提出一个几何图景（geometric picture），将有效自旋约束与标准布居建模假设（population modeling assumptions）进行比较。

Result: 仅使用边缘有效自旋分布信息，即可复现自旋倾角分布中的峰值，表明该峰值并不必然反映真实的倾角结构。据此，与标准布居建模假设的几何对比显示，当前对偏好自旋取向（preferred spin orientation）的推断缺乏依据。

Conclusion: 作者认为，现有关于黑洞自旋倾向轨道平面内的推断可能是伪证（spurious），源于对有效自旋分布的外推；有效自旋观测不足以唯一确定天体的形成通道与自旋倾角分布。

Abstract: Gravitational waves carry information on the spins of merging binary-black holes. The orientations of their spins relative to their orbits ("tilts")---while challenging to measure---differentiate the astrophysical formation mechanisms by which merging black hole binaries may form. Multiple analyses have reported tentative evidence that black hole spins are preferentially oriented in the plane of the binary orbit. We show that these results are likely extrapolated from more easily measurable "effective spin" parameters which characterize the gravitational-wave inspiral of black hole binaries but may not uniquely constrain their astrophysical formation. In particular, we reproduce a peak in the spin tilt distribution with information about the marginal effective spin distributions alone. We propose a geometric picture to compare constraints on effective spins to standard population modeling assumptions, suggesting that current inferences of a preferred spin orientation are spurious.

</details>


### [96] [Massive quantum divergence on the Cauchy horizon of a black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10755)
*Marc Casals,Lorenzo Pisani,Peter Taylor*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究带电球对称（Reissner-Nordström）黑洞内部的有质量量子标量场，考察其在 Hartle-Hawking 与 Unruh 两种量子态下随黑洞电荷、场质量与耦合常数的行为。结果表明，与无质量场不同，真空极化与量子应力-能量张量的角向分量在 Cauchy 视界上发散；Eddington-Finkelstein 坐标下的能量通量在 Cauchy 视界上一般不消失，对应 Kruskal 坐标下的入射通量按 V^{-2} 发散，暗示半经典爱因斯坦方程的反作用会产生强奇异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察有质量（massive）量子标量场在 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞内部的量子效应，特别是其与无质量（massless）场行为的差异。作者关注真空极化（vacuum polarization）与量子应力-能量张量（quantum stress-energy tensor）在 Cauchy 视界（Cauchy horizon）上的表现，以理解黑洞内部稳定性与奇异性性质。

Method: 在带电球对称 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞内部的时空中研究有质量量子标量场，变化黑洞电荷、场质量与耦合常数（coupling constant）等参数。分析在两种量子态——Hartle-Hawking 态（对应热平衡黑洞）与 Unruh 态（对应通过 Hawking 辐射蒸发的黑洞）——下场的行为。计算真空极化、量子应力-能量张量的角向分量，并在 Eddington-Finkelstein 坐标 {u,v} 下计算能量通量（energy fluxes）。

Result: 真空极化与量子应力-能量张量的角向分量在 Cauchy 视界上发散，这与无质量场的情形形成鲜明对比。Eddington-Finkelstein 坐标下的能量通量在 Cauchy 视界上一般不消失，意味着在正则 Kruskal 坐标 {U,V} 下入射通量在 Cauchy 视界（V=0）处以 V^{-2} 形式发散。存在例外：一是在极端极限（extremal limit，即黑洞电荷最大时），二是在参数空间中某些精细调节（fine-tuned）区域，此时通量改变符号。

Conclusion: Cauchy 视界上入射通量的 V^{-2} 发散表明，经半经典爱因斯坦方程（semiclassical Einstein equations）的反作用（backreaction）将产生强奇异性（strong singularity），而非经典的较弱奇异性对应物。极端极限与参数空间中通量变号的精细调节区域构成该一般性结论的例外。

Abstract: We investigate a quantum massive scalar field in the interior of a charged and spherically-symmetric (Reissner-Nordström) black hole. We examine the behaviour for varying values of the black hole charge, field mass and coupling constant when the field is in two quantum states: Hartle-Hawking (representing a black hole in thermal equilibrium) and Unruh (representing a black hole evaporating via the emission of Hawking radiation). We show that the vacuum polarization as well as the angular components of the quantum stress-energy tensor diverge on the Cauchy horizon, in stark contrast to what happens for massless fields. We also calculate the energy fluxes in Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates $\{u,v\}$. We show that these fluxes on the Cauchy horizon do not generically vanish. This implies, in particular, that in regular, Kruskal coordinates $\{U,V\}$ the ingoing flux diverges like $V^{-2}$ on the Cauchy horizon (where $V=0$). This divergence suggests that its backreaction via the semiclassical Einstein equations would yield a strong singularity, as opposed to its weaker, classical counterpart. Interestingly, there are exceptions, in which the energy fluxes in Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates vanish: (i) in the extremal limit (where the black hole is maximally charged); (ii) certain fine-tuned regions of parameter space, where the fluxes change sign.

</details>


### [97] [WKB approximation for quasi-bound states and trapped modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10880)
*R. A. Konoplya,Z. Stuchlík,A. Zhidenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出一种半解析（semi-analytic）方法，用于计算由有效势（effective potential）局域极小值支持的弱阻尼准束缚态（quasi-bound states）与陷模（trapped modes），并给出 Mathematica 实现，在黑洞与星体几何中验证其精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱阻尼准束缚态的实频与极小虚部（衰减率）在数值上难以同时精确求解，尤其对非有理有效势与不同致密天体几何（compact-object geometries）缺乏通用手段，因此需要一种易于推广的半解析方案。

Method: 实频由任意高阶局域 WKB 展开（local WKB expansion）结合 Padé 重求和（Padé resummation）获得；指数小的虚部则通过 Gamow 近似（Gamow approximation），即穿越单势垒或双势垒的隧穿（tunnelling）来估计。局域量子化仅需势在其极小值处的导数，故适用于非有理势与多种几何，并提供实现该流程的 Mathematica notebook。

Result: 与连分式（continued-fraction）方法及直接数值结果对比，涵盖 Schwarzschild 与 Kerr 黑洞周围的大质量标量场（massive scalar fields），以及均匀密度星体（uniform-density star）的轴向陷模（axial trapped modes），该方法给出准确的实频，并在超辐射区间（superradiant regime）之外给出有用的衰减率估计。

Conclusion: 该方法可半自动、半解析地计算弱阻尼准束缚态与陷模，具备对非有理势及不同致密天体几何的广泛适用性；其实频精度高，衰减率估计在非超辐射区域具有参考价值。

Abstract: We develop a largely automatic semi-analytic method for calculating weakly damped quasi-bound states and trapped modes supported by a local minimum of an effective potential. The real part of the frequency is obtained from an arbitrarily high-order local WKB expansion and Padé resummation, while the exponentially small imaginary part is estimated using the Gamow approximation for tunnelling through one or two potential barriers. Because the local quantization requires only derivatives of the potential at its minimum, the method applies readily to non-rational effective potentials and to different compact-object geometries. We provide a Mathematica notebook implementing the procedure. Comparisons with continued-fraction and direct numerical results for massive scalar fields around Schwarzschild and Kerr black holes and for axial trapped modes of a uniform-density star show that the method yields accurate real frequencies and useful estimates of decay rates outside the superradiant regime.

</details>


### [98] [Electrovacuum Black Hole Uniqueness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11140)
*Qing Han,Marcus Khuri,Gilbert Weinstein,Jingang Xiong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在轴对称、稳态、电真空（electrovacuum）设定下证明了黑洞唯一性猜想（black hole uniqueness conjecture），并借助奇异调和映射（singular harmonic maps）的精细渐近分析（含轴上的解析性假设）刻画了多视界解的结构：或为对偶旋转下的 Majumdar--Papapetrou 解（对数角度缺陷全为零），或所有有限轴杆对数角度缺陷严格为负、相互作用力严格吸引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞唯一性猜想在多视界情形下的推广尚需在电真空与轴对称稳态框架中得到严格处理，特别是轴上的奇异结构与相互作用力的符号问题。该工作旨在将真空 Kerr 唯一性所用的奇异调和映射方法延伸至 Einstein--Maxwell 情形。

Method: 方法上沿用并扩展文献 [18] 中用于真空 Kerr 唯一性的奇异调和映射方法，对相关奇异调和映射进行精细的渐近分析（refined asymptotic analysis），并引入轴上的解析性假设（analyticity hypothesis）。

Result: 结果表明：该类的任何渐近平坦（asymptotically flat）Einstein--Maxwell 解，若具有多于一个黑洞视界分支，则要么（至多相差一个对偶旋转 duality rotation）为 Majumdar--Papapetrou 解，此时所有对数角度缺陷（logarithmic angle defects）消失；要么每个有限轴杆对数角度缺陷严格为负，从而每个相互作用力严格为吸引。

Conclusion: 在所述假设下，多视界轴对称稳态电真空解被严格约束为两类：缺陷全零的 Majumdar--Papapetrou 型，或缺陷全负且相互作用力严格吸引的情形，由此给出该设定下黑洞唯一性猜想的证明。

Abstract: We prove the black hole uniqueness conjecture in the axially symmetric, stationary, electrovacuum setting, subject to the refined asymptotic analysis of the associated singular harmonic maps, which includes an analyticity hypothesis at the axes. More precisely, it is shown that any asymptotically flat solution of the Einstein--Maxwell equations in this class, with more than one black hole horizon component is either: Majumdar--Papapetrou, up to a duality rotation, in which case all logarithmic angle defects vanish, or every finite axis rod logarithmic angle defect is strictly negative and hence every interaction force is strictly attractive. The proof extends the singular harmonic map method used for vacuum Kerr uniqueness in [18].

</details>


### [99] [Quasi-isotropic Asymptotic Expansions in Varying Speed of Light Cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11217)
*Dimitrios Trachilis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将爱因斯坦方程在宇宙初始奇点附近的准各向同性解推广至光速可变和/或引力常数可变的理论，并在同步参考系中构造形式渐近级数展开，发现所得解不包含足够数目的任意函数，因而不构成这些理论的通解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多基于 Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker（FLRW）模型，其结果的普适性受限。本文旨在纳入光速可变与引力"常数"可变所引入的额外自由度，对变光速宇宙学（varying speed of light cosmologies）进行更一般的渐近分析。

Method: 采用同步参考系（synchronous reference system）构造形式渐近级数展开（formal asymptotic series expansion），并应用 Landau-Lifshitz 准各向同性方法（quasi-isotropic method），将爱因斯坦方程在宇宙初始奇点附近的准各向同性解推广至含两个可变"常数"的模型。

Result: 所构造的解包含由两个可变"常数"引入的额外自由度，但在任意函数数目上不满足要求。

Conclusion: 所得解不含有成为这些理论通解所需的任意函数数目，因此不能视为光速可变与引力常数可变理论的通解（general solutions）。

Abstract: We generalize the quasi-isotropic solution of the Einstein equations near a cosmological initial singularity to models with a varying speed of light and/or a varying gravitational 'constant'. We construct a formal asymptotic series expansion in a synchronous reference system, taking into account the additional degrees of freedom introduced by the two varying 'constants'. By applying the Landau-Lifshitz quasi-isotropic method, we go beyond the standard results of previous studies that based on the Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) model, providing a more general asymptotic analysis of varying speed of light cosmologies. We show that the resulting solutions do not contain the required number of arbitrary functions to qualify as general solutions of these theories.

</details>


### [100] [Constraining Modified Mass-to-Horizon Cosmology Through Primordial Inflationary Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11221)
*A. Sheykhi,G. G. Luciano,A. Benkrane*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在广义质量-视界关系（mass-to-horizon relation, MHR）$M=\gamma c^2 L^n/G$ 下研究慢滚暴胀，发现幂律势（power-law potential）无法满足当前 CMB 限制，而 Starobinsky 势对偏离 $n=1$ 高度敏感，其归一化在 $N=60$ 时给出 $0.960\lesssim n\lesssim 1.040$ 的严格约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宇宙学中的质量-视界关系（MHR）可能只是更一般标度关系的特例，检验其偏离对早期宇宙暴胀动力学的影响具有理论意义。作者旨在探索暴胀，尤其是 Starobinsky 类模型，能否作为检验广义视界热力学（horizon thermodynamics）与偏离标准 MHR 标度的灵敏探针。

Method: 基于 Padmanabhan 的涌现范式（emergence paradigm），推导平坦 FRW 宇宙下的修正 Friedmann 方程；在慢滚近似（slow-roll approximation）下分析正则标量场（inflaton）的动力学；分别考察幂律势与 Starobinsky 势的暴胀唯象学，并对 $n=1+\Delta$ 情形进行微扰分析（perturbative analysis），以获得暴胀可观测量的修正。

Result: 对幂律势，MHR 修正无法使模型与当前 CMB 对张量-标量比（$r$）和标量谱指数（$n_s$）的限制相符。Starobinsky 暴胀则对偏离 $n=1$ 显著敏感；微扰分析给出可观测量的修正，且在固定 Starobinsky 归一化方案下，标量功率谱归一化（scalar power-spectrum normalization）要求 $N=60$ 个 e 折时 $0.960\lesssim n\lesssim 1.040$，该约束明显强于谱指数限制。

Conclusion: 暴胀，特别是 Starobinsky 类模型，可作为检验广义视界热力学及偏离标准 MHR 标度的灵敏探针；其中功率谱归一化提供的约束比谱指数更为严格，表明暴胀观测对广义视界标度关系具有显著敏感性。

Abstract: We investigate slow-roll inflation in a modified cosmological framework inspired by a generalized mass-to-horizon relation (MHR), $M=γ{c^2 L^n}/{G}$, where $n$ is a real parameter and $γ$ a dimensional constant. Using Padmanabhan's emergence paradigm, we derive the modified Friedmann equations for a flat FRW universe and analyze the dynamics of a canonical scalar field (inflaton) under the slow-roll approximation. We study the resulting inflationary phenomenology for power-law and Starobinsky potentials. For power-law potentials, the MHR modification fails to reconcile these models with current CMB constraints on $r$ and $n_s$. In contrast, Starobinsky inflation exhibits significant sensitivity to deviations from $n=1$. A perturbative analysis ($n=1+Δ$) yields corrections to inflationary observables. We observe that the scalar power-spectrum normalization, under a fixed-Starobinsky prescription, imposes the stringent constraint $0.960 \lesssim n \lesssim 1.040$ for $N=60$ efolds. This is considerably tighter than spectral-index bounds. Our results establish inflation, particularly Starobinsky-like models, as a sensitive probe of generalized horizon thermodynamics and departures from standard MHR scaling.

</details>


### [101] [Improving the Sensitivity of Gravitational Wave Detection with Weighted Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11401)
*Ann-Kristin Malz,Gregory Ashton,Nicolo Colombo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对引力波（gravitational-wave）搜寻中多搜寻流程（pipeline）输出融合的统计可靠性问题，提出在共形预测（conformal prediction）框架中引入似然比重加权（likelihood-ratio reweighting）以应对协变量偏移（covariate shift），并在模拟数据集上证明其可恢复良好校准的覆盖率并提升探测阈值附近事件的置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年公里级干涉引力波探测器已观测到数百例致密双星并合，但数据由噪声主导，通常依赖多个独立搜寻算法（pipeline）以提升灵敏度与稳健性。传统做法仅选取最显著的单一流程输出，缺乏对候选事件的严格统计置信度估计；同时训练/校准用模拟数据与真实无标签观测之间存在分布偏移（distribution shift），会破坏覆盖保证并导致置信度估计有偏。

Method: 采用基于共形预测的框架融合所有流程的输出，为候选事件提供统计上严格的置信度估计；并针对模拟数据集与真实观测之间的协变量偏移（covariate shift），在该框架中引入似然比重加权（likelihood-ratio reweighting）进行校正。评估使用含模拟信号的模拟数据集（mock datasets）。

Result: 在含模拟信号的模拟数据集上，加权共形预测（weighted conformal prediction）在协变量偏移下恢复了良好校准的覆盖率（well-calibrated coverage），并提升了探测阈值附近事件的置信度，从而找回原本会被漏检的真实信号。

Conclusion: 融合多流程输出可提升灵敏度与排序稳健性，但需搭配在其数据属性随观测轮次（observing runs）演变时仍保持有效的原则性统计框架。将似然比重加权纳入共形预测可应对协变量偏移，为引力波候选事件提供更可靠的置信度估计。

Abstract: In the last decade, kilometre-scale interferometric gravitational-wave detectors have observed hundreds of compact binary mergers, the majority of which are binary black holes. However, the data are noise-dominated, and multiple independent search algorithms (pipelines) are used to enhance sensitivity and improve robustness. Rather than the standard approach of selecting the most significant pipeline output, we combine the outputs from all pipelines using a conformal prediction-based framework to provide statistically rigorous confidence estimates for candidate events. While combining pipelines improves sensitivity and ranking robustness, it requires a principled statistical framework that remains valid as data properties evolve across observing runs. A key challenge is distribution shifts between simulated datasets used for training and calibration and the real, unlabelled, observations used for testing, which can invalidate coverage guarantees and bias confidence estimates. In this work, we address this challenge by incorporating likelihood-ratio reweighting into our conformal prediction framework to account for covariate shift. Using mock datasets containing simulated signals, we demonstrate that weighted conformal prediction restores well-calibrated coverage under covariate shift and increases the confidence of events near the detection threshold, recovering true signals that would otherwise be missed.

</details>


### [102] [Towards the consistent perturbative expansion in discrete gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02181)
*V. M. Khatsymovsky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文讨论离散引力（discrete gravity，单纯形或 Regge 微积分）中微扰展开（perturbative expansion）的正确定义问题，核心是格点传播子（propagator）的极点倍增与解析结构偏差。作者提出同时使用两种差分算符并配合同步规范（synchronous gauge）的修正作用量，通过主值传播子（principal value propagator）使各费曼图在 ε→0 时分别有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究引力单圈修正（graviton loop corrections）等物理效应需要离散引力中的微扰展开。然而对称差分算符 Δ^{(s)}_λ 导致传播子出现极点倍增（pole doubling），使 dp_0 积分结果较连续情形翻倍；常用算符 Δ_λ 又因 Δ_λ≠-Δ̄_λ 带来棘手的解析结构，同样与连续理论不符。

Method: 作者构造含 Δ^{(s)}_λ 与 Δ_λ 两者的作用量 Š_g，并施加同步规范 g_{0λ}=g_{0λ}^{(0)}，其实现方式是加入对 n^λ(g_{λμ}-g_{λμ}^{(0)}) 双线性的项，取 n^λ=[1,-ε(Δ^{(s)α}Δ^{(s)}_α)^{-1}Δ^{(s)β}]、ε→0 以消除 p_0=0 处的奇异性。基于传播子 Ĝ(n,n̄)，通过对实 n=n̄ 的解析延拓构造主值传播子 [Ĝ(n,n)+Ĝ(n̄,n̄)]/2，奇点按 p_0^{-j}→[(p_0+iε)^{-j}+(p_0-iε)^{-j}]/2 处理。作者分析了一个使用 Δ_λ 或 Δ^{(s)}_λ 的单参数作用量族，并考察主值规范固定项的有限性与鬼场（ghost）贡献的消失。

Result: 在单参数作用量族中，仅有一个作用量能在小外部动量下重现收敛的连续图（convergent continuum diagrams），这一要求被视为离散化中自然应满足的条件。主值传播子的奇点处理使各费曼图在 ε→0 时分别保持有限，规范固定项与鬼场贡献的分析亦得到落实。分析辅以电磁（Yang-Mills）情形作示例说明。

Conclusion: 同时引入 Δ^{(s)}_λ 与 Δ_λ 并采用同步规范与主值规范固定，可规避极点倍增与解析结构不匹配的问题，唯一满足小动量下连续收敛判据的作用量给出了自洽的离散引力微扰方案。该方法在 Yang-Mills 情形中亦得到验证性说明。

Abstract: We consider correctly defining the perturbative expansion in a discrete gravity (simplicial or Regge calculus) needed to study physical effects like graviton loop corrections to Newton's potential.
  For the symmetric derivative $Δ^{(s)}_λ=i\sin p_λ$ in the finite-difference action, the propagator has a graviton pole at $\sin^2p_0=\sum^3_{α=1}\sin^2p_α$, or, at small $p_α$, at $p_0$ close to 0 or $\pmπ$. This pole doubling means doubling the result of integration over d$p_0$ compared to the continuum.
  The usual derivative $Δ_λ=\exp(ip_λ)-1$ leads to a tricky analytical structure of the propagator, since $Δ_λ\neq-\barΔ_λ$, and again to a discrepancy with the continuum.
  The way out is to use an action $\check{S}_{\rm g}$ with both $Δ^{(s)}_λ$ and $Δ_λ$ and the synchronous gauge $g_{0λ}=g_{0λ}^{(0)}$ (implemented by adding a term bilinear in $n^λ(g_{λμ}-g_{λμ}^{(0)})$, $n^λ=[1,-\varepsilon(Δ^{(s)α}Δ^{(s)}_α)^{-1}Δ^{(s)β}]$, $\varepsilon\to0$, thus removing singularities at $p_0=0$).
  Given the propagator $\check{G}(n,\bar{n})$, we form a principal value propagator $[\check{G}(n,n)+\check{G}(\bar{n},\bar{n})]/2$ by analytically continuing from real $n=\bar{n}$. Singularities are resolved like $p_0^{-j}\to[(p_0+i\varepsilon)^{-j}+(p_0-i\varepsilon)^{-j}]/2$ leading to separate diagram finiteness at $\varepsilon\to0$.
  We analyze a 1-parameter family of actions differing in using $Δ_λ$ vs $Δ^{(s)}_λ$, find the only one reproducing convergent continuum diagrams for small external momenta (which is natural to demand from discretization), consider finiteness of the principal value gauge-fixing term and vanishing ghost contribution. The analysis is illustrated by the electromagnetic (Yang-Mills) case.

</details>


### [103] [Gravitational lensing in a spacetime with extra dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11423)
*Mattia Villani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文为系列研究的第四篇，探讨以宇宙学额外维度（cosmological extra dimensions）替代暗物质（dark matter）与暗能量（dark energy），本文聚焦引力透镜（gravitational lensing），推导时间延迟（time delay）表达式并提出修正的似然函数（likelihood）用于质量重建（mass reconstruction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系列研究旨在不引入暗物质与暗能量（dark matter and dark energy）的前提下，解释宇宙加速膨胀（accelerated expansion of the Universe）与旋涡星系旋转曲线（rotation curve of spiral galaxies），本文延续该思路并转向引力透镜这一观测探针。

Method: 在额外维度可膨胀或收缩（expansion or contraction of the extra dimensions）的框架下研究引力透镜（gravitational lensing），推导相应的时间延迟（time delay）表达式，并构建考虑该效应的修正质量重建（mass reconstruction）似然函数（likelihood）。

Result: 给出引力透镜场景下的时间延迟解析表达式，并提供一种纳入额外维度膨胀或收缩影响的修正似然函数，可用于质量重建流程。

Conclusion: 额外维度效应对引力透镜的时间延迟与质量重建具有可纳入统计推断的影响，表明该模型可通过透镜观测加以检验，但摘要未给出具体数值结果或观测约束。

Abstract: This is the fourth paper of a series in which we consider the possibility to use cosmological extra dimensions to explain the accelerated expansion of the Universe and the rotation curve of spiral galaxies without introducing dark matter and dark energy. Here we study gravitational lensing in this setting; we derive an expression for time delay and present a modified likelihood for the mass reconstruction procedure which takes into account the effect of the expansion or contraction of the extra dimensions.

</details>


### [104] [Discrete gravitational diagram technique and corrections to the Newtonian potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03228)
*V. M. Khatsymovsky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于单纯形 Regge 引力（simplicial Regge gravity），通过对联络做泛函积分得到钟形测度，并在超立方（hypercubic）结构上将其参数化为勒贝格测度（Lebesgue measure）。该参数化的线性部分给出离散费曼图（Feynman diagrams）并与有限连续图近似、对无穷图保持有限，非线性部分产生新顶点与图。测度在边长尺度 b=b_s∼η^{1/2} 处取极大，且微扰展开在该点附近才不出现 η 的递增幂次。方法用于牛顿势（Newton's potential），部分新图相互抵消，S 矩阵展开由小参数 η^{-1} 的标准图级数构成，单圈图（one-loop diagrams）对应领头阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以单纯形 Regge 引力为出发点，借助对联络的泛函积分构造钟形测度，并寻求使其可作勒贝格处理的参数化。核心动机是找到微扰展开（perturbative expansion）的合适起点，使展开不出现 η（测度中的自由因子指数参数）的递增幂次，从而为展开起点的选择提供动力学机制。

Method: 采用超立方结构（部分变量被冻结），由格点度规 g_{λμ} 描述，并将其参数化以使测度为勒贝格型。参数化的线性部分对应离散费曼图，非线性部分引入新顶点与图。使用软同步规范（soft synchronous gauge）的离散版本，配合主值型处方（principal value type prescription）与精细差分作用量，把类时长度尺度固定在测度可闭式表示的层级。

Result: 测度在 b=b_s∼η^{1/2} 处取极大；为保证对测度和 S 矩阵的微扰展开均不含有 η 的递增幂次，展开起点须足够接近 b_s。应用于牛顿势时，部分新图（含潜在的大图）相互抵消；S 矩阵展开由标准图组成，并形成以小参数 η^{-1} 为展开参数的级数，文献中计算的一圈图对应领头阶。

Conclusion: 线性参数化使离散图近似有限连续图并在无穷情形下保持有限，非线性部分则给出新顶点与图，其中部分新图相互抵消。b 接近 b_s 的要求构成确立 b 为微扰展开最优起点的动力学机制；软同步规范离散化与主值处方允许将类时长度尺度固定于测度已知闭式的低层级，从而支持对牛顿势与 S 矩阵的系统分析。

Abstract: Starting from simplicial Regge gravity, we use a bell-shaped form of the measure obtained using functional integration over connection. A "hypercubic" structure is considered (some variables are frozen), it is described by the metric $g_{λμ}$ at the sites.
  The metric is parameterized to make the measure Lebesgue. The linear part of this parametrization leads to a discrete form of standard Feynman diagrams that approximates finite continuum diagrams and is finite for infinite ones; the nonlinear part gives new vertices and diagrams.
  The maximum of the measure is at the edge length scale $b = b_{\rm s} \sim η^{1 / 2}$, where $η$ defines the free factor like $ ( - \det \| g_{λμ} \| )^{ η/ 2}$ in the measure and should be a large parameter to ensure true action upon integration over connection. For general perturbative expansion (including both that for the measure and S matrix) to be free of increasing powers of $η$, its starting point must be at $b$ sufficiently close to $b_{\rm s}$; this appears to be a dynamic mechanism for establishing $b$ as an optimal starting point of the perturbative expansion.
  We use a discrete version of the soft synchronous gauge in the principal value type prescription we discuss in a recent paper (with a refined finite-difference form of the action to match the analytical properties of the propagator to the continuum case). This allows one to fix the timelike length scale at a low level for which the measure is known in closed form.
  This technique is applied to Newton's potential. Some of new diagrams, including potentially large ones, are mutually cancelled. The S matrix expansion is analyzed to consist of standard diagrams. These diagrams form series with a small parameter $η^{- 1}$; one-loop diagrams calculated in the literature represent the leading order.

</details>


### [105] [Signatures of charged rotating regular black holes: quasinormal modes, grey-body factors and shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11525)
*Abhisek Barman Maji*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用 Newman-Janis 算法构造了两种带电正则黑洞（ABG 与 BPR）的旋转版本，并系统研究其无质量标量扰动、准正规模（QNM）谱、灰体因子（GBF）、超辐射放大、Lyapunov 指数及阴影轮廓，以检验其可区分性。结果表明旋转 BPR 与 Kerr-Newman 黑洞几乎无法区分，而旋转 ABG 在较大自旋与电荷下呈现明显不同的扰动、散射与几何特征，有望通过引力波与阴影观测加以甄别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则黑洞（regular black hole）作为奇点问题的候选解，其旋转化的天体物理可观测特征尚待系统刻画。本文旨在检验两种带电正则黑洞旋转版本是否可与 Kerr-Newman 黑洞区分，从而评估引力波与阴影观测的甄别能力。

Method: 采用 Newman-Janis 算法（Newman-Janis algorithm）构造 ABG 与 BPR 时空的旋转对应体并验证其正则性；进而计算无质量标量扰动与准正规模（quasinormal mode, QNM）谱，考察其随自旋与电荷参数的变化。另计算灰体因子（grey-body factor, GBF）并检验 QNM-GBF 对应关系，通过匹配渐近展开（matched asymptotic expansions）分析低频超辐射放大因子，并借助 Lyapunov 指数将程函近似 QNM 阻尼率与光子轨道不稳定性相联系。最后基于阴影轮廓与事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope, EHT）观测约束参数空间。

Result: 在所有探测手段下，旋转 BPR 黑洞与 Kerr-Newman 黑洞的行为几乎不可区分；旋转 ABG 几何则展现出不同的微扰、散射与几何性质，且差异在自旋与电荷取值较大时尤为明显。

Conclusion: 分析表明，借助引力波（gravitational wave）与阴影（shadow）观测，或有可能区分不同的带电旋转正则黑洞模型。

Abstract: We construct and study possible astrophysical signatures of the rotating counterparts of two charged regular black holes, namely the Ayón--Beato--García (ABG) and Balart--Panotopoulos--Rincón (BPR) spacetimes -- obtained using the Newman-Janis algorithm. After verifying the regularity of the rotating metrics, we study massless scalar perturbations, compute the quasinormal mode (QNM) spectra, and characterise their dependence on the spin and charge parameters. Grey-body factors (GBFs) are computed, and the QNM-GBF correspondence is verified. The low-frequency superradiant amplification factor is also examined via matched asymptotic expansions. The Lyapunov exponents are used to connect the eikonal QNM damping rate to photon-orbit instability. Additionally, from shadow profiles, we constrain the parameter space of rotating regular black holes using the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) observations. Across all the probes, we find that the rotating BPR and Kerr--Newman black holes behave almost indistinguishably from one another, while the rotating ABG geometry exhibits distinct perturbative, scattering, and geometric properties, particularly at larger values of spin and charge. Our analysis indicates that differentiating between charged rotating regular black hole models may be possible using gravitational wave and shadow observations.

</details>


### [106] [How much chaos can be generated by gravitational collapse before reaching the Planck scale?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11570)
*David Brizuela,Sara F. Uria,Edward Wilson-Ewing*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在经典广义相对论（general relativity）框架下，从强场经典情景演化至普朗克尺度（Planck scale），量化引力坍缩过程中混沌的产生程度，发现到达普朗克尺度时混沌特征仍相对不发达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BKL 猜想（Belinski-Khalatnikov-Lifshitz conjecture）指出奇点附近广义相对论动力学高度混沌，但广义相对论在奇点处失效，通常预期需要量子引力（quantum gravity）等更基础理论描述大曲率区域。由于缺乏公认的量子引力理论，量子效应如何修正经典演化仍不明确，故采取保守立场研究经典动力学。

Method: 研究引力坍缩（gravitational collapse）的经典动力学，从强场（但经典）情景一直演化至普朗克尺度，以量化爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equations）可信期间所产生的混沌量。具体采用香农熵（Shannon entropy）、Kullback-Leibler 散度（Kullback-Leibler divergence）以及傅里叶分析（Fourier analysis）作为量化工具。

Result: 当达到普朗克尺度时，模型的混沌特征仍相对不发达（underdeveloped）。此外，研究刻画了广义相对论中混沌 Bianchi IX 动力学的最终不变相空间分布（invariant phase-space distribution），为其不变排斥子（invariant repeller）提供了新的描述。

Conclusion: 至少在经典区域（classical regime）内，混沌强度不足以抹除关于宇宙初始状态的全部信息，也不足以抹除反弹宇宙学（bouncing cosmologies）语境下前一经典宇宙的信息。

Abstract: According to the Belinski-Khalatnikov-Lifshitz (BKL) conjecture the dynamics of general relativity near singularities is highly chaotic. However, since general relativity breaks down at singularities, it is generally expected that a more fundamental theory, such as quantum gravity, is needed to describe the spacetime dynamics in regions with large spacetime curvature. In the absence of a widely accepted theory of quantum gravity, it remains unclear how exactly quantum effects may modify the classical evolution. Taking a conservative point of view, in this paper we study the classical dynamics of a gravitational collapse, starting from a strong-field (though classical) scenario up to the Planck scale to quantify how much chaos is generated during the time the Einstein equations can be trusted. Specifically, we use the Shannon entropy and Kullback-Leibler divergence, as well as Fourier analysis, to find that, when the Planck scale is reached, the chaotic features of the model remain relatively underdeveloped. This suggests that, at least during the classical regime, chaos is not strong enough to erase all information about the initial state of the universe, or about a previous classical universe in the context of bouncing cosmologies. In addition, we also characterize the final invariant phase-space distribution for the chaotic Bianchi IX dynamics in general relativity, which provides a novel description of its invariant repeller.

</details>


### [107] [From the Test-Mass Limit to Binary Black-Hole Waveforms in Higher-Derivative Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11726)
*Chaoyi Yang,Neev Khera,Dongjun Li,Huan Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文以宇称偶三次引力（parity-even cubic gravity）为范例，构建了从黑洞微扰论（black hole perturbation theory）到可比质量波形（comparable-mass waveforms）的推导路径，并指出次黑洞（secondary black hole）的潮汐响应（tidal response）在微扰阶上与高阶曲率修正同等重要，是得到一致领头阶波形的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多高阶导数理论（higher-derivative theories）预言低质量黑洞对广义相对论（General Relativity）的偏离更强，但其非线性场方程通常使完整双星演化（binary evolution）的可靠模拟难以实现。因此需要一套不依赖理论专属数值相对论模拟的波形构造方案。

Method: 基于修正的 Teukolsky 形式（modified Teukolsky formalism）开展受控的黑洞微扰论计算，将强场通量（strong-field fluxes）与守恒动力学（conservative dynamics）信息嵌入有效单体模型（effective-one-body model），从而构造可比质量双星的旋近-并合-铃宕（inspiral-merger-ringdown）波形。

Result: 次黑洞的潮汐响应与直接高阶曲率修正出现在同一微扰阶，因而对一致的领头阶波形不可或缺；所得强场通量与守恒动力学产生随接近并合而增大的累积旋近退相位（accumulated inspiral dephasing）；嵌入有效单体模型后，得到依赖耦合的退相位与波形峰值位移（waveform-peak shifts）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在缺乏理论专属数值相对论（numerical-relativity）模拟时，强场测试质量（test-mass）计算可用于锚定高阶导数引力的波形模型，提供了连接微扰论与可比质量波形的一条可行路径。

Abstract: Many higher-derivative theories predict stronger deviations from General Relativity for lower-mass black holes, while their nonlinear field equations often prevent reliable simulations of the full binary evolution. Here we develop a route from controlled black hole perturbation theory based on the modified Teukolsky formalism to comparable-mass waveforms, using parity-even cubic gravity as a representative example. We find that the tidal response of the secondary black hole enters at the same perturbative order as the direct higher-curvature correction and is therefore essential for a consistent leading-order waveform. The resulting strong-field fluxes and conservative dynamics produce an accumulated inspiral dephasing that grows toward merger. Embedding this test-mass information into an effective-one-body model, we construct inspiral-merger-ringdown waveforms for comparable-mass binaries and find coupling-dependent dephasing and waveform-peak shifts. Our results demonstrate how strong-field test-mass calculations can anchor waveform models for higher-derivative gravity when theory-specific numerical-relativity simulations are unavailable.

</details>


### [108] [Universal Structure of Horizon Formation in Generic Binary Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11819)
*Yu-Cun Xie,Vaishak Prasad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文推导了双黑洞并合中首个共同表观视界（common apparent horizon）形成时的局域结构，证明其具有普适描述：稳定性算子（stability operator）失逆、主特征值为零，Lyapunov-Schmidt 约化给出平方根分支分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共同视界形成发生在完全非线性（fully nonlinear）区域，超出后牛顿旋近理论（post-Newtonian inspiral theory）与稳态黑洞微扰（perturbations of a stationary black hole）的适用范围，但该事件仍可能存在普适刻画，因而值得给出不依赖对称性假设的理论描述并进行数值检验。

Method: 在不假设对称性的前提下分析边缘外陷落面（marginally outer trapped surface）稳定性算子的可逆性；由最外层稳定性（outermost stability）确定零特征值为主特征值及其严格正特征函数；通过 Lyapunov-Schmidt 约化（Lyapunov-Schmidt reduction）得到平方根型分支分离与共享线性漂移，并用三个双黑洞数值模拟（含偏心、进动、不等质量系统）进行检验。

Result: 共同视界位于与形成切片相切的平滑边缘外陷落管（marginally outer trapped tube）上，后续切片与它相交于外、内两支，其间距按 (t−t_*)^{1/2} 标度；沿零模有一阶非零响应的视界量继承平方根分离与共享线性项。三个模拟中世界管几何与准局域标量均符合预测；计入次阶项后自由指数拟合恢复 1/2 到 0.5% 以内，形成时间诊断一致到 5×10^{−5}M。

Conclusion: 共同视界形成具有普适的平方根标度结构，并在数值模拟中得到验证；视界剪切（shear）与引力波 news 的相关性提示该形成过程可能在并合波形的一小段中留下特征信号。

Abstract: We derive the local structure of the first common apparent horizon in a generic binary-black-hole merger. This event occurs in the fully nonlinear regime, outside the standard regimes of post-Newtonian inspiral theory and perturbations of a stationary black hole, yet it admits a universal description. Without assuming symmetry, we show that the stability operator of the marginally outer trapped surface must lose invertibility at formation. Outermost stability then implies that the vanishing eigenvalue is the principal one, with a strictly positive eigenfunction. Lyapunov-Schmidt reduction yields square-root branch separation with a shared linear drift. Together these terms give a tilted parabola through linear order in time. The common horizon lies on a smooth marginally outer trapped tube tangent to the formation slice, and nearby later slices intersect it in outer and inner branches whose separation scales as $(t-t_*)^{1/2}$. Horizon quantities with a nonzero first response along the zero mode inherit the square-root separation and a shared linear term. We test these predictions in three binary black hole simulations, including an eccentric, precessing, unequal-mass system. In all three, the worldtube geometry and quasilocal scalars follow the predicted scaling. With the next-order term included, free-exponent fits to the surface geometry and quasilocal functionals recover $1/2$ to within half a percent, and diagnostics agree on the formation time within $5\times10^{-5}M$. The correlation between horizon shear and gravitational-wave news suggests that common horizon formation could have a signature in a short segment of the merger waveform.

</details>


### [109] [Octupolar bremsstrahlung waveform up to the two-loop level and the third-and-a-half post-Newtonian accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11835)
*Donato Bini,Thibault Damour,Andrea Geralico*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在多重极后闵可夫斯基（Multipolar Post-Minkowskian, MPM）形式下，计算了两质量散射中引力波形的偶宇称八极（octupolar）贡献 $U_3$，并给出其频率域表达式至 2-loop（$O(G^4)$）精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作仅覆盖波形偶宇称四极（quadrupolar）部分，偶宇称八极贡献 $U_3$ 尚未计算，需扩展以补全波形多极结构。

Method: 采用 MPM 形式，并使用 3.5 后牛顿（Post-Newtonian, PN）精度的辐射反作用准开普勒（quasi-Keplerian）双曲运动描述；在频率域计算 $\hat U_3(\omega,\theta,\varphi)$，达到 2-loop 即 $O(G^4)$ 的 $h_{ij}$ 贡献（对应 $\hat U_3$ 的 $O(G^3)$）。

Result: 得到 $\hat U_3(\omega,\theta,\varphi)$ 至 2-loop 水平的显式结果；其 1-loop 截断在考虑与先前四极比较所得相同的 2.5PN 级质心原点定义差异后，与已有有效场论（Effective Field Theory, EFT）结果一致。

Conclusion: 八极波形计算通过与 EFT 的一致性获得关键部分验证，并复现了两种形式间质心原点定义差异，支持 MPM 框架下该结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Extending our recent work (which focussed on the even-parity quadrupolar part of the waveform), we compute the even-parity octupolar contribution, $U_3$, to the gravitational waveform $W \equiv \frac{c^4 r}{4G} \bar m^{i} \bar m^{j } h_{i j}$ emitted during the scattering of two masses. We work within the Multipolar Post-Minkowskian (MPM) formalism, and use the 3.5 Post-Newtonian (PN) accurate radiation-reacted quasi-Keplerian representation of the hyperbolic motion. We explicitly evaluate the frequency-domain value $\hat U_3(ω, θ,φ)$ of $U_3$ up to the 2-loop level, i.e. $ O(G^4)$ contributions to $h_{ij}(ω, θ,φ)$, corresponding to $O(G^3)$ contributions to $\hat U_3(ω, θ,φ)$. As a crucial partial confirmation of our result, we find that the 1-loop truncation of our 3.5 PN frequency-domain MPM waveform agrees with corresponding existing Effective Field Theory (EFT) results when taking into account exactly the {\it same} (2.5PN-level) difference in the definitions of the center-of-mass origins within the two formalisms that was deduced from our previous quadrupolar comparison.

</details>


### [110] [Energy accreted onto a compact star](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11902)
*Kaiser Arf,Kai Schwenzer*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文基于 Hartle-Thorne 度规的慢转动展开，计算薄吸积盘内边缘物质落到致密星上所获得的能量，并给出与物态方程无关的自旋加速与加热能量表达式，指出观测到的毫秒吸积源结果可能与现有估计显著偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对吸积致密星能量增益的估计可能未一致地纳入转动、相对论与核效应，作者希望借助受控的慢转动展开检验各阶转动修正的相对重要性，并判断其影响是否可忽略。

Method: 采用基于 Hartle-Thorne 度规（Hartle-Thorne metric）的受控慢转动展开（slow-rotation expansion）计算度规修正；对磁化中子星（magnetized neutron star）考虑吸积盘延伸至共转半径（co-rotation radius）的情形；再依据一般守恒定律（conservation laws）推导能量表达式。

Result: 对一般中子星，仅描述参考系拖曳（frame dragging）的最低阶转动度规修正量级可观；但对磁化中子星，因吸积盘大致延伸至共转半径，连参考系拖曳效应也较小。由此得到与物态方程（equation of state）无关的自旋加速与加热能量表达式，在观测到的毫秒吸积源（accreting millisecond sources）中结果可显著偏离现行估计。

Conclusion: 转动修正的重要性取决于具体天体：一般中子星以最低阶参考系拖曳为主，磁化中子星则连该效应都较小；一致纳入转动、相对论与核效应后，毫秒吸积源的能量增益估计需重新评估。

Abstract: We compute the energy gained by accretion onto a compact star due to matter falling from the inner edge of a thin accretion disk. We employ a controlled slow-rotation expansion based on the Hartle-Thorne metric and find that in neutron stars in general only leading order rotational corrections to the metric, describing frame dragging, are sizable. However, for accretion onto a magnetized neutron star, where the disk roughly extends to the co-rotation radius, even frame dragging effects are minor. Based on general conservation laws we then derive simple, equation-of-state-independent expressions for the spin-up and heating energy, consistently including the relevant rotational, relativistic and nuclear effects, and find that in observed accreting millisecond sources the results can strongly deviate from presently employed estimates.

</details>


### [111] [Theoretical Aspects of Direct Waves in Kerr Black Holes: Pole-Splitting Method for Ringdown Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11908)
*Nao Nakamoto,Naritaka Oshita,Hiroki Takeda*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将极端质量比旋进（extreme-mass-ratio inspiral, EMRI）中的直接波（direct waves, DWs）理论化，给出其复频率 ω_G 的格林函数推导、势垒屏蔽效应及与动态激发准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）的关系，并提出极点分裂（pole-splitting）方法将波形分解为准正规模极点部分与非极点部分。数值模拟表明非极点部分的主模 ℓ=m=2 频率与衰减率随 ω_G（或其屏蔽对应量 ω_screen）一致演化，从而确立直接波作为探测黑洞能层（ergoregion）与红移效应的探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于刻画极端质量比并合中由源驱动的直接波（DW）的物理性质，特别是其频率如何受到参考系拖曳（frame dragging）与源红移的支配，以及如何借此探测黑洞能层（ergoregion）区域。同时，现有准正规模滤波（QNM filtering）方法会因频率依赖的群延迟（group delay）而扭曲波形，因此需要一种不改变波形的分解手段。

Method: 采用格林函数（Green's function）技术推导源驱动频率 ω_G，分析其被势垒屏蔽的行为，并讨论其与动态激发准正规模（QNMs）的关联；进而提出极点分裂（pole-splitting）方法，将整体波形划分为 QNM 极点部分与非 QNM 部分，仅将传递函数分解为极点与非极点两部分。以中等与快速自旋的 Kerr 黑洞（Kerr black hole）准圆旋进（quasi-circular plunge）为对象进行数值模拟。

Result: 数值模拟显示，主模 ℓ=m=2 中非极点部分的频率与衰减率随 ω_G 或其屏蔽对应量 ω_screen 一致演化；此外，DW 的晚期衰减被预测为以三阶视界模（third-order horizon mode）衰减。极点分裂方法与 QNM 滤波不同，不会通过频率依赖的时间平移（即群延迟）使波形频谱发生形变。

Conclusion: 直接波（DW）的频率实部由参考系拖曳（frame dragging）支配、虚部由源红移支配，因而可作为探针用于探测黑洞周围的能层（ergoregion）与红移效应；极点分裂方法提供了一种不扭曲波形的波形分离途径。

Abstract: We formulate the theoretical aspects of direct waves (DWs) in the case of extreme-mass merger. A DW is a source-driven waveform characterized by a complex frequency $ω_{\rm G}$, which reflects the orbital motion of the particle in the vicinity of the black hole, including a part of the orbit inside the ergoregion: its real part is governed by frame dragging and its imaginary part by the redshift of the source. Using the Green's function technique, we derive the source-driven frequency $ω_{\rm G}$, describe its screening by the potential barrier, and discuss its relation to dynamically excited quasinormal modes (QNMs). We also discuss the late-time decay of DW and predict that it decays with the third-order horizon mode. We then introduce a pole-splitting method, which divides the whole waveform into a QNM-pole sector and a non-QNM sector. Unlike QNM filtering, which multiplies the waveform spectrum by a filter function and thereby deforms it through a frequency-dependent time shift (i.e., group delay), our pole-splitting method merely divides the transfer function into pole and non-pole parts, separating the full waveform. Simulating a quasi-circular plunge into a Kerr black hole with medium and rapid spins, we find that the frequency and decay rate of the non-pole sector in the dominant mode, $\ell = m = 2$, evolve consistently with $ω_{\rm G}$-or with its screened counterpart $ω_{\rm screen}$-establishing the DW as a probe of the ergoregion and of the redshift effect around a black hole.

</details>

### [112] [Dark Matter-Baryon Separability Predicts the Dynamics of an Almost-Dark Galaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10661)
*Oem Trivedi,Abraham Loeb*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文将暗物质-重子可分离性条件推广至重子亏损的共轭分支，并以近暗星系 TTT J1237327+143535 为例推导动力学一致性关系，提出用球状星团等观测检验其所属分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有暗物质-重子可分离性框架主要用于解释暗物质缺失星系，本文意在检验该框架是否同样能容纳重子优先亏损的共轭情形，从而判断两类系统可否纳入统一描述。

Method: 以新发现的近暗星系 TTT J1237327+143535 作为工作实例，在未知前身比（progenitor ratio）μ_i 参数化下推导一族动力学一致性关系，并将可分离性框架与预期球状星团（globular cluster）族群相联系；同时利用室女座星系团（Virgo Cluster）的潮汐性质对重子比（baryon ratio）给出约束。

Result: 推导出包括视向速度弥散（σ_los）、动力学质量（M_dyn）、包围重子分数及动力学质光比在内的阈值关系；通过球状星团族群提供对推断晕质量（halo mass）的独立一致性检验，并获得重子比的若干上限/界限。

Conclusion: 未来对恒星运动学、气体含量与球状星团的测量可判定该星系是否位于正可分离性分支，并检验暗物质缺失系统与重子亏损系统能否由同一框架描述。

Abstract: We extend the Dark Matter-Baryon Separability Condition to show that the same framework developed for dark matter deficient galaxies naturally admits a conjugate branch describing preferential baryonic depletion. Using the recently discovered almost-dark galaxy TTT J1237327+143535 as a worked example, we derive a family of dynamical consistency relations parameterized by the unknown progenitor ratio $μ_i$, including thresholds for $σ_{\rm los}$, $M_{\rm dyn}$, the enclosed baryonic fraction and the dynamical mass to light ratio. We further connect the separability framework to the expected globular cluster population, providing an independent consistency test of the inferred halo mass. We also provide some bounds on the baryon ratio using the tidal properties of Virgo Cluster. Future measurements of stellar kinematics, gas content and globular clusters can therefore determine whether this galaxy occupies the positive separability branch and also test whether dark matter deficient and baryon depleted systems can be described within a common framework.

</details>


### [113] [Constraining spinning primordial black holes with interstellar dust heating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10985)
*Qianyong Li,Yupeng Yang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该研究通过在星际尘埃加热（dust-heating）约束中引入原初黑洞（primordial black holes, PBHs）自旋（spin）效应，并计入强子（hadrons）衰变产生的次级光子（secondary photons），给出了对 PBH 暗物质丰度分数 $f_{\rm PBH}$ 的新上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBHs 是受理论支持较多的暗物质候选者，其丰度受多类观测限制；质量区间 $10^{15}\,\text{g}{-}10^{17}\,\text{g}$ 的 PBHs 正通过霍金辐射（Hawking radiation）蒸发并可加热星际尘埃。已有工作仅讨论非自旋 PBHs，本文旨在将自旋效应与更完整的次级粒子贡献纳入该约束框架。

Method: 将 PBH 自旋（spin parameter $a_{*}$）纳入尘埃加热分析，同时考虑来自规范玻色子（gauge bosons）衰变之外的、由霍金辐射发射的初级夸克与胶子（quarks and gluons）碎裂（fragmentation）产生的强子（hadrons）衰变的次级光子。将自旋 PBH 引起的尘埃加热率与尘埃最大冷却率比较，并同时考虑硅酸盐（silicate）与石墨（graphite）尘埃颗粒。

Result: 约束同时依赖 PBH 质量与自旋：质量更小、自旋更高的 PBHs 给出更强的限制。在所考察情形中，最强约束为 $M_{\rm PBH} = 10^{15}{\rm g}$、自旋参数 $a_{*} = 0.9999$ 时 $f_{\rm PBH} \sim 1.5 \times 10^{-4}$。

Conclusion: 这些上限虽弱于同质量区间已有的约束，但提供了一种独立且互补的 PBH 丰度限制途径。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) are a well-motivated dark matter candidate, and their cosmic abundance is constrained by a variety of observational probes. PBHs in the mass range $10^{15}\,\text{g}\,{-}\,10^{17}\,\text{g}$ are evaporating today via Hawking radiation, a process that can heat interstellar dust and modify its thermal emission. Recent studies have used this effect to place constraints on the abundance of non-spinning PBHs. We extend this approach by investigating the influence of PBH spin on dust-heating constraints. Furthermore, we account for secondary photons that originate not only from the decay of gauge bosons but also from the decay of hadrons produced via the fragmentation of primary quarks and gluons emitted through Hawking radiation. By comparing the dust heating rate induced by spinning PBHs with the maximum cooling rate of dust, considering both silicate and graphite grains, we derive new upper limits on the fraction of dark matter in the form of PBHs, $f_{\rm PBH}$. Our results show that the constraints depend on both PBH mass and spin. Smaller PBHs with higher spin yield stronger limits. For example, in the cases we investigated, the strongest constraint is $f_{\rm PBH} \sim 1.5 \times 10^{-4}$ for $M_{\rm PBH} = 10^{15}{\rm g}$ and spin parameter $a_{*} = 0.9999$. Although these limits are less stringent than existing constraints in the same mass range, they provide a distinct and complementary approach to constraining the abundance of PBHs.

</details>

### [114] [Antimony for broadband nanophotonics across the ultraviolet, visible and infrared](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11501)
*Fernando Chacón-Sánchez,Jacek Wojcik,Marina García Pardo,Fátima Cabello,Peter Mascher,Fernando Agulló-Rueda,Rosalía Serna,Johann Toudert*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 首次对锑（Sb）纳米尺度薄膜在紫外至远红外（4–0.04 eV，约0.3–30 μm）宽谱范围内给出完全一致且精确测量的介电函数（dielectric function），揭示其光谱响应由可见至中红外的巨带间跃迁（interband transitions）主导，并据此展示可调谐等离激元与介电共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半金属锑纳米结构在相变光存储、非线性光学、光热治疗、光电探测与光催化等纳米光子学应用中潜力巨大，但设计先进锑基光子器件需要准确可靠地掌握块体与纳米尺度锑的光学响应，而既有报道的光谱范围有限、覆盖不足。

Method: 对锑纳米尺度薄膜进行光谱测量，覆盖紫外到远红外（4–0.04 eV，约0.3–30 μm）的宽光谱范围，获得完全一致且精确的介电函数；并基于该介电数据设计并分析两类纳米结构：锑纳米光栅（nanograting）与锑/介质/金属共振腔（Sb/dielectric/metal resonant cavity）。

Result: 锑的光谱响应在可见至中红外（4–0.4 eV，约0.3–3 μm）由巨带间跃迁专门驱动，且其贡献在低至0.12 eV（约10 μm）时仍超过自由载流子（free carriers）。该响应使锑纳米结构呈现光谱选择且可调谐的纳米光子共振：在可见至近红外实现带间等离激元共振（interband plasmonic resonances），在中红外实现纳米结构化的巨大折射率介电共振（dielectric resonances）。

Conclusion: 该工作为优化平面锑纳米尺度设计、实现定制化光-物质相互作用提供了路径，可服务于集成数据、电信、医疗、光电与能量转换等宽光谱范围内工作的器件。

Abstract: Semimetal elemental antimony (Sb) nanostructures show great potential for applications where nanophotonic properties play a key role, such as phase-change optical memories, non-linear optical elements, photothermal therapy agents, photodetectors and photocatalysts. However, designing advanced Sb-based photonic devices critically requires an accurate and reliable knowledge of the optical response of bulk and nanoscale Sb. Herein, we report for the first time a fully consistent and accurately measured dielectric function for Sb nanoscale films in a wide spectral range from the ultraviolet to the far infrared (4 - 0.04 eV, i.e. ~ 0.3 - 30 $μ$m), surpassing previous reports that explored a limited spectral range. It is found that the Sb spectral response is driven exclusively by giant interband transitions in the visible up to mid infrared (4 - 0.4 eV, i.e. ~ 0.3 - 3 $μ$m), and that their contribution dominates over that of free carriers down to 0.12 eV (i.e. ~ 10 $μ$m). Such spectral response enables Sb nanostructures to display spectrally selective and tunable nanophotonic resonances. First, we showcase interband plasmonic resonances in the visible-to-near infrared for Sb nanogratings. Second, we report giant refractive index dielectric resonances in the mid infrared for nanostructured Sb/dielectric/metal resonant cavities. These findings open a pathway to optimized planar Sb nanoscale designs enabling a tailored light-matter interaction, which will be useful for integrated data, telecom, medical, optoelectronic and energy conversion devices operating in a broad spectral range.

</details>
