### [115] [Slow Dynamics and the Geometry of Jammed Packings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11036)
*Eddie Bautista,Eric Corwin*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究比较颗粒堆积（granular packings）与 $p$-spin 模型的离散最速下降（steepest descent）最小化路径，发现颗粒堆积路径与其光滑能量景观对应物类似，会被鞍点（saddle points）吸引；所有模型的指数-时间曲线均遵循平移拉伸指数（stretched exponential），但形状参数存在未解差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒堆积能量景观中鞍点主导离散最速下降动力学，并决定非力学平衡堆积的演化路径与最终稳定极小值；而解析能量景观模型（如 $p$-spin）中高指数鞍点会牵引系统经过指数递减的鞍点。作者希望检验颗粒堆积是否具有类似行为并进行对照。

Method: 对颗粒堆积的最速下降最小化路径进行考察，并与 $p$-spin 模型对比；同时将颗粒堆积能量景观分别修改为解析形式（谐振子阱中的高斯势，Gaussian potential in a harmonic well）与非局域形式（Mari-Krzakala-Kurchan），以检验形状参数的变化。

Result: 颗粒堆积最速下降路径与光滑能量景观对应物表现一致，受鞍点吸引；所有模型的指数-时间曲线遵循平移拉伸指数。颗粒堆积在解析或非局域能量景观下的形状参数保持不变，而 $p$-spin 的形状参数显著更大。差异原因并非维度、堆积分数、非解析性或哈密顿量的局域性。

Conclusion: 颗粒堆积与光滑景观模型的最速下降路径在鞍点吸引行为上具有共性，形状参数对能量景观解析性与局域性不敏感；但 $p$-spin 形状参数显著偏大的确切原因仍未解决，属于未解之谜。

Abstract: Saddle points in the energy landscape of granular packings dominate the discrete steepest descent dynamics and ultimately determine the path that an out of mechanical equilibrium packing will follow and the resulting stable minimum that it will find. The saddle points that ultimately determine the resulting minima tend to be low-index saddle points. For models with an analytic energy landscape, such as the $p$-spin model, the steepest descent minimization path is affected by higher-index saddle points, which pull the system towards saddle points of decreasing index before arriving at the minima. Here, we examine the steepest descent minimization path of granular packings and compare them to the $p$-spin model. We show that the granular packing steepest descent minimization paths act like their smooth energy landscape counterparts and get attracted by saddle points. The index versus time curves for all models follow a shifted, stretched exponential. We further show that the shape parameter for the granular packings is unchanged when the energy landscape is modified to become analytic (Gaussian potential in a harmonic well) or non-local (Mari-Krzakala-Kurchan). The $p$-spin, on the other hand, has a significantly larger shape parameter. The reason is not due to the dimensionality, packing fraction, nonanalyticity, or the locality of the Hamiltonian of the models. The exact reason for the discrepancy in the shape parameter is \st{still} an unsolved mystery.

</details>


### [116] [A Phase-Field Study of Desiccation Crack Pattern Maturation under Drying-Wetting Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11048)
*Hiroki Fukushima,Satoshi Yukawa*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一种引入裂纹愈合（crack healing）与疤痕效应（scar effect）的干裂相场模型（phase-field model），通过有限元模拟（finite element simulation）重复干湿循环（drying--wetness cycles），再现了交叉角向约120°松弛的实验现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干裂特征交叉角在反复干湿循环下由近90°向约120°松弛，但现有理论理解不足，尤其是对干湿循环过程的建模尚不充分。

Method: 在既有干裂相场模型基础上扩展，加入裂纹愈合机制与历史裂纹遗留的疤痕效应，并在有限元模拟中重复施加干湿循环以统计交叉角分布。

Result: 随循环次数增加，角度分布在约120°附近形成逐渐增强的峰，与实验一致；交叉角偏离120°的标准差呈指数松弛，特征时间约2.85个循环（略小于实验值）。裂纹能量主导总能量，并以近似相同的特征循环数指数衰减。

Conclusion: 该衰减主要由有效裂纹长度（effective crack length）缩短驱动，而非有效断裂韧性（effective fracture toughness）变化；模型结果与实验总体吻合。

Abstract: The characteristic intersection angle of the desiccation crack relaxes from near \ang{90} toward \ang{120} under repeated drying--wetting cycles. However, the theoretical understanding of this relaxation is insufficient, especially the modeling of the drying--wetting cycles. Here we introduce a phase-field model of desiccation fracture, extending the model proposed in previous studies by adding crack healing and a scar effect left by past cracks. By repeating drying--wetting cycles in a finite element simulation, we find that the angle distribution develops a growing peak near \ang{120} as the cycle number increases, consistent with experiments. The standard deviation of the intersection angle from \ang{120} relaxes exponentially with a characteristic time of about 2.85 cycles. These results are consistent with experiments, except that the characteristic time is slightly smaller than the experimental value. Crack energy dominates the total energy and also relaxes exponentially with nearly the same characteristic cycle as the angle relaxation. This decay is driven mainly by a shortening of the effective crack length rather than a change in effective fracture toughness.

</details>


### [117] [Kinetics of ferritin crystal formation and melting in acoustically levitated droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11226)
*Anita Girelli,Iason Andronis,Aigerim Karina,Sampad Bag,Lennart Bergström,Tomás Plivelic,Felix Roosen-Runge,Fivos Perakis*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究结合声悬浮（acoustic levitation）与SAXS/WAXS，实时监测蒸发过程中铁蛋白（ferritin）在PEG水溶液中的结晶过程，揭示其晶序形成后随脱水而丧失，并证明PEG分子量可切换结晶路径并影响晶体稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解蛋白质结晶路径对结构生物学、材料科学与制药领域的结晶控制至关重要，而经典成核理论（classical nucleation theory）无法充分描述包括铁蛋白在内的多种蛋白质的结晶过程，因此需要新的实验手段加以探究。

Method: 将声悬浮与同步小角及广角X射线散射（SAXS and WAXS）相结合，在蒸发的聚乙二醇（PEG）水溶液中监测铁蛋白结晶；声悬浮快速驱动液滴跨越宽范围的蛋白质与聚合物浓度，实现蒸发过程的时间分辨测量。

Result: 散射数据表明铁蛋白晶体在蒸发过程中形成，并在进一步脱水后丧失晶序；改变PEG分子量可切换两种不同的结晶路径——一种由蛋白质-蛋白质吸引相互作用主导，另一种由排斥相互作用与排除体积效应（excluded-volume effects）主导；低分子量PEG（1000 g/mol）相比高分子量PEG（6000 g/mol）可抑制脱水诱导的晶序丧失。

Conclusion: PEG分子量是调控铁蛋白结晶路径与晶体稳定性的关键因素，低分子量PEG（1000 g/mol）提供了一种改善蛋白质晶体稳定性的简便策略。

Abstract: Understanding protein crystallization pathways is essential for controlling crystallization in structural biology, materials science, and pharmaceutical applications. Classical nucleation theory does not fully capture crystallization processes for several proteins, including ferritin. Here, we combine acoustic levitation with small- and wide-angle X-ray scattering (SAXS and WAXS) to monitor ferritin crystallization in evaporating aqueous polyethylene glycol (PEG) solutions. Acoustic levitation rapidly drives the droplets through a broad range of protein and polymer concentrations, enabling time-resolved measurements of crystallization during evaporation. The scattering data show that ferritin crystals form during evaporation and subsequently lose their crystalline order upon further dehydration. Varying the PEG molecular weight switches between distinct crystallization pathways: one dominated by attractive protein-protein interactions and another dominated by repulsive interactions and excluded-volume effects. Furthermore, we find that lower molecular weight PEG (1000 g/mol) suppresses the dehydration-induced loss of crystalline order observed for higher molecular weight PEG (6000 g/mol), providing a simple strategy for improving protein crystal stability.

</details>


### [118] [Impact waves in soft bilayer tissues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11705)
*Zhenwei Liu,Yang Liu,Michel Destrade,Yue-Sheng Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究在非线性弹性（nonlinear elasticity）框架下分析预应力可压缩超弹性双层结构（hyperelastic bilayer）中的冲击波传播，结合傅里叶-拉普拉斯变换（Fourier-Laplace transform）、驻相法（method of stationary phase）与有限元（finite element）验证，揭示瞬态位移场特征与导波模态群速度（group velocities）的关联，并以关节软骨、牙龈和皮肤三类生物双层模型说明高阶模态的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层软材料中瞬态冲击波场难以用单一特征表面波速度描述，需要建立瞬态波形与导波模态特性之间的联系。研究旨在为多层软材料的瞬态波场解读及未来无创力学表征（non-invasive mechanical characterization）提供理论基础。

Method: 针对置于无摩擦刚性基底（frictionless rigid substrate）上的预应力可压缩超弹性双层，采用傅里叶-拉普拉斯变换推导局部表面冲击所致瞬态位移场的半解析解（semi-analytical solutions），并用驻相法获得长时间渐近行为（long-time asymptotic behavior）；同时开展有限元模拟（finite element simulations）验证理论预测，并考察关节软骨、牙龈与皮肤三类双层模型中层刚度与厚度变化的影响。

Result: 瞬态位移场特征与导波模态群速度之间存在直接关联，色散传播（dispersive propagation）与各模态贡献共同塑造观测波形。层刚度与厚度的结构、材料变化会显著改变冲击波的空间分布、到达位置与特征速度；在特定参数区间，高阶模态（higher-order modes）对位移响应贡献显著，波场主导特征无法仅由基阶模态（fundamental mode）刻画。

Conclusion: 瞬态波测量包含超出单一特征表面波速度所能提供的力学信息。研究结果为多层软材料中瞬态波场的解读及未来无创力学表征提供了理论依据，并表明需考虑高阶模态与色散效应。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate impact-wave propagation in a pre-stressed compressible hyperelastic bilayer resting on a frictionless rigid substrate within the framework of nonlinear elasticity. Semi-analytical solutions for the transient displacement fields induced by localized surface impulses are derived using the Fourier-Laplace transform, and their long-time asymptotic behavior is obtained through the method of stationary phase. Finite element simulations are further performed to validate the theoretical predictions. The results establish a direct connection between characteristic features of the transient displacement fields and the group velocities of the guided-wave modes, revealing how dispersive propagation and modal contributions shape the observed waveforms. Three representative bilayer models corresponding to human articular cartilage, gingiva, and skin are then considered to examine the effects of variations in layer stiffness and thickness. The results show that structural and material variations can substantially modify the spatial distribution, arrival positions, and characteristic velocities of impact waves. In particular, higher-order modes can make significant contributions to the displacement response in certain parameter regimes, such that the dominant features of the wavefield cannot always be characterized by the fundamental mode alone. These findings demonstrate that transient-wave measurements contain mechanical information beyond that captured by a single characteristic surface-wave velocity and provide a theoretical basis for interpreting transient wavefields in multilayered soft materials and for future non-invasive mechanical characterization.

</details>

### [119] [The Chen-Yang volume conjecture for long integral fillings of fundamental shadow links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11839)
*Ce Shen*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Chen–Yang 体积猜想（Chen–Yang volume conjecture）对任意固定带标记基本影子链环外部（marked fundamental shadow-link exterior）的所有足够长整系数 Dehn 填充（integral Dehn filling）成立。其 $SO(3)$ Turaev–Viro 不变量（Turaev–Viro invariants）的指数增长沿全部奇数层级序列恢复双曲体积（hyperbolic volume）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chen–Yang 体积猜想将 $SO(3)$ 量子不变量的指数增长率与双曲体积相联系，但一般情形下整系数 Dehn 填充的带符号手术和（signed surgery sum）存在强烈抵消，难以直接控制渐近行为。本文旨在将猜想推广至基本影子链环外部的一整类填充，并处理混合符号填充系数带来的技术困难。

Method: 核心分析工具是将连续模型（continuous model）中的精确反射对称性（reflection symmetry）转移到有限量子和上，从而控制误差低于存活贡献的指数尺度。方法上建立了带符号 $SO(3)$ Witten–Reshetikhin–Turaev 不变量（Witten–Reshetikhin–Turaev invariant）的完整渐近展开，并将绝对首项系数用伴随 Reidemeister 挠率（adjoint Reidemeister torsion）显式表出。此外，将该方法应用于限制在中心颜色（central colors）的单边状态和（one-edge state sum）。

Result: 对所有足够长的整系数 Dehn 填充，填充系数的符号可混合、量级可互不相关，$SO(3)$ Turaev–Viro 不变量的指数增长沿完整奇数层级序列恢复其双曲体积。渐近展开的首项系数由伴随 Reidemeister 挠率给出；填充核心上固定的偶数颜色（even colors）可恢复几何 holonomy 的特征标（characters of the geometric holonomy）。在单边中心颜色状态和中，主导贡献相消，对每个足够大的固定块数确定较小的存活指数率及其非零首项系数。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Chen–Yang 体积猜想推广到基本影子链环外部的整系数 Dehn 填充族，并给出带符号 $SO(3)$ WRT 不变量的完整渐近展开与显式首项系数。所发展的反射对称性转移技术可处理手术和中的抵消问题，并进一步适用于中心颜色状态和的分析。

Abstract: We prove the Chen--Yang volume conjecture for all sufficiently long integral Dehn fillings of any fixed marked fundamental shadow-link exterior. For each fixed filling, the exponential growth of its $SO(3)$ Turaev--Viro invariants recovers its hyperbolic volume along the full sequence of odd levels. The filling coefficients may have mixed signs and unrelated magnitudes. We also establish a complete asymptotic expansion of the signed $SO(3)$ Witten--Reshetikhin--Turaev invariant and identify the absolute leading coefficient explicitly in terms of adjoint Reidemeister torsion. Fixed even colors on the filling cores recover characters of the geometric holonomy. The key difficulty is cancellation in the signed surgery sum. Our main analytic tool transfers an exact reflection symmetry from a continuous model to the finite quantum sums. We control the error below the exponential scale of the surviving contribution. We also apply the method to a one-edge state sum restricted to central colors. The dominant contributions cancel, and for each sufficiently large fixed number of blocks we determine the smaller surviving exponential rate and its nonzero leading coefficient.

</details>

### [120] [An exposition on some viscosity dependent issues in refined potential flow theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10649)
*Taofiq Omoniyi Amoloye,Leke Thaddeus Oladimeji,Mahmoud A. Hayajnh,Olalekan Adebayo Olayemi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究评估了精化势流理论（refined potential flow theory, RPT）在不可压缩流体绕脉冲启动圆柱流动中由黏性引起的适用局限，该理论通过准无旋黏性流函数求解连续性与Navier-Stokes方程并同时满足自由流与无滑移边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典势流理论（potential flow theory）无法刻画黏性效应，而RPT试图在保留势流框架的同时纳入黏性、流动脉动与三维变量，因此需要评估其黏性相关的适用边界。

Method: RPT采用准无旋黏性流函数（quasi-irrotational viscous stream function），对连续性方程与Navier-Stokes方程进行求解，并同时满足自由流（free-stream）与无滑移（no-slip）边界条件。

Result: 摘要未提供具体数值或对比结果，仅说明该框架被应用于脉冲启动圆柱（impulsively started circular cylinder）的不可压缩流动场景。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在明确RPT在黏性影响下的局限性；摘要未给出具体结论性判断。

Abstract: This study evaluates viscosity-dependent limitations in refined potential flow theory (RPT) applied to incompressible flow over an impulsively started circular cylinder. RPT adapts classical potential flow theory to account for viscous effects, flow fluctuations, and three-dimensional variables. By utilizing a quasi-irrotational viscous stream function, the framework solves the continuity and Navier-Stokes equations while satisfying both free-stream and no-slip boundary conditions.

</details>


### [121] [Giesekus Stick-Slip Singularity: Asymptotic Theory in the Log-Conformation Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10836)
*Florian Becker,Philipp Knechtges*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文分析性地研究了平面粘滑奇异性（stick-slip singularity）附近 Giesekus 流体的对数构象（log-conformation）重构。这是该重构方法首次被用于超出其数值用途的场景，结果与已有渐近应力分析一致，并显著扩展了现有渐近理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数构象重构此前主要作为数值计算工具使用；本文旨在将其应用于平面粘滑奇异性附近 Giesekus 流体的解析研究，以检验并推广已有的渐近应力分析结果。

Method: 在给定牛顿速度场（Newtonian velocity field）假设下，对 Giesekus 流体的对数构象方程进行解析渐近分析，覆盖粘着区（stick）、滑移区（slip）与核心区（core）。

Result: 所得结果与 Evans（JNNFM 222 (2015) 24-33）的渐近应力分析相吻合；对数重构进一步表明构象张量（conformation tensor）的行列式渐近为常数，并刻画了边界层（boundary layers）与核心区之间的过渡行为。

Conclusion: 综合上述结果，得到对数构象方程的复合渐近解（composite asymptotic solution），该解在奇异性近邻区域内一致有效。

Abstract: In this paper, the log-conformation reformulation of a Giesekus fluid near a planar stick-slip singularity is investigated analytically. In what is, to our knowledge, the first use of the log-conformation formulation beyond its numerical purpose, we present results that match the asymptotic stress analysis of Evans [JNNFM 222 (2015) 24-33] for the stick, slip, and core regions under the assumption of a given Newtonian velocity field. Furthermore, the logarithmic reformulation allows us to extend the existing asymptotic theory substantially, showing, e.g., that the determinant of the conformation tensor is asymptotically constant, as well as thoroughly characterizing the transition behavior between the boundary layers and the core region. Combining these results, we obtain a composite asymptotic solution of the log-conformation equation that is valid uniformly throughout a close neighborhood of the singularity.

</details>

### [122] [Properties of the positive and negative parity charm-strange and bottom-strange mesons $D_s$, $D_s^*$, $D_{s0}^*$, $D_{s1}$, $B_s$, $B_s^*$, $B_{s0}^*$, $B_{s1}$ from lattice QCD: masses, decay constants, and compositeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11026)
*Forrest Guyton,Stefan Meinel*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）计算了最轻的重-奇异介子（heavy-strange mesons）性质，包括标量、赝标量、矢量与轴矢量的衰变常数（decay constants）、束缚能（binding energies）及 Weinberg 复合度（compositeness）参数，结果支持正宇称态以分子态（molecular）为主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为从第一性原理确定重-奇异介子的衰变常数与正宇称态性质，并检验其内部结构（分子态或紧致态），研究采用格点 QCD 方法开展系统计算。其中部分衰变常数为格点 QCD 首次给出。

Method: 轻夸克与奇异夸克采用畴壁费米子（domain-wall fermions），粲夸克与底夸克采用各向异性 Wilson（clover）作用量；使用 RBC/UKQCD 生成的七个系综，π 介子质量范围 431–139 MeV，格距 0.114–0.073 fm，以进行手征与连续极限联合外推。正宇称态通过 GEVP 从包含协变导数算符与介子-介子散射算符的关联矩阵中提取有限体积能量与衰变常数，并用 Lüscher 方法求无限体积束缚态质量。

Result: 负宇称介子衰变常数为 f_{D_s}=251.4(2.1)(0.4)(2.5) MeV、f_{D_s^*}=272.5(4.3)(1.0)(2.7) MeV、f_{B_s}=228.4(5.8)(0.5)(2.3) MeV、f_{B_s^*}=229.2(4.4)(0.8)(2.3) MeV，比值 f_{D_s^*}/f_{D_s}=1.086(14)(11)、f_{B_s^*}/f_{B_s}=1.003(22)(10)。正宇称态衰变常数为 f_{D^*_{s0}}=136.6(8.0)(4.0)(1.4) MeV、f_{D_{s1}}=200(33)(24)(2) MeV、f_{B^*_{s0}}=207(12)(8)(2) MeV、f_{B_{s1}}=196(16)(11)(2) MeV，其中 f_{B^*_{s0}} 与 f_{B_{s1}} 为格点 QCD 首次结果。束缚能结果为 m_{D^*_{s0}}-m_D-m_K=-48(14)(4) MeV、m_{D_{s1}}-m_{D^*}-m_K=-61(15)(2) MeV、m_{B^*_{s0}}-m_B-m_K=-69(13)(4) MeV、m_{B_{s1}}-m_{B^*}-m_K=-77(10)(5) MeV。

Conclusion: 分析结果与正宇称态以分子态（molecular）为主的图像一致，并首次提供了 f_{B^*_{s0}} 与 f_{B_{s1}} 的格点 QCD 数值。

Abstract: We present a lattice-QCD determination of properties of the lightest scalar, pseudoscalar, vector, and axial-vector heavy-strange mesons. This includes the decay constants of all mesons, and the binding energies and Weinberg compositeness parameters of the positive-parity states. The calculations are performed with domain-wall fermions for the light and strange quarks and anisotropic clover actions for the charm and bottom quarks. We use seven ensembles generated by RBC/UKQCD with pion masses ranging from 431 MeV to 139 MeV and lattice spacings ranging from 0.114 fm to 0.073 fm, which allows us to perform combined chiral and continuum extrapolations. For the negative-parity mesons, we obtain $f_{D_s}=251.4(2.1)(0.4)(2.5)\:{\rm MeV}$, $f_{D_s^*}=272.5(4.3)(1.0)(2.7)\:{\rm MeV}$, $f_{B_s}=228.4(5.8)(0.5)(2.3)\:{\rm MeV}$, $f_{B_s^*}=229.2(4.4)(0.8)(2.3)\:{\rm MeV}$, $f_{D_s^*}/f_{D_s}=1.086(14)(11)$, and $f_{B_s^*}/f_{B_s}=1.003(22)(10)$. In the positive-parity sector, the finite-volume energies and decay constants are extracted using the GEVP from correlation matrices with three different types of hadron interpolating operators, including operators with covariant derivatives and meson-meson-scattering operators at both source and sink. After extrapolation to the physical point, we obtain $f_{D^*_{s0}}=136.6 (8.0)(4.0)(1.4)$ MeV, $f_{D_{s1}}=200 (33)(24)(2)$ MeV, $f_{B^*_{s0}}=207 (12)(8)(2)$ MeV, and $f_{B_{s1}}= 196 (16)(11)(2)$ MeV. Our results for $f_{B^*_{s0}}$ and $f_{B_{s1}}$ are the first from lattice QCD. Lüscher's method is used to find the infinite-volume bound-state masses. At the physical point, we obtain $m_{D^*_{s0}}-m_D-m_K=-48 (14)(4)$ MeV, $m_{D_{s1}}-m_{D^*}-m_K=-61 (15)(2)$ MeV, $m_{B^*_{s0}}-m_B-m_K= -69 (13)(4)$ MeV, and $m_{B_{s1}}-m_{B^*}-m_K=-77 (10)(5)$ MeV. Our analysis shows consistency with the positive-parity states being predominantly molecular.

</details>

# physics.data-an [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Combinations of measurements that are simultaneous fits of parameters of interest and systematic uncertainties using the BLUE method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11461)
*Tomas Dado,Mark Owen,Michele Pinamonti*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 该文提出将最佳线性无偏估计（Best Linear Unbiased Estimator, BLUE）方法扩展应用于同时包含感兴趣参数（parameters of interest）与干扰参数（nuisance parameters）的组合分析，并指出已有的近似似然组合（approximate likelihood combination）会低估不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合来自不同测量的同一物理参数估计可提升参数确定的精度与稳健性；现代粒子物理测量多基于含干扰参数的似然拟合（likelihood fit），而高统计量分析中干扰参数可在似然拟合中被约束，因此有必要说明如何对干扰参数一并开展组合。

Method: 采用适用于组合的最佳线性无偏估计（BLUE）方法，将其同时应用于感兴趣参数的估计与干扰参数的估计；通过具体组合实例以及伪实验（pseudo-experiments）进行验证。

Result: 具体组合实例表明，纳入干扰参数可提升感兴趣参数的精度；伪实验结果显示组合所报告的不确定度是可靠的，而此前文献中提出并已在 Convino 软件中实现的近似似然组合所报告的不确定度被低估。

Conclusion: 该方法已实现于开源软件工具 Combiner 中，可支持包含干扰参数的组合分析并给出可靠的不确定度评估。

Abstract: Combining estimates of the same physics parameter obtained from different measurements improves the precision and robustness of the parameter determination. Modern particle physics measurements are often performed using likelihood fits that include the physics parameter(s) of interest together with nuisance parameters representing systematic uncertainties. In high-statistics analyses, typical of many analyses at the Large Hadron Collider, these nuisance parameters can be constrained in the likelihood fits. We describe how the Best Linear Unbiased Estimator method for combinations can be applied to both the estimates of the parameters of interest and the estimates of the nuisance parameters. We show with concrete example combinations that including the nuisance parameters can improve the precision on the parameters of interest. We show with pseudo-experiments that the uncertainty reported by the combination is reliable and that the approximate likelihood combination proposed in a previous publication and implemented in the Convino software reports an underestimated uncertainty. The method is implemented in an open-source software tool, Combiner.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Bargmann Invariants Are Insufficient for Complete Local-Unitary Orbit Discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11090)
*Lin Zhang,Bing Xie*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定由二分密度算符及其两个提升边缘（lifted marginals）构造的 Bargmann 不变量所编码的信息。结果表明，当子系统为量子比特时，普通边缘词族决定部分转置（partial transpose）的完整谱，并据此给出可分性判据；但该代数与完整局部酉不变量环（local unitary invariant ring）等价当且仅当两个子系统均为量子比特，缺失信息具有几何性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断量子态的可分性（separability）及局部酉等价（local unitary equivalence）是量子信息中的核心问题，而 Bargmann 不变量作为局部酉共轭的多项式不变量，其编码信息此前未被精确刻画。本文旨在厘清这些不变量究竟能决定哪些性质，以及其能力边界。

Method: 以二分密度算符及其两个提升边缘构造的 Bargmann 不变量为对象，分析其信息内容。在子系统为量子比特的情形下研究普通边缘词族与部分转置谱的关系；在 2⊗2 与 2⊗3 系统中考察完备可分性判据，并在双量子比特系统中寻找分离局部酉轨道及生成多项式不变量环的有限子族。同时构造量子比特-量子三能级（qubit-qutrit）及更高维情形中的反例。

Result: 当一子系统为量子比特时，普通边缘词族决定部分转置的完整谱，从而判定正部分转置（positive-partial-transpose）性质；在 2⊗2 与 2⊗3 系统中给出完备可分性判据。双量子比特系统中，有限子族可分离局部酉轨道，有限扩展可生成多项式不变量环。对量子比特-量子三能级态，存在满秩、局部极大混合（locally maximally mixed）态在所有普通边缘词不变量上一致但算子-Schmidt 秩（operator-Schmidt rank）不同，并由一个四次关联不变量区分。当两个局部维度均至少为三时，局部极大混合扇区出现同谱对：一个可分态与一个部分转置为负的纠缠态。

Conclusion: 普通 Bargmann 代数与完整局部酉不变量环一致，当且仅当两个子系统均为量子比特。所缺失的数据具有几何性质：它们编码全局本征空间相对于张量积分解（tensor-product decomposition）的位置。

Abstract: Bargmann invariants constructed from a bipartite density operator and its two lifted marginals are polynomial invariants of local-unitary conjugation. We determine the precise information encoded in these invariants. Whenever one subsystem is a qubit, the ordinary marginal-word family determines the full spectrum of the partial transpose and hence decides whether the state has the positive-partial-transpose property. In $2\otimes2$ and $2\otimes3$ systems, this yields complete separability criteria. For the two-qubit system, a finite subfamily additionally separates local-unitary orbits, and a finite extension generates the polynomial invariant ring. These three tasks already diverge for qubit-qutrit states: we exhibit full-rank, locally maximally mixed states that agree on all ordinary marginal-word invariants yet have different operator-Schmidt ranks, together with a quartic correlation invariant that separates them. When both local dimensions are at least three, the analogous collapse on the locally maximally mixed sector produces isospectral pairs consisting of one separable state and one entangled state with negative partial transpose. The ordinary Bargmann algebra therefore coincides with the full local unitary invariant ring if and only if both subsystems are qubits. The missing data are geometric: they encode the placement of global eigenspaces relative to the tensor-product decomposition

</details>


### [125] [From Analytic Structure to Quantum Complexity: Walsh-Pauli Representations of Continuum Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11350)
*Raphael De Sousa,Partha Nandi,Francesco Petruccione*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究连续算子结构如何出现在有限量子比特寄存器（qubit register）中，针对二进制基下对角算子的解析函数，构建了Walsh-Pauli谱（Walsh-Pauli spectra）的算子级生成函数框架（generating-function framework）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是理解连续算子结构在有限量子比特寄存器中的呈现方式。作者聚焦于Jordan-Lee-Preskill（JLP）动量算子，试图为这类算子的Walsh-Pauli谱建立系统性的解析刻画，以阐明其与Pauli局域性（Pauli locality）的关系。

Method: 发展了一套算子级生成函数框架，用于计算二进制基下对角算子解析函数的Walsh-Pauli谱，并以JLP动量算子为主要对象。此外将广义不确定性原理（GUP）运动学作为可调的非线性奇形变动量情形进行分析。

Result: 该构造解析地确定了谱，并揭示出精确的支集（support）与宇称选择定则（parity selection rules），以及由二进制编码诱发的非平凡壳内层级（intra-shell hierarchy）。由此给出了Walsh可压缩性（Walsh compressibility）及其与Pauli局域性关系的系统刻画；在GUP情形下，高阶动量项将谱权重重新分配到逐步更高的Pauli权重扇区（Pauli-weight sectors）。

Conclusion: 该框架为有限量子比特寄存器中连续算子结构（以JLP动量算子为例）的Walsh-Pauli谱提供了系统性的解析表征，并借助GUP运动学展示了谱权重随非线性形变向高Pauli权重扇区迁移的行为。

Abstract: How does continuum operator structure appear in a finite qubit register? We address this question for analytic functions of diagonal operators represented in a binary basis, focusing on the Jordan--Lee--Preskill (JLP) momentum operator, by developing an operator-level generating-function framework for their Walsh-Pauli spectra. The construction determines the spectrum analytically and reveals exact support and parity selection rules, together with a nontrivial intra-shell hierarchy induced by the binary encoding. This provides a systematic characterization of Walsh compressibility and its relation to Pauli locality. As a controlled application, we consider generalized uncertainty-principle (GUP) kinematics as a tunable nonlinear odd deformation of momentum, showing how higher-order momentum terms redistribute spectral weight into progressively higher Pauli-weight sectors

</details>


### [126] [The Quantum Composition Paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11402)
*Jacob Biamonte*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子理论中，由Born规则从各个酉步骤（unitary step）得到的概率律一般无法拼接成一致谱系（genealogy）。该工作刻画了例外情形，并将此组合问题与可逆确定性状态机（reversible deterministic state machines）及量子音乐理论联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论通常允许从各酉步骤经Born规则（Born rule）导出概率，但这些概率律能否"缝合成"一致的谱系并不显然。作者旨在分类可忠实组合（faithful composition）的例外条件，并揭示从初始边界可组合、但从内部重启（internal restart）后失败的现象。

Method: 针对有限维、组合封闭的酉族（composition-closed unitary families），证明普适组合（universal composition）当且仅当对酉单项式（unitary monomials）成立；分类所有两量子比特（qubit）酉步骤对，给出三量子比特（qutrit）酉步骤对的充要判据，并定义Born–Chapman–Kolmogorov流（current）以量化端点律与逐步检查律的一致性。

Result: 证明边界稳定酉序列（boundary-stable unitary sequence）在d维Hilbert空间上至多含d个完全混合步骤（fully mixing steps），且在每个素数维度取等号；构造任意长的真实混合序列，其从初始边界可组合、但在内部重启后失败；并给出带主动干涉（active interference）的qutrit控制两量子比特实现。

Conclusion: 该方法建立了状态机与量子的联系，为量子音乐理论（quantum theory of music）奠定基础：酉操作为音符、时间边界为提示。音乐转移预测（musical-transition prediction）被证明为PromiseBQP完全（PromiseBQP-complete），保留谱系的逐步模式对应节奏规则，而内部提示导致谱系失败即"量子音乐悖论"（quantum music paradox）。

Abstract: Quantum theory does not generally permit the probability laws obtained from individual unitary steps by the Born rule to be sewn into a consistent genealogy; we classify the exceptions and show that faithful composition can hold from an initial boundary yet fail after an internal restart. For finite-dimensional, composition-closed unitary families, universal composition holds exactly for unitary monomials, the phase-dressed permutations whose Born kernels realize reversible deterministic state machines. Prescribed sequences evade this obstruction. We classify all pairs of qubit unitary steps, give a necessary-and-sufficient criterion for pairs of qutrit unitary steps, and prove that a boundary-stable unitary sequence on a $d$-dimensional Hilbert space contains at most $d$ fully mixing steps, with equality in every prime dimension. We also construct arbitrarily long genuinely mixing sequences that compose from their initial boundary but fail after an internal restart, and a qutrit-controlled two-qubit realization with active interference. We define a Born--Chapman--Kolmogorov current that vanishes exactly when coherent and stepwise-checked endpoint laws agree, together with an associated measure of how many bits the endpoint reveals about the intermediate checking schedule. This state-machine connection provides a foundation for a quantum theory of music, in which unitary operations are notes and temporal boundaries are cues. Musical-transition prediction is proved PromiseBQP-complete, and the stepwise patterns that preserve a genealogy specify rules for rhythm, whereas the exceptional failure of that genealogy from an internal cue is the quantum music paradox.

</details>


### [127] [A Geometric Theory of Quantum Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11720)
*Lorenzo Capra,Lucio de Simone,Roberto Franzosi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明纠缠距离（Entanglement Distance, ED）与 Meyer-Wallach、Scott 线性熵度量在任意有限维纯态下数学等价，并给出其作为局域可观测量子代数上 Fubini-Study 度规张量迹的几何表述，进而建立其与量子费舍尔信息（Quantum Fisher Information, QFI）的正比关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠距离源自投影希尔伯特空间上的 Fubini-Study 度规，而 Meyer-Wallach 与 Scott 度量基于线性熵，二者表面上属于不同框架。本文旨在揭示这两个框架之间的数学联系，并为其寻找可操作的解释。

Method: 通过解析证明，将纠缠距离表述为 Fubini-Study 度规张量在局域可观测量子代数上的迹，从而证明该几何度量与线性熵型多体纠缠度量在任意有限维纯态下等价。

Result: 该几何统一给出直接的操作性解释：纯态的全局纠缠与可用于局域酉估计的总量子费舍尔信息（QFI）精确成正比。这一结果连接了抽象信息几何与量子计量学，并表明纠缠距离可动态刻画分布式量子传感（distributed quantum sensing）的资源性，在标准基于方差的判据失效的情形下识别出海森堡极限（Heisenberg-limited）灵敏度。

Conclusion: 纠缠距离与基于线性熵的多体纠缠度量在数学上等同，且可作为局域酉估计中量子费舍尔信息的几何对应量，为分布式量子传感提供纠缠资源的操作性刻画。

Abstract: Entanglement Distance (ED) was originally proposed as a geometric measure of entanglement derived from the Fubini-Study metric on the projective Hilbert space. Independently, the Meyer-Wallach and Scott measures quantify multipartite entanglement via linear entropy. In this work, we demonstrate that these two seemingly distinct frameworks are mathematically identical for pure states of arbitrary finite dimensions. We prove that ED arises naturally as the trace of the Fubini-Study metric tensor over the local subalgebra of observables. Crucially, this geometric unification yields a direct operational interpretation: the global entanglement of a pure state is exactly proportional to the total Quantum Fisher Information (QFI) available for local unitary estimation. This bridges abstract information geometry with quantum metrology, demonstrating that ED dynamically quantifies resourcefulness for distributed quantum sensing, identifying Heisenberg-limited sensitivity in regimes where standard variance-based witnesses fail.

</details>


### [128] [The generalised semi-Clifford conjecture is false](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11903)
*Nadish de Silva,Oscar Lautsch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一个处于 Clifford 层级（Clifford hierarchy）第五层却不属于广义半-Clifford（generalised semi-Clifford）形式的五量子比特门，从而否定了 Zeng-Chen-Chuang 猜想，并表明该层级对取逆运算不封闭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford 层级是可在标准量子纠错方案（quantum error correction）下通过门隐形传态（gate teleportation）容错执行的嵌套门集合，其结构备受关注。Zeng-Chen-Chuang 于 2007 年猜想所有层级门均为广义半-Clifford，Beigi-Shor 于 2008 年证明其对第三层门成立，由此引出对该猜想在更高层是否成立的研究需求。

Method: 构造一个属于 Clifford 层级第五层（fifth level）但非广义半-Clifford 的五量子比特（five-qubit）门；论文不仅给出并验证该反例（counterexample），还展示了其形式的推导过程。

Result: 所构造的五量子比特门位于 Clifford 层级第五层，却不满足 C₁ΠDC₂ 形式（其中 C₁、C₂ 为 Clifford 门，Π 为置换门，D 为对角门），构成对广义半-Clifford 猜想的反例；该反例同时表明 Clifford 层级对取逆（inverses）并不封闭。

Conclusion: 广义半-Clifford 猜想在第五层被否定，Clifford 层级的结构比该猜想所设想的更为复杂，且其不具备对逆运算的封闭性。

Abstract: The Clifford hierarchy is a nested sequence of sets of quantum gates that can be fault-tolerantly performed using gate teleportation within standard quantum error correction schemes. The importance of these gates has motivated numerous studies of their structure. Zeng-Chen-Chuang conjectured in 2007 that all hierarchy gates are generalised semi-Clifford, i.e. take the form $C_1 ΠD C_2$ for Clifford gates $C_1, C_2$, a permutation gate $Π$, and a diagonal gate $D$; Beigi-Shor proved in 2008 that this holds for all third-level gates.
  We construct a five-qubit gate that is in the fifth level of the Clifford hierarchy but is not generalised semi-Clifford. Rather than simply present and verify our counterexample to the generalised semi-Clifford conjecture, we show how its form can be deduced. Our counterexample also demonstrates that the Clifford hierarchy is not closed under inverses.

</details>


### [129] [Quantifying Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11926)
*Koji Yamaguchi,Hiroyasu Tajima*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在紧致李群（compact Lie group）对称性下、i.i.d. 渐近极限中完整求解了任意混合态（mixed states）之间的最优转换率问题，给出由单参数量子 Fisher 信息（quantum Fisher information, QFI）矩阵族决定的单字母公式（single-letter formula），并由此刻画渐近可逆转换、量子时钟的束缚不对称性（bound asymmetry）及激活机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子资源理论（quantum resource theories）以操控极限刻画量子态性质是核心目标。对称破缺（symmetry breaking）情形下，量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor）已支配渐近纯态转换，但一般混合态的完整刻画仍悬而未决。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在有限维系统、紧致李群对称性与 i.i.d. 渐近区域下求解不对称性资源理论（resource theory of asymmetry）中的最优转换率，允许迹距离误差（trace-distance error）消失。证明依赖两项独立结果：将量子局部渐近正态性（quantum local asymptotic normality）推广至任意秩与谱简并（spectral degeneracy）的酉模型（unitary models）；以及以同一单参数 QFI 族刻画量子高斯位移模型（quantum Gaussian shift models）间的可转换性。

Result: 得到任意态间最优转换率的单字母公式，速率由介于对称 QFI 与右对数导数 QFI（right-logarithmic-derivative QFI）之间的单参数 QFI 矩阵族决定。一般情况下不存在与态无关的有限子集足以刻画，即使对 U(1) 对称性亦然，这揭示其与纯态转换的定性区别。公式进一步给出以广义量子几何张量表示的纯态蒸馏率（pure-state distillation rates）精确表达式、刻画渐近可逆转换，并识别量子时钟的束缚不对称性；QFI 族成员间的互补性（complementarity）还揭示量子时钟的激活机制（activation mechanism）。

Conclusion: 研究为 i.i.d. 渐近区域中一般量子态的对称破缺提供了操作性刻画（operational characterization），并揭示不同 QFI 约束如何导致不可逆性（irreversibility）与激活现象。

Abstract: Quantifying properties of quantum states through the limits of their manipulation is a central goal of quantum resource theories. For symmetry breaking, the quantum geometric tensor governs asymptotic pure-state conversion, but a complete characterization for general mixed states has remained elusive.
  Here we fully resolve this problem for finite-dimensional systems under compact Lie group symmetries in the i.i.d. asymptotic regime. Specifically, we establish a single-letter formula for the optimal conversion rate between arbitrary states, with vanishing trace-distance error, in the resource theory of asymmetry. The rate is determined by a one-parameter family of quantum Fisher information (QFI) matrices that interpolates between the symmetric- and right-logarithmic-derivative QFIs. No state-independent finite subset of this family suffices in general, even for $U(1)$ symmetry, revealing a qualitative distinction from pure-state conversion. Our formula further yields an exact formula for pure-state distillation rates in terms of the generalized quantum geometric tensor, characterizes asymptotically reversible interconversion, and identifies bound asymmetry for quantum clocks. Complementarity among different members of the QFI family also uncovers an activation mechanism for quantum clocks.
  Our proof relies on two developments of independent interest. First, we extend quantum local asymptotic normality to unitary models with arbitrary rank and spectral degeneracy. Second, we characterize convertibility between quantum Gaussian shift models in terms of the same one-parameter family of QFIs. Together, these results provide an operational characterization of symmetry breaking for general quantum states in the i.i.d. asymptotic regime and reveal how distinct QFI constraints give rise to irreversibility and activation.

</details>


### [130] [Krylov Edge Spectroscopy of Symmetry-Protected Topological Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10676)
*Heiko Georg Menzler,Rishabh Jha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出 Krylov 边缘谱学（Krylov edge spectroscopy），一种基于局域边界动力学、在算符空间探测并分类一维对称保护拓扑相的多体协议。方法无需严格对角化、基态波函数、纠缠谱或预设的缀饰边缘/弦算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有识别对称保护拓扑相的手段常依赖多体严格对角化（exact diagonalization）、显式基态波函数或纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum），或需猜测缀饰边缘算符与弦算符，代价高且缺乏直接性。该文旨在以局域边界演化作为拓扑的直接探针。

Method: 引入厄米边界算符（Hermitian boundary operator），生成半无限 Krylov 跳跃链，其边界权重满足精确的零频可归一化判据。通过开-周期、边界-体、以及保对称边界微扰三类检验识别受保护的边界记忆，并用有限深度泄漏残差（leakage residual）判定显式重构算符是否已接近零频。分类通过在固定电荷扇区内最小化对称分辨边界算符上的归一化对易子实现；对玻色 Z_N × Z_N 相，恢复的端点电荷给出上同调标签。

Result: 在有限程哈密顿量中，定 Krylov 深度下的局域性先于深度极限组织了热力学极限。协议在 cluster、clock、Haldane 与平凡自旋-1 链中得到验证。在可精确求解的 cluster 链中，拓扑能隙相与平凡相之间的转变表现为 Krylov 链上边缘模的局域-离域转变，该转变恰发生于体能隙闭合处，且其局域长度指数与 Ising 关联长度指数一致。

Conclusion: 通过算符 Krylov 动力学（operator Krylov dynamics），该方法将局域边界演化转化为多体拓扑的直接谱学，为拓扑相探测与分类提供了不依赖基态信息与预设边缘算符的新途径。

Abstract: We introduce $Krylov$ $edge$ $spectroscopy$, a many-body operator-space protocol for detecting and classifying one-dimensional symmetry-protected topological phases from local boundary dynamics. A Hermitian boundary operator generates a semi-infinite Krylov hopping chain whose boundary weight obeys an exact zero-frequency normalizability criterion. Open-periodic, boundary-bulk, and symmetry-preserving boundary-perturbation tests identify protected boundary memory, while a finite-depth leakage residual certifies when an explicitly reconstructed operator is already near zero frequency. Classification minimizes the normalized commutator over symmetry-resolved boundary operators in fixed charge sectors. For bosonic $\mathbb{Z}_N \times \mathbb{Z}_N$ phases, the recovered endpoint charge gives the cohomology label. The method requires neither many-body exact diagonalization, an explicit ground-state wavefunction or entanglement spectrum, nor a guessed dressed edge or string operator. For finite-range Hamiltonians, locality organizes the thermodynamic limit at fixed Krylov depth before the depth limit. We demonstrate the protocol in cluster, clock, Haldane, and trivial spin-1 chains. In the exactly solvable cluster chain, the transition between the gapped topological and trivial phases manifests as a localization-delocalization transition of the Krylov edge mode on the Krylov chain. This transition occurs precisely at the bulk gap closing, and its localization-length exponent coincides with the Ising correlation-length exponent. Through operator Krylov dynamics, our work turns local boundary evolution into a direct spectroscopy of many-body topology.

</details>


### [131] [Floquet Majorana XYZ Codes with Tunable Logical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10683)
*Xinyu Sun,Hong Yao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作基于 Majorana XYZ 子系统码（Majorana XYZ subsystem code）构造了一类 Floquet 码（Floquet code），在量子比特层面与微观 Majorana 模式层面均具局部实现，并揭示逻辑自由度的静态/部分动力学形态可由测量时序切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立同时具备局部量子比特表示与直接微观 Majorana 实现的 Floquet 码族，并探究测量周期（measurement schedule）与格点奇偶性（parity）如何影响逻辑自由度的静态性。

Method: 在 $L\times L$ 格点上由 Majorana XYZ 子系统码构造 Floquet 码，采用三步周期（three-step cycle）并分析其局部实现（量子比特与微观 Majorana 模式），并考虑具有一个开放方向（open direction）的边界条件。

Result: 三步周期下，奇数 $L$ 支持一个静态逻辑量子比特（static logical qubit），偶数 $L$ 支持两个；其中 $L=4n+2$ 时两个逻辑量子比特均具不依赖时间的泡利代表（time-independent Pauli representatives），而 $L=4n$ 时第二逻辑量子比特仅一个泡利保持固定，其共轭的所有代表须在测量周期中演化。该差异源于宇称相关的代数阻碍（parity-dependent algebraic obstruction），并在周期缩短为两步后消失，恢复完全静态的逻辑对。

Conclusion: 同一编码逻辑自由度可在静态与部分动力学形态间由测量时序切换；借助一个开放方向，仅需局部 Majorana 宇称测量（local Majorana parity measurements）即可实现相应协议。据作者所知，这是首个同时具有局部量子比特表示与直接微观 Majorana 实现的 Floquet 码族。

Abstract: We construct Floquet codes from the Majorana XYZ subsystem code with local realizations both in qubits and directly in microscopic Majorana modes with lattice size $L\times L$. For a three-step cycle, odd $L$ supports one static logical qubit, whereas even $L$ supports two. For even $L$ with $L=4n+2$, both logical qubits admit time-independent Pauli representatives. For $L=4n$, by contrast, one Pauli of the second logical qubit remains fixed throughout the cycle, while every representative of its conjugate must evolve through the measurement cycle. This distinction follows from a parity-dependent algebraic obstruction and disappears when the cycle is reduced to two steps, which restores a fully static logical pair. Thus, the same encoded logical degree of freedom can be switched between static and partially dynamical forms by the measurement schedule. With one open direction, suitable protocols can be implemented using only local Majorana parity measurements. To our knowledge, this is the first Floquet-code family with both a local qubit representation and a direct microscopic Majorana realization.

</details>


### [132] [Local decoders for fault-tolerant quantum computation and translation-invariant stabilizer codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11457)
*Nathaniel Selub,Aditya Bhardwaj,Ethan Lake*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了首个基于拓扑码（topological code）的全空间局域容错量子计算机（fault-tolerant quantum computer），其架构为二维，仅使用几何局域的量子与经典操作、有限速度的经典通信与计算，且资源密度恒定。核心是面向表面码（surface code）的新型时间平移不变元胞自动机解码器（cellular-automaton decoder）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于突破此前容错量子计算架构需四个及以上空间维度或非局域操作的局限，实现真正空间局域、可在二维平面内物理实现的容错方案。

Method: 方法上，构造了二维架构，仅采用几何局域（geometrically local）的量子与经典操作、有界速度（bounded-speed）的经典通信与计算，以及恒定密度的量子与经典资源；核心技术为一种时间平移不变（time-translation-invariant）的元胞自动机解码器，用于表面码。

Result: 结果表明，该解码器可在码距（code distance）的拉伸指数（stretched-exponential）时间内保持逻辑信息，并在态注入（state injection）、稳定子态制备（stabilizer-state preparation）、格点手术（lattice surgery）与横向读出（transversal readout）过程中持续运行。此外，论文证明每个平移不变拓扑 Pauli 稳定子码（translation-invariant topological Pauli stabilizer code）在唯象噪声（phenomenological noise）下均可局域解码。

Conclusion: 结论是首次实现了低于四维、基于拓扑码的完全空间局域容错量子计算架构，并将局域可解码性推广至所有平移不变拓扑 Pauli 稳定子码。

Abstract: We construct the first fully spatially local fault-tolerant quantum computer based on topological codes in fewer than four spatial dimensions. Our construction is a two-dimensional architecture that uses only geometrically local quantum and classical operations, bounded-speed classical communication and computation, and a constant density of quantum and classical resources. The core component is a new time-translation-invariant cellular-automaton decoder for the surface code. This decoder preserves logical information for a time stretched-exponential in the code distance and operates continuously during state injection, stabilizer-state preparation, lattice surgery, and transversal readout. We also prove that every translation-invariant topological Pauli stabilizer code is locally decodable under phenomenological noise.

</details>


### [133] [Nonlinear dynamics and mechanical frequency combs with a Meissner-levitated micromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11668)
*Yiqi Wang,Trisha Madhavan,Vanessa Wachter,J. DaLi Schaefer,Addison NewRingeisen,Zhiyuan Wei,Frankie Fung,Benjamin A. Stickler,Mikhail D. Lukin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用迈斯纳悬浮（Meissner-levitated）微磁体的质心与转动耦合运动，展示了低耗散、磁可调、强本征杜芬非线性（Duffing nonlinearity）与非线性模间耦合相结合的非线性多模力学平台，并实现可调间距的声子频率梳（phononic comb）及低频摆动模的参量激发与锁相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模机械系统中的非线性（nonlinearity）可产生丰富动力学现象，在传感与量子实验中有应用潜力。研究旨在寻找兼具低耗散、可调性与强非线性耦合的机械平台。

Method: 以迈斯纳悬浮微磁体为对象，利用其质心与转动运动耦合，通过参量激发（parametric excitation）两个平动模，并借助级联非线性频率混合（cascaded nonlinear frequency mixing）产生声子梳；在大振幅下研究其与低频摆动模（librational mode）的非线性耦合。

Result: 实现了机械频率梳（mechanical frequency comb），即间距可调的声子梳；在大振幅下，与低频摆动模的非线性耦合导致该模的参量激发与相位锁定（phase locking），在驱动频率的次谐波处形成密集的频谱精细结构。

Conclusion: 结果确立了悬浮微磁体作为非线性多模力学平台的地位，并指出其在精密传感（precision sensing）与量子极限计量（quantum-limited metrology）中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Nonlinearities in multimode mechanical systems can give rise to rich dynamical phenomena with great potential for sensing applications and for future quantum experiments. We demonstrate that the coupled center-of-mass and rotational motion of a Meissner-levitated micromagnet offer a promising platform for nonlinear dynamics, combining low dissipation, magnetic tunability, strong intrinsic Duffing nonlinearities, and nonlinear intermodal couplings. We use this tunability to demonstrate the generation of a mechanical frequency comb in the micromagnet dynamics, realized by parametric excitation of two translational modes followed by cascaded nonlinear frequency mixing, which produces a phononic comb with tunable spacing. At large amplitudes, nonlinear coupling to a low-frequency librational mode of the magnet leads to parametric excitation and phase locking of that mode, generating a dense spectral fine structure at subharmonics of the drive. These results establish levitated micromagnets as a platform for nonlinear multimode mechanics, with potential applications in precision sensing and quantum-limited metrology.

</details>


### [134] [Learning structural balance of graphs from quantum spectral features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11736)
*Stefano Scali,Oleksandr Kyriienko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于哈密顿量态密度（DOS）的量子谱特征提取方法，将符号图（signed graph）嵌入为Ising模型，用DOS的标准化矩作为学习特征，并给出DOS-QPE协议高效采样谱密度以预测阻挫指数（frustration index）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号图上的结构平衡（structural balance）等任务与NP-hard量（如阻挫指数）相关，需要有效且规模无关的特征表达；同时希望借助量子方法获取经典方法难以高效采样的谱特征。

Method: 将符号图嵌入为含正负相互作用的Ising模型实例，取Ising DOS的标准化矩作为特征（其计数符号闭合游走、具切换不变性且与规模无关）；提出DOS-QPE，即在纯化的最大混合探针（purified maximally mixed probe）上做相位估计（phase estimation）以采样谱密度，并将特征输入经典训练的模型。

Result: 在1.4×10^5个带标签图上，精确DOS可确定阻挫指数，五个矩恢复该指标的平均误差为0.4，远低于一次符号翻转；零场下模型可经典采样，用于验证量子提取流程与精确真值一致；DOS-QPE相较基于Hadamard检验的迹采样（trace sampling）所需采样次数少若干数量级（orders of magnitude）；非零场下其迹估计问题为DQC1-complete。

Conclusion: 该框架为社交网络平衡分析、自旋玻璃（spin-glass）研究、相关聚类（correlation clustering）及蛋白质相互作用网络等提供了量子应用路径，并可获取尚无高效经典采样方法的谱特征。

Abstract: We develop a quantum approach to spectral feature extraction from the density of states (DOS) of a problem-dependent Hamiltonian, and apply it to machine learning on signed graphs. We propose to embed a signed graph as an Ising model instance with positive and negative interactions, and use the standardized moments of the Ising DOS as features for learning. We show that these moments count signed closed walks, are switching-invariant, and are size-free by construction. As a benchmark, we target learning the frustration index, an NP-hard measure of structural balance that can be labeled exactly at moderate size. At zero field, the models can be sampled classically, allowing the quantum extraction procedure to be certified against exact ground truth. We propose DOS-QPE, a phase estimation on a purified maximally mixed probe, which samples the spectral density with orders of magnitude fewer shots than Hadamard test-based trace sampling and feeds the resulting features directly into classically trained models. On $1.4\times10^5$ labeled graphs the exact DOS determines the frustration index, and five moments recover it with a mean error of 0.4, well below one sign flip. Beyond zero field, the underlying trace-estimation problem is DQC1-complete, providing access to spectral features for which no efficient classical sampling method is known. Our work opens routes towards quantum applications in social network balance analysis, spin-glass studies, correlation clustering, and protein-interaction networks.

</details>


### [135] [Computational framework for quantum state tomography of spin ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11868)
*Jiwoo Seo,Vesna F. Mitrović*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Tomography-NMR 是一个开源 Python 软件包，基于乘积算符（product operator）形式体系，从谱学测量数据重建量子密度矩阵（density matrix），并在 NMR 实验制备的双量子比特态上实现 0.975–0.995 的重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层析（quantum state tomography）的理论框架已较成熟，但从实验谱中提取密度矩阵的具体操作流程在文献中常缺乏充分记录，且被封装于专有软件中，导致可复现性不足。该工作旨在填补这一透明性与可复现性缺口。

Method: 软件实现完整的双量子比特量子态层析分析流水线：将时域原始信号经傅里叶变换（Fourier transform）转为频域谱，把谱峰强度映射为密度矩阵的展开系数，进而重建完整量子态。提供三种积分方法：直接峰高测量、固定参数数值积分（两者无需理论参照，适用于未知态），以及针对已知目标态的系统参数优化。

Result: 在已知态基准集上，无需理论参照的两种方法保真度约为 98%，参数优化方法保真度超过 99%。软件在耦合 $^{31}$P 核的 NMR 谱实验制备的双量子比特态上得到验证，20 个基准态（含计算基态、Bell 态及 CNOT、H、T 三种基本量子门输出）的平均重建保真度为 0.975–0.995。

Conclusion: 该软件提供了从谱预处理到密度矩阵可视化各分析步骤的完全透明、可复现实现，弥补了实验流程文档不足的问题；尽管面向 NMR 开发，其模块化架构便于迁移至其他谱学平台与替代测量协议。

Abstract: We present Tomography-NMR, an open-source Python package that reconstructs quantum density matrices from spectroscopic measurement data. The package implements a complete analysis pipeline for two-qubit quantum state tomography based on the product operator formalism: raw time-domain signals are Fourier-transformed into frequency-domain spectra, spectral peak intensities are mapped to expansion coefficients of the density matrix, and the full quantum state is reconstructed. Three integration methods are provided for different use cases: direct peak height measurement and fixed-parameter numerical integration require no theoretical reference and are suited to unknown states, achieving fidelities of approximately 98\% on a benchmark set of known states, while a systematic parameter optimization against a known target state achieves reconstruction fidelities exceeding 99\% for the same benchmark states. While the detailed theoretical framework for quantum state tomography is well established, the practical procedures for extracting density matrices from experimental spectra are often inadequately documented in the literature and obscured within proprietary software. This package addresses that gap by providing a fully transparent, reproducible implementation of every analysis step, from spectral preprocessing to density matrix visualization. The software has been validated on experimentally prepared two-qubit states measured via nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectroscopy of coupled $^{31}$P nuclei. Average reconstruction fidelities range from 0.975 to 0.995 across a benchmark set of 20 two-qubit states, including the computational basis states, Bell states, and the outputs of three fundamental quantum gates (CNOT, H, and T). Although developed for NMR, the modular architecture facilitates adaptation to other spectroscopic platforms and alternative measurement protocols.

</details>


### [136] [Hierarchy of Rényi Coherent Information in Stabilizer Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11930)
*Akash Vijay,Luis Colmenarez,Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作针对稳定子码（stabilizer codes），证明了在独立伯努利事件生成的泡利噪声（Pauli noise）下，Rényi-$n$ 相干信息（Rényi-$n$ coherent information）随 $n \in \mathbb{Z}^+$ 单调不减，并通过“数据块与 $n-1$ 个辅助块综合征匹配”的后选择（postselection）方案，为其赋予操作意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rényi 相干信息是冯·诺依曼相干信息（von Neumann coherent information）的可计算代理，广泛用于研究混合态物相（mixed-state phases of matter）与含噪量子纠错码的可解码性转变。但作为两个 Rényi 熵之差，它未必随 Rényi 指标单调，亦缺乏对应 von Neumann 版本的操作意义，本文即针对稳定子码解决这两个问题。

Method: 第一，对由独立伯努利事件产生的泡利噪声，借助一条一般性定理——独立随机比特经线性映射到细标签 $T$ 与粗标签 $C$ 时，Rényi 熵差 $H_n(C)-H_n(T)$ 随 $n$ 单调不减——将稳定子码中 $T$ 取为综合征与逻辑类的联合、$C$ 取为综合征，从而证明 Rényi-$n$ 相干信息单调性。第二，对任意随机泡利噪声，构造在一个数据块与 $n-1$ 个辅助块之间进行综合征匹配后选择的方案，并判定其何时定义量子信道（quantum channel）。

Result: 所证单调性定理同时覆盖经典线性码（classical linear codes）与独立探测器错误模型（independent detector error models）。在后选择框架下，Rényi-$n$ 相干信息的饱和等价于后选择信道的渐近完美恢复；此外，Rényi-$n$ 相干信息还对任意综合征条件恢复（syndrome-conditioned recovery）后可达的普通相干信息给出上界。

Conclusion: 该研究为稳定子码的 Rényi 相干信息补足了单调性与操作意义两项缺口，既统一解释了其在经典线性码与探测器错误模型中的行为，也通过后选择信道的可恢复性刻画与上界关系，将其与 von Neumann 相干信息建立了更明确的联系。

Abstract: Rényi coherent information, a computable proxy for the von Neumann coherent information, is widely used to study mixed-state phases of matter and decodability transitions in noisy quantum error-correcting codes. However, being a difference of two Rényi entropies, it need not be monotonic in the Rényi index, and lacks the operational meaning of its von Neumann counterpart. Here we address both issues for stabilizer codes. First, for Pauli noise generated by independent Bernoulli events, we prove that the Rényi-$n$ coherent information is nondecreasing in $n \in \mathbb{Z}^+$. This follows from a general theorem: if independent random bits are mapped linearly to a fine label $T$ and a coarse label $C$, then the Rényi entropy difference $H_n(C)-H_n(T)$ is nondecreasing in $n$. For stabilizer codes, $T$ is the joint syndrome--logical class and $C$ is the syndrome, and the difference is the Rényi-$n$ coherent information up to a constant. The same theorem covers classical linear codes and independent detector error models. Second, for arbitrary stochastic Pauli noise, we give the Rényi-$n$ coherent information an operational meaning via postselection on matching syndromes between one data block and $n-1$ auxiliary blocks. We determine when this defines a quantum channel and show that saturation of the Rényi-$n$ coherent information is equivalent to asymptotically perfect recovery of the postselected channel. Moreover, the Rényi-$n$ coherent information also upper-bounds the ordinary coherent information achievable after any syndrome-conditioned recovery.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Transdimensional quantum droplets in an optically trapped Bose mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11047)
*Xiaoran Ye,Yi Zhang,Ziheng Zhou,Zhaoxin Liang*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作研究具有种间 $p$ 波相互作用（interspecies $p$-wave interactions）的对称双组分玻色混合物（two-component Bose mixture）在二维横向光学晶格（two-dimensional transverse optical lattice）中的量子液滴（quantum droplets），给出高斯级别（Gaussian level）的基态能与量子耗尽（quantum depletion），并展示晶格驱动的跨维度（transdimensional）自束缚机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察光学晶格所驱动的从各向异性三维气体到弱耦合一维管（one-dimensional tubes）的渡越（crossover）如何改变量子液滴的形成条件。特别地，在裸平均场（bare mean field）为排斥、自由空间不存在液滴的参数点 $y=g_{12}/g=-0.95$ 上，探索是否存在自束缚（self-bound）极小值。

Method: 采用高斯级别（Gaussian level）计算基态能与量子耗尽，并推导其在三维极限与准一维极限下的解析限定形式（limiting forms）。系统为对称双组分玻色混合物，含种间 $p$ 波相互作用，置于二维横向光学晶格中；种间 $p$ 波相互作用仅修正自旋分支（spin branch）。

Result: 计算所得体态物态方程（bulk equation of state）在跨越渡越的整个区间支持自束缚极小值：在三维区，$n^{2}$ 阶的负晶格贡献提供吸引；在准一维区，吸引性涨落（attractive fluctuations）提供吸引；介于两者之间的跨维度区间无法由任一极限定量描述。在所研究的参数范围内，增强种间 $p$ 波相互作用强度会降低渡越过程中的平衡密度（equilibrium density），与诱导束缚（induced binding）减弱一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在无自由空间液滴的排斥平均场条件下，光学晶格仍可通过不同维度区间的不同吸引机制产生自束缚量子液滴，且种间 $p$ 波相互作用可作为调控平衡密度的参数。中间跨维度区间缺乏单一极限的定量描述，提示需要超越两个极限的理论处理。

Abstract: We study quantum droplets in a symmetric two-component Bose mixture with interspecies $p$-wave interactions and a two-dimensional transverse optical lattice. The lattice drives a crossover from an anisotropic three-dimensional gas to weakly coupled one-dimensional tubes. We calculate the ground-state energy and quantum depletion at the Gaussian level and derive their limiting forms. At $y=g_{12}/g=-0.95$, where the bare mean field is repulsive and no free-space droplet exists, the calculated bulk equation of state supports a self-bound minimum across the crossover: a negative lattice contribution at order $n^{2}$ supplies the attraction in the three-dimensional regime, and attractive fluctuations do so in the quasi-one-dimensional regime, with the intermediate, transdimensional range described quantitatively by neither limit. The interspecies $p$-wave interaction modifies only the spin branch. In the parameter range studied, increasing its strength lowers the equilibrium density across the crossover, consistently with a weakening of the induced binding.

</details>


### [138] [Microwave-controlled interactions and stripe formation of static-field-shielded polar molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11395)
*Tiziano Arnone Cardinale,Malte Schubert,Stephanie M. Reimann*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究通过静电场与椭圆偏振微波场构成的屏蔽方案抑制极性分子的短程损耗，推导有效相互作用势并验证其准确性，进而在准二维（quasi-two-dimensional）约束下预测了条纹相（stripe phase）形式的超固态（supersolidity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极性分子（polar molecules）体系中的短程损耗（short-range losses）限制了稀薄分子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensate）的稳定性与平均场描述的有效性，因此需要寻求能够抑制损耗的屏蔽方案。

Method: 采用屏蔽方案，即结合静电场（static electric field）与椭圆偏振微波场（elliptically polarized microwave field）；使用微扰论（perturbation theory）推导有效相互作用势（effective interaction potential），并与耦合通道计算（coupled channel calculations）进行对照验证；随后计算集体激发（collective excitations）。

Result: 识别出一个参数区间，其中两体损耗（two-body losses）被强烈抑制，稀薄分子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚的扩展平均场描述（extended mean-field description）得以成立；在准二维约束下，即使微波椭圆率（microwave ellipticity）取值较小，超固态仍以条纹相形式出现。

Conclusion: 所提出的屏蔽方案可有效抑制损耗并支持扩展平均场描述，且在准二维几何中为小椭圆率下实现超固态条纹相提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We study polar molecules where short-range losses are suppressed by a shielding scheme involving a static electric field and an elliptically polarized microwave field. Using perturbation theory, we derive the effective interaction potential and validate it against coupled channel calculations. We identify a parameter regime where two-body losses are strongly suppressed and the extended mean-field description of dilute molecular Bose-Einstein condensates is justified. We calculate the collective excitations and show that intriguingly, supersolidity in quasi-two-dimensional confinement emerges as a stripe phase even at small values of microwave ellipticity.

</details>


### [139] [Finite-time effects in periodically kicked systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11568)
*F. Revuelta,R. Chacón,F. Borondo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文在超冷原子体系中引入波形与持续时间可变、且仅由单一参数调控的时变调制，研究有限时间效应对动力学局域化的影响，并给出增强局域化的最优条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多以δ踢转子（δ-kicked rotor）模型描述超冷原子受周期踢击的行为，但该理想化模型无法刻画实际脉冲持续时间有限所带来的有限时间效应（finite-time effects）。本文旨在揭示此类效应对动力学局域化（dynamical localization）的深层影响。

Method: 采用含时调制方案，其波形（waveform）与持续时间通过调节单一参数控制，并保持每周期与单位振幅所传递的冲量（impulse）恒定。通过分析不同调制振幅、周期与波形的影响，并将结果与δ踢转子极限情形对比。

Result: 对任意短脉冲，模型可还原标准的踢转子（kicked rotor）情形；且有限时间效应显著影响动力学局域化，这是δ踢转子模型无法捕捉的。动力学局域化强度随系统接近δ踢转子极限而急剧增强；在给定振幅与形状参数下，存在使动力学局域化最大化的最优周期值。

Conclusion: 有限时间效应在超冷原子周期驱动体系中不可忽略，并可被用于调控与增强动力学局域化；δ踢转子模型仅是描述该现象的极限特例。

Abstract: In this work, we study finite-time effects in ultracold atomic systems by considering time-dependent modulations with variable waveforms and durations. These two characteristics can be controlled by adjusting only a single parameter. For arbitrarily short pulses, our model recovers the paradigmatic kicked rotor while maintaining the impulse transmitted per period and unit amplitude constant. Furthermore, we demonstrate that finite-time effects have a profound impact on dynamical localization, a result that cannot be captured by the δ-kicked-rotor model. Through a detailed analysis of the effects of different modulation amplitudes, periods, and waveforms, we identify the conditions for which dynamical localization is significantly enhanced. We show that the strength of dynamical localization increases sharply as the system approaches the δ-kicked-rotor limiting case. Moreover, we establish the existence of an optimal value of the period that maximizes dynamical localization for given values of the amplitude and shape parameter.

</details>


### [140] [Condensation temperature and magnetic phases of trapped coherently coupled Bose gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11779)
*Sunilkumar V,Rajat,Sandeep Gautam,Arko Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该论文研究了三维谐振子势中相干（Rabi）耦合玻色气体的凝聚与有限温度磁性相变，通过半经典方法解析给出临界温度与凝聚体分数，并在 Hartree-Fock-Bogoliubov-Popov 框架下考虑排斥相互作用，得到温度-Rabi 耦合平面内的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于理解相干耦合（coherent coupling）对受限玻色气体凝聚行为与有限温度磁性相变的影响，特别是临界温度随粒子数、有限尺寸效应及相互作用的变化规律。

Method: 对无相互作用体系，采用单粒子谱与态密度的半经典描述（semiclassical description）推导临界温度与凝聚体分数的解析表达式；随后在 Hartree-Fock-Bogoliubov-Popov 框架内引入排斥相互作用，研究温度-Rabi 耦合平面内的有限温度相图。

Result: 固定粒子数时，相干耦合相对于无耦合体系提高临界温度，该增强随粒子数 N 增大而减弱；有限尺寸修正降低转变温度，其效应随 N 增大而减小。体系随温度升高依次经历从铁磁（ferromagnetic）到顺磁（paramagnetic）凝聚体、再到热气体（thermal gas）的转变。由凝聚体分数消失确定的相变边界与解析 Hartree-Fock 预测高度一致。相互作用显著抑制凝聚温度随相干耦合增大的变化。

Conclusion: 相干耦合与有限尺寸效应分别对临界温度产生相反方向的修正；排斥相互作用会削弱相干耦合对凝聚温度的调节作用，且相变边界可由 Hartree-Fock 解析结果良好描述。

Abstract: We investigate condensation and finite-temperature magnetic phase transitions in a coherently (Rabi) coupled Bose gas confined in a three-dimensional harmonic trap. For the noninteracting system, we derive analytical expressions for the critical temperature and condensate fraction using a semiclassical description of the single-particle spectrum and density of states. At fixed particle number, coherent coupling enhances the critical temperature relative to the uncoupled system, and this enhancement decreases with increasing particle number $N$. The finite-size correction, in contrast, lowers the transition temperature, with its effect diminishing for larger $N$. We then incorporate repulsive interactions within the Hartree--Fock--Bogoliubov--Popov framework to investigate the finite-temperature phase diagram in the temperature--Rabi coupling plane. The system undergoes successive transitions from a ferromagnetic to a paramagnetic condensate and, at higher temperature, to a thermal gas. The transition boundaries obtained from the vanishing of the condensate fraction show excellent agreement with the analytical Hartree-Fock predictions. We find that the interactions substantially suppress the variation in the condensation temperature with an increase in the coherent coupling.

</details>

### [141] [A new generation of effective core potentials: Selected heavy 5d and 6p elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10853)
*Omar Madany,Lubos Mitas*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该工作扩展了相关一致有效核势（correlation-consistent effective core potentials, ccECPs）库，为5d（Hf、Os、Hg）与6p（Tl、Po、At、Rn）重元素构建半局域赝势及配套基组，并采用分层核-价划分策略，实现高原子精度与分子可迁移性，同时提升量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对重元素体系，需同时准确描述标量相对论效应（scalar relativistic effects）、自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）与电子关联（electron-electron correlation），而现有ccECP库在5d、6p重元素及核-价划分精度与效率平衡方面存在不足。

Method: 开发半局域赝势（semi-local pseudopotentials）与匹配基组，采用三种分辨率的核-价分层划分策略：60核（Hf、Os、Hg）、78核（主族元素）、以及Hg与Tl的中间68核；以相对论全电子CCSD(T)为参考进行优化，并在原点施加正则化有限势以增强随机量子蒙特卡洛方法的数值稳定性。

Result: 原子低激发态全球平均偏差仅0.045 eV；分子层面解离能偏差控制在0.03 eV以内，平衡键长偏差在0.005 Å以内，显示良好的分子可迁移性。

Conclusion: 该库为重元素体系与材料的预测性多体模拟去除了关键方法瓶颈，其中68核架构在精度与计算效率间实现了此前轻元素中未探索的平衡，属ccECP库的独特进展。

Abstract: We expand the correlation-consistent effective core potentials (ccECPs) library by developing semi-local pseudopotentials and matching basis sets by heavy-elements from $5d$ (Hf, Os, Hg) and $6p$ (Tl, Po, At, Rn) blocks. In order to accurately capture scalar relativistic effects, spin-orbit coupling, and electron-electron correlation, we implement a tiered core-valence partitioning strategy across three distinct resolutions. This includes a small 60-core (Hf, Os, Hg) that explicitly correlates subvalence shells, a large 78-core definition for the main-group elements that rigorously accounts for core polarization and relaxation effects in sparse valence environments, and an intermediate 68-core partition for Hg and Tl. This 68-core architecture represents a unique development in the ccECP library, optimizing the balance between accuracy and computational efficiency in a manner unexplored for ligther elements. Optimized against relativistic all-electron CCSD(T) references, the ccECPs deliver outstanding atomic precision, achieving a global average atomic low-lying states deviation of just 0.045 eV. This accuracy translates directly to robust molecular transferability, systematically restricting dissociation energy discrepancies to under 0.03 eV, equilibrium bond lengths to within 0.005 Å. By enforcing a regularized, finite potential at the origin for enhanced numerical stability in stochastic quantum Monte Carlo methods, this library removes a critical methodological bottleneck for predictive many-body simulations of heavy-element systems and materials.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Consensus in Effective Model Inference for Disordered Ising Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10731)
*Ashveed Ashraf*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文以二维随机键 Ising 模型（random-bond Ising model）的教师-学生框架，量化独立学习者在有限含噪数据下所推断物理描述的可重复性，通过共识分布（consensus distribution）宽度刻画学习者间的一致程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立学习者基于同一无序系统（disordered system）的有限含噪样本可能得出不同的物理描述，因此需要量化这类描述的可重复性。论文以“共识”（consensus）——即独立训练模型之间的一致程度——为度量对象展开分析。

Method: 在二维随机键 Ising 模型的教师-学生框架中，每位学生从单一淬火键实现（quenched bond realization）的有限平衡构型集中推断一个统一的等效耦合（uniform effective coupling）。将推断耦合在学生与实现上聚合得到共识分布，并对其方差进行分解。

Result: 共识分布的方差可精确分解为有限采样贡献与淬火无序（quenched disorder）贡献；前者下界由 Fisher 信息（Fisher information）的倒数给出。两项之和在临界区附近取极小，故共识宽度在赝临界温度（pseudocritical temperature）附近存在极小值，且随无序增强而变锐。两类极小机制不同：采样项继承 Fisher 信息的临界峰，而无序项在最低阶与温度无关，仅通过键无序的线性与三次响应之间的协方差（covariance）在无序强度的四阶上获得临界极小。

Conclusion: 由于学生对异质晶格拟合统一耦合，推断耦合还带有在无限数据极限下仍存在的误设偏差（misspecification bias）。该偏差使均方误差（mean-squared error）的极小值高于共识极小值，因此独立学习者一致程度最高的温度并非其共同答案最忠实的温度。

Abstract: When independent learners are trained on data from the same disordered system, finite and noisy samples can lead them to different descriptions of the underlying physics. We quantify the reproducibility of such descriptions through consensus, the degree of agreement among independently trained models. In a teacher-student framework on the two-dimensional random-bond Ising model, an ensemble of students each infers a single uniform effective coupling from a finite set of equilibrium configurations drawn from one quenched bond realization. Aggregating the inferred couplings across students and realizations yields the consensus distribution, whose width quantifies the agreement among learners. The variance of this distribution separates exactly into a finite-sampling contribution, bounded below by inverse of Fisher information, and a quenched-disorder contribution. Sum of these two variance is minimized near critical region, so the consensus width has a minimum near the pseudocritical temperature that sharpens with increasing disorder. The two minima arise from different mechanisms: the sampling term inherits the critical peak of the Fisher information, while the disorder term is temperature-independent at leading order and acquires its critical minimum only at fourth order in the disorder strength, through the covariance between the linear and cubic responses to the bond disorder. Because the students fit a uniform coupling to a heterogeneous lattice, the inferred coupling also carries a misspecification bias that survives in the infinite-data limit. This bias displaces the minimum of the mean-squared error above the consensus minimum, so the temperature at which independent learners agree most closely is not the temperature at which their shared answer is most faithful.

</details>


### [143] [Universal sampling of spin systems across quenched disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11336)
*Jing Liu,Yeyuan Wu,Ying Tang,Pan Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种通用的神经变分框架（neural variational framework），通过摊销推断（amortized inference）跨越整个无序系综（disorder ensemble），一次训练后即可对新无序实现直接给出玻尔兹曼分布（Boltzmann distribution）的显式近似，无需逐实例平衡或重训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序系统（disordered systems）需在微观自由度平均之外，再对淬火无序（quenched randomness）做第二次平均，而该平均极为困难。复制法与空腔法（replica and cavity methods）在平均场或树状极限之外失去可控性；并行回火（parallel tempering）等数值方法则须为每个无序实现独立且昂贵地平衡。

Method: 构建基于编码器-解码器 Transformer（encoder-decoder Transformer）架构的通用神经变分框架，将推断在所有无序实现间摊销，从而免除逐实例马尔可夫链平衡与实例特定变分拟设的重训成本；训练一次后，对未见过的无序实现直接输出玻尔兹曼分布的显式近似。

Result: 在二维 Edwards-Anderson 模型上完成验证，并应用于随机键 Ising 模型（random-bond Ising model），成功捕捉到 Nishimori 多临界点（Nishimori multicritical point）附近的 Binder 累积量交叉（Binder cumulant crossings）。

Conclusion: 该研究将变分推断的对象从单一实例转向无序系综，为逐实例计算难以承受的阻挫多体系统（frustrated many-body systems）开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Statistical physics extracts macroscopic laws by averaging over the many microscopic degrees of freedom of a system. Disordered systems demand a second and far harder average, one over the quenched randomness itself. The classic analytical routes, the replica and cavity methods, become uncontrolled outside mean-field or tree-like limits, and conventional numerical algorithms like parallel tempering require expensive, independent equilibration for every disorder realization. In this work, we introduce a universal neural variational framework that amortizes inference across the disorder ensemble, eliminating both the need for per-instance Markov chain equilibration and the cost of retraining instance-specific variational ansatzes. Built on an encoder-decoder Transformer architecture, after training once, it produces an explicit approximation to the Boltzmann distribution given previously unseen disorder realizations without further optimization. We validate this framework on 2D Edwards-Anderson models, and apply it to the random-bond Ising model, successfully capturing the Binder cumulant crossings near the Nishimori multicritical point. These results shift the object of variational inference from the single instance to the disorder ensemble, opening a route to frustrated many-body systems where instance-by-instance computation is prohibitive.

</details>


### [144] [Information-Theoretic Characterization of Macroscopic Chaos Emerging from the Chemical Master Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11340)
*Kenshin Matsumoto,Shin-ichi Sasa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文从理论上证明，由双时互信息构建的信息损失率在确定性极限下可恢复Kolmogorov-Sinai熵，并用七反应三组分体系的Markov跳跃过程数值模拟加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放化学反应网络（open chemical reaction networks）在有限系统尺寸下呈现随机浓度动力学，而其宏观极限由可表现混沌（chaos）的确定性速率方程支配；如何在随机描述与确定性混沌描述之间建立定量联系尚待澄清。

Method: 采用理论推导，构造基于双时互信息（two-time mutual information）的信息损失率（rate of information loss），并分析其在确定性极限下的行为；同时以含七种反应、三类组分的Markov跳跃过程（Markov jump process）开展数值模拟进行检验。

Result: 理论结果表明，由双时互信息构建的信息损失率在确定性极限下可恢复Kolmogorov-Sinai熵（Kolmogorov-Sinai entropy）；该结果在七反应三组分体系的Markov跳跃过程数值模拟中得到验证。

Conclusion: 随机化学反应网络中的信息损失率可作为连接随机动力学与确定性混沌的桥梁，其确定性极限对应Kolmogorov-Sinai熵，为有限尺寸随机性与宏观混沌动力学之间的关系提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Open chemical reaction networks exhibit stochastic concentration dynamics at finite system sizes, whereas their macroscopic limit is governed by deterministic rate equations that can display chaos. In this Letter, we show theoretically that a rate of information loss constructed from two-time mutual information recovers the Kolmogorov-Sinai entropy in the deterministic limit. We verify this result through numerical simulations of a Markov jump process for a three-species system involving seven reactions.

</details>


### [145] [Orientational order on non-orientable domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11367)
*Gianmarco Spera,Axel Fotso Ndefo,Keaton J. Burns,Alexander Mietke*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究非可定向（non-orientable）流形上具有取向自由度的被动与主动多体系统的统计性质。作者将拓扑约束重述为可定向双覆盖（orientable double-cover）上的非局域对称关系，揭示非可定向性在不充当外场的情况下消除全局旋转软模，并在XY模型与主动模型中导致"拓扑笼效应"（topological caging）等新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究取向自由度（orientational degrees of freedom）在非可定向域（non-orientable domains）上的统计行为，以区分拓扑约束与外场效应的作用机制。该问题连接拓扑与多体统计物理，且涉及被动与主动两类系统。

Method: 将非可定向域上的拓扑约束重新表述为可定向双覆盖（orientable double-cover）上的非局域对称关系（non-local symmetry relations）。据此分析被动XY模型与主动自驱动粒子、主动Ising自旋模型中的取向涨落与有序行为。

Result: 在被动XY模型中，非可定向性导致拓扑笼效应：环面（torus）上呈常规扩散行为的取向涨落在克莱因瓶（Klein bottle）上饱和至有限值，并在热力学极限下精确计算该值。主动自驱动粒子模型中，尽管相互作用邻域持续变化，拓扑笼效应依然存在。主动Ising自旋模型中，非可定向性强制有序反平行畴（anti-parallel domains）共存且全局极化序参量消失，该状态在可定向域上不存在。

Conclusion: 非可定向性通过非局域对称关系消除全局旋转软模，起到不同于外场的作用，从而在被动与主动多体系统中产生拓扑笼效应及新的有序共存态。拓扑约束是调控此类系统集体行为的关键因素。

Abstract: We study the statistical properties of passive and active many-body systems with orientational degrees of freedom on non-orientable domains. By rephrasing topological constraints as non-local symmetry relations on an orientable double-cover, we show that non-orientability eliminates global rotational soft modes without acting like an external field. In a passive XY model, this results in topological caging, where orientational fluctuations that exhibit conventional diffusive behavior on a torus saturate on a Klein bottle to a finite value that we compute exactly in the thermodynamic limit. In models of active self-propelled particles with orientational degrees of freedom, topological caging persists despite continuously changing interaction neighborhoods. In an active Ising spin model, non-orientability enforces the coexistence of ordered anti-parallel domains with vanishing global polar order, a state that is absent on orientable domains.

</details>


### [146] [Disordered ground states in one-dimensional exactly solvable fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11394)
*Igor Travěnec,Ladislav Šamaj*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在等温-等压系综（isothermal-isobaric ensemble）下精确求解了一维软排斥硬核粒子流体在零温（T→0）极限下的基态性质，包括状态方程与对关联函数，并揭示凹形势（concave potential）下存在不可压缩压力（incompressibility pressure）p_i 及非超均匀（non-hyperuniform）无序基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一维软排斥势流体在零温基态的精确可解性，特别是势函数凹性（concavity）如何决定基态是否无序，以及无序基态是否具有超均匀性（hyperuniformity），是理解低维流体相行为的基础问题。

Method: 考虑直径 a 的经典硬核粒子，粒子对相互作用为有限程软排斥势 φ(x)=ε[(a'-x)/(a'-a)]^{1/ν}（a≤x≤a'），采用等温-等压系综；当 a'≤2a 时相互作用退化为最近邻（nearest-neighbour）相互作用，从而实现热平衡的精确求解，重点分析 T→0 基态的平均最近邻距离 l_0 与对关联函数 g_0(x)。

Result: 若 φ(x) 为凹函数（ν≥1），存在不可压缩压力 p_i=ε/(a'-a)：0<p<p_i 时基态为间距 l_0=a' 的等距粒子链，p>p_i 时间距为 l_0=a。ν>1（严格凹）时，p=p_i 处基态无序，l_0=[νa+(ν-1)a']/(2ν-1)，g_0(x) 为离散位置上加权狄拉克 δ 函数（Dirac delta functions）的叠加；ν=1（线性）时基态亦无序，l_0=(a+a')/2，连续 g_0(x) 为阶梯函数（Heaviside step functions）与 x 的多项式之积的叠加。ν≥1 时 p=p_i 处的等温磁化率（isothermal susceptibility）非零，对应无序基态为非超均匀。

Conclusion: 凹势（ν≥1）在 p=p_i 处的无序基态表现为非超均匀，类似非零温下的无序流体；仅出现在单一压力 p_i 的无序基态对关联函数，其预测能力可延伸至 p_i 附近更宽压力范围内的非零温状态。

Abstract: One-dimensional fluids of classical hard-core particles of diameter $a$, interacting in pairs via a soft repulsive (monotonically decreasing) potential of finite range $\varphi(x)=\varepsilon \left[ (a'-x)/(a'-a)\right]^{1/ν}$ $(a\le x\le a')$ with real positive parameters $\varepsilon$ and $ν$, are studied in an isothermal-isobaric ensemble. If $a'\le 2a$, the pairwise interactions are reduced to nearest-neighbour interactions, which allows for an exact solution of the thermal equilibrium. We focus on the $T\to 0$ ground states, specifically on the equation of state for the mean distance between nearest neighbours $l_0$ and the pair correlation function $g_0(x)$. If $\varphi(x)$ is concave $(ν\ge 1)$, there exists an ``incompressibility'' pressure $p_i=\varepsilon/(a'-a)$ such that the ground state is an equidistant chain of particles with spacing $l_0=a'$ for $0<p<p_i$ and with spacing $l_0=a$ for $p>p_i$. If $ν>1$ (strict concavity), the ground state at $p=p_i$ is disordered with $l_0=\left[ νa +(ν-1)a'\right]/(2ν-1)$ and $g_0(x)$ being a superposition of weighted Dirac delta functions over discrete positions. If $ν=1$ (linear ramp), the ground state at $p=p_i$ is disordered with $l_0=(a+a')/2$ and the continuous $g_0(x)$ is a superposition of Heaviside step functions multiplied by polynomials in $x$. The isothermal susceptibility at $T=0$ is nonzero for concave $\varphi(x)$ ($ν\ge 1$) at $p=p_i$ and, therefore, the corresponding disordered ground states are non-hyperuniform, i.e., they resemble disordered fluids at nonzero temperatures. It turns out that pair correlation functions of disordered ground states, which occur only at a single pressure $p_i$, extend their predictive power to thermodynamic states at nonzero temperatures over a wider range of pressures around $p_i$.

</details>


### [147] [Rare-History Transitions in Temporally Random Integrable Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11415)
*Tingfei Li,Jie Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究时间随机可积量子线路（integrable quantum circuit）中的电流涨落，利用驱动历史的可交换性将其精确约化为层组成，并通过退火大偏差分析预测两类主导历史的首次转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间随机性通常被视为破坏量子相干与可积性的噪声来源，但其在轨迹空间（trajectory space）中对电流涨落统计的组织作用尚不清晰，本文旨在揭示其结构性效应。

Method: 基于驱动的可交换性（commutativity）将每条驱动历史精确约化为层组成；对固定组成，采用均匀热力学贝特拟设（homogeneous thermodynamic Bethe ansatz）修饰并结合弹道涨落理论（ballistic fluctuation theory）获得条件电流统计；再通过退火大偏差收缩（annealed large-deviation contraction）进行分析。

Result: 退火大偏差收缩预测两类主导历史之间存在一阶转变（first-order switch），终止于一条规则尖点端点（cusp endpoints）线；有限时间分析显示该转变如何被平滑（rounded）。

Conclusion: 时间随机性可在轨迹空间中充当涌现的类序参量坐标（emergent order-parameter-like coordinate），为随机可积系统中的涨落统计提供新的组织框架。

Abstract: We study current fluctuations in a temporally random integrable quantum circuit. Commutativity reduces every drive history exactly to its layer composition, turning annealed fluctuations into a competition between current gain and the large-deviation cost of rare compositions. For each fixed composition, homogeneous thermodynamic Bethe ansatz dressing supplemented by ballistic fluctuation theory yields the conditional current statistics. Their annealed large-deviation contraction predicts a first-order switch between two dominant history classes, terminating on a line of regular cusp endpoints. Finite-time analysis shows how the switch is rounded. Thus temporal randomness can act as an emergent order-parameter-like coordinate in trajectory space.

</details>


### [148] [Work as a function of protocol duration for the efficient erasure of an underdamped memory: isothermal to adiabatic transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11473)
*Nicolas Barros,Stephen Whitelam,Sergio Ciliberto,Ludovic Bellon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究利用演化强化学习为欠阻尼双势阱悬臂梁（1比特存储器）设计高效的时间依赖擦除协议，揭示擦除平均功随协议时长的两种标度区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是确定欠阻尼体系（underdamped system）中高效的时间依赖擦除协议（erasure protocols），并理解擦除所需平均功（mean work）如何随协议时长（protocol duration）变化，进而检验朗道尔极限（Landauer's bound）在含惯性体系中的适用性。

Method: 采用演化强化学习（evolutionary reinforcement learning）为一维双势阱（double-well potential）中运动的欠阻尼悬臂梁（underdamped cantilever）优化擦除协议，并以协议时长τ与系统弛豫时间（relaxation time）t_r的比较划分区间；同时借助思想实验式最优协议（gedanken optimal protocol）进行下界推导，并与过阻尼擦除（overdamped erasure）对照。

Result: 存在两个区间：τ≫t_r 时为准静态等温区（quasistatic isothermal regime），平均功回归朗道尔极限并叠加按1/τ缩放的额外开销，类似过阻尼情形；τ<t_r 时擦除变为绝热（adiabatic），平均功增长慢于等温情形，且其增长以1/τ为下界。学习得到的协议可优于在平衡边界条件下最优的协议。

Conclusion: 欠阻尼1比特存储器的擦除功随协议时长呈现等温与绝热两种标度行为，绝热区增长受1/τ下界约束；演化强化学习所获协议在性能上可超越受平衡边界条件限制的最优协议。

Abstract: We use evolutionary reinforcement learning to determine efficient time-dependent erasure protocols for an underdamped cantilever moving in a double-well potential, an experimental realization of a 1-bit memory. We investigate how the mean work $\langle W \rangle $ needed to erase a bit scales as a function of the protocol duration $τ$. We find two regimes, depending on how $τ$ compares to the relaxation time of the system $t_r$. For $τ\gg t_r$, the quasistatic isothermal regime, we recover Landauer's bound plus an overhead that scales as $1/τ$, similar to the overdamped case. By contrast, for $τ<t_r$ erasure becomes adiabatic and $\langle W \rangle$ grows more slowly than in the isothermal case. This growth is bounded from below as $1/τ$, which we derive using a gedanken optimal protocol. Finally, comparison with overdamped erasure shows that learned protocols can outperform protocols that are optimal subject to equilibrium boundary conditions.

</details>
