# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Phases in a class of associative memories via hidden neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10976)
*Toshihiro Ota,Masato Taki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 Krotov-Hopfield 二分架构（记为 class H），通过拉格朗日量（Lagrangian）为每层建模，将隐藏神经元视为检索的序参量，统一分析多项式与指数两种存储尺度，并指出可见层拉格朗日量决定稳定性、隐藏层决定存储尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopfield 网络的联想记忆（associative memory）可视为无序多体系统的吸引子动力学，高阶（higher-order）与指数型扩展将其更新规则变为 softmax 注意力（softmax attention）。但多项式与指数两种情形此前由不同方法分析，缺乏共同架构来追问何种因素决定存储尺度（storage scale）。

Method: 采用 Krotov-Hopfield 的二分架构（bipartite architecture），以每层拉格朗日量（Lagrangian）固定模型，将隐藏神经元作为检索序参量（order parameter）。多项式负载下使用复本方法（replica method）求得复本对称相图（replica-symmetric phase diagrams）与闭式容量（closed-form capacities）；softmax 隐藏层对应指数负载，通过拷贝表示（copy representation）将热力学映射为随机能量模型计数（random-energy-model counting）。

Result: 多项式负载下得到复本对称相图与闭式容量，且 Ising 与球面可见神经元（spherical visible neurons）的串扰矩（crosstalk moment）相同，差异源于可见熵（visible entropy）。指数负载下相图含顺磁相、凝聚相与冻结相（paramagnetic, condensed, frozen phases）；升温通过注意力的量子化重分配（quantized reassignments of attention）破坏检索，典型高斯模式（Gaussian patterns）在各负载下均保持亚稳（metastable）。两种情形的串扰统计不同：多项式负载为中心极限（central-limit），指数负载为大偏差（large-deviation）。

Conclusion: class H 将检索拆分为两种角色：可见层拉格朗日量决定稳定性，隐藏层拉格朗日量决定存储尺度，这两个维度或可指导新拉格朗日量的设计。

Abstract: Associative memory in the Hopfield network is attractor dynamics in a disordered many-body system, and higher-order and exponential extensions turn its retrieval update into softmax attention. The polynomial and exponential regimes have been analyzed by different methods, with no common architecture in which to ask what fixes the storage scale. In this paper we study the bipartite architecture of Krotov and Hopfield, which we call the class $H$, whose model is fixed by a Lagrangian for each layer, taking the hidden neurons as the order parameter of retrieval. At polynomial load the replica method yields the replica-symmetric phase diagrams and closed-form capacities, and the crosstalk moment is common to Ising and spherical visible neurons, so their differences come from the visible entropy. With a softmax hidden layer the load is exponential, and a copy representation maps the thermodynamics onto random-energy-model counting, with paramagnetic, condensed, and frozen phases. Heating destabilizes retrieval by quantized reassignments of attention, and typical Gaussian patterns remain metastable at every load. The regimes differ in their crosstalk statistics, central-limit at polynomial load and large-deviation at exponential load, and the class $H$ splits retrieval into two roles, the visible Lagrangian fixing stability and the hidden one the storage scale, two axes that may also guide the design of new Lagrangians.

</details>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Low-Mass Magnetic Monopoles in the Galaxy: Simulations and Comparison with Ultra-High-Energy Cosmic-Ray Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10784)
*Ala'a AL-Zetoun,Igor Ostrovskiy,Peiwen Mi,Daniele Perri*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作通过扩展CRPropa模拟低质量磁单极子在银河系环境中的传播，发现其到达方向呈各向异性并集中于天区小区域，与Pierre Auger数据比较显示多数情形下零假设（无磁单极贡献）被统计偏好，并给出90%置信水平的积分磁单极通量上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高能宇宙线（ultra-high energy cosmic rays）的起源与成分仍不确定，尤其是超过GZK截断（Greisen-Zatsepin-Kuzmin cutoff）且视到达方向指向本地空洞（Local Void）的稀有事件。作者据此探讨这些宇宙线是否包含近期理论模型预言的轻质量磁单极子（magnetic monopoles）。

Method: 对宇宙线传播建模代码CRPropa进行自定义扩展，在质量、磁荷与初始相空间分布的合理假设下追踪磁单极子在银河系环境中的传播轨迹，并将模拟到达方向与Pierre Auger Observatory数据集进行比较。进一步采用包含方向与能量信息的轮廓似然分析（profile-likelihood analysis）设定通量上限。

Result: 模拟显示遗迹磁单极子（relic MMs）沿丝状“银河磁漏斗（Galactic magnetic funnels）”传播，在地球处产生各向异性的到达方向，且集中于天区的一小片区域。与Pierre Auger数据的比较表明，对绝大多数情形，零假设（无磁单极贡献）在统计上更受偏好。基于方向与能量的轮廓似然分析，得到积分磁单极通量（integral MM flux）的90% C.L. 上限为 $< 2.1\times10^{-23}$ $\mathrm{cm^{-2}s^{-1}sr^{-1}}$。

Conclusion: 预测的到达方向集中于天区小区域，对未来搜寻的设计与数据解读具有重要影响；在现有Pierre Auger数据下，多数情形不支持磁单极子贡献，并据此给出严格的积分通量上限。

Abstract: Origin and composition of ultra-high energy cosmic rays are still uncertain, particularly the rare events exceeding the Greisen-Zatsepin-Kuzmin cutoff and whose apparent arrival directions point to the Local Void. This work is an in-depth investigation of the possibility that such cosmic rays contain low-mass magnetic monopoles predicted in several recent theoretical models. Using a custom extension to CRPropa, a state-of-the-art code for modeling cosmic ray propagation, monopoles are tracked in the galactic environment under realistic assumptions on mass, magnetic charge, and initial phase-space distributions. Our simulations demonstrate that relic MMs follow filamentary ``Galactic magnetic funnels'', producing anisotropic arrival directions at Earth. The expected arrival directions are concentrated in a small region of the sky, which has significant implications for the design and interpretation of future searches. A comparison of the simulated arrival directions with the Pierre Auger Observatory dataset shows that the null hypothesis - no monopole contribution - is statistically preferred for the majority of cases. Using a profile-likelihood analysis that incorporates both directional and energy information, we set 90% C.L. upper limits on the integral MM flux of $< 2.1\times10^{-23}$ $\mathrm{cm^{-2}s^{-1}sr^{-1}}$.

</details>


### [151] [Cosmological Evolution of Primordial Black Holes: UV/IR Decoupling and the KM3NeT 220 PeV Neutrino Anomaly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11511)
*Debdatta Dey,Tausif Parvez,S. Shankaranarayanan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: KM3NeT 观测到的 220 PeV 中微子事件（KM3-230213A）可由原初黑洞（Primordial Black Hole, PBH）末期蒸发爆发解释。通过 McVittie 时空将 PBH 嵌入宇宙学背景，表明早期宇宙吸积与膨胀抑制的霍金辐射（Hawking emission）改变了初始质量窗口，而今日爆发仍由标准 Schwarzschild 热力学主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准天体物理源模型难以解释 KM3NeT 观测到的 220 PeV 超高能中微子事件（KM3-230213A），因此需检验其是否源于原初黑洞（PBH）末期蒸发爆发这一替代机制。已有 PBH 研究多采用静态真空近似，无法刻画其跨越宇宙历史的演化动力学。

Method: 采用 McVittie 时空将原初黑洞（PBH）嵌入宇宙学背景，以替代静态真空近似，并据此考察早期宇宙宇宙学吸积与膨胀抑制的霍金辐射（Hawking emission）对黑洞演化及初始质量窗口的影响。

Result: 早期宇宙环境决定黑洞的整体寿命，但今日（$z \approx 0$）的末期爆炸仍由标准 Schwarzschild 热力学支配。该机制可自然产生 220 PeV 的强局域通量，同时抑制早期辐射，从而满足弥散各向同性背景（diffuse isotropic background）限制。

Conclusion: 该动力学框架改变了当前超高能中微子观测量与其种子原初曲率扰动（primordial curvature perturbations）之间的映射关系，提示需在宇宙学演化背景下重新解读相关观测约束。

Abstract: The recent observation of a 220 PeV neutrino event (KM3-230213A) by the KM3NeT observatory presents a formidable challenge to standard astrophysical source models. We investigate the hypothesis that this ultra-high-energy signature originates from the terminal evaporation burst of a Primordial Black Hole (PBH). Since PBH evolution spans cosmic history, static vacuum approximations fail to capture early-universe dynamics. Embedding the PBH in a cosmological background via the McVittie spacetime, we demonstrate that early-universe cosmological accretion and expansion-suppressed Hawking emission shift the required initial mass window for a terminal burst occurring today. We show that although the early universe environment dictates the black hole's overall lifespan, its final explosion today ($z \approx 0$) is governed by standard Schwarzschild thermodynamics. This mechanism naturally produces the intense 220 PeV local flux while suppressing early emissions, thereby satisfying diffuse isotropic background limits. Consequently, this dynamical framework alters the mapping between current ultra-high-energy neutrino observables and the primordial curvature perturbations that seeded them.

</details>


### [152] [Hierarchical Population Inference with Normalizing Flows for Binary Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11885)
*Leonardo Iampieri,Simone Mastrogiovanni*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于归一化流（normalizing flow）的引力波族群推断流程，直接在分层似然（hierarchical likelihood）中训练，以（z, m1, m2）表示源框架双黑洞族群，并在注入支持下引入互信息（mutual information）诊断以区分真实演化与支撑几何诱导的伪相关。应用于 GWTC-5.0 未发现主质量谱随红移的内在演化证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维参数化质量模型（parametric mass models）在引力波族群推断中虽为标准做法，但其刚性会对复原分布及由此得出的结论产生偏差。作者希望以更灵活的非参数表示替代固定函数形式，并解决观测选择效应与测量不确定性带来的推断困难。

Method: 将源框架（source-frame）双黑洞族群在（z, m1, m2）空间用归一化流（normalizing flow）表示，并直接通过分层似然（hierarchical likelihood）训练；事件后验样本（posterior samples）传播测量不确定性，探测注入（detected injections）用于校正选择效应（selection effects）。族群陈述限制在注入活动（injection campaign）所支持的区域，并引入互信息（mutual information）诊断，在相同支撑限制下与红移无关参考分布比较，以检验超出支撑几何所诱导的依赖关系。

Result: 在两个仅区别于主质量特征尺度是否随红移演化的模拟目录（simulated catalogues）上验证：重建均能恢复注入的质量结构，基准测试确立了诊断在存在与不存在内在演化时的表现。应用于 GWTC-5.0 目录时，方法恢复了约 10 M⊙ 与 35 M⊙ 附近的质量特征，与同一目录的 LVK 族群分析一致，且未发现主质量谱随红移发生内在演化的证据。

Conclusion: 该流程可处理观测科学中普遍存在的一类问题：从成员测量中带有噪声、间接且受选择偏差（selection-biased）影响的数据中复原族群分布；同时提示在受限支撑区域内做族群陈述时需借助诊断区分真实关联与支撑几何伪影。

Abstract: Low-dimensional parametric mass models are standard in gravitational-wave population inference, but their rigidity can bias the recovered distribution and the conclusions drawn from it. We present a pipeline in which the source-frame binary-black-hole population in $(z,m_1,m_2)$ is represented by a normalizing flow trained directly through the hierarchical likelihood, with event posterior samples propagating measurement uncertainty and detected injections accounting for selection effects. We restrict population statements to the region supported by the injection campaign; conditioning on this region can itself induce an apparent association between mass and redshift. We therefore introduce a mutual-information diagnostic that compares the reconstruction with a redshift-independent reference under the same support restriction, testing for dependence beyond that induced by the support geometry. We validate the method on two simulated catalogues differing only in whether the characteristic primary-mass scale evolves with redshift. The reconstruction recovers the injected mass structure in both, and the benchmarks establish how the diagnostic behaves in the presence and absence of intrinsic evolution. Applied to the GWTC-5.0 catalogue, the method recovers features near $10\,M_\odot$ and $35\,M_\odot$, consistent with the LVK population analysis of the same catalogue, and finds no evidence for intrinsic evolution of the primary-mass spectrum with redshift. More broadly, the pipeline addresses a problem common to many observational sciences: recovering a population distribution from noisy, indirect, and selection-biased measurements of its members.

</details>

Suter,A. Sutton,K. Sutton,S. K. Swain,A. Sztuc,N. Talukdar,P. Tas,J. Thomas,E. Tiras,M. Titus,Y. Torun,D. Tran,J. Trokan-Tenorio,J. Urheim,B. Utt,P. Vahle,Z. Vallari,K. J. Vockerodt,A. V. Waldron,M. Wallbank,B. Wang,C. Weber,M. Wetstein,D. Whittington,D. A. Wickremasinghe,J. Wolcott,W. Wu,Y. Xiao,B. Yaeggy,A. Yahaya,A. Yallappa Dombara,A. Yankelevich,K. Yonehara,S. Zadorozhnyy,J. Zalesak,L. Zhao,R. Zwaska*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: NOvA 长基线中微子振荡实验（long-baseline neutrino oscillation experiment）为控制系统误差，在费米实验室测试束流设施（Fermilab Test Beam Facility）部署了 30 吨分段液体闪烁体探测器（segmented liquid scintillator detector）测试束实验，于 2019 年 4 月至 2022 年 7 月采集数据，以厘清探测器响应与能量标定等主要系统不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NOvA 旨在研究中微子混合参数、质量顺序（mass ordering）与轻子扇区 CP 破坏（CP violation），其实验成功的关键在于对探测器响应与标定相关系统不确定性（systematic uncertainties）的可靠理解。为应对这一需求，需通过专门实验手段约束这些不确定性。

Method: 在费米实验室测试束流设施部署 NOvA 测试束实验（Test Beam experiment），采用与 NOvA 近探测器（Near Detector）和远探测器（Far Detector）功能一致的 30 吨分段液体闪烁体探测器，分析由 p-Cu 碰撞产生的标记粒子（tagged particles）；探测仪器具备筛选与鉴别 0.4–1.5 GeV/c 动量范围内电子、μ子、π介子、K 介子与质子的能力。

Result: 该实验于 2019 年 4 月至 2022 年 7 月完成数据采集；对采集数据的分析增进了对影响 NOvA 分析的最大系统不确定性的理解，涉及探测器响应、能量标定（energy calibration），以及强子与电磁能量分辨率（hadronic and electromagnetic energy resolutions）。

Conclusion: NOvA 测试束实验通过功能等效的探测器与粒子鉴别能力，为约束 NOvA 主要系统不确定性提供了数据基础，有助于提升其振荡分析的可靠性。

Abstract: NOvA is a long-baseline neutrino oscillation experiment designed to study the neutrino mixing parameters, mass ordering, and CP violation in the lepton sector. A key component of the success of the experiment is a robust understanding of the systematic uncertainties associated with detector response and calibration. To address this, NOvA deployed a Test Beam experiment at the Fermilab Test Beam Facility, which collected data from April 2019 through July 2022. The NOvA Test Beam experiment used a 30-ton segmented liquid scintillator detector functionally identical to the NOvA Near and Far Detectors to analyze tagged particles produced from p-Cu collisions, with instrumentation capable of selecting and identifying electrons, muons, pions, kaons, and protons with momentum ranging from 0.4-1.5GeV/c. Analysis of the collected data provides a better understanding of the largest systematic uncertainties impacting NOvA's analyses, which include the detector response, energy calibration, and hadronic and electromagnetic energy resolutions.

</details>


### [154] [Production of Light Nuclei and Hypernuclei in Heavy-Ion Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10963)
*Guannan Xie,Yue-Hang Leung*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本综述汇总 STAR 与 ALICE 在 SQM 2026 报告的光核与超核产额、飞秒关联及集体流测量，指出轻核生成时序与核内部结构会在观测量中留下可测印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估现有理论基线（统计强子化与聚合模型）对轻核、超核产额及相关观测量在不同碰撞能量与系统尺度下的描述能力，并厘清残余偏差的来源。

Method: 综述性研究，基于 STAR 与 ALICE 的轻核/超核产额、飞秒关联（femtoscopic correlations）与集体流（collective flow）实验数据，与统计强子化（statistical hadronization）及聚合（coalescence）计算进行对比。

Result: 统计强子化可作积分产额基线，但无法同时描述所有轻核产额比；对质量数 A<4 的束缚态，聚合计算总体上与产额、飞秒关联和集体流一致，但超氚（hypertriton）的定量比较依赖少体波函数假设；共振态 $^{4}$Li 相对紧凑的 $^{4}$He 产额受抑制，指示核结构与晚期动力学效应；高多重数 p+p 中 π–d 飞秒关联表明多数（反）氘核经短寿命共振态强衰变后的核子融合形成。

Conclusion: 轻核生成的时间序列（production chronology）与核内部结构对可观测物理量具有可测量的影响；定量模型比较仍受少体波函数与不稳定态衰变馈入（feed-down）处理方式的制约。

Abstract: We review recent STAR and ALICE measurements of light-nucleus and hypernucleus yields, femtoscopic correlations, and collective flow presented at SQM 2026. Statistical-hadronization calculations provide a useful baseline for integrated yields but do not simultaneously describe all measured light-nucleus ratios across collision energies and system sizes. For bound states with mass number $A<4$, current coalescence calculations provide a broadly consistent description of yields, femtoscopic correlations, and collective flow, although the quantitative hypertriton comparison depends on the assumed few-body wave function. The suppressed production of resonant $^{4}$Li relative to compact $^{4}$He indicates an effect of nuclear structure and late-stage dynamics. However, the quantitative model comparison also depends on the treatment of feed-down from unstable states. In high-multiplicity $p$+$p$ collisions, pion-deuteron femtoscopy further indicates that most observed (anti)deuterons are formed through nucleon fusion after strong decays of short-lived resonances. Taken together, these measurements show that production chronology and internal nuclear structure leave measurable imprints on the physics observables.

</details>


### [155] [Search for the process $e^+e^-\to f_1(1285)$ at the SND detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11057)
*SND Collaboration,M. N. Achasov,A. E. Alizzi,A. Yu. Barnyakov,E. V. Bedarev,K. I. Beloborodov,A. V. Berdyugin,D. E. Berkaev,A. G. Bogdanchikov,A. A. Botov,D. E. Chistyakov,T. V. Dimova,V. P. Druzhinin,R. A. Efremov,L. V. Kardapoltsev,A. S. Kasaev,A. A. Kattsin,V. R. Khamidullin,A. G. Kharlamov,I. A. Koop,A. A. Korol,D. P. Kovrizhin,A. S. Kupich,A. P. Kryukov,A. R. Mamedov,N. A. Melnikova,N. Yu. Muchnoi,A. E. Obrazovsky,A. A. Oorzhak,I. V. Ovtin,E. V. Pakhtusova,I. A. Polomoshnov,K. V. Pugachev,S,A. Rastigeev,Yu. A. Rogovsky,V. A. Romanov,S. I. Serednyakov,D. A. Shtol,Z. K. Silagadze,K. D. Sungurov,I. K. Surin,Yu. V. Usov,V. N. Zhabin,V. V. Zhulanov*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于 SND 探测器在 VEPP-2000 对撞机上的数据，搜索 $e^+e^-$ 碰撞中 $C$-偶 $f_1(1285)$ 共振态的直接产生，测得截面与本征宽度相关分支比，但显著性仅为 2.5σ，并给出 90% 置信水平上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在直接寻找 $e^+e^-$ 碰撞中 $C$-偶（$C$-even）$f_1(1285)$ 共振态的产生，以测量其电子对产生截面与衰变到 $e^+e^-$ 的分支比。

Method: 利用 VEPP-2000 $e^+e^-$ 对撞机上 SND 探测器的实验数据，积分亮度（integrated luminosity）约 200 pb$^{-1}$，质心系能量（center-of-mass energy）范围 1.14--1.46 GeV，其中约 72 pb$^{-1}$ 在 $f_1(1285)$ 共振峰附近采集。

Result: 测得共振峰处的产生截面 $\sigma(e^+e^-\to f_1)=(31\pm 13\pm 2)$ pb，分支比（branching fraction）$B(f_1(1285)\to e^+e^-)=(3.5\pm 1.4\pm 0.3)\times 10^{-9}$；观测显著性（significance）为 $2.5\sigma$。因显著性较低，另给出 90% 置信水平（confidence level）上限：$\sigma(e^+e^-\to f_1)<48$ pb 与 $B(f_1(1285)\to e^+e^-)<5.4\times 10^{-9}$。

Conclusion: 该实验对 $e^+e^-\to f_1(1285)$ 过程给出了截面与分支比的测量值，但观测显著性仅为 2.5σ，尚不足以确证直接产生，故同时报告 90% 置信水平的上限。

Abstract: In the experiment with the SND detector at the VEPP-2000 $e^+e^-$ collider, a search is performed for the direct production of the $C$-even $f_1(1285)$ resonance in $e^+e^-$ collisions. The analysis is based on data with an integrated luminosity of about 200 pb$^{-1}$, accumulated in the center-of-mass energy range of 1.14--1.46 GeV, of which about 72 pb$^{-1}$ were recorded near the maximum of the $f_1(1285)$ resonance. The $f_1(1285)$ production cross section at the resonance maximum $σ(e^+e^-\to f_1)=(31\pm 13\pm 2)$ pb and the branching fraction $B(f_1(1285)\to e^+e^-)=(3.5\pm 1.4\pm 0.3)\times 10^{-9}$ have been measured. The significance of the observation of the $e^+e^-\to f_1(1285)$ process is $2.5σ$. Since the significance is low, we also present the upper limits at the 90% confidence level: $σ(e^+e^-\to f_1)<48\mbox{ pb}$ and $B(f_1(1285)\to e^+e^-)<5.4\times 10^{-9}$.

</details>


### [156] [Projected Sensitivity to Slow Muonphilic Dark Matter with Accelerator Muon Beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11617)
*Rongfeng Zhang,Cheng-en Liu,Ruihu Zhu,Yu Xu,Zijian Wang,Leyun Gao,Xueheng Zhang,Qite Li,Liangwen Chen,Chen Zhou,Qiang Li,Zhiyu Sun*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文基于宇宙线缪子散射方法，利用加速器缪子束与Geant4模拟，评估对慢速缪子亲和暗物质（muonphilic DM）的探测灵敏度，显示可在极短时间内超越现有宇宙线限制，并在一天曝光下提升近两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质（dark matter, DM）的本质仍是现代物理最持久的未解问题之一，而缪子亲和暗物质（muonphilic DM）作为补充传统候选者的方案具有前景。论文旨在探索利用加速器缪子束探测慢速缪子亲和暗物质的可能性。

Method: 沿用近期建立的宇宙线缪子散射（cosmic-ray muon scattering）方法，构建基于Geant4的模拟框架，纳入PKMu缪子层析成像系统的探测器几何结构，并加入专门的弹性 μ-DM 散射过程；同时引入基于高 intensity 重离子加速器设施（HIAF）模拟的真实缪子束相空间分布。

Result: 当束流完全包含在探测器接受度（acceptance）内时，投影灵敏度对束流能量与横向束斑尺寸基本不敏感。在基准束流强度 10^5/s 下，纯缪子束在 m_DM=1 GeV 处约11秒内即超越现有宇宙线限制 1.61×10^-17 cm^2；基于HIAF的真实束流在一天曝光下将限制改善近两个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果表明，束流-缪子散射实验（beam-muon scattering experiment）为显著提升对慢速缪子亲和暗物质的探测灵敏度提供了一条稳健且有前景的途径。

Abstract: The nature of dark matter (DM) remains one of the most enduring open questions in modern physics, and muonphilic DM has emerged as a promising scenario that complements traditional DM candidates. Following the recently established cosmic-ray muon scattering approach, we investigate the sensitivity for probing slow muonphilic DM with accelerator muon beams. A Geant4-based simulation framework is developed, incorporating the detector geometry from the PKMu muon tomography system and a dedicated elastic $μ$-DM scattering process. The projected sensitivity is found to be largely insensitive to both the beam energy and the transverse beam size when the beam is fully contained within the detector acceptance. For a benchmark beam intensity of $10^5/\rm{s}$, the simulated pure-muon beam surpasses the existing cosmic-ray limit of $1.61\times10^{-17}$ cm$^2$ at $m_{\rm DM}=1$ GeV within approximately 11 seconds. A realistic muon beam phase-space distribution based on simulations for the High Intensity heavy-ion Accelerator Facility (HIAF) is also implemented, yielding projected limits that improve upon the cosmic-ray results by nearly two orders of magnitude in a one-day exposure. These results demonstrate that a beam-muon scattering experiment offers a robust and promising route toward significantly improved sensitivity to slow muonphilic DM.

</details>

### [157] [The $E_6$ Restricted Hyperplane Arrangement and its $E_7$ Shadow: Weyl Transport on a Minuscule Bruhat Poset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10701)
*Saber Ahmed,Mboyo Esole*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究 $E_6$ 的 $27$ 维微小表示（minuscule representation）的权在对偶基本 Weyl 室（dual fundamental Weyl chamber）内所截出的受限扇形切割（fan cut），证明 $27$ 个权中仅 $11$ 个的内核与内部相交却决定整个扇形；该扇形恰有 $14$ 个室与 $18$ 条极射线，且各室均为六维单纯锥。主要结果属于表示论：$E_7$ 的 $\mathbf{56}$ 中一个 $14$ 元可见子偏序集，其 Hasse 图恰等于该 $E_6$ 扇形的室邻接图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望完整刻画 $E_6$ 的 $27$ 维微小表示所诱导的扇形结构（室的单纯结构、极射线与关联关系此前未被确定），并进一步揭示该几何与三次曲面（cubic surface）上 $27$ 条直线之间的联系。更深层的动机是寻找 $E_7$ 侧对这一 $E_6$ 结构的表示论“影子”，从而解释其室结构的来源。

Method: 在对偶基本 Weyl 室内，以 $E_6$ 的 $27$ 维微小表示（两个互为对偶的此类表示给出同一构型）的权做受限扇形切割，识别出内核与内部相交的 $11$ 个活跃权。随后枚举全部面、计算两个特征多项式（characteristic polynomial）、格指数（lattice index）与射影室体积，并给出有向拟阵（oriented matroid）描述；同时借助三次曲面上 $27$ 条直线的几何组织室结构，并在 $E_7$ 的 $\mathbf{56}$ 内构造可见子偏序集及其三种 $7+7$ 分裂。结论中仅依据摘要信息。

Result: 该扇形恰有 $14$ 个室与 $18$ 条极射线，每个室都是六维单纯锥，所有面、射线与关联关系均被确定（室数此前由 Diaconescu 与 Entin 得到，单纯结构、极射线与关联数据为新结果）；该构型不是超可解的（not supersolvable）。在 $E_7$ 侧，$\mathbf{56}$ 中一个 $14$ 元可见子偏序集的 Hasse 图等于 $E_6$ 扇形的室邻接图；其三种 $7+7$ 分裂（类型 $A_7$、$D_7$、$E_7$）是 Levi 中心 $\mathfrak{u}(1)$-荷分解的可见迹，并重现 $E_6$ 侧三种 level-$8$ 分解。

Conclusion: $E_7$ 影子不仅记录室图，而且在与独立的 $E_6$ 分类匹配后，可逐室恢复全部六个面，并在整体上恢复 $11$ 个活跃权超平面与对偶 Weyl 室的边界墙，即 $I(E_6,\mathbf{27})$ 的完整局部墙结构。主要结论是表示论意义上的：这一对应完全在 $E_7$ 内部定义。

Abstract: We study the restricted fan cut inside the dual fundamental Weyl chamber by the weights of a $27$-dimensional minuscule representation of $E_6$; the two such representations are dual and give the same arrangement.
  Only $11$ of the $27$ weights have kernels meeting its interior, and we prove that they determine the entire fan. It has exactly $14$ chambers and $18$ extreme rays, every chamber is a six-dimensional simplicial cone, and we determine all facets, rays, and incidence relations. The chamber count was previously obtained by Diaconescu and Entin; the simplicial structure, extreme rays, and incidence data are new. The geometry of the $27$ lines on a cubic surface then explains and organizes the resulting chamber architecture. We also enumerate all faces, compute both characteristic polynomials---the arrangement is not supersolvable---together with lattice indices and projective chamber volumes, and describe the oriented matroid.
  Our main result is representation-theoretic. A distinguished $14$-element visible subposet of the minuscule $\mathbf{56}$ of $E_7$, defined entirely inside $E_7$, has Hasse diagram equal to the chamber adjacency graph of the $E_6$ arrangement. Three canonical $7+7$ splittings of it, of types $A_7$, $D_7$, and $E_7$, are the visible traces of Levi-center $\mathfrak{u}(1)$-charge decompositions of the $\mathbf{56}$ and reproduce the three level-$8$ decompositions on the $E_6$ side. More strongly, the simple-root labels on its covers, transported by minimal-length coset representatives, recover chamber by chamber all six facets and, globally, the $11$ active weight hyperplanes and the boundary walls of the dual Weyl chamber. Thus the $E_7$ shadow records not merely the chamber graph but, once matched with the independent $E_6$ classification, the full local wall architecture of $I(E_6,\mathbf{27})$.

</details>
