### [158] [Generating is not discovering: a pre-registered physics judge for AI-proposed superconductors, calibrated on six known superconductors and one negative control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10614)
*Reinaldo Inácio*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个仅从晶体结构出发的“物理裁判（physics judge）”，同时判定候选超导体能否配对（并给出确定的能隙对称性）与能否成线，并据此审计多个生成模型给出的候选结构：生成环节只是重新发现已知，判断才是瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型（generative models）已提出数百万个“稳定”晶体结构，但其自身的评审者已表明稳定性不等于发现；目前没有公开基准（benchmark）检验候选是否具备完成某项任务所需的物理性质。对超导体而言，任务包含配对（pairing）与成线（becoming wire）两个层面。

Method: 构建一个从结构直接作答的物理裁判流程，包含 DFT+U、门控 Wannier 模型（gated Wannier model）、在 24³ 网格上的完整四阶 RPA 磁化率（rank-4 RPA susceptibility）、线性化能隙方程（linearized gap equation）、不可约表示分类器（irreducible-representation classifier）与可制造性漏斗（manufacturability funnel）；答案键（answer keys）在计算前预先注册（pre-registered）。

Result: 裁判在六种已知超导体（Nb、Nb₃Sn、MgB₂、BaFe₂As₂、La₂₋ₓSrₓCuO₄、YBa₂Cu₃O₇）上经两次注明日期的修正后全部复原预期类别；在铁磷族化合物（iron pnictide）的钌类似物上，于铁给出 s± 的相同物理相互作用下返回零结果（λ₁ ≤ 0.0007 对 0.096；临界 U 4.17 对 0.97 eV），且等 alpha 运行未满足预注册准则的字面要求，故将“以绝对 U 判定的结论”声明为事后（post-hoc）。漏斗在阈值预先固定的条件下复现了工业版图。三项生成审计显示：对 150 个 Materials Project 金属的描述符扫描重新找到经典化合物；1,248 个 MatterGen 结构未产出既新、又稳定、且带配对motif 的候选；对 47,893 个化合物的机制筛选（经盲法留出集验证）重新导出学界已有的类比，即几何加 d 电子计数是必要但不充分条件。

Conclusion: 生成过程只是重新发现已知结构，判断构成真正的瓶颈。作者倡议建立一个覆盖十一份 AI 提出的超导体候选列表的公开基准，而现有这些列表均未对能隙对称性（gap symmetry）进行分类。

Abstract: Generative models now propose millions of "stable" crystal structures, and their own referees have shown that stability is not discovery; no public benchmark asks whether a candidate has the physics to do a job. For superconductors the job is twofold: to pair, with a definite gap symmetry, and to become wire. We present a physics judge that answers both from a structure alone (DFT+U, a gated Wannier model, the full rank-4 RPA susceptibility on a $24^3$ mesh, the linearized gap equation, an irreducible-representation classifier, a manufacturability funnel) and measure what it says about what generators propose. With answer keys pre-registered before computing, the judge was calibrated on six known superconductors (Nb, Nb$_3$Sn, MgB$_2$, BaFe$_2$As$_2$, La$_{2-x}$Sr$_x$CuO$_4$, YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_7$), recovering the expected class in all six after two dated corrections. On the ruthenium analogue of the iron pnictide, with two runs and their criterion pre-registered the day before, it returns a null verdict at the same physical interaction where iron gives s$\pm$ ($λ_1 \le 0.0007$ versus 0.096; critical U 4.17 versus 0.97 eV); the pre-registered equal-alpha run fails the letter of the criterion, and we declare the resolution in favour of absolute U as post-hoc. The funnel reproduces the industrial map with thresholds fixed before running. Three generation audits follow: a descriptor sweep over 150 Materials Project metals re-finds the canon; 1,248 MatterGen structures yield 0 candidates that are new, stable and carry a pairing motif; mechanism filters over 47,893 compounds, validated by a blind hold-out, re-derive the community's analogies: geometry plus d-count is necessary but not sufficient. Generation re-finds what is known; judgment is the bottleneck. We propose a public benchmark over the eleven lists of AI-proposed superconductor candidates, none of which classifies gap symmetry.

</details>


### [159] [Quantum interference between vortex- and impurity-bound states boosts thermoelectricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10687)
*Erik Haatuft,Jacob Linder*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文通过格点Bogoliubov-de Gennes方法与线性响应隧穿理论，研究多重涡旋或非磁性杂质邻近时超导体局域热电响应的变化，发现缺陷诱导态的空间交叠可显著增强Seebeck系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导体中的热电效应通常因准粒子谱的近似粒子-空穴对称性（particle-hole symmetry）而受抑制，但在具有粒子-空穴不对称束缚态的缺陷附近可变得显著；论文旨在探究多个涡旋或非磁性杂质相互靠近时局域热电响应如何被改变。

Method: 采用格点Bogoliubov-de Gennes方法（lattice Bogoliubov-de Gennes approach）结合线性响应隧穿理论（linear-response tunneling theory），计算s波与d波超导体中空间分辨的态密度与Seebeck系数。

Result: 当空间延展的缺陷诱导态发生交叠时，热电响应可被强烈增强。对涡旋而言，该增强超出涡旋核心区域，源于涡旋束缚态（vortex-bound states）之间的干涉；对杂质而言，s波与d波情形下热电响应对杂质间距的依赖相当，但s波超导体中相应的谱重构（spectral reconstruction）更为局域。

Conclusion: 缺陷诱导准粒子态的空间延展与干涉为调控非均匀超导体中的局域热电性提供了手段，可能对低温热电传感（cryogenic thermoelectric sensing）与能量转换具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Thermoelectric effects in superconductors are generally suppressed by the approximate particle-hole symmetry of the quasiparticle spectrum, but can become pronounced near defects that host particle-hole asymmetric bound states. Here, we investigate how the local thermoelectric response is modified when multiple vortices or nonmagnetic impurities are brought into proximity. Using a lattice Bogolioubov-de Gennes approach combined with linear-response tunneling theory, we calculate the spatially resolved density of states and Seebeck coefficient in $s$- and $d$-wave superconductors. We find that the thermoelectric response can be strongly enhanced when spatially extended defect-induced states overlap. For vortices, this enhancement persists beyond the immediate core regions and originates from interference between vortex-bound states. For impurities, the thermoelectric response exhibits a comparable dependence on impurity separation in the $s$- and $d$-wave cases, although the associated spectral reconstruction is considerably more localized in the $s$-wave superconductor. Our results show that the spatial extent and interference of defect-induced quasiparticle states provide a means of controlling local thermoelectricity in inhomogeneous superconductors, with potential relevance for cryogenic thermoelectric sensing and energy conversion.

</details>


### [160] [Dissipative quantum mechanics of Andreev bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10691)
*Mikhail S. Kalenkov,Andrei D. Zaikin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一个微观理论方案，用亚能隙安德烈夫束缚态的耗散量子动力学描述超导结中的交流约瑟夫森效应，并给出低偏压区间的非平衡电流-相位关系及亚欧姆耗散贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高透明超导结在亚能隙偏压下的交流约瑟夫森效应难以用传统方法刻画，其中安德烈夫能级间的朗道-齐纳隧穿与准粒子逃逸至连续谱所导致的不稳定性存在非平凡的耦合，需要新的微观理论框架。

Method: 建立基于亚能隙安德烈夫束缚态耗散量子动力学的微观方案，将交流约瑟夫森效应映射为该束缚态的耗散量子动力学；在低偏压区间，对任意透射率计算非平衡电流-相位关系。

Result: 识别出电流中一个依赖相位的亚欧姆耗散贡献，该贡献由超导能隙边缘附近的准粒子动力学控制；该理论适用于高透明结及亚能隙偏压。

Conclusion: 安德烈夫束缚态的耗散量子动力学为描述高透明超导结的交流约瑟夫森效应提供了有效途径，并揭示朗道-齐纳隧穿与准粒子逃逸不稳定性共同作用下的新耗散机制。

Abstract: We propose a microscopic scheme that allows to describe ac Josephson effect in superconducting junctions in terms of dissipative quantum dynamics of subgap Andreev bound states. This approach is particularly useful for highly transparent junctions at subgap bias voltages in which case a non-trivial combination of Landau-Zener tunneling between Andreev levels and their instability due to quasiparticle escape into continuum may play an important role. In the low bias regime, we evaluate the non-equilibrium current-phase relation of superconducting junctions at arbitrary transmissions and identify a sub-Ohmic phase-dependent dissipative contribution to the current controlled by quasiparticle dynamics near the superconducting gap edge.

</details>


### [161] [Out-of-equilibrium relaxation dynamics of the superconducting order parameter in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11686)
*Avi Shragai,Alexander Won,Jonathan M. DeStefano,Andrea Capa Salinas,Ganesh Pokharel,Stephen D. Wilson,B. J. Ramshaw*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在笼目超导体（kagome superconductor）CsV$_3$Sb$_5$的超导态中发现一个超声衰减峰，其源于序参量弛豫时间（order-parameter relaxation time）在 $T_{\rm c}$ 附近与超声驱动频率共振，由此提取的微观散射时间为 25 ps。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变应变场（time-varying strain field）可将超导序参量（superconducting order parameter）驱离平衡态，而其弛豫过程同时取决于超导能隙结构与准粒子散射（quasiparticle scattering）性质，因此可作为探测超导配对对称性与散射机制的手段。

Method: 对笼目超导体 CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ 施加超声应变场，测量其超导态内的超声衰减（ultrasonic attenuation）随频率的变化，并分析衰减峰的位置与驱动频率的关系。

Result: 在超导态内观测到一个超声衰减峰，该峰源于 $T_{\rm c}$ 附近序参量弛豫时间与超声驱动频率的匹配；由峰的频率依赖关系提取出微观散射时间 $τ_N = 25$ ps。

Conclusion: 该 $τ_N$ 比电阻率测量给出的弹性散射时间（elastic scattering time）长约两个数量级，但与热输运给出的非弹性散射时间（inelastic scattering time）相当；在常规序参量弛豫框架下，这表明弹性散射无法有效弛豫超导凝聚体，与遵守安德森定理（Anderson's theorem）的符号保持 $s$-波态一致。

Abstract: The application of a time-varying strain field drives a superconducting order parameter out of equilibrium. How the order parameter relaxes back to equilibrium depends both on the structure of the superconducting gap and on the nature of quasiparticle scattering. We report the discovery of an ultrasonic attenuation peak inside the superconducting state of the kagome superconductor CsV$_3$Sb$_5$. This peak is the natural consequence of the order parameter relaxation time matching the ultrasonic drive frequency near $T_{\rm c}$. From the measured frequency dependence of the peak, we extract a microscopic scattering time of $τ_N = 25$ ps. This timescale is two orders of magnitude longer than the elastic scattering time as determined by resistivity measurements, but is comparable to the inelastic scattering time determined by thermal transport. Within the conventional framework of order-parameter relaxation, this implies that elastic scattering is ineffective at relaxing the superconducting condensate, consistent with a sign-preserving $s$-wave state obeying Anderson's theorem.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Moiré Topology in Twisted Structures with Noncollinear Spin-Orbit Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10720)
*Xilong Xu,Liangtao Peng,Shaffique Adam,Li Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出利用中心对称第二类自旋轨道耦合（SOC）双层中的非共线自旋轨道耦合实现拓扑莫尔微带的新路径。在四方 HgI2 中，层间杂化产生局域赝能隙与强局域贝里曲率，经扭转重构为孤立拓扑微带。结合物理信息机器学习代理模型，识别出更强 SOC 为关键设计原则并引导以 Pb 替代 Hg，得到更窄、隔离性更好的 PbI2 微带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数已确立的莫尔拓扑平台依赖六角晶格中的谷对比贝里曲率（valley-contrasting Berry curvature），机制受限。该文旨在探索不依赖谷自由度或六角对称性的拓扑莫尔微带实现路径，以拓展莫尔平坦带与关联拓扑相（correlated topological phases）的材料平台。

Method: 基于中心对称第二类 SOC 双层中的非共线自旋轨道耦合（noncollinear spin orbit coupling）提出机制：层间杂化（interlayer hybridization）打开局域赝能隙并产生强局域贝里曲率，经扭转重构为孤立拓扑微带。以四方 Dresselhaus-SOC 的 HgI2 为例进行演示，其中类 Lieb 莫尔势（Lieb-like moiré potential）产生拓扑平坦带。并开发物理信息机器学习代理模型（physics-informed machine-learning surrogate）用于微带隔离优化与材料替换设计。

Result: 在四方 Dresselhaus-SOC HgI2 中，类 Lieb 莫尔势产生拓扑平坦带。机器学习代理模型识别出更强 SOC 为关键设计原则，并引导以 Pb 替代 Hg；所得 PbI2 微带更窄、隔离性更好，并可支持关联驱动磁性（correlation-driven magnetism）以及可调的量子自旋霍尔（quantum spin Hall）与陈绝缘（Chern insulating）相。

Conclusion: 该机制无需谷自由度或六角对称性即可获得孤立拓扑莫尔微带，并通过 SOC 增强策略获得优化材料 PbI2，为关联拓扑物态的实验探索提供了可行的材料实现方案。

Abstract: Moiré superlattices provide a powerful platform for flat bands and correlated topological phases, yet most established examples rely on valley-contrasting Berry curvature in hexagonal lattices. Here, we propose a different route to achieve topological moiré minibands based on noncollinear spin orbit coupling in centrosymmetric type II SOC bilayers. Interlayer hybridization opens local pseudogaps and produces sharply localized Berry curvature, which twisting reconstructs into isolated topological minibands without requiring valley degrees of freedom or hexagonal symmetry. We demonstrate this mechanism in tetragonal Dresselhaus-SOC HgI2, where a Lieb-like moiré potential yields topological flat bands. To improve miniband isolation, we develop a physics-informed machine-learning surrogate that identifies stronger SOC as a key design principle and guides the replacement of Hg by Pb. The resulting PbI2 minibands are narrower and better isolated, supporting correlation-driven magnetism and tunable quantum spin Hall and Chern insulating phases, thereby providing an optimized material realization for experimental exploration.

</details>


### [163] [On the physical origins of switching diversity in Cu-embedded SiO$_x$ memristive devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10799)
*Sahitya Yarragolla,Rouven Lamprecht,Tobias Gergs,Ole Gronenberg,Hermann Kohlstedt,Thomas Mussenbrock,Jan Trieschmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作针对亚化学计量 SiO$_x$ 与饼状铜纳米颗粒（pancake-like Cu nanoparticles, Cu-PCs）构成的阻变器件，建立一维 cloud-in-a-cell 模拟框架，将氧空位漂移扩散、肖特基限制注入（Schottky-limited injection）与 Poole-Frenkel 体陷阱辅助输运耦合，以统一解释界面型与丝状型两类开关行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一材料体系呈现出电容型/渐变型（interface-type）与突变型/阻性型（filamentary-type）两类截然不同的宏观电流-电压特性，其背后的微观机制差异尚缺乏统一解释，故需从器件缺陷景观（defect landscape）与输运过程角度进行分析。

Method: 采用有效一维 cloud-in-a-cell 模拟框架，纳入两类带电缺陷——可动氧空位（mobile oxygen vacancies）与不可动铜相关缺陷（immobile Cu-related defects），并耦合氧空位漂移扩散（drift-diffusion）、金属/氧化物界面肖特基限制注入及经 Poole-Frenkel 传导的体陷阱辅助输运；Cu-PCs 以等效方式处理于上界面附近。通过改变电压应力、扫描速率与氧化物厚度的参数化研究考察其影响。

Result: 参数化研究揭示了电压应力、扫描速率与氧化物厚度如何重新平衡电压分配（voltage partitioning）及电场时空分布，进而改变空位再分布与界面限制/体限制传导的相对贡献；使用由既有器件级研究导出的代表性物理参数组，模拟准确再现了七种实验观测开关响应的特征 $I$-$V$ 曲线。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，微观缺陷景观与输运过程可在单一自洽建模框架内与实验测得的宏观响应相关联，为理解阻变器件开关类型的多样性提供了统一描述。

Abstract: Resistive switching devices with sub-stoichiometric SiO$_x$ and pancake-like Cu nanoparticles (Cu-PCs) exhibit distinct macroscopic current-voltage characteristics classified as capacitive or gradual (interface-type switching) and abrupt or resistive (filamentary-type switching), motivating an analysis of the microscopic processes underlying this diversity. It is proposed that the device defect landscape is largely shaped by two charged defect types, mobile oxygen vacancies and immobile Cu-related defects, whose distributions jointly govern interfacial and bulk transport. An effective one-dimensional cloud-in-a-cell simulation framework is employed to reproduce the phenomenological picture of both interface-type and filamentary-type switching by incorporating the dominant coupled ionic and electronic processes underlying these mechanisms. The model includes oxygen-vacancy drift-diffusion, Schottky-limited injection at the metal/oxide interfaces, and bulk trap-assisted transport via Poole-Frenkel conduction, with Cu-PCs near the top interface treated effectively. A simulation-based parametric study varying voltage stress, sweep rate, and oxide thickness is used to examine how these factors rebalance voltage partitioning and the spatiotemporal electric field distribution, thereby altering vacancy redistribution and the relative contributions of interface- and bulk-limited conduction. Using representative, physically motivated parameter sets informed by prior device-level studies, the simulations accurately reproduce the characteristic $I$-$V$ signatures of seven different experimentally observed switching responses. Overall, the findings help to link microscopic defect landscapes and transport processes to experimentally measured macroscopic responses within a single, self-consistent modeling framework.

</details>


### [164] [Hyperspin Ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10930)
*Hai-Yang Ma,Yuanchang Li,Hu Xu,Shengbai Zhang,Jin-Feng Jia*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出“超自旋（hyperspin）”概念，用于刻画一类非共线磁体（non-collinear magnets）中在高维空间定义的守恒自旋量，并据此将其归类为超自旋交替磁体（hyperspin altermagnets, HAMs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共线交替磁体（altermagnets, AMs）的自旋量子（spin quantum）在动量空间（k-space）行为是识别其作为第三类共线磁性的关键；而非共线磁体虽在自然界普遍存在，却缺乏良好定义的自旋量子数，自旋织构复杂，限制其自旋电子学（spintronic）应用潜力。

Method: 通过分析一类非共线磁体中自旋与哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）之间的对易关系（commutation relations），在高维空间中定义超自旋，并考察其与哈密顿量的对易性质。

Result: 研究发现与哈密顿量对易的是超自旋而非通常的自旋；这类非共线磁体在动量空间应表现出类似共线AMs的共线自旋劈裂能带（spin-split bands），因而被归类为HAMs。

Conclusion: 该理论阐明了AMs与HAMs的基础物理，并为探索可能具有其他守恒量（conserved quantities）的广泛非共线磁体提供了框架。

Abstract: The behavior of spin quantum in k-space is key to identifying altermagnets (AMs) as the third kind of fundamental collinear magnetism. In contrast, non-collinear magnets,though abundant in nature,lack well-defined spin quantum numbers, and the resulting spin textures are often highly complex, which limits their potential for next-generation spintronic applications. Here we propose hyperspin, which lives in a higher-dimensional space, to address these drawbacks. Through analyzing the commutation relations between spin and Hamiltonian for a class of non-collinear magnets, we reveal it is a hyperspin, rather than the usual spin, that commutes with Hamiltonian. Unexpectedly, these non-collinear magnets should also show collinear spin-split bands in k-space like collinear AMs. We therefore classify such non-collinear magnets as hyperspin altermagnets (HAMs), as opposed to the usual collinear AMs. Our theory elucidates the fundamental physics of AMs and HAMs and provides a framework for exploring the wide range of non-collinear magnets that may possess other kinds of conserved quantities.

</details>


### [165] [Measuring chiral phonons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10973)
*Rahul Rao,Hanyu Zhu,Nicholas Kotov,Thuc T. Mai,Maria F. Muñoz,Dali Sun,Jun Liu,Wonjin Choi,Renee R. Frontiera,Angela R. H. Hight Walker*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该展望（perspective）文章系统梳理了在手性与非手性材料中实验测量手性声子（chiral phonons）的现有方法，评述其优缺点，并展望新的测量手段，旨在为跨材料体系的手性声子研究提供实验指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性声子具有角动量并对圆偏振光（circularly polarized light）具有选择性，在自旋电子学（spintronics）、自旋选择性化学反应、热输运、量子信息处理与生物传感等领域受到日益关注。然而，不同材料平台上的手性声子研究多在各领域内独立发展，实验技术却往往相互关联，缺乏统一的实验方法梳理。

Method: 本文为展望性文章（perspective），对当前在手性（chiral）与非手性（achiral）材料中实验测量手性声子的各类方法进行详细描述，并分析各方法的优势与不足，进而讨论新的测量方法。

Result: 文章给出了现有手性声子实验测量手段的详细描述及其优缺点对比，并提出了若干新的测量方法方向；未提供具体实验数据或定量结果。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在为系统研究各类材料体系与应用中的手性声子性质提供实验指南，推动分散于不同领域的研究方法走向整合。

Abstract: Chiral phonons are quantized vibrations where the atomic motion in a solid breaks improper rotation symmetries. In many cases, chiral phonons possess angular momenta and are therefore selective to circularly polarized light. Both fundamental and applied research efforts on chiral phonons have been gaining increasing attention owing to their importance in a variety of fields including spintronics, spin-selective chemical reactions, thermal transport, quantum information processing and biosensing, where the bi-directional spin-lattice coupling enabled by chiral phonons can be harnessed in new ways, and potentially lead to new functionalities. Thus far, the studies of chiral phonons across diverse materials platforms have evolved largely independently within these fields, but the experimental techniques are often interrelated. In this perspective, we present a detailed description, as well as advantages and disadvantages of the current approaches for experimentally measuring chiral phonons in chiral and achiral materials. We conclude with a discussion of new methods for measuring chiral phonons. Ultimately, this work seeks to offer an experimental guide for systematically investigating the properties of chiral phonons in various materials systems and applications.

</details>


### [166] [A model of grain growth in UN integrating molecular dynamics, phase-field modeling, and uncertainty quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10977)
*Mohamed AbdulHameed,Fadel M. Nasr,Wen Jiang,Mahmoud Yaseen,Benjamin Beeler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过分子动力学（MD）、相场模拟与代理辅助不确定性量化的多尺度框架，首次为 UN 建立了定量的晶粒生长动力学与晶界性质描述，并识别出迁移率参数不确定性的降低是未来实验的首要目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铀单氮化物（UN）作为先进核燃料候选材料，其晶粒生长动力学与晶界（GB）性质缺乏定量认识，尤其缺乏对孔洞拖曳效应与内在晶界迁移率的系统评估。

Method: 采用多尺度集成框架：MD 计算 0–2000 K 下 27 个对称倾斜晶界的晶界能；以机理型孔洞拖曳（pore-drag）模型分析锕系氮化物唯一的晶粒生长数据；开展 1500–2000 K 相场模拟；并结合主成分分析、高斯过程回归与 Sobol 分解进行代理辅助全局敏感性分析。

Result: MD 所得晶界能与可用 DFT 值一致，平均晶界能在 1000 K 以下近乎与温度无关、高温下升高；孔洞拖曳迁移率降低因子 s≈0.93–0.99，与 1 无统计差异，表明孔洞拖曳可忽略；由此提取 M0=2.05×10⁻¹⁵ m⁴/(J·s)、QM=0.89 eV；相场模拟证实正常的曲率驱动晶粒生长，晶粒尺寸分布趋近 Hillert 型；敏感性分析显示 M0 主导输出方差，其次为 QM，γ 贡献极小。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 UN 的首个定量晶粒生长框架，并指出降低 M0 与 QM 的不确定性是未来实验研究的最高优先级方向。

Abstract: Grain growth kinetics and grain-boundary (GB) properties in uranium mononitride (UN) are investigated through an integrated multiscale framework combining molecular dynamics (MD), phase-field modeling, and surrogate-assisted uncertainty quantification. MD simulations yield GB energies for 27 symmetric tilt boundaries from 0--2000~K, which are consistent with available DFT values. The average GB energy is nearly temperature-independent below 1000~K and increases at higher temperatures. A mechanistic pore-drag model applied to the only available grain growth dataset for actinide nitrides yields a mobility reduction factor of $s \approx 0.93$--$0.99$, statistically indistinguishable from unity, confirming that pore drag is negligible under the experimental conditions. The intrinsic GB mobility is therefore extracted directly from the effective mobility, yielding $M_0 = 2.05\times10^{-15}$~m$^4$/(J$\cdot$s) and $Q_M = 0.89$~eV. Phase-field simulations conducted from 1500--2000~K confirm normal curvature-driven grain growth, with grain size distributions converging to the Hillert-like form. A surrogate-assisted global sensitivity analysis---combining principal component analysis, Gaussian process regression, and Sobol decomposition---reveals that the mobility prefactor $M_0$ dominates output variance at all times, followed by the activation energy $Q_M$, while the GB energy $γ$ contributes minimally. These results establish the first quantitative grain growth framework for UN and identify the reduction of uncertainty in $M_0$ and $Q_M$ as the highest-priority target for future experimental efforts.

</details>


### [167] [Silicon Solar Cell Design for >30% Efficiency via Singlet Fission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10979)
*Shona McNab,Phoebe Pearce,Pietro P. Altermatt,Jingnan Tong,Ruy Sebastian Bonilla,Timothy W. Schmidt,Murad J. Y. Tayebjee,Bram Hoex,Alison Ciesla,Michael P. Nielsen,Nicholas J. Ekins Daukes*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过耦合光学与电学模拟，评估了多种工业相关硅电池架构与单线态裂分（singlet fission, SF）材料结合后的效率潜力，指出叉指背接触（interdigitated back-contact, IBC）电池效率可超过33%，但性能对前表面钝化质量高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单线态裂分材料可将高能光子转化为多个电荷载流子，有望在无需多结叠层设计复杂性的前提下突破单结硅太阳能电池的效率极限。2025年首个SF增强硅电池问世后，亟需理解SF材料如何有效集成到高效工业硅器件中，并从概念验证走向可制造技术。

Method: 采用耦合光学与电学模拟（coupled optical and electrical simulations），评估若干工业相关硅电池架构与SF材料结合后的效率潜力，并分析前表面钝化、表面掺杂与界面固定电荷等因素的影响。

Result: 叉指背接触（IBC）电池因前表面无遮挡而具备最大改进潜力，效率可超过33%；但其性能对前表面钝化质量高度敏感。合理的硅设计，尤其是受控的表面掺杂与固定界面电荷，可缓解复合损失并放宽对超薄激子转移层的钝化要求。

Conclusion: SF材料与工业硅电池的集成具有显著效率提升空间，IBC架构最具前景；通过优化硅表面设计与界面电荷调控，可缓解复合损失并降低钝化要求，为SF技术从概念验证向可制造技术转化提供设计指引。

Abstract: Singlet fission (SF) materials convert high-energy photons into multiple charge carriers, providing a route to exceed the efficiency limits of single-junction silicon solar cells without many of the complexities of multi-junction tandem designs. Following the first demonstration of an SF-enhanced silicon solar cell in 2025, there is a need to understand how SF materials can be effectively integrated into high-efficiency industrial silicon devices and translated from proof of concept to a manufacturable technology. Using coupled optical and electrical simulations, we assess the efficiency potential of several industrially relevant silicon cell architectures combined with SF materials. Interdigitated back-contact (IBC) cells offer the greatest potential for improvement due to unrestricted front-surface access and can achieve efficiencies exceeding 33%. However, performance is highly sensitive to front-surface passivation quality. Appropriate silicon design, particularly controlled surface doping and fixed interfacial charge, can mitigate recombination losses and relax passivation requirements for ultra-thin exciton-transfer layers.

</details>


### [168] [U-Shaped Fermi-Level Dependence of Point-Defect Aggregation Enthalpy in Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10995)
*Yin Wu,Xiao Kong,Pai Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过无偏全局结构搜索与第一性原理计算，筛选超过1500种候选结构，揭示硅中点缺陷聚集反应焓随费米能级呈U形依赖关系，并指出该现象源于复合物相较孤立组分更趋电中性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅中点缺陷的构型与能量同时受电荷态与费米能级（Fermi level, EF）调控，但二者耦合如何影响缺陷聚集尚缺乏系统性认识。该研究旨在厘清电荷态与EF对缺陷聚集热力学的影响机制。

Method: 采用无偏全局结构搜索（unbiased global structure search）结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），筛选1500余种候选结构，涵盖本征缺陷及C、H、O、N、P、B杂质，电荷态范围为-2至+2。

Result: 缺陷聚集反应焓随EF呈U形变化，在反应净电荷转移为零处最强，向两侧带边减弱。该U形源于复合物比孤立组分更趋于电中性，使净电荷转移在带隙中改变符号，符号转变点由具体缺陷的跃迁能级决定。典型反应的驱动力可调范围约0.3-0.5 eV，室温下可使平衡复合物浓度变化5至8个数量级。

Conclusion: 费米能级可作为硅缺陷工程（defect engineering）的实用调控手段，适用于原生生长及辐照硅材料。

Abstract: Point defects govern the electronic and structural behavior of silicon, yet their configuration and energy depend on both the charge state and the Fermi level (EF). Using an unbiased global structure search with first-principles calculations, we screened more than 1,500 candidate structures, covering intrinsic defects and C, H, O, N, P, and B impurities in charge states from -2 to +2. The reaction enthalpy of defect aggregation depends on EF in a U shape, being strongest where the net charge transferred during the reaction vanishes and weaker toward both band edges. This U arises because complexes are more charge-neutral than their isolated constituents, so the net charge transfer changes sign across the gap. The point where this sign change occurs is set by the transition levels of the specific defects. For typical reactions, the driving force is tunable by 0.3-0.5 eV, which shifts the equilibrium complex concentration by five to eight orders of magnitude at room temperature. Our results make the Fermi level a practical lever for defect engineering in as-grown and irradiated silicon.

</details>


### [169] [High-Performance Scaled P-Type SnOx Transistor by Atomic Layer Deposition with CFET Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11114)
*Ziheng Wang,Wen He,Chen Gu,Kai Jiang,Liankai Zheng,Jinxiu Zhao,Zhiyu Lin,Xiangjun Liu,Di Geng,Ling Li,Mengwei Si*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用原子层沉积（atomic layer deposition, ALD）制备高性能 p 型 SnOx 晶体管，作为兼容后段制程（back-end-of-line, BEOL）的器件，并进一步实现与 n 型 In2O3 晶体管垂直堆叠的互补场效应晶体管（complementary FET）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现单片三维集成（monolithic 3D integration）的互补逻辑（complementary logic）需要高性能 p 型氧化物半导体（p-type oxide semiconductors），但此类材料性能仍显著落后于 n 型对应物。因此需要开发可与后段制程兼容的高性能 p 型氧化物晶体管。

Method: 采用原子层沉积（atomic layer deposition, ALD）制备 p 型 SnOx 晶体管，并通过前驱体与反应工程（precursor and reaction engineering）抑制 SnOx 薄膜中的 Sn4+ 组分；同时将沟道长度缩小至 80 nm，并构建 ALD SnOx p-FET 垂直堆叠于 ALD In2O3 n-FET 之上的互补场效应晶体管。

Result: SnOx 晶体管表现出 6.9 cm2/Vs 的高场效应迁移率（field-effect mobility）、185 mV/dec 的低亚阈值摆幅（subthreshold swing, SS）、约 1.8×10⁴ 的开关比（ION/IOFF）与高偏压稳定性（bias stability）。沟道长度缩小至 80 nm 时，在 VDS = -1 V 下获得 38.7 mA/mm 的高开态电流。互补场效应晶体管在 VDD = 4 V 时实现 21 V/V 的最大电压增益（voltage gain）。

Conclusion: 抑制 Sn4+ 组分的前驱体与反应工程是性能提升的关键。ALD SnOx 有望成为可缩放、高性能的后段制程 p 型晶体管候选材料。

Abstract: The development of high-performance p-type oxide semiconductors is essential for realizing complementary logic for monolithic 3D integration, yet p-type oxide semiconductors still exhibit substantially inferior performance compared with their n-type counterparts. In this work, we demonstrate high-performance p-type SnOx transistors by atomic layer deposition (ALD), as back-end-of-line compatible devices for monolithic 3D integration. The SnOx transistors exhibit high field-effect mobility of 6.9 cm2/Vs, low subthreshold swing (SS) of 185 mV/dec, decent on/off ratio (ION/IOFF) of 1.8*104 and high bias stability. By scaling the channel length down to 80 nm, a high on-current of 38.7 mA/mm at VDS of -1 V is achieved. It is understood that precursor and reaction engineering to suppress Sn4+ component in SnOx film are the key for performance enhancement. Furthermore, a complementary field-effect transistor with ALD SnOx p-FET vertically stacking on ALD In2O3 n-FET is also demonstrated, achieving maximum voltage gain of 21 V/V at VDD of 4 V. These findings suggest ALD SnOx as a promising candidate for scaled high-performance BEOL p-type transistors.

</details>


### [170] [Interplay of spin-lattice and electronic coupling far above Neel ordering in 2D antiferromagnetic CrPS4 and its interface manifestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11203)
*Divya Jangra,Binoy Krishna De,Mukesh Kumar Dasoundhi,Sourav Chowdhury,Pragati Sharma,Kartick Biswas,Arup Basak,Dheeraj Kumar Gupta,Koushik chakraborty,Praveen Kumar Velpula,Pavan Nukala,U. Chandni,Arvind Kumar yogi,Mukul Gupta,Markus Hucker,Vasant G. Sathe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在低维范德华反铁磁体 CrPS4 中，于奈尔温度 TN（~38K）之上发现由短程自旋关联驱动的强耦合自旋-声子-电子态，并证明该耦合异常可跨界面影响相邻 In2Se3 层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究低维反铁磁体中远高于 TN 的自旋-声子耦合及其与电子自由度的关联机制，并考察此类耦合态能否在人工设计的异质结构中实现界面调控与相关多功能性。

Method: 采用温度依赖拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）追踪自旋-声子耦合与局域晶格畸变；利用光致发光光谱（photoluminescence spectra）中的振动进展（vibronic progression）判断电子-声子耦合增强；并结合电输运与光电响应测量；进一步构建 CrPS4/In2Se3 异质结构以研究界面耦合动力学。

Result: 自旋-声子耦合持续至 T*~120K，伴随局域晶格畸变；T* 附近电子-声子耦合增强，电输运与光电响应同时显著变化；在 CrPS4/In2Se3 异质结构中，In2Se3 晶格动力学在 CrPS4 磁性异常处被显著改变，并在 T* 处观测到耦合动力学。

Conclusion: CrPS4 中高于 TN 的自旋-声子耦合源于短程磁关联引起的局域晶格畸变，进而增强电子-声子相互作用；该耦合自由度异常可界面传递至相邻 In2Se3，为人工设计异质结构中的相关多功能性提供新途径。

Abstract: A short-range spin correlation driven, strongly intercoupled spin-phonon-electronic state far above TN (~38K) is identified in the low dimensional van der Waals antiferromagnet CrPS4. Temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy reveals spin-phonon coupling persisting up to T*~120K, concomitant with local lattice distortion. The setting of vibronic progression in photoluminescence spectra suggests the strengthening of electron-phonon coupling around T*. Furthermore, both the electrical transport and optoelectronic response also change significantly at T*. The results indicate that spin-phonon coupling above TN in CrPS4 originates from local lattice distortion induced by short range magnetic correlations which in turn enhances the electron-phonon interaction. Furthermore, using a CrPS4/In2Se3 heterostructure, we demonstrate that the anomaly associated with the coupled degrees of freedom in CrPS4 also influences the adjacent In2Se3 layer. The lattice dynamics of In2Se3 is significantly modified across the magnetic anomaly of CrPS4, and the coupled dynamics is observed at T*. These interfacial manifestation opens up new possibilities for achieving correlated multifunctionalities in artificially designed heterostructure.

</details>


### [171] [Interface-Controlled Phase Stability in Polymorphic HfO$_2$ Revealed by Machine-Learning Atomistic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11307)
*Xudong Zhu,Junhong Li,Lixin He*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以机器学习原子模拟（machine-learning atomistic simulations）结合以铪（Hf）为中心的局域相分类方案，揭示 HfO$_2$ 中晶态界面匹配（crystallographic interface matching）会重定向相竞争与相变路径，表明相界是相稳定性与相变的主动参与者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HfO$_2$ 存在丰富多型（polymorphism），其功能性质源于不同相之间的竞争；但仅凭体相自由能（bulk free-energy）关系无法解释混合相界处的相选择，因为界面取向与结构连续性会约束集体重排（collective rearrangements）。

Method: 采用机器学习原子模拟（machine-learning atomistic simulations）与以 Hf 为中心的局域相分类方案，并在 300–1800 K、最长 3 ns 条件下考察多类界面（M/T、M/PO、M/AO、PO/AO、T/PO）的相共存行为；同时使用微动弹性带（nudged elastic band）计算界面介导路径的势垒，并比较长胞（~12 nm）与短胞（~3 nm）。

Result: M(100)/T(100) 界面在整个 3 ns、300–1800 K 模拟中保持钉扎为 M/T 混合；M/PO、M/AO、PO/AO 界面在 900 K 前维持两相共存，而所有 T/PO 界面均变为 PO 主导；1800 K 时所有非 M 界面转为 T 主导，含 M 界面仍保持单斜（monoclinic）多数。对 M(100)$\rightarrow$T(100)，长胞（~12 nm）界面介导路径势垒为 136.03 meV/f.u.，比短胞（~3 nm）直接路径低 29.2 %，该差异与短胞中缺失的顺序相前沿运动（sequential phase-front motion）相关。

Conclusion: 结果将相界识别为相稳定性与相变的主动参与者，并确立界面取向与晶态匹配（crystallographic matching）作为稳定亚稳多型（metastable polymorphs）与定向调控 HfO$_2$ 相变的变量。

Abstract: HfO$_2$ exhibits rich polymorphism, and competition among different phases underpins many of its functional properties. Yet bulk free-energy relations alone cannot explain phase selection at mixed-phase boundaries, where interface orientation and structural continuity constrain collective rearrangements. Here, using machine-learning atomistic simulations and a Hf-centered local phase classification scheme, we show that crystallographic interface matching redirects phase competition and accessible transformation pathways. The M(100)/T(100) interface remains pinned as an M/T mixture throughout 3 ns simulations from 300 to 1800 K. M/PO, M/AO, and PO/AO interfaces retain two-phase coexistence up to 900 K, whereas all T/PO interfaces become PO-dominant. At 1800 K, all non-M interfaces become T-dominant, while M-containing interfaces retain a monoclinic majority. Nudged elastic band calculations reveal lower-barrier routes through interface states. For M(100)$\rightarrow$T(100), the interface-mediated route in a long cell ($\sim$12 nm) yields a barrier of 136.03 meV/f.u., 29.2 % lower than the direct route in a short cell ($\sim$3 nm). This difference is associated with sequential phase-front motion absent from the short cell. These results identify phase boundaries as active participants in phase stability and transformation and establish interface orientation and crystallographic matching as variables for stabilizing metastable polymorphs and directing phase conversion in HfO$_2$.

</details>


### [172] [Correlation enhanced altermagnetism mediated by spin-lattice coupling in CrSb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11346)
*Charmi Bhalani,Banasree Sadhukhan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合密度泛函理论（DFT）、DFT+U 与动力学平均场理论（DMFT），研究电子关联与自旋-晶格耦合（SLC）对 NiAs 型 CrSb 中非相对论性交错磁自旋劈裂（NRASS）的调控作用，并揭示 SLC 可作为描述交错磁强度的微观参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体（altermagnets）具有动量依赖的自旋劈裂且无净磁化，在自旋电子学（spintronics）中潜力显著；其 NRASS 的对称性与多极起源已较清楚，但电子关联与 SLC 对 NRASS 的作用尚缺乏研究，而 SLC 提供了通过晶格自由度调控交错磁性的直接途径。

Method: 以 NiAs 型材料 CrSb 为对象，采用 DFT、DFT+U 与 DMFT 逐级引入电子关联，考察其对交错磁强度的影响；随后评估 SLC，建立其与 NRASS 演化的联系，并在另一 NiAs 型化合物 MnTe 中进行验证。

Result: 增强电子关联显著提升 NRASS，并驱动非公度自旋螺旋（spin-spiral, SS）态的稳定；动力学关联通过 Cr-3d 态的准粒子重整化进一步放大自旋劈裂，DFT+DMFT 下 NRASS 比 DFT 大 12.5%。SLC 与 NRASS 演化直接相关，且在 MnTe 中同样成立，表明该 SLC-NRASS 关联在交错磁体中具有普适性。

Conclusion: 电子关联与晶格自由度（SLC）的共同作用是调控交错磁性的统一框架，SLC 可作为交错磁强度的微观描述符（microscopic descriptor），并适用于不同交错磁材料。

Abstract: Altermagnets (AMs) exhibit momentum-dependent spin splitting without net magnetization, making them promising platforms for spintronic applications. While the symmetry and multipolar origins of nonrelativistic altermagnetic spin splitting (NRASS) are well established, the role of electronic correlations and spin-lattice coupling (SLC) in controlling NRASS remain unexplored. In particular, SLC provides a direct pathway for tuning altermagnetism through lattice degrees of freedom. Here we first investigate the role of electronic correlations on altermagnetic strength in NiAs-type material CrSb using density functional theory (DFT), DFT+$U$, and dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). We find that increasing electronic correlations substantially enhance NRASS and drive the stabilization of an incommensurate spin-spiral (SS) state, while dynamical correlations further amplify the spin splitting through quasiparticle renormalization of the Cr-$3d$ states. We find that the NRASS remains 12.5\% larger in DFT+DMFT than in DFT. By evaluating SLC in next step, we establish its direct connection to the evolution of NRASS and identify SLC as a microscopic descriptor between electronic correlation and enhanced altermagnetism in CrSb. Investigating it in another NiAs-type compound MnTe, we show that the SLC-NRASS correlation is generic across AMs and establish SLC as a microscopic descriptor of altermagnetic strength. Our results provide a unified framework for tuning altermagnetism through the interplay between the electronic correlations and lattice degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [173] [Anisotropy of Ultrafast Strain in $V_2O_3$ Thin Films: Out-of-Equilibrium Phase Transitions under Interfacial Clamping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11419)
*J. Guzman-Brambila,R. Mandal,D. Lea,E. Trzop,M. Servol,J. C. Ekström,F. Pawula,J. Tranchant,L. Cario,M. Lorenc,E. Janod,C. Mariette*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用方位分辨的时间分辨X射线衍射，追踪c-cut蓝宝石衬底上颗粒状V₂O₃薄膜中取向依赖的应变动力学，揭示界面热膨胀失配导致的各向异性夹持效应（clamping）会静态选择超快相变路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联材料（correlated materials）中的超快光致绝缘体-金属相变常由晶格畸变介导，但界面晶格约束（interfacial lattice constraints）对非平衡相变路径的作用尚不明确。

Method: 采用方位分辨时间分辨X射线衍射（azimuth-resolved time-resolved X-ray diffraction），追踪c-cut蓝宝石上颗粒V₂O₃薄膜在热相变与光激发下的取向依赖应变；结合能量密度依赖测量分离相变分数与受夹持限制的晶格畸变。

Result: (110)_H应变的方位轮廓在热相变前后曲率反转，直接证明六方基面晶格（a_H,b_H）存在部分夹持；光激发反铁磁绝缘相后，弱约束晶粒族达到金属态特征的完整基面收缩，而强约束晶粒族畸变显著减小。

Conclusion: 界面夹持作为超快相切换的静态选择器，并提供了量化异质结构中各向异性应变动力学的通用途径。

Abstract: Ultrafast photoinduced insulator-to-metal transitions in correlated materials are often mediated by lattice distortions, yet the role of interfacial lattice constraints in shaping nonequilibrium pathways remains largely unexplored. We use azimuth-resolved time-resolved X-ray diffraction to track orientation-dependent strain dynamics in granular V$_2$O$_3$ thin films on c-cut sapphire, where thermal-expansion mismatch imposes anisotropic interfacial strain. Across the thermal transition, the azimuthal profile of the (110)$_H$ strain inverts curvature, providing direct evidence of partial clamping of the hexagonal basal-plane lattice (a$_H$,b$_H$). After photoexcitation of the antiferromagnetic insulating phase, the structural response remains clamp-limited: weakly constrained grain families reach the full basal-plane contraction characteristic of the metallic-like state, whereas strongly constrained families exhibit a strongly reduced distortion. Fluence-dependent measurements further disentangle transformed fraction from clamping-limited lattice distortion. Our results show that interfacial clamping acts as a static selector for ultrafast phase switching and provide a general route to quantify anisotropic strain dynamics in heterostructures.

</details>


### [174] [Vacancy Order and Physical Properties of a Ternary Compound Fe0.68Pd0.80Te with an α-Fe1+xTe-Type Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11441)
*Bingxian Shi,Manyu Wang,Chenglin Shang,Yanyan Geng,Yangyang Fan,Xiaoxiao Pei,Li Huang,Daye Xu,Juanjuan Liu,Jinchen Wang,Hongxia Zhang,Hongliang Wang,Lijie Hao,Rui Xu,Zhongyi Lu,Zhihai Cheng,Peng Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究报道了一种具有 α-Fe1+xTe 原型结构的新化合物 Fe0.68Pd0.80Te，其特征为 Pd 方网与约 68% 的间隙 Fe 位占据率，并观测到 3×3×3 的 Pd 空位有序。该材料在 40 K 以下呈自旋玻璃基态，为带隙小于 10 meV 的半导体，表现出弱负磁阻与空穴型载流子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fe1+xTe 以 Fe 方网和少量间隙 Fe 位占据为特征，本文试图通过引入 Pd 构建结构类似但性质不同的新化合物，以拓展相关量子现象的研究平台。研究动机在于对比 Fe 方网与 Pd 方网体系的结构与物性差异。

Method: 采用非接触原子力显微镜（noncontact atomic force microscopy）与 X 射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）表征结构与 Pd 空位有序；通过磁学表征确定基态；通过电输运测量确定带隙、磁阻与载流子类型。

Result: Fe0.68Pd0.80Te 具有 Pd 方网与近 68% 的间隙 Fe 位占据率，并存在 3×3×3 的 Pd 空位有序。磁学表征显示其在 Tg 40 K 以下为自旋玻璃（spin-glass）基态；电输运表明其为带隙小于 10 meV 的半导体，在室温以下具有弱负磁阻与空穴型（holelike）载流子。

Conclusion: 结果表明 Fe0.68Pd0.80Te 具备进一步探索多种量子现象（quantum phenomena）的潜力，可作为相关层状材料研究的新候选体系。

Abstract: We report the identification and characterization of a new compound Fe0.68Pd0.80Te with an α-Fe1+xTe prototype structure. Different from the Fe-square net and minor occupancy of interstitial Fe-sites in Fe1+xTe, Fe0.68Pd0.80Te is featured by a Pdsquare net and near 68% occupancy of the corresponding interstitial Fe-sites. Furthermore, noncontact atomic force microscopy and X-ray diffraction provide evidence for the existence of a 3*3*3 Pd-vacancy order in this layered material. A spin-glass ground state below Tg 40 K is identified via magnetic characterization. Electrical transport measurements show that Fe0.68Pd0.80Te is a semiconductor with a very small band gap below 10 meV. It has weak negative magnetoresistance and holelike charge carriers below room temperature. Our results demonstrate its potentials for further exploring various quantum phenomena.

</details>


### [175] [Bottom-up Synthesis of Metastable 2D Hexagonal Copper(I) Iodide on Monolayer and within Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11459)
*David Kaiser,Guobin Jia,Janis Köster,Sadegh Ghaderzadeh,Christian E. Halbig,Siegfried Eigler,Andrey Turchanin,Benjamin Dietzek-Ivanšić,Arkady V. Krasheninnikov,Jonathan Plentz,Elena Besley,Ute Kaiser*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过气相合成（vapor-phase synthesis）在低缺陷、大面积单层及双层还原氧化石墨烯（reduced oxo-graphene, r-oxo-G）上直接生长出亚稳态二维六方相碘化亚铜（h-CuI），实现了在开放表面上自下而上（bottom-up）生长 2D h-CuI 的首次报道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CuI 常压下稳定于三维 γ 相，其层状范德华 β 相仅在 643–673 K 稳定；已有 2D h-CuI 依赖液相剥离或石墨烯夹层封装获得，而在开放表面上自下而上生长尚未实现。因此需要一种在低温、开放表面选择性制备亚稳 2D h-CuI 的路径。

Method: 以铜 TEM 栅网作为固态铜前驱体，在 40 °C 下经 HI 蒸气暴露引发成核，并在 180 °C 退火促进 h-CuI 畴区扩展；采用球差校正 HRTEM 解析原子结构、局部扭转角与晶格各向异性，STEM-EDX 测定 Cu:I 比，并结合第一性原理计算与从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics）模拟。

Result: 在低缺陷、大面积单层及双层 r-oxo-G 上获得了扩展的 h-CuI 畴区；HRTEM 分辨出 CuI/r-oxo-G 纳米杂化体的原子结构、局部扭转角与晶格各向异性，STEM-EDX 给出的 Cu:I 比与 1:1 一致；计算表明范德华粘附稳定了负载的六方层，且异质结构在 600 K 仍保持六方晶格有序，包括在开放单层石墨烯载体上。

Conclusion: 在所提出的低温前驱体条件下，γ 相成核路径不可用，使六方相在石墨烯界面选择性形成；生长的横向尺度主要受残余界面吸附物限制。该结果确立了在化学惰性石墨烯模板上制备亚稳 2D h-CuI 的路线，或可用于宽禁带电子与光电子器件。

Abstract: Copper(I) iodide (CuI) is a wide-bandgap semiconductor crystallizing in the 3D $γ$-phase under ambient conditions; its layered van der Waals bulk phase $β$-CuI is stable only between 643 and 673 K. The two-dimensional (2D) h-CuI form has been obtained via liquid-phase exfoliation of mechanochemically prepared precursors and via encapsulation between graphene sheets, whereas bottom-up growth of 2D h-CuI on open surfaces has not yet been demonstrated. Here, we report a vapor-phase synthesis of h-CuI directly on low-defect, large-area monolayer and within bilayer reduced oxo-graphene (r-oxo-G) at low temperatures. Using a copper TEM grid as the solid-state precursor for copper, HI-vapor exposure at 40 $°$C initiates nucleation, while annealing at 180 $°$C promotes the growth of extended h-CuI domains. Aberration-corrected HRTEM resolves the atomic structure, local twist angles, and lattice anisotropy of the CuI/r-oxo-G nanohybrid, while STEM-EDX yields a Cu:I ratio consistent with 1:1. First-principles calculations show that van der Waals adhesion to graphene stabilizes the supported hexagonal layer. Under the presented low-temperature precursor conditions, pathways for nucleation of the $γ$-phase are not available, allowing the hexagonal phase to form selectively at the graphene interface. Ab initio molecular dynamics simulations show that the heterostructure retains its hexagonal lattice order at 600 K, including on an open monolayer graphene support. The lateral extent of the growth is limited mainly by remaining interfacial adsorbates. These results establish a route to metastable 2D h-CuI on a chemically inert graphene template, which may be useful for wide-bandgap electronic and optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [176] [A Community-Developed Domain Ontology for Magnetic Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11464)
*Wilfried Hortschitz,Santa Pile,Claas Fillies,Harald Oezelt,Alexander Kovacs,Hans Fangohr,Samuel J. R. Holt,Martin Lang,Andrea Petrocchi,Swapneel Pathak,Michael P. Adams,Jonas Winkler,Thomas G. Woodcock,William Rigaut,Pierre Le Berre,Nora M. Dempsey,Alena Vishina,M. Nur Hasan,Georgia A. Marchant,Heike C. Herper,Thomas Schrefl*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向磁性材料（magnetic materials）的领域本体（domain ontology），在欧洲MaMMoS项目框架下开发，并与EMMO对齐，用于跨尺度形式化描述磁性能并支持语义互操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性材料在能源、交通与传感等技术中至关重要，但其复杂的多尺度行为及多种单位体系共存，长期对数据交换与解读构成障碍。该研究旨在提升数据可复现性、可追溯性，并推动本体在磁学领域的采用。

Method: 在欧盟资助的Magnetic Multiscale Modelling Suite（MaMMoS）项目内开发领域本体，并与Elementary Multiperspective Material Ontology（EMMO）保持一致；通过EMMOntoPy框架实现基于代码且人类可读的本体结构，支持直接操作与版本管理，避免依赖不透明的.owl或.ttl文件。

Result: 该本体形式化了跨多个长度尺度的内禀、磁滞与微结构性质，支持仿真工具、数据库与实验工作流之间的语义互操作，并已作为多个处理各类磁性材料数据的软件工具的基础；本体遵循FAIR原则并开放供社区扩展。

Conclusion: 该本体支持协作开发与透明度提升，有助于促进磁学领域的数据可复现性、可追溯性及本体的推广应用。

Abstract: Magnetic materials play a crucial role in energy-related technologies, mobility, and sensing, but their complex multiscale behaviour and the coexistence of multiple unit systems pose persistent challenges for data exchange and interpretation. This paper presents a domain ontology for magnetic materials, developed within the European Union funded Magnetic Multiscale Modelling Suite (MaMMoS) project and aligned with the Elementary Multiperspective Material Ontology (EMMO). The ontology formalises intrinsic, hysteretic, and microstructural properties across multiple length scales and supports semantic interoperability between simulation tools, databases, and experimental workflows. One key feature of the ontology is its code-based and human-readable structure, enabled through the EMMOntoPy framework, which allows for direct manipulation and versioning without relying on opaque .owl or .ttl files. This facilitates collaborative development and improves transparency. The ontology supports FAIR (Findable, Accessible, Interoperable, Reusable) principles and is openly available for extension by the community. The aim of the MaMMoS project is to foster reproducibility, improve traceability, and promote the adoption of ontology in the magnetism domain. The ontology that has been developed already serves as the foundation for multiple software tools that handle all kinds of magnetic material data.

</details>


### [177] [Skyrmion nucleus resolves the Landauer paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11551)
*Jiyuan Yang,Denan Li,Shi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过拓扑视角解决铁电开关的Landauer佯谬，提出无缺陷PbTiO₃中的临界核是三维极性斯格明子，据此推导出厚度依赖的矫顽场新定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landauer佯谬指出理想单畴铁电体应因三维反相核的巨大退极化惩罚而动力学上无法翻转，导致矫顽场（coercive field）预测值远大于实验，这一长期矛盾亟需解释。

Method: 采用无缺陷PbTiO₃的大规模分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations），识别本征临界核的拓扑结构，并据此建立解析的厚度依赖矫顽场定律。

Result: 临界核为三维极性斯格明子（polar skyrmion），其连续极化旋转与Néel型畴壁自补偿束缚电荷，使退极化能降低数个数量级，预测矫顽场与实验一致；同时推翻了60年的Janovec-Kay-Dunn定律。

Conclusion: 该框架受母体铁电态场致软化控制并由单一材料描述符决定，可涵盖多种铁电体系的实验趋势，并将Kay-Dunn指数-2/3重新解读为有效有限窗口行为，提示隐藏的拓扑过渡态可能广泛影响非平衡相变。

Abstract: Ferroelectric switching is among the most fundamental and widely studied symmetry-breaking processes in physics. However, the Landauer paradox asserts that an ideal single-domain ferroelectric should be kinetically unswitchable, because a three-dimensional reversed nucleus would carry a prohibitive depolarization penalty, leading to unphysically large coercive field (the electric field required to switch the polarization). Here we show that this long-standing paradox is resolved by topology. In defect-free PbTiO3, large-scale molecular dynamics simulations reveal that the intrinsic critical nucleus is a three-dimensional polar skyrmion. Its continuous polarization rotation and Néel-type boundary walls self-compensate bound charge, suppressing the depolarization energy by orders of magnitude and bringing the predicted coercive field into agreement with experiment. This topological nucleus further overturns the 60-year-old Janovec-Kay-Dunn law. We derive an analytical thickness-dependent coercive-field law governed by field-induced softening of the parent ferroelectric state and controlled by a single material descriptor. This framework captures diverse experimental trends across ferroelectric families and recasts the Kay-Dunn exponent of -2/3 as an effective finite-window behavior. These results establish polar topology as an organizing principle for intrinsic switching and suggest that hidden topological transition states may broadly shape nonequilibrium phase transformations traditionally understood as Landau-type symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [178] [Effect of Stress and Surface Roughness on Electrodeposition in All-Solid-State Batteries: A Computational Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11571)
*Kaniza Islam,Ayush Morchhale,Jung-Hyun Kim,Yanzhou Ji,Noriko Katsube*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文构建电-化学-力学耦合（electro-chemo-mechanical coupling）二维连续介质模型，揭示机械应力在锂/固态电解质界面电流分布中的主导作用，并探讨人工中间层工程以提升全固态电池（ASSBs）长期可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全固态电池（all-solid-state batteries, ASSBs）虽具备高能量密度与安全性优势，但固-固界面（solid-solid interfaces）的不稳定性与不兼容性制约其发展；在锂金属 ASSBs 中，即便陶瓷电解质刚度较高，锂仍会经晶界（grain boundaries）穿透，且常由微小的 Li/SE 界面不规则性引发。

Method: 采用带表面粗糙化银汞矿（argyrodite）电解质的锂 ASSB 体系，建立电-化学-力学耦合的二维连续介质模型（two-dimensional continuum model），考察堆叠压力（stack pressures）与外施电流密度下的界面电流分布，并进行参数化研究。

Result: 理论与模拟表明机械应力对界面电流分布起关键作用：细长表面凸起（elongated surface protrusions）周围的显著应力变化是锂非均匀沉积的主因，若去除该应力效应，即使表面粗糙沉积也趋于均匀；在低界面电流密度与交换电流密度比（interfacial current density to exchange current density ratio）下应力效应主导过电位与电流分布，反之表面粗糙度引起的界面面积增大导致的高界面电阻占主导。

Conclusion: 研究提出可通过人工中间层（artificial interlayers）工程调控界面电流分布，为改善 ASSBs 的长期性能与可靠性提供指导。

Abstract: All-solid-state batteries (ASSBs) promise high energy density and enhanced safety, but their development is hindered by instability and incompatibility at solid-solid interfaces. In Li-metal ASSBs, lithium penetration occurs despite stiff ceramic electrolytes via grain boundaries, often initiated by minor Li/SE interfacial irregularities. Here we introduce a two-dimensional continuum model with electro-chemo-mechanical coupling to investigate interfacial current distribution in Li ASSBs with surface-roughened argyrodite electrolyte under stack pressures and applied current density. Our theoretical analysis and simulation studies highlight the critical role of mechanical stress in interfacial current distribution. We find that prominent stress variations around elongated surface protrusions are the key to nonuniform Li deposition, without which Li deposition becomes uniform even on a rough surface. Moreover, our parametric study elucidates that stress effects dominate the overpotential and current distribution under low interfacial current density to exchange current density ratios, otherwise the high interfacial resistance due to surface-roughness-induced interfacial area becomes dominant. With these insights, we also discuss the potential of engineering artificial interlayers to modulate interfacial current distributions, offering guidance for improving the long-term performance and reliability of ASSBs.

</details>


### [179] [High-throughput thermodynamic screening of oxide-scale adhesion across the CoCrFeMnNiAl high-entropy alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11640)
*Dennis Boakye,Chuang Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文以宏观原子模型（macroscopic atom model）结合 McLean 等温式（McLean isotherm）与 Guttmann 模型，对九类 CoCrFeMnNiAl 亚族在 \(\ce{Cr2O3}\) 与 \(\ce{Al2O3}\) 界面的氧化层黏附（oxide-scale adhesion）进行高通量筛选，并建立可迁移的合金设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性元素（reactive element, RE）添加可显著提升高温氧化时的氧化层保持能力，但高熵合金（high-entropy alloys）中最佳 RE 掺杂的选择仍依赖经验，原因在于所需成分分辨率下的热力学图景无法由第一性原理（first-principles）获得。

Method: 采用宏观原子模型，并耦合 McLean 等温式与 Guttmann 模型，在九类 CoCrFeMnNiAl 亚族的 \(\ce{Cr2O3}\) 和 \(\ce{Al2O3}\) 界面上筛选黏附性能，对五种 RE（Hf、Y、Zr、La、Ti）在偏聚（segregation）、黏附增强与硫置换（sulfur displacement）三方面进行排序。

Result: 筛选揭示出与氧化物类型相关的排序反转：Hf 在 \(\ce{Cr2O3}\) 界面占优，La 在 \(\ce{Al2O3}\) 界面占优，其机制为 RE–O 与 RE–基体（RE–matrix）相互作用焓之间的竞争。含 Mn 合金表现出本征抗硫性，与实验观测的氧化特征一致。硫免疫相图（sulfur immunity phase diagram）指出 Al + Mn \(\gtrsim\) 25 at% 的成分在热力学上免疫于硫致黏附损失。所有交叉浓度（crossover concentration）均收敛于由偏聚焓差控制的普适指数关系，提供了可迁移的设计规则。逆向设计（inverse design）给出 \(\ce{Co16Cr16Fe16Ni16Al35}\) 为最优硫免疫成分，未掺杂 RE 时 \(W_\text{sep} = 5.95\) J/m\(^2\)。

Conclusion: 该框架可在缺少第一性原理数据的高熵合金成分空间中预测 RE 掺杂效果与抗硫能力，并给出氧化物依赖的 RE 选择准则、硫免疫成分区间及普适的交叉浓度设计规则，支持无需 RE 掺杂的逆向成分设计。

Abstract: One significant benefit of reactive element (RE) additions is the colossal improvement in oxide-scale retention during high-temperature oxidation. Selecting optimal RE dopants in high-entropy alloys remains empirical because the relevant thermodynamic landscape is inaccessible to first-principles at the required compositional resolution. Here we apply the macroscopic atom model, coupled with McLean isotherm and Guttmann models, to screen adhesion across nine CoCrFeMnNiAl sub-families at \ce{Cr2O3} and \ce{Al2O3} interfaces, ranking five REs (Hf, Y, Zr, La, Ti) for segregation, adhesion enhancement, and sulfur displacement. The screening reveals an oxide-dependent ranking inversion, with Hf dominating at \ce{Cr2O3} and La dominating at \ce{Al2O3}, driven by the interplay between RE--O and RE--matrix interaction enthalpies. Mn-containing alloys exhibit intrinsic sulfur resistance, consistent with their experimentally observed oxidation characteristics. A sulfur immunity phase diagram identifies compositions with Al~+~Mn~$\gtrsim$~25~at\% as thermodynamically immune to S-induced adhesion loss. All crossover concentrations collapse onto a universal exponential governed by the segregation enthalpy difference, providing a transferable design rule. Inverse design identifies \ce{Co16Cr16Fe16Ni16Al35} as the optimal S-immune composition with $W_\text{sep} = 5.95$~J/m$^2$ without RE doping.

</details>


### [180] [Competition between vacancy creation and filling in defect-engineering of hBN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11666)
*Shrirang Chokappa,Manuel Laängle,Barbara Maria Mayer,Vladimir Zoba\vc,Jacob Madsen,Diana Propst,David Lamprecht,Philipp Irschik,Arixin Bo,Clara Kofler,Vinzent Hana,Fabian Kraft,Clemens Mangler,Lado Filipovic,Toma Susi,Jani Kotakoski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过超低能（150 eV）Ar+ 离子辐照自支撑单层六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride, hBN）样品，用扫描透射电子显微镜（scanning transmission electron microscopy, STEM）表征辐照前后的缺陷密度与分布，发现主要产生硼单空位而非模拟预测的其它构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: hBN 被视为可承载量子发射体（quantum emitters）的材料，其发光与点缺陷相关；但要将特定缺陷与其光谱对应，必须实现对缺陷生成机制的可控性，因此需要实验验证实际辐照产生的缺陷类型。

Method: 制备自支撑单层 hBN 样品，施加超低能（150 eV）Ar+ 离子辐照，并在辐照前后通过扫描透射电子显微镜表征缺陷密度与分布，同时与分析势分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟预测进行对比。

Result: 与解析势分子动力学模拟预测相反，辐照后主要观察到硼单空位（boron single vacancies），其次为数量约一半的双空位（double vacancies）；此外，空位被 Si 与 C 杂质原子填充的现象在所生成缺陷中比以往假设更为显著。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明辐照缺陷类型与模拟预测不符，且杂质填充空位的作用被低估，这可能对在 hBN 中选择性制备量子发射体构成挑战。

Abstract: Hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) has recently become the focus of intense research as a material that can host quantum emitters. It is known that such emission is related to point defects, but in order to conclusively correlate specific defects to their spectra, having control over the defect creation mechanism is required. Here, we prepare freestanding, monolayer hBN samples and irradiate them with ultra-low-energy (150 eV) Ar+ ions. The samples are characterized before and after irradiation via scanning transmission electron microscopy to assess the defect density and distribution. Contrary to what analytical potential molecular dynamics simulations have predicted, we predominantly observe boron single vacancies after ion irradiation, followed by double vacancies at half the count. Moreover, we also observe that vacancy filling with Si and C impurity atoms plays a more significant role in the created defects than previously assumed, potentially posing a problem for selective creation of quantum emitters in hBN.

</details>


### [181] [Device Engineering and Performance Optimization of Cu2NiGeS4 Thin-Film Solar Cells with In2S3/MoTe2 Charge-Selective Layers: A Computational Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11755)
*Md Tashfiq Bin Kashem,Hasib Md Abid Bin Farid*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过 SCAPS-1D 模拟设计并优化了 In2S3/CNGS/MoTe2 薄膜太阳能电池结构，预测其光电转换效率可达 28.44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效可持续薄膜光伏需要兼具强光吸收与地球储量丰富、环境友好组分的吸收层材料。Cu2NiGeS4（CNGS）作为四元硫属化合物（quaternary chalcogenide）吸收层具有良好光电性能与高吸收系数，但其光伏潜力、尤其与优化电荷选择层结合的潜力仍研究不足。

Method: 采用 SCAPS-1D 软件构建并系统研究基于 CNGS 的太阳能电池结构，以 In2S3 作为电子传输层（electron-transport layer）、MoTe2 作为空穴传输层（hole-transport layer）。分析能带分布、电场、载流子分布及产生-复合（generation-recombination）特性，并系统优化层厚度、掺杂浓度、体缺陷与界面缺陷密度、复合系数、寄生电阻、温度及光照强度。

Result: 优化器件在 300 K、AM1.5G 光照下预测光电转换效率（PCE）为 28.44%，开路电压（VOC）0.984 V，短路电流密度（JSC）34.39 mA/cm2，填充因子（FF）84.04%。该模拟效率超过本研究涉及的 CNGS 基太阳能电池此前报道的 6.25-21.17% 范围。

Conclusion: In2S3/CNGS/MoTe2 结构被视为下一代薄膜光伏的有前景平台，并为吸收层优化、界面工程及未来实验实现提供基于物理的设计指南。

Abstract: The pursuit of efficient and sustainable thin-film photovoltaics increasingly demands absorber materials that combine strong optical absorption with earth-abundant and environmentally benign constituents. Cu2NiGeS4 (CNGS) has emerged as a promising quaternary chalcogenide absorber owing to its favorable optoelectronic properties and high absorption coefficient, yet its photovoltaic potential remains comparatively underexplored, particularly in conjunction with optimized charge-selective layers. Here, we introduce and comprehensively investigate an CNGS-based solar-cell architecture using SCAPS-1D, with In2S3 and MoTe2 serving as the electron and hole-transport layers respectively. The analysis of energy-band profiles, electric fields, carrier distributions, and generation-recombination characteristics reveals the mechanisms governing carrier separation and extraction across the heterojunctions. Systematic optimization of layer thickness, doping density, bulk and interface defect densities, recombination coefficients, parasitic resistances, temperature, and illumination intensity identifies the key factors limiting device performance. The optimized device is predicted to achieve a power conversion efficiency (PCE) of 28.44% with a open-circuit voltage (VOC) of 0.984 V, short-circuit current density (JSC) of 34.39 mA/cm2, and fill factor (FF) of 84.04%, under AM1.5G illumination at 300 K. This simulated efficiency exceeds the previously reported 6.25-21.17% range for CNGS-based solar cells considered in this study. The findings establish In2S3/CNGS/MoTe2 as a promising platform for next-generation thin-film photovoltaics and provide physically grounded design guidelines for absorber optimization, interface engineering, and future experimental realization.

</details>


### [182] [Piezomagnetism in a model cubic noncollinear altermagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11816)
*Sudarshan Sharma,Luca Buiarelli,Richard Spieker,Ivan Jakovac,Damjan Pelc,Turan Birol,Martin Greven*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文在立方交变磁体（altermagnet）MnTe<sub>2</sub>中发现显著压磁响应（piezomagnetism），通过膨胀测量（dilatometry）与核磁共振（nuclear magnetic resonance, NMR）联合观测并验证了正逆压磁效应，结果与对称性分析和第一性原理计算一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体兼具补偿磁序与自旋极化能带，而压磁效应（晶格应变与净磁矩的线性关系）被视为区分交变磁性与反铁磁性的关键证据，因此需要在具体材料中确证并量化该效应。

Method: 选取具有非共线自旋排列（noncollinear spin arrangement）和弱自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的立方交变磁体 MnTe<sub>2</sub>，将膨胀测量与核磁共振这一体相局域探针相结合，并与对称性分析与第一性原理计算相比对。

Result: 膨胀测量观测到与外加磁场成正比的剪切形变，磁共振探测到由剪切应变诱导的铁磁矩，二者给出的压磁耦合强度相互吻合，并被第一性原理结果所重现。

Conclusion: MnTe<sub>2</sub>被确立为模型交变磁体，证明了核磁共振在压磁研究中的实用性，并为自旋电子学与存储应用中的多模态应变与磁场调控开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Altermagnets constitute a distinct class of magnetic materials that combine compensated magnetic order with spin-polarized electronic bands, hence carrying characteristics of both antiferromagnets and ferromagnets. Piezomagnetism - the linear relationship between lattice strain and a net magnetic moment - has emerged as smoking-gun evidence of altermagnetism that distinguishes it from antiferromagnetism. Here, we uncover a large piezomagnetic response in MnTe2, a cubic altermagnet with a noncollinear spin arrangement and weak spin-orbit coupling. We combine dilatometry with nuclear magnetic resonance, a bulk local probe, to observe signatures of both direct and inverse piezomagnetism consistent with symmetry considerations and first-principles calculations. The dilatometry measurements reveal a shear deformation proportional to an applied magnetic field, while magnetic resonance detects a ferromagnetic moment induced by shear strain. The respective results for the piezomagnetic coupling strength are in good agreement, and they are captured by first-principles results. These findings for simple binary MnTe2 position this material as a model altermagnet, demonstrate the utility of nuclear magnetic resonance in investigations of piezomagnetism, and open new avenues for multimodal strain and magnetic-field control in spintronic and memory applications.

</details>


### [183] [High-fidelity k$\cdot$p representations of first-principles electronic band structures through covariant renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11861)
*Kristian Berland*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过重整化厄米动量矩阵的本征值谱，可显著改善k·p模型对第一性原理能带结构的收敛性与精度，并在GaP、AlN、PbTe等体系验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k·p方法（k·p method）虽可直接用于第一性原理能量与动量矩阵元，但其模型随能带数目收敛缓慢，且在下层哈密顿量为非局域时不再精确，亟需克服这两项限制。

Method: 对厄米动量矩阵（Hermitian momentum matrices）的本征值谱进行重整化（renormalization）；利用其规范不变性（gauge invariance）与对称相关笛卡尔分量谱的同一性，仅需少量缩放参数即保持简并与晶体对称性，无需构造对称适配基。以高对称点发出射线上的参考本征值与能带速度（band velocities）为拟合基准，并可在同一方案内下折叠（downfolding）得到紧凑四带哈密顿量或扩展至全布里渊区建模。

Result: GaP的15带模型在近带隙参考区可于几meV内重现能带结构；闪锌矿（zincblende）与纤锌矿（wurtzite）AlN需30带或66带模型，前者归因于[110]方向的强烈翘曲（warping）。方案可推广至含自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）、L点价带与导带极值的岩盐PbTe。59带GaP模型可在远密于参考计算的网格上求解，用于分辨态密度（density of states）精细结构并计算低温空穴电导率（hole conductivity）。

Conclusion: 重整化动量矩阵本征值谱的方案能有效缓解k·p模型的慢收敛与非局域不精确问题，兼具紧凑模型下折叠与全布里渊区建模能力，并可推广至多类晶体结构与含自旋轨道耦合体系。

Abstract: The k$\cdot$p method can be applied directly to first-principles energies and momentum matrix elements, but the resulting models converge slowly with the number of bands and are inexact wherever the underlying Hamiltonian is nonlocal. We show that both limitations can be largely removed by renormalizing the eigenvalue spectra of the Hermitian momentum matrices. These spectra are gauge invariant, and are identical for symmetry-related Cartesian components, hence scaling the recurring magnitudes provides a modest set of parameters that preserves degeneracies and crystal symmetry without needing to construct a symmetry-adapted basis. We choose to renormalize the models against reference eigenvalues and band velocities on rays out of a high-symmetry point. For GaP, a 15-band model can reproduce the band structure in the near-gap reference regions within a few meV. For zincblende and wurtzite AlN, similar accuracy requires 30- or 66-band models, which in the case of zincblende is traced to the strong warping in the [110] direction. The scheme readily generalizes to rocksalt PbTe, which includes spin-orbit coupling, and has L-centered valence and conduction band extrema. Further, we demonstrate, for GaP, how compact four-band Hamiltonians can be obtained by downfolding within the same renormalization scheme. Moreover, by changing the fitting regime to encompass the entire Brillouin zone, the scheme can be used to generate full-zone models that reproduce the density of states. The practical utility is illustrated with a 59-band k.p model for GaP, which can be evaluated on meshes far denser than the reference calculation, that in turn can be used both to resolve fine features in the density of states and to compute the hole conductivity at low temperature.

</details>


### [184] [High-Temperature ferromagnetism from site-selective filling in (Fe,Ni)$_{6-δ}$GeTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11862)
*Tyler L. Werner,Jonathan T. Reichanadter,Xiang Chen,Pranab K. Nag,Luna Y. Liu,Yu-Tsun Shao,Hongrui Zhang,Mingyang Guo,Wenxin Li,Zhibo Kang,Han Wu,Makoto Hashimoto,Donghui Lu,Turgut Yilmaz,Elio Vescovo,Sung-Kwan Mo,Barat Achinuq,Alexei Fedorov,Jacob C. Ruff,Ming Yi,Qiong Ma,David A. Muller,Eduardo H. da Silva Neto,Robert J. Birgeneau,Jeffrey B. Neaton,Yu He*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作揭示 (Fe,Ni)$_{5+\delta}$GeTe$_2$ 中 478 K 纪录高居里温度源于应变稳定的 Fe$_6$GeTe$_2$ 纳米析出相，而非均匀镍替代，并给出位点分辨的电子结构图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属性范德华（van der Waals, vdW）磁体 Fe$_N$GeTe$_2$ 的高温铁磁性使二维（two-dimensional, 2D）磁体进入技术相关温区，其中 N=5 时镍替代反常地将居里温度（Curie temperature, T$_C$）提升至 478 K，但其机制因结构复杂、巡游与局域磁贡献共存及介观成分畴而难以厘清。

Method: 结合结构与电子性质表征、第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）以及自旋分辨与角分辨光电子能谱（spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）进行协同分析，并将富金属 vdW 磁体与二元磁性合金进行类比。

Result: 识别出高温 T$_C$ 磁相来自应变稳定的 Fe$_6$GeTe$_2$ 纳米析出相（nano-precipitate）；发现位点特异的电子景观：内部铁原子主要承载局域磁矩，邻近碲层的外层铁原子产生可跨越 vdW 间隙的自旋极化巡游载流子；均匀镍替代的高能量代价倾向促使该晶体学与电子学上"干净"的高 T$_C$ 相自发析出。

Conclusion: 研究为 (Fe,Ni)$_{5+\delta}$GeTe$_2$ 的纪录高 T$_C$ 铁磁性提供机制解释，指出纳米析出相在稳定原本难以获得的体相中的普适作用，并为基于直接电子特征的 vdW 磁性金属原子级工程奠定基础。

Abstract: The discovery of high-temperature ferromagnetism in the metallic van der Waals (vdW) system Fe$_N$GeTe$_2$ has brought two-dimensional (2D) magnets into technologically relevant temperature scales. Specifically at N = 5, dilution of magnetic moments by nickel substitution counterintuitively achieves a record high Curie temperature of 478~K. Unraveling the origin of this nickel-substitution-induced enhancement is complicated by the compound's structural complexity, coexistent itinerant and local magnetic contributions, and mesoscopic compositional domains. Through coordinated structural and electronic characterization, we identify that the high-T$_C$ magnetic phase arises from a strain-stabilized Fe$_6$GeTe$_2$ nano-precipitate. Combining first-principles calculations and spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), we uncover a site-specific electronic landscape in which interior iron atoms primarily host localized moments while the outer iron atoms neighboring the tellurium layers produce spin-polarized itinerant carriers that cross the vdW gap. The large energy cost associated with homogeneous nickel substitution is found to favor the spontaneous precipitation of the crystallographically and electronically ``clean'' high-T$_C$ phase. Finally, we compare metal-rich vdW magnets with binary magnetic alloys, and discuss the unifying roles of nano-precipitates in stabilizing otherwise unattainable bulk phases. Our work provides mechanistic insights into the record-high T$_C$ ferromagnetism in (Fe,Ni)$_{5+δ}$GeTe$_2$, establishing a rigorous foundation for the atomic engineering of vdW magnetic metals informed by direct electronic signatures.

</details>


### [185] [Sparse data limit what a mechanistic corrosion model can predict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11869)
*Conrard Giresse Tetsassi Feugmo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: Mechanistic corrosion models are routinely fitted with four to six parameters to a few measurements and extrapolated across service lifetimes, yet whether the data determine them is rarely tested. We identify a reduced point defect model for the duplex oxide on Nb-stabilized AISI 347 in simulated boiling-water-reactor water from published depth profiles at three exposure times, by differentiable inversion graded against a closed-form reference. Profile likelihood, a 1000-member bootstrap and a prior-relaxation test agree that one of the five parameters is undetermined and a second only weakly so, and that 95% of the fit statistic rests on three chromium points. The kinetics track an empirical power law over the calibration window but diverge from it by a factor of 3.5 to 6.3 at ten years, outside the propagated uncertainty band. That separation, not the thickness it brackets, is what these data determine; an exposure near 3000 hours would resolve it.

</details>
