### [186] [Moduli spaces of geometric functorial field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11812)
*Jacek Kenig,Dmitri Pavlov*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套工具，将几何函子量子场论（geometric functorial field theory）的模空间（moduli space）刻画为等变单纯预层（equivariant simplicial presheaves）之间的映射空间（mapping space）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为带有几何结构 F 的函子场论提供模空间的统一构造方式，将模空间表示为映射空间，从而使其可通过等变单纯预层的框架进行计算。

Method: 给定表示在 d 维流形光滑族位点（site）上的预层（presheaf）F，定义其笛卡尔实现（Cartesian realization），即笛卡尔空间位点上的 O(d)-等变单纯预层；并以此将模空间表示为 O(d)-等变单纯预层之间的映射空间。

Result: 建立了以笛卡尔实现呈现函子场论模空间的核心结果，即该模空间可表示为 O(d)-等变单纯预层之间的映射空间。具体的模空间计算在姊妹论文中完成。

Conclusion: 该框架为计算几何函子场论模空间提供了通用工具；其应用（光滑一维有向黎曼函子场论的模空间计算）见姊妹论文。

Abstract: We develop tools to compute moduli spaces of geometric functorial field theories as mapping spaces of equivariant simplicial presheaves. Given a d-dimensional geometric structure F, presented as a presheaf on the site of smooth families of d-manifolds, we define its Cartesian realization, which is an O(d)-equivariant simplicial presheaf on the site of Cartesian spaces. We use Cartesian realizations to present the moduli space of functorial field theories with geometric structure F as a mapping space between O(d)-equivariant simplicial presheaves. In a companion paper, we use this result to compute the moduli space of smooth one-dimensional oriented Riemannian functorial field theories valued in an arbitrary smooth symmetric monoidal infinity-category.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Physics of Information Geometry - Part II: Small-Step Active Inference on the Probability Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11187)
*C. Emre Koksal,Deniz Sargun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文是信息几何（information geometry）物理基础两部分研究的第二篇，独立考察该框架在主动推断（active inference）中的体现。研究通过小步分布移动，将KL散度（Kullback-Leibler divergence）作为概率单纯形（probability simplex）上的离散动能，导出分布运动的最小作用量原理（least-action principle）与闭式最优更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Part I 已为概率单纯形上的分布运动建立物理基础，本文旨在探究该框架如何在主动推断中显现。研究关注由小步局部移动所诱导的几何结构，以及此类局部运动相对大跳跃的优劣势。

Method: 从初始分布出发，智能体经一系列受约束更新向目标分布演化。作者定义相对自由能泛函（relative free energy functional）及其相对势能（relative potential energy），类比Helmholtz/Gibbs自由能分解；并施加每步由相邻分布间KL散度表达的动能约束。利用KL球（KL balls）上的信息几何勾股定理（Pythagorean theorem）分析局部移动与直接跳跃。

Result: 证明足够小的局部移动优于大直接跳跃，且在动能约束下贪婪最大化自由能下降具有全局最优性。由此得到序贯变分原理（sequential variational principle），其最优轨迹极小化优化问题的拉格朗日量（Lagrangian），形式为动能与势能项之差。最优更新呈闭式，为当前分布向偏好分布的指数倾斜（exponentially tilted）形式，由类逆温度乘子参数化。

Conclusion: 分布运动在单纯形上确立了最小作用量原理，主动推断可由受动能约束的局部几何更新刻画。框架进一步扩展以纳入状态依赖测地线（state-dependent geodesic），凸显信息几何与经典力学结构的类比。

Abstract: This paper is the second in a two-part investigation of the physics of information geometry. While Part I develops a physical foundation for distributional motion on the probability simplex, the present paper studies how that framework manifests in active inference. The treatment is fully self-contained and does not require familiarity with Part I. We focus in particular on active inference through small distributional steps and the geometric structure induced by such local motion. Starting from an initial distribution, an agent evolves its belief state toward a final target distribution through a sequence of constrained updates. We define a relative free energy functional with respect to the preferred distribution and extend it to a relative potential energy analogous to the Helmholtz/Gibbs free-energy decomposition. The evolution is subject to a per-step kinetic constraint expressed through the Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence between consecutive distributions, which serves as a discrete kinetic energy on the probability simplex. Using the information-geometric Pythagorean theorem on KL balls, we show that sufficiently small local moves dominate large direct jumps, and that greedy maximization of free-energy reduction is globally optimal under the kinetic constraint. This leads to a sequential variational principle in which the optimal trajectory minimizes the associated Lagrangian of the optimization problem. Similar to classical mechanics, the Lagrangian takes on the form as the difference between the kinetic and potential terms, establishing a least-action principle for distributional motion on the simplex. The resulting optimal update admits a closed form as an exponentially tilted version of the current distribution toward the preferred distribution, parametrized by an inverse-temperature-like multiplier. We further extend the framework to incorporate state-dependent geodesic...

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Thermal Stability of Encapsulated Molecular Structures with Extended OH-Hydrogen-Bond Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10777)
*Alexander V. Savin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 采用粗粒化模型（coarse-grained model）模拟线性分子链在h-BN晶体表面的吸附动力学，发现含苯环与羟基的分子可形成稳定氢键链（hydrogen-bond chains），h-BN片层包覆（van der Waals encapsulation）可显著提升其热稳定性，可用于高温无水质子交换膜（anhydrous proton-exchange membranes）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究吸附于平坦基底（h-BN晶体表面）的线性分子链的动力学行为，并考察氢键链的形成与热稳定性，以评估其在高温无水质子交换膜中的应用潜力。

Method: 使用粗粒化模型进行数值模拟，研究对象为具有苯环与羟基结构的线性分子链（如苯酚、4-苯基苯酚、对乙酰氨基酚、4-羟基苯甲酰苯胺），并进一步模拟h-BN片层包覆（范德华封装）后的体系。

Result: 未包覆时，上述四种分子的氢键链解离温度分别为T1=190、240、300、400 K；经h-BN片层包覆后，氢键链可保持至T2=470、800、880、1140 K，热稳定性显著增强。

Conclusion: 模拟结果表明，h-BN包覆的该类分子链可用于构建可在高温下运行的无水质子交换膜，其中对乙酰氨基酚与4-羟基苯甲酰苯胺的包覆链最具应用前景。

Abstract: Using a coarse-grained model, we performed numerical simulations of the dynamics of linear molecular chains adsorbed on a flat substrate (on the surface of an h-BN crystal). It is shown that molecules containing benzene rings and hydroxyl groups in their structure can form stable hydrogen-bond chains OH$\cdots$OH$\cdots$OH. Such chains can be formed by phenol C$_6$H$_5$OH, 4-phenylphenol C$_6$H$_5$--C$_6$H$_4$OH, paracetamol CH$_3$C(O)NHC$_6$H$_4$OH, and 4-hydroxybenzanilide C$_6$H$_5$C(O)NHC$_6$H$_4$OH molecules. The dissociation of these chains occurs at temperatures above $T_1=190$, 240, 300, and 400K, respectively. Coating such molecular systems with a hexagonal boron nitride sheet (their van der Waals encapsulation) significantly enhances their thermal stability. Such encapsulated molecular structures retain hydrogen-bond chains up to temperatures of $T_2=470$, 800, 880, and 1140K, respectively. The simulations allow us to conclude that h-BN-encapsulated chains of these molecules can be used to create anhydrous proton-exchange membranes capable of operating at high temperatures. The most promising are encapsulated chains of paracetamol and 4-hydroxybenzanilide molecules.

</details>


### [189] [Dissipation-induced nonlocal phase-coherent transport in a topological superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10821)
*S. V. Aksenov,M. S. Shustin,I. S. Burmistrov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在 Kitaev 链中引入马尔可夫耗散（Markovian dissipation），可在无需库仑阻塞（Coulomb blockade）的条件下实现马约拉纳束缚态（Majorana bound states）介导的非局域相位相干输运（即“隐形传态”，teleportation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域相位相干输运通常依赖库仑阻塞来抑制局域安德烈夫反射（local Andreev reflection），该要求构成实现马约拉纳隐形传态的限制条件；本文旨在寻找可替代库仑阻塞的机制。

Method: 将 Kitaev 链与马尔可夫浴（Markovian bath）耦合，研究耗散对输运的影响，并识别非局域电导贡献与局域电导贡献量级相当的参数区间。

Result: 在该区间内，最低能量子空间（lowest-energy subspace）处于最大混合态，类似于直接接地的非平凡拓扑超导体（topological superconductor）；相位相干性由阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆振荡（Aharonov-Bohm oscillations）得到确认。

Conclusion: 耗散（dissipation）可作为控制马约拉纳系统中量子隐形传态的工具。

Abstract: Nonlocal phase-coherent transport via Majorana bound states, so-called teleportation, requires Coulomb blockade to suppress local Andreev reflection. We show that dissipation can replace the need for Coulomb blockade. We study a Kitaev chain coupled to a Markovian bath and identify a regime where the magnitude of nonlocal conductance contribution reach the local one. In this regime, the lowest-energy subspace is maximally mixed, reminiscent of a directly-grounded nontrivial topological superconductor. Phase coherence is confirmed by Aharonov-Bohm oscillations. Our results establish dissipation as a tool for controlling quantum teleportation in Majorana systems.

</details>


### [190] [Controlling topology in flux-mismatched Hofstadter bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10837)
*Adel Ali,Alexey Belyanin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 层间隧穿通常使双层材料能带杂化并消除改变拓扑所需的能带交叉，但在受不同磁通作用的 Hofstadter 双层（Hofstadter bilayer）中，能带交叉与外尔点（Weyl point）被拓扑强制保留，从而提供电控陈相（Chern phase）的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 堆叠二维材料被视为电控拓扑电子态（topological electronic states）的平台，但层间隧穿会杂化能带并移除拓扑转变所需的交叉点，构成实现电控拓扑相的核心障碍。

Method: 研究受不同磁通作用的 Hofstadter 双层体系；当共振磁布洛赫多重态（resonant magnetic Bloch multiplet）携带不等的陈数（Chern number）时，其投影隧穿被拓扑阻碍并在孤立动量处必须为零；通过扫描层偏压扫过这些零点，在动量-偏压空间中产生合成外尔单极子（synthetic Weyl monopole）以转移陈数失配。

Result: 展示两个后果：绝缘陈相之间的直接转变；以及一种重入型补偿金属（reentrant compensated metal），其中 Lifshitz 转变限定金属窗口，内部外尔事件重构能带拓扑。固定填充下的霍尔响应保持连续且非量子化，即使整数陈数在能带间转移。Berry 通量（Berry-flux）、TKNN 与界面计算独立验证该机制及其多通道手性特征。

Conclusion: 该工作确立磁通失配（flux mismatch）为实验上可及的途径，用于实现电可编程陈相（electrically programmable Chern phase）与手性输运（chiral transport），并给出在莫尔（Moiré）与反常霍尔异质结构（anomalous-Hall heterostructure）中的实现方案。

Abstract: Stacked two-dimensional materials provide a promising platform for electrically controlling topological electronic states. However, tunneling between layers hybridizes their bands and removes the crossings needed to change topology. We show that this does not always have to be the case. Band crossings and associated Weyl points are topologically enforced in Hofstadter bilayers whose layers experience different magnetic fluxes. When resonant magnetic Bloch multiplets carry unequal Chern numbers, their projected tunneling is topologically obstructed and must vanish at isolated momenta. Sweeping the layer bias through these zeros creates synthetic Weyl monopoles in momentum-bias space that transfer the Chern mismatch. We demonstrate two consequences: a direct transition between insulating Chern phases and a reentrant compensated metal in which Lifshitz transitions bound the metallic window while internal Weyl events reconstruct the band topology. Consequently, the fixed-filling Hall response remains continuous and nonquantized even as integer Chern number is transferred between bands. Berry-flux, TKNN, and interface calculations independently verify the mechanism and its multichannel chiral signature. We outline realizations in Moire and anomalous-Hall heterostructures, establishing flux mismatch as an experimentally accessible route to electrically programmable Chern phases and chiral transport.

</details>


### [191] [Emergence of spin-orbit coupling among spin, atomic orbital, and Bloch dynamics in Janus double-transition-metal MXenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10953)
*Tetsuro Habe*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在 Janus 双过渡金属 MXene（Mo$_2$HfC$_2$OS 与 W$_2$HfC$_2$OS）中，自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）同时关联电子自旋、轨道与 Bloch 动力学三个自由度，并产生非常规形式的自旋-动量锁定（spin-momentum locking）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究 Janus 双过渡金属 MXene 的电子结构，尤其是自旋-轨道耦合如何影响其自旋、轨道与 Bloch 动力学，并检验其是否可归入已有常规耦合形式。

Method: 作者通过电子结构计算研究 Mo$_2$HfC$_2$OS 与 W$_2$HfC$_2$OS，构建了一个约化哈密顿量（reduced Hamiltonian）以描述其电子态，并在低能与小波数近似下与 LS、Rashba、Dresselhaus 等常规耦合形式进行对比分析。

Result: 自旋-轨道耦合导致 $Γ$ 点附近绝缘带隙（insulating gap）周围出现具有三角图案的交错自旋构型（staggered spin configuration）；由于导带的固有形状，出现了自旋轴垂直于层平面的三角交替自旋-动量锁定。该耦合无法等同于 LS、Rashba 或 Dresselhaus 耦合，即使在低能与小波数近似下亦如此。

Conclusion: 这些 Janus 材料可作为探索三角型自旋-动量锁定所引发自旋相关现象的平台，其机制不同于 Rashba 与 Dresselhaus 类型。

Abstract: We found a spin-orbit coupling to cause a simultaneous correlation among three degrees of freedom, the electronic spin, orbital, and Bloch dynamics in an investigation into the electronic structure of Janus double-transition-metal MXenes, Mo$_2$HfC$_2$OS and W$_2$HfC$_2$OS. In this paper, it is also revealed that the spin-orbit coupling causes a staggered spin configuration with a trigonal pattern around the $Γ$ point near the insulating gap. We developed a reduced Hamiltonian describing the electronic states and show that the spin-orbit coupling cannot be equated with conventional forms for a single electron in solids, LS, Rashba, and Dresselhaus couplings, even in the approximation under the low-energy and small wave number condition. Because of the intrinsic shape of the conduction band, a trigonally alternating spin-momentum locking emerges with the spin axis perpendicular to the layer plane. The theoretical analysis shows that these Janus materials can provide a platform for exploring the spin-related phenomena due to the trigonal spin-momentum locking other than Rashba and Dresselhaus types.

</details>


### [192] [A substrate booster for P-type 2D ferromagnetic semiconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11015)
*Hai Wang,Woye Pei,Ridong Cong,Xiaoyan Liu,Yuping Tian,Kenji Watanabe,Teng Yang,Takashi Taniguchi,Xiangru Kong,Weijiang Gong,Guowei Zhou,Xiaoxi Li,Hanwen Wang,Jixuan Wu,Jiezhi Chen,Xiaohong Xu,Tongyao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种衬底工程（substrate engineering）范式，将少层 Cr$_{2}$Ge$_{2}$Te$_{6}$（FL-CGT）与反铁磁绝缘体 CrOCl 构建界面，借助界面电荷转移耦合将双极性半导体转变为高性能 P 型铁磁半导体，并实现栅压辅助的磁开关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋晶体管（spin transistor）需同时调控电荷与自旋，但其物理基础即磁性半导体（magnetic semiconductor）极为稀缺，仅见于稀磁半导体（DMS）与二维铁磁半导体（2D-FMS）等少数体系，且这些体系的电学和/或磁学性质栅压可调性不足。

Method: 采用衬底工程策略，将少层 Cr$_{2}$Ge$_{2}$Te$_{6}$（FL-CGT）与反铁磁绝缘体 CrOCl 界面接触，利用界面电荷转移耦合，并制备衬底增强的二维铁磁半导体场效应晶体管（FMS-FET），结合计算加以支持。

Result: CGT 由双极性半导体显著转变为高性能 P 型半导体；在低于居里温度（Curie temperature）时，该 FMS-FET 的开关比（ON-OFF ratio）达 10$^{5}$，磁滞回线的矫顽场（coercive field, $H_{c}$）可调幅度超过 200%，实现栅压辅助磁开关；重空穴掺杂下还观察到从临界幂律标度（critical power-law scaling）向双幂律行为的转变。

Conclusion: 研究表明界面电荷转移与电学调控的磁各向异性能（magnetic anisotropy energy）由计算支持；该高性能 P 型 FMS-FET 体系表明主动衬底增强范式可能为未来栅压可调自旋电子学器件（gate-tunable spintronic devices）研究提供有力路径。

Abstract: Spin transistors with its both charge and spin properties tuned via electrostatic gating are believed capable for widespread use, which however have proven challenging due to the extreme rareness of their physical base -- magnetic semiconductors. The latter are limited within very few systems including diluted magnetic semiconductors (DMS) and two-dimensional ferromagnetic semiconductors (2D-FMS), and known to suffer from inadequate gate-tunability of their electric and/or magnetic properties. Here, we show a substrate engineering paradigm by interfacing few-layered Cr$_{2}$Ge$_{2}$Te$_{6}$ (FL-CGT) with an antiferromagnetic insulator CrOCl. Owing to the subtle interfacial charge transfer couplings, CGT can be drastically turned from an ambipolar semiconductor into a high performance P-type semiconductor. When cooled below the Curie temperature, the ON-OFF ratio in such substrate-boosted FMS field-effect transistor (FET) reaches 10$^{5}$ with its coercive field $H_{c}$ of magnetic hysteresis loop tunable by a factor of more than 200$\%$, enabling {gate-assisted magnetic switching in the prototype semiconducting spin transistor architecture}. A crossover from critical power-law scaling to a dual power-law behaviour under heavy hole doping was further observed. Our findings {signify} an efficient interfacial charge transfer and electrically modulated magnetic anisotropy energy supported by calculations. This high performance P-type FMS-FET system suggests that active substrate-boosting paradigm might be a powerful path for the investigation of future gate-tunable spintronic devices.

</details>


### [193] [In-plane magnetic field control of anomalous Hall response enabled by magnetic anisotropy engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11017)
*J. C. Rodriguez E.,E. De Biasi,J. I. Facio,D. Salomoni,S. Auffret,R. C. Sousa,I. L. Prejbeanu,A. Bruchhausen,J. Curiale,L. Avilés-Félix*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作研究了具有Tb厚度梯度、使单轴各向异性轴略微偏离薄膜法线的亚铁磁[Tb/Co]×5多层膜的磁输运行为，揭示了倾斜各向异性对磁输运响应的关键作用，并据此实现了面内磁场对垂直异常霍尔响应的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程化磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）是控制磁化取向并解锁光自旋电子学与电流驱动器件新功能的重要途径。除在磁化反转中的作用外，有效各向异性还可显著影响磁输运响应，为调控器件新功能提供额外自由度。

Method: 生长具有Tb厚度梯度的亚铁磁[ Tb / Co ]×5多层膜，其楔形结构使单轴各向异性轴（uniaxial anisotropy axis）略微偏离薄膜法线；在80 K至300 K进行异常霍尔电阻率（anomalous Hall resistivity）测量及磁输运响应的角度依赖测量；并采用简化的宏自旋模型（macrospin model）进行拟合。

Result: 测量揭示出自旋重取向转变（spin reorientation transition），角度依赖的磁输运响应凸显了倾斜各向异性的关键作用。简化宏自旋模型可复现整个转变过程的完整角度响应，并表明面内磁场下观测到的异常霍尔效应源于单轴各向异性轴的倾斜，该倾斜提供了内建对称性破缺机制，使面内磁场能够控制垂直异常霍尔响应（含符号）。

Conclusion: 研究确立倾斜磁各向异性（tilted magnetic anisotropy）是实现基于霍尔效应传感器应用的有前景路径，并表明Tb/Co多层膜是各向异性工程自旋电子器件（anisotropy-engineered spintronic devices）的多功能平台。

Abstract: Engineering magnetic anisotropy provides a powerful route to control magnetization orientation and unlock emerging functionalities in opto-spintronic and current-driven devices. Beyond its role in magnetization reversal, the effective anisotropy can strongly influence the magnetotransport response, offering an additional degree of freedom to tune new device functionalities. In this work, we report a magnetotransport study of a ferrimagnetic [Tb/Co]$_{\times 5}$ multilayer grown with a Tb thickness gradient, whose wedge-shaped tilts the uniaxial anisotropy axis slightly away from the film normal. Anomalous Hall resistivity measurements from 80 K to 300 K reveal a spin reorientation transition, while the angular dependence of the magnetotransport responses exposes the crucial role of the tilted anisotropy. A simplified macrospin model reproduces the full angular response across the transition and shows that the observed anomalous Hall effect when the in-plane magnetic field is applied originates from the tilt of the uniaxial anisotropy axis, which supplies a built-in symmetry-breaking mechanism, enabling in-plane field control over the out-of-plane anomalous Hall response, sign included. These findings establish tilted magnetic anisotropy as a promising route toward Hall effect-based sensor applications and highlight Tb/Co multilayers as a versatile platform for anisotropy-engineered spintronic devices.

</details>


### [194] [Fingerprints of Excitonic Collective Modes in the Two-Dimensional Electron Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11138)
*Jakob Wolff,Silvana Botti,Lucia Reining,Matteo Gatti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用含时密度泛函理论（time-dependent density functional theory, TDDFT）研究低密度二维均匀电子气（2D homogeneous electron gas, 2D HEG）的集体电荷激发，发现除传统等离激元（plasmon）外存在新的集体激子模式（excitonic modes），并预言其可观测特征及向电荷密度波（charge-density-wave）相的不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维均匀电子气是可实验实现的基准体系，其低密度集体激发行为对理解多体效应（many-body effects）具有基础意义；传统框架主要关注等离激元，低密度区域是否存在其他集体模式尚待澄清。

Method: 采用含时密度泛函理论（time-dependent density functional theory, TDDFT）计算低密度下二维均匀电子气的集体电荷激发，分析损耗函数（loss function）与静态线性密度响应。

Result: 当维格纳-赛茨半径（Wigner-Seitz radius）大于约 r_s ≈ 1 时，除传统等离激元外出现新的集体激子模式；其表现为损耗函数中的非对称峰结构，以及静态线性密度响应中随体系趋近不稳定区而增强的强烈弗里德尔型振荡（Friedel-like oscillations）。在足够低密度下，集体模式穿过零能轴。

Conclusion: 低密度下集体模式穿越零能轴表明顺磁性二维均匀电子气（paramagnetic 2D HEG）存在向激子起源的电荷密度波（charge-density-wave）相转变的不稳定性，为在可调二维电子系统中实验探测激子集体模式提供依据，并推进对低密度电子气多体效应的理解。

Abstract: The two-dimensional homogeneous electron gas (2D HEG) is a prototype system of fundamental interest that can be realized experimentally over a wide range of densities. Here, we investigate its collective charge excitations at low densities, using time-dependent density functional theory. We demonstrate that, beyond traditional plasmons, new collective excitonic modes emerge for a Wigner-Seitz radius larger than $r_{\mathrm s} \approx 1$. These excitonic modes leave characteristic fingerprints in experimentally accessible quantities, namely, asymmetric peak structures in the loss function and very strong Friedel-like oscillations in the static linear density response that increase when the system approaches a regime of instability. Indeed, at low enough densities the collective modes cross the zero energy axis, indicating an instability of the paramagnetic 2D HEG towards the formation of a charge-density-wave phase with excitonic origin. These findings provide valuable insights for the experimental detection of excitonic collective modes in tunable 2D electron systems and contribute to the fundamental understanding of many-body effects in low-density electron gases.

</details>


### [195] [Enhancing charge stability of Ge quantum well heterostructures via SiGe layer composition engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11214)
*Ding-Ming Huang,Jun-Hang Liu,Han Gao,Jie-Yin Zhang,Jian-Huan Wang,Fang-Ze Liu,Xin-Yu Zhou,Yi Luo,Bin-Xiao Fu,Xiao-Fei Liu,Ji-Yin Wang,Jian-Jun Zhang,H. Q. Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作将组分调制（composition modulation）引入 SiGe/Ge 量子阱异质结，通过梯度组分调控 SiGe 顶势垒能带结构，抑制界面电荷积累并增强对空穴气的有效限制，从而获得显著提升的电荷稳定性（charge stability），并在量子点器件中观测到 $1\,\mathrm{Hz}}$ 下平均 $0.46\,\mathrm{μeV}/\mathrm{\sqrt{Hz}}}$ 的低电荷噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋量子比特（spin qubit）器件面临电荷稳定性不足的关键挑战，而组分调制是一种可为半导体器件定制材料性质的有效手段，因此本文尝试将该技术应用于 Ge 量子阱异质结以解决该问题。

Method: 利用分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy）的原子级精度，对 SiGe 顶势垒进行梯度组分调制以工程化其能带结构，从而减少 SiGe-介质界面处的电荷积累态并增强对 Ge 量子阱中空穴气（hole gases）的有效限制；随后在霍尔器件（Hall devices）与量子点器件（quantum dot devices）中验证其电荷稳定性。

Result: 组分调制后的 SiGe/Ge 量子阱异质结在霍尔器件中表现出扩大的稳定栅压范围；由其制备的量子点器件在 $1\,\mathrm{Hz}}$ 处呈现平均幅度为 $0.46\,\mathrm{μeV}/\mathrm{\sqrt{Hz}}}$ 的极低电荷噪声，为硅衬底上生长的 Ge 量子阱已报道的最低值；该稳定性在少空穴区（few-hole regime）持续存在，约数小时内无可观测的电压漂移。

Conclusion: 凭借降低的电荷噪声与增强的能量稳定性，组分调制 SiGe/Ge 异质结在构建高性能量子器件（包括具有长相干时间的自旋量子比特）方面具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Composition modulation is a powerful technique for designing materials with tailored properties, fueling the development of advanced semiconductor devices. In this work, we have implemented this technique into Ge quantum well heterostructures, offering a promising avenue to address the critical challenge of charge stability in spin qubit devices. Harnessing the atomic-scale precision of molecular beam epitaxy, we have engineered the band structure of the SiGe top barrier via graded composition modulation, thereby reducing charge accumulation states at the SiGe-dielectric interface and strengthening the effective confinement to the hole gases in the Ge quantum wells. The enhanced charge stability of composition-modulated SiGe/Ge quantum well heterostructures is confirmed in Hall devices, featuring an enlarged stable gate voltage range. We have further fabricated quantum dot devices from the composition-modulated SiGe/Ge quantum well heterostructures and observed remarkably low charge noise with an averaged amplitude of $0.46\,\mathrm{μeV}/\mathrm{\sqrt{Hz}}$ at $1\,\mathrm{Hz}$---the lowest reported value for Ge quantum wells grown on silicon. This exceptional charge stability of the quantum dots persists in the few-hole regime, with no observable voltage drift over $\sim$hours. With reduced charge noise and enhanced energy stability, composition-modulated SiGe/Ge heterostructures exhibit significant potential for applications in building high-performance quantum devices, including spin qubits with a long coherence time.

</details>


### [196] [Néel-Vector-Dependent Altermagnetic Spin Splitting in the One-Dimensional Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11547)
*Xin Chen,Duo Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作指出在一维受限的全补偿共线磁体中，非相对论性自旋简并可与相对论性自旋劈裂共存：当边界兼容的子晶格交换操作不改变唯一布洛赫动量时，常规交替磁（altermagnetism）判据失效，但自旋轨道耦合（SOC）仍可通过锁定自旋与晶格产生可观测的能带劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁（altermagnetism）通常依靠全补偿共线磁体中的非相对论性自旋劈裂能带被识别，但该判据在一维受限下可能失效：边界兼容的子晶格交换操作可保持唯一布洛赫动量不变，迫使自旋向上与自旋向下谱重合，因此需要重新审视一维体系中的交替磁特性及其可观测判据。

Method: 基于 Lieb 型构造（Lieb-based construction）进行分析，指出沿对角方向的限域会抵消投影后的 $d$ 波自旋劈裂，同时保留实空间子晶格交换图案；随后引入自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC），使自旋与晶格锁定，并由 Néel 矢量取向选择可展现自旋劈裂能带的磁性线群（magnetic line group）。以完全补偿的 $[110]$ Ta$_2$TeSeO 纳米带（nanoribbon）为原型进行验证。

Result: 在 $[110]$ Ta$_2$TeSeO 纳米带中，继承的易轴（easy-axis）磁畴（$\mathbf N\parallel[100]$ 或 $[010]$）里存在非相对论性自旋简并能带与可观的自旋轨道耦合（SOC）劈裂。对于足够宽且保留母体易轴序的纳米带，该劈裂是平衡态属性；易轴纳米带在有限理想电导下还支持约 $35\%$ 的右行模式自旋极化。

Conclusion: 一维完全补偿的交替磁功能可以隐藏在看似常规的反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）能带之中，表明交替磁判据在一维限域下需结合相对论效应（如 SOC）与 Néel 矢量取向来识别。

Abstract: Altermagnetism is normally identified by nonrelativistic spin-split bands in a compensated collinear magnet. Under one-dimensional confinement, however, this diagnostic can disappear: a boundary-compatible sublattice-exchange operation may leave the only Bloch momentum unchanged, forcing the nonrelativistic spin-up and spin-down spectra to coincide. We show that nonrelativistic spin degeneracy can coexist with relativistic spin splitting in a compensated one-dimensional magnet. In a Lieb-based construction, confinement along the diagonal cancels the projected $d$-wave spin splitting while preserving the real-space sublattice-exchange motif. Spin-orbit coupling (SOC) then locks spin to the lattice, so the Néel-vector orientation selects a magnetic line group that can reveal spin-split bands. Using fully compensated $[110]$ Ta$_2$TeSeO nanoribbons as a prototype, we find spin-degenerate nonrelativistic bands and sizable SOC splitting in the inherited easy-axis domain, $\mathbf N\parallel[100]$ or $[010]$. For sufficiently wide ribbons that retain the parent easy-axis order, this splitting is an equilibrium property. The easy-axis ribbon also supports a right-moving-mode spin polarization of about $35\%$ at finite ideal conductance. Our results establish one-dimensional fully compensated altermagnetic functionality hidden inside apparently conventional antiferromagnetic bands.

</details>


### [197] [Phase-Controlled Majorana Zero Modes in Altermagnetic Topological-Insulator Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11633)
*Hao Dong,Xun-Jiang Luo,Xiao-Hong Pan,Xin Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出利用晶面依赖的安德烈夫相位移动，在三维交变磁性拓扑绝缘体约瑟夫森结中通过超导相位偏置调控拓扑超导性，实现并空间操控马约拉纳零能模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻求一种无需精细调节即可实现并空间控制马约拉纳零能模（Majorana zero modes, MZMs）的平台，是关键动机。

Method: 在两侧传统 s 波超导体之间的弱连接中引入 d 波交变磁序，分析其对表面狄拉克锥（Dirac cones）动量的晶面依赖移动，并计算由此产生的安德烈夫反射（Andreev reflection）传播相位与晶面分辨的安德烈夫谱。

Result: 晶面分辨的安德烈夫谱在不同相位偏置下出现能隙闭合，形成承载 MZMs 的拓扑超导区；调节相位偏置可控制这些模式的空间局域化；相关拓扑超导转变对化学势的中等变化仅弱敏感。

Conclusion: 交变磁性拓扑绝缘体约瑟夫森结可作为通过相位偏置实现并空间调控 MZMs 的平台。

Abstract: We exploit facet-dependent Andreev phase shifts to control topological superconductivity with a phase bias in a three-dimensional altermagnetic topological-insulator Josephson junction. In the weak link between two conventional s-wave superconductors, the d-wave altermagnetic order produces facet-dependent momentum shifts of the surface Dirac cones. The resulting net momentum of the states involved in Andreev reflection generates additional propagation phases that differ between facets. Consequently, the facet-resolved Andreev spectra exhibit gap closings at distinct phase biases, giving rise to topological superconducting regimes that host Majorana zero modes(MZMs). We further show that tuning the phase bias controls the spatial localization of these modes. The associated topological superconducting transitions are only weakly affected by moderate variations in the chemical potential, obviating the need for fine-tuning to the Dirac point. Our results establish a platform for realizing and spatially controlling MZMs by tuning the superconducting phase bias in altermagnetic topological-insulator Josephson junctions.

</details>


### [198] [Bridging steady-state and time-domain descriptions of molecular electron transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11684)
*Thibaut Lacroix,Namgee Cho,Clemens Vittmann,James Lim,Susana F. Huelga,Martin B. Plenio*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将有限尺寸波包（wave packet）透射与非平衡格林函数（NEGF）的能量分辨透射建立定量关系，并据此构造非高斯辅助波包以直接从时间演化获得电流-电压特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子从输入电极经分子系统到输出电极的输运通常用稳态非平衡格林函数（NEGF）方法研究；近期波包方法可获取瞬态动力学及分子内部自由度，并被用于手性分子的对映特异性电子输运，但两类方法之间的定量联系尚不明确。

Method: 推导有限尺寸波包透射与能量分辨 NEGF 透射之间的定量关系，证明前者是后者以波包能量分布为权重的谱平均（spectral average）；基于该对应关系构造非高斯辅助波包（non-Gaussian auxiliary wave packets），使其谱权重编码 Landauer 能量窗口；并通过时间传播获得电流-电压特性。

Result: 所推导的对应关系成立：有限尺寸波包透射等于 NEGF 透射的加权谱平均；利用非高斯辅助波包可直接由时间传播得到电流-电压特性；该对应关系进一步扩展至手性诱导自旋选择性（chirality-induced spin selectivity）的自旋-声子模型中的自旋分辨输运。

Conclusion: 波包方法与 NEGF 方法之间存在定量的谱平均对应关系，可用于从瞬态时间演化直接获取稳态输运量（如电流-电压特性），并可推广至自旋分辨输运情形。

Abstract: Electron transmission from an input electrode, through a molecular system, to an output electrode has been widely studied using the steady-state non-equilibrium Green's function (NEGF) method. Recently, the wave packet method, which provides access to the transient dynamics of electrons as well as internal molecular degrees of freedom, has been employed to investigate enantiospecific electron transport through chiral molecules. In this work, we derive the quantitative relation between the transmission of a finite-size wave packet and the energy-resolved NEGF transmission, showing that the former corresponds to a spectral average of the latter weighted by the wave packet energy distribution. Exploiting this correspondence, we construct non-Gaussian auxiliary wave packets whose spectral weight encodes the Landauer energy-window, allowing current-voltage characteristics to be obtained directly from time propagation. We further show that the correspondence extends to spin-resolved transport in a spin-phonon model of chirality-induced spin selectivity.

</details>


### [199] [Interplay of Kekulé order, flat bands and electron-phonon coupling in graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11711)
*Dominik Szczȩśniak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过晶格畸变导致的碳键与跃迁差异化，统一解释了Kekulé序石墨烯中平带与电子-声子耦合协同增强的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kekulé序石墨烯已知会在范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularity, VHS）附近产生扩展平带并增强电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling），但二者共存的微观机制尚不清楚。

Method: 从晶格畸变出发，将Kekulé序描述为碳键差异及其对应电子跃迁（electronic hoppings）的变化，并据此分析M点附近导带宽度、VHS处态密度（density of states）及无量纲电子-声子耦合对Kekulé序的依赖关系。

Result: M点附近导带宽度随Kekulé序近乎线性减小；VHS与无量纲电子-声子耦合呈近似二次上升响应；电子-声子耦合增强主要由VHS处态密度增大主导，有效形变势（deformation potential）受畸变影响较弱。

Conclusion: 结果与既有理论与实验研究一致，并提示在特征1/4填充下导带可能变浅，以及超出常规Migdal近似（Migdal approximation）的非绝热电子-声子效应（nonadiabatic electron-phonon effects）重要性上升；表明可控Kekulé序有望用于调控石墨烯中的关联量子相。

Abstract: The Kekulé order in graphene is known to give rise to extended flat bands near the van Hove singularity (VHS) and enhanced electron-phonon coupling. However, the microscopic mechanism underlying the coexistence of these effects remains unclear. Here, it is shown that they jointly emerge as a consequence of the lattice distortion manifested through the differentiation of carbon bonds and associated electronic hoppings. In this picture, the conduction band around the $M$ point decreases almost linearly in width with increasing Kekulé order, while the VHS and dimensionless electron-phonon coupling exhibit approximately quadratic upward responses. The resulting enhancement of the electron-phonon coupling is governed predominantly by the increasing density of states at the VHS, with the effective deformation potential remaining weakly affected by the distortion. As such, the obtained results are consistent with previous related theoretical and experimental studies, while also pointing toward a potential shallowing of the conduction band under the characteristic $1/4$ filling and an increasing relevance of nonadiabatic electron-phonon effects beyond the conventional Migdal approximation. Altogether, these findings highlight the potential of controlled Kekulé ordering for engineering emergent correlated quantum phases in graphene.

</details>


### [200] [Hilbert-space selected switch of helical edges in an artificial quantum Hall insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11732)
*Naijie Ren,Zhiren Xiong,Kaining Yang,Yanran Shi,Hanwen Wang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Neng Wan,Xiaojun Jia,Jianpeng Liu,Zheng Vitto Han,Yaning Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在CrOCl与大角度扭转双层石墨烯界面电荷转移的调控下，两层石墨烯原本解耦的Dirac朗道能级阶梯发生偏移并形成复合填充态；其中电中性处的(+2,-2)态完全绝缘（纵向电阻达GΩ量级），而(+6,-6)、(+10,-10)等态保留螺旋边缘通道并表现出显著的非局域输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔效应（quantum Hall effects, QHE）具有一维拓扑保护边缘通道，是构建奇异量子电子系统的重要要素，但通过静电约束或对称性破缺来人工重构朗道能级拓扑在实验上仍具挑战。该工作旨在寻找一种可直接调控朗道能级拓扑的途径。

Method: 采用CrOCl与大角度扭转双层石墨烯（large-angle twisted bilayer graphene）构成的界面体系，利用界面电荷转移使两层石墨烯中原本解耦的Dirac朗道能级阶梯发生偏移，从而构造出新的复合填充构型；并结合实验与理论分析所观测能隙态（gapped states）的相互作用本质。

Result: 电中性处的复合(+2,-2)态仅涉及零级朗道能级并完全绝缘，纵向电阻达到GΩ量级；而更高填充的复合零填充量子霍尔态（包括(+6,-6)与(+10,-10)）保留反向传播的螺旋边缘通道（helical edge channels），非局域输运显著，最高可达局域响应的50%。该开关行为归因于朗道旋量希尔伯特空间（Landau-spinor Hilbert space）：填充从(+6,-6)降至(+2,-2)时，正交的N=±1轨道分量被移除，边缘兼容通道消失，体态与边界输运同时被打开能隙。

Conclusion: 结果表明，电荷转移为人工工程量子霍尔绝缘体（artificial quantum Hall insulators）提供了一条直接途径，并为螺旋边缘模式（helical edge modes）的波函数选择性调控开辟了可能。

Abstract: Quantum Hall effects (QHE) host one-dimensional topologically-protected edge channels, which can serve as an essential ingredient in exotic quantum electronic systems. Yet the manual reconstruction of Landau-level topology, by electrostatic confinement or symmetry breaking, remains experimentally challenging. Here, we show that interfacial charge transfer in between CrOCl and large-angle twisted bilayer graphene offsets the two otherwise decoupled Dirac Landau-level ladders in each graphene layer, creating a new sequence of composite filling configurations. At charge neutrality, the composited $(+2,-2)$ state involves only the zeroth Landau levels and becomes fully insulating, with longitudinal resistance reaching the G$Ω$ regime. By contrast, higher composite zero-filling quantum Hall states, including $(+6,-6)$ and $(+10,-10)$, retain counter-propagating helical edge channels and exhibit pronounced non-local transport, reaching up to $50\%$ of the local response. We attribute such switching-behavior to the Landau-spinor Hilbert space -- as the filling is reduced from $(+6,-6)$ to $(+2,-2)$, the orthogonal $N=\pm1$ orbital components are removed, eliminating the edge-compatible channel and gapping both bulk and boundary transport. The interaction nature of the observed gapped sates was further examined both experimentally and theoretically. Our results suggest that charge transfer provides a direct route to engineer artificial quantum Hall insulators, opening possibilities for wavefunction-selective control of helical edge modes.

</details>


### [201] [Interacting Tomonaga-Lüttinger liquid with impurity for interaction constant $K = 1/2$: Thermopower investigation, entropy variation and heat capacity densities associated with thermoelectric particle transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11810)
*Abdellah Touati,Redoune Zamoum,Farhi Houssam Eddine*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了含局域杂质、相互作用参数 \(K=1/2\) 的 Tomonaga-Luttinger 液体（Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid）的热电与热力学性质，借助玻色化（bosonization）与重费米化（refermionization）导出非线性区热电势（thermopower）的精确表达式及线性响应下的塞贝克系数（Seebeck coefficient）。结果表明，低温下塞贝克系数遵循与透射系数能量依赖相关的 Mott 型关系，并与单粒子熵（entropy per particle）密切相关。由此进一步给出熵密度变化的热电势贡献与热容密度的载流子贡献，二者在低温区均呈现 Mott 型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究相互作用一维量子系统中热电输运与热力学性质之间的内在联系。为此选取含局域杂质的 \(K=1/2\) Tomonaga-Luttinger 液体这一可精确处理的具体模型，以刻画热电势与熵、热容等热力学量的对应关系。

Method: 采用玻色化与重费米化方法，推导非线性区热电势的精确表达式，并在线性响应下得到相应的塞贝克系数；进一步借助 Kelvin 公式（Kelvin formula）导出熵密度变化的热电势贡献与热容密度的载流子贡献。

Result: 低温下塞贝克系数遵循由透射系数能量依赖所支配的 Mott 型关系，且与单粒子熵密切相关；经 Kelvin 公式得到的熵密度变化热电势贡献与热容密度载流子贡献在低温区均表现出 Mott 型行为。结果被应用于分数量子霍尔量子点接触（fractional quantum Hall quantum point contact）以及耦合于欧姆环境（Ohmic environment）的单通道量子导体。

Conclusion: 结果证明了相互作用一维量子系统中热电输运与热力学性质之间存在紧密联系。

Abstract: We investigate thermoelectric and thermodynamic properties of a Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid with interaction parameter $K=1/2$ in the presence of a localized impurity. Using bosonization and refermionization, we derive an exact expression for the thermopower in the nonlinear regime and obtain the corresponding Seebeck coefficient in linear response. At low temperature, the Seebeck coefficient follows a Mott-like relation governed by the energy dependence of the transmission coefficient and is found to be closely related to the entropy per particle. This connection allows us to derive using Kelvin formula the thermopower contribution to the entropy density variation and the charge-carriers contribution to the heat capacity density. Both quantities exhibit Mott-like behavior in the low-temperature regime. We apply our results to a fractional quantum Hall quantum point contact and to a one-channel quantum conductor coupled to an Ohmic environment. Our results demonstrate the close connection between thermoelectric transport and thermodynamic properties in interacting one-dimensional quantum systems.

</details>
